// GGX surface, Beer-Lambert thickness, approximate SSS/HG and thin-film tint.
struct Camera {
    eye:vec4<f32>,right:vec4<f32>,up:vec4<f32>,forward:vec4<f32>,view:vec4<f32>,material:vec4<f32>,light:vec4<f32>,post:vec4<f32>
}
@group(0) @binding(0) var depth:texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var pigment:texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> cam:Camera;
struct Quad {
    @builtin(position) pos:vec4<f32>,@location(0) uv:vec2<f32>
}
@vertex fn fullscreen(@builtin(vertex_index) i:u32)->Quad{
    var p=array<vec2<f32>,3>(vec2(-1.,-1.),vec2(3.,-1.),vec2(-1.,3.));
    var out:Quad;
    out.pos=vec4(p[i],0.,1.);
    out.uv=p[i]*vec2(.5,-.5)+.5;
    return out;
}
fn ray(p:vec2<f32>)->vec3<f32>{
    let uv=(p/cam.view.xy*2.-1.)*vec2(1.,-1.);
    return normalize(cam.forward.xyz+cam.right.xyz*uv.x*cam.forward.w*cam.view.x/cam.view.y+cam.up.xyz*uv.y*cam.forward.w);
}
fn position(p:vec2<i32>,fallback:f32)->vec3<f32>{
    let size=vec2<i32>(textureDimensions(depth));
    let q=clamp(p,vec2(0),size-1);
    var z=textureLoad(depth,q,0).r;
    if(z>=9.99){
        z=fallback;
    }
    let r=ray(vec2<f32>(q)+.5);
    return cam.eye.xyz+r*z/max(dot(r,cam.forward.xyz),.01);
}
fn environment(r:vec3<f32>)->vec3<f32>{
    let ceiling=pow(max(r.y,0.),5.);
    let panel=pow(max(dot(r,normalize(vec3(-.5,.7,.4))),0.),45.);
    return mix(vec3(.012,.018,.035),vec3(.15,.19,.25),r.y*.5+.5)+vec3(.9,.8,.7)*ceiling+vec3(5.,5.5,6.)*panel;
}
fn phase(cosine:f32,g:f32)->f32{
    return (1.-g*g)/(12.5663706*pow(max(1.+g*g-2.*g*cosine,.001),1.5));
}
fn brdf(n:vec3<f32>,v:vec3<f32>,l:vec3<f32>,albedo:vec3<f32>,F0:vec3<f32>)->vec3<f32>{
    let h=normalize(v+l);
    let nl=max(dot(n,l),0.);
    let nv=max(dot(n,v),.001);
    let nh=max(dot(n,h),0.);
    let vh=max(dot(v,h),0.);
    let a=max(.025,cam.material.x*cam.material.x);
    let a2=a*a;
    let denom=nh*nh*(a2-1.)+1.;
    let D=a2/(3.14159265*denom*denom);
    let k=(cam.material.x+1.)*(cam.material.x+1.)/8.;
    let G=nv/(nv*(1.-k)+k)*nl/max(nl*(1.-k)+k,.001);
    let F=F0+(1.-F0)*pow(1.-vh,5.);
    return ((1.-F)*albedo/3.14159265+D*G*F/max(4.*nl*nv,.001))*nl;
}
@fragment fn shade(in:Quad)->@location(0) vec4<f32>{
    let pixel=vec2<i32>(in.pos.xy);
    let z=textureLoad(depth,pixel,0).r;
    let r=ray(in.pos.xy);
    let background=environment(r)*.12+vec3(.008,.006,.018);
    if(z>=9.99){
        return vec4(background,1.);
    }
    let p=position(pixel,z);
    let xp=position(pixel+vec2(1,0),z)-p;
    let xm=p-position(pixel-vec2(1,0),z);
    let yp=position(pixel+vec2(0,1),z)-p;
    let ym=p-position(pixel-vec2(0,1),z);
    let dx=select(xm,xp,abs(dot(xp,cam.forward.xyz))<abs(dot(xm,cam.forward.xyz)));
    let dy=select(ym,yp,abs(dot(yp,cam.forward.xyz))<abs(dot(ym,cam.forward.xyz)));
    var n=normalize(cross(dx,dy)+vec3(1e-8));
    let v=normalize(cam.eye.xyz-p);
    if(dot(n,v)<0.){
        n=-n;
    }
    let raw=textureLoad(pigment,pixel,0);
    let thickness=clamp(raw.a,0.,4.);
    let albedo=clamp(raw.rgb/max(raw.a,.00001),vec3(.015),vec3(.98));
    let sigma=-log(albedo)*cam.material.y;
    let trans=exp(-sigma*thickness);
    let nv=max(dot(n,v),0.);
    let wavelength=vec3(650.,510.,475.);
    let film=.5+.5*cos(12.5663706*1.33*cam.material.w*max(.2,nv)/wavelength);
    let F0=mix(vec3(.035),vec3(.035)+.18*film,cam.light.w);
    var color=vec3(0.);
    let l1=normalize(vec3(-.6,.9,.8));
    let l2=normalize(vec3(.8,.1,-.6));
    let l3=normalize(vec3(-.4,-.5,-.8));
    color+=brdf(n,v,l1,albedo,F0)*vec3(4.,3.8,3.5);
    color+=brdf(n,v,l2,albedo,F0)*vec3(1.1,1.5,2.4);
    color+=brdf(n,v,l3,albedo,F0)*vec3(1.8,.6,1.2);
    let reflection=reflect(-v,n);
    let F=F0+(1.-F0)*pow(1.-nv,5.);
    color+=environment(reflection)*F;
    let back=pow(max(dot(-n,l2),0.),2.)+phase(dot(-v,l2),cam.light.z)*2.;
    color+=cam.material.z*(1.-trans)*albedo*(.4+back)*vec3(.9,1.1,1.5);
    let bend=r+(.18*(1.-trans.r))*n;
    let behind=environment(normalize(bend))*.2;
    color+=trans*behind*(1.-F);
    color+=.02*albedo*pow(1.-nv,3.);
    return vec4(max(color,vec3(0.)),1.);
}

ARQUIVO: nova-core/src/compute_shaders/chromatic/fluid_post.wgsl

// Bounded image-space lens/bloom; ACES fitted display curve, not full ACES OCIO.
struct Post {
    view:vec4<f32>,lens:vec4<f32>,style:vec4<f32>
}
@group(0) @binding(0) var hdr:texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var depth:texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(2) var linear:sampler;
@group(0) @binding(3) var<uniform> cfg:Post;
struct Quad {
    @builtin(position) pos:vec4<f32>,@location(0) uv:vec2<f32>
}
@vertex fn fullscreen(@builtin(vertex_index) i:u32)->Quad{
    var p=array<vec2<f32>,3>(vec2(-1.,-1.),vec2(3.,-1.),vec2(-1.,3.));
    var out:Quad;
    out.pos=vec4(p[i],0.,1.);
    out.uv=p[i]*vec2(.5,-.5)+.5;
    return out;
}
fn aces(x:vec3<f32>)->vec3<f32>{
    return clamp((x*(2.51*x+.03))/(x*(2.43*x+.59)+.14),vec3(0.),vec3(1.));
}
@fragment fn post(in:Quad)->@location(0) vec4<f32>{
    let uv=in.uv;
    let size=cfg.view.xy;
    let z=textureLoad(depth,vec2<i32>(clamp(in.pos.xy,vec2(0.),size-1.)),0).r;
    let coc=min(12.,cfg.lens.y*abs(z-cfg.lens.x)/max(z,.1));
    var color=textureSampleLevel(hdr,linear,uv,0.).rgb;
    var weight=1.;
    var bloom=vec3(0.);
    for(var i=0;i<16;i++){
        let a=f32(i)*2.399963;
        let radius=sqrt((f32(i)+.5)/16.);
        let direction=vec2(cos(a),sin(a));
        let aperture=direction*radius*coc/size;
        let sample=textureSampleLevel(hdr,linear,clamp(uv+aperture,vec2(0.),vec2(1.)),0.).rgb;
        if(coc>.5){
            color+=sample;
            weight+=1.;
        }
        let light=textureSampleLevel(hdr,linear,clamp(uv+direction*radius*24./size,vec2(0.),vec2(1.)),0.).rgb;
        bloom+=max(light-vec3(1.),vec3(0.))/16.;
    }
    color=color/weight+cfg.lens.z*bloom;
    let vignette=1.-cfg.style.x*dot(uv-.5,uv-.5);
    let noise=fract(sin(dot(in.pos.xy,vec2(12.9898,78.233))+cfg.view.z*.13)*43758.5453)-.5;
    let mapped=pow(aces(color*cfg.lens.w),vec3(1./2.2))*vignette+noise*cfg.style.y;
    return vec4(clamp(mapped,vec3(0.),vec3(1.)),1.);
}

// Surface splats: sphere depth and additive pigment optical thickness.
struct Particle {
    p:vec4<f32>,v:vec4<f32>,previous:vec4<f32>
}
struct Camera {
    eye:vec4<f32>,right:vec4<f32>,up:vec4<f32>,forward:vec4<f32>,view:vec4<f32>,material:vec4<f32>,light:vec4<f32>,post:vec4<f32>
}
@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage,read> particles:array<Particle>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var<uniform> cam:Camera;
@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage,read> palette:array<vec4<f32>>;
struct Vertex {
    @builtin(position) position:vec4<f32>,@location(0) uv:vec2<f32>,@location(1) @interpolate(flat) depth:f32,@location(2) @interpolate(flat) pigment:vec3<f32>
}
@vertex fn splat(@builtin(vertex_index) vi:u32,@builtin(instance_index) pi:u32)->Vertex{
    var corners=array<vec2<f32>,6>(vec2(-1.,-1.),vec2(1.,-1.),vec2(-1.,1.),vec2(-1.,1.),vec2(1.,-1.),vec2(1.,1.));
    let p=particles[pi];
    let corner=corners[vi];
    let radius=cam.view.z;
    let local=p.p.xyz-cam.eye.xyz;
    let z=dot(local,cam.forward.xyz);
    let x=dot(local,cam.right.xyz)+corner.x*radius;
    let y=dot(local,cam.up.xyz)+corner.y*radius;
    let near=.05;
    let far=10.;
    var out:Vertex;
    out.position=vec4(x/(cam.forward.w*cam.view.x/cam.view.y),y/cam.forward.w,far/(far-near)*z-far*near/(far-near),z);
    out.uv=corner;
    out.depth=z;
    out.pigment=palette[u32(p.p.w)].rgb;
    return out;
}
struct DepthOut {
    @location(0) depth:f32,@builtin(frag_depth) hardware:f32
}
@fragment fn sphere_depth(in:Vertex)->DepthOut{
    let q=dot(in.uv,in.uv);
    if(q>=1.||in.depth<.06){
        discard;
    }
    let z=max(.051,in.depth-cam.view.z*sqrt(1.-q));
    var out:DepthOut;
    out.depth=z;
    out.hardware=10./9.95-.5/(9.95*z);
    return out;
}
@fragment fn optical_thickness(in:Vertex)->@location(0) vec4<f32>{
    let q=dot(in.uv,in.uv);
    if(q>=1.||in.depth<.06){
        discard;
    }
    let thickness=2.*cam.view.z*sqrt(1.-q);
    return vec4(in.pigment*thickness,thickness);
}


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova-core/src/compute_shaders/chromatic/particle_life.wgsl
========================================================================================
// NOVA Particle Life + optional finite PBF constraints. All state stays on GPU.
struct Particle {
    p:vec4<f32>, v:vec4<f32>, previous:vec4<f32>
}
struct Sim {
    counts:vec4<u32>, dynamics:vec4<f32>, fluid:vec4<f32>, art:vec4<f32>, extra:vec4<f32>
}
@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage,read> src:array<Particle>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage,read_write> dst:array<Particle>;
@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage,read_write> heads:array<atomic<i32>>;
@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage,read_write> links:array<i32>;
@group(0) @binding(4) var<storage,read_write> density:array<vec4<f32>>;
@group(0) @binding(5) var<storage,read> affinity:array<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(6) var<storage,read_write> stats:array<atomic<u32>>;
@group(0) @binding(7) var<uniform> cfg:Sim;
fn wrap(p:vec3<f32>)->vec3<f32>{
    return p-2.*floor((p+1.)/2.);
}
fn delta(a:vec3<f32>,b:vec3<f32>)->vec3<f32>{
    return wrap(a-b);
}
fn cell(p:vec3<f32>)->vec3<i32>{
    return vec3<i32>(floor((wrap(p)+1.)*.5*f32(cfg.counts.y)));
}
fn key(p:vec3<i32>)->u32{
    let n=i32(cfg.counts.y);
    let q=((p%vec3(n))+vec3(n))%vec3(n);
    return u32((q.z*n+q.y)*n+q.x);
}
fn weight(r2:f32)->f32{
    let h=cfg.dynamics.y;
    let q=max(h*h-r2,0.);
    return 315./(64.*3.14159265*pow(h,9.))*q*q*q;
}
fn gradient(d:vec3<f32>)->vec3<f32>{
    let r=length(d);
    let h=cfg.dynamics.y;
    if(r<1e-6||r>=h){
        return vec3(0.);
    }
    return -45./(3.14159265*pow(h,6.))*(h-r)*(h-r)*d/r;
}
fn neighbor_cell(c:vec3<i32>,k:i32)->u32{
    return key(c+vec3(k%3-1,(k/3)%3-1,k/9-1));
}
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn clear_grid(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id:vec3<u32>){
    let i=id.x;
    let n=cfg.counts.y;
    if(i<n*n*n){
        atomicStore(&heads[i],-1);
    }
}
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn clear_stats(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id:vec3<u32>){
    if(id.x<4u){
        atomicStore(&stats[id.x],0u);
    }
}
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn build_grid(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id:vec3<u32>){
    let i=id.x;
    if(i>=cfg.counts.x){
        return;
    }
    links[i]=atomicExchange(&heads[key(cell(src[i].p.xyz))],i32(i));
}
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn predict(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id:vec3<u32>){
    let i=id.x;
    if(i>=cfg.counts.x){
        return;
    }
    let old=src[i];
    let c=cell(old.p.xyz);
    let h=cfg.dynamics.y;
    var force=vec3(0.);
    var viscous=vec3(0.);
    var shear=0.;
    var neighbors=0.;
    var visited=0u;
    var truncated=false;
    for(var k=0;k<27;k++){
        var j=atomicLoad(&heads[neighbor_cell(c,k)]);
        loop{
            if(j<0){
                break;
            }
            if(visited>=cfg.counts.w){
                truncated=true;
                break;
            }
            visited++;
            let other=src[u32(j)];
            if(u32(j)!=i){
                let d=delta(other.p.xyz,old.p.xyz);
                let r=length(d);
                if(r>1e-5&&r<h){
                    let q=r/h;
                    let a=affinity[u32(old.p.w)*8u+u32(other.p.w)];
                    let beta=cfg.extra.x;
                    var f=q/beta-1.;
                    if(q>=beta){
                        f=a*(1.-abs(2.*q-1.-beta)/(1.-beta));
                    }
                    force+=d/r*f;
                    let w=1.-q;
                    viscous+=(other.v.xyz-old.v.xyz)*w;
                    shear+=length(other.v.xyz-old.v.xyz)*w/max(r,.01);
                    neighbors+=1.;
                }
            }
            j=links[u32(j)];
        }
        if(truncated){
            break;
        }
    }
    if(truncated){
        atomicAdd(&stats[0],1u);
    }
    if(cfg.extra.y>.5){
        force/=max(neighbors,1.);
    }
    viscous/=max(neighbors,1.);
    shear/=max(neighbors,1.);
    let dt=cfg.dynamics.x;
    let structure=(old.v.w+dt*.5)/(1.+dt*(.5+shear));
    let eta=clamp(cfg.fluid.w+cfg.art.w*structure/(.05+shear),0.,2.);
    let p=old.p.xyz;
    let t=f32(cfg.counts.z)*dt;
    let curl=vec3(sin(2.*p.z+t*.2)+cos(2.*p.y),sin(2.*p.x)+cos(2.*p.z+t*.2),sin(2.*p.y)+cos(2.*p.x));
    force=cfg.dynamics.z*force+eta*viscous+cfg.art.x*curl+vec3(0.,cfg.art.y,0.)-cfg.art.z*p*max(dot(p,p)-.64,0.);
    var velocity=(old.v.xyz+dt*force)*exp(-cfg.dynamics.w*dt);
    let speed=length(velocity);
    if(speed>cfg.extra.z){
        velocity*=cfg.extra.z/speed;
        atomicAdd(&stats[1],1u);
    }
    var out=old;
    out.previous=old.p;
    if(cfg.extra.w>.5){
        velocity.z=0.;
        out.previous.z=0.;
    }
    out.p=vec4(wrap(p+dt*velocity),old.p.w);
    if(cfg.extra.w>.5){
        out.p.z=0.;
    }
    out.v=vec4(velocity,clamp(structure,0.,1.));
    dst[i]=out;
}
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn solve_density(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id:vec3<u32>){
    let i=id.x;
    if(i>=cfg.counts.x){
        return;
    }
    let p=src[i].p.xyz;
    let c=cell(p);
    var rho=0.;
    var norm=0.;
    var sum_gradient=vec3(0.);
    var visited=0u;
    var truncated=false;
    for(var k=0;k<27;k++){
        var j=atomicLoad(&heads[neighbor_cell(c,k)]);
        loop{
            if(j<0){
                break;
            }
            if(visited>=cfg.counts.w){
                truncated=true;
                break;
            }
            visited++;
            let d=delta(p,src[u32(j)].p.xyz);
            let r2=dot(d,d);
            if(r2<cfg.dynamics.y*cfg.dynamics.y){
                rho+=cfg.fluid.x*weight(r2);
                let g=cfg.fluid.x/cfg.fluid.y*gradient(d);
                if(u32(j)!=i){
                    norm+=dot(g,g);
                    sum_gradient+=g;
                }
            }
            j=links[u32(j)];
        }
        if(truncated){
            break;
        }
    }
    if(truncated){
        atomicAdd(&stats[0],1u);
    }
    let constraint=max(rho/cfg.fluid.y-1.,0.);
    density[i]=vec4(-constraint/(norm+dot(sum_gradient,sum_gradient)+cfg.fluid.z),rho,0.,0.);
    atomicMax(&stats[2],u32(min(rho,1000.)*1000.));
}
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn project(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id:vec3<u32>){
    let i=id.x;
    if(i>=cfg.counts.x){
        return;
    }
    let old=src[i];
    let p=old.p.xyz;
    let c=cell(p);
    var correction=vec3(0.);
    var visited=0u;
    var truncated=false;
    for(var k=0;k<27;k++){
        var j=atomicLoad(&heads[neighbor_cell(c,k)]);
        loop{
            if(j<0){
                break;
            }
            if(visited>=cfg.counts.w){
                truncated=true;
                break;
            }
            visited++;
            if(u32(j)!=i){
                let d=delta(p,src[u32(j)].p.xyz);
                if(dot(d,d)<cfg.dynamics.y*cfg.dynamics.y){
                    let relative=weight(dot(d,d))/max(weight(.09*cfg.dynamics.y*cfg.dynamics.y),1e-6);
                    let anti_clump=-.0005*pow(relative,4.);
                    correction+=(density[i].x+density[u32(j)].x+anti_clump)*gradient(d)*cfg.fluid.x/cfg.fluid.y;
                }
            }
            j=links[u32(j)];
        }
        if(truncated){
            break;
        }
    }
    if(truncated){
        atomicAdd(&stats[0],1u);
    }
    let magnitude=length(correction);
    if(magnitude>.04){
        correction*=.04/magnitude;
        atomicAdd(&stats[3],1u);
    }
    var out=old;
    out.p=vec4(wrap(p+correction),old.p.w);
    dst[i]=out;
}
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn finalize(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id:vec3<u32>){
    let i=id.x;
    if(i>=cfg.counts.x){
        return;
    }
    var out=src[i];
    var velocity=delta(out.p.xyz,out.previous.xyz)/cfg.dynamics.x;
    if(cfg.extra.w>.5){
        velocity.z=0.;
    }
    let speed=length(velocity);
    out.v=vec4(velocity*min(1.,cfg.extra.z/max(speed,1e-6)),out.v.w);
    dst[i]=out;
}


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova-core/src/compute_shaders/fields.wgsl
========================================================================================
// Periodic 3D, nondimensional f32 model. Each invocation writes one cell.
struct Cell { chem: vec4<f32>, flow: vec4<f32>, aux: vec4<f32> }
struct Params { n:u32, stage:u32, dt:f32, feed:f32, kill:f32, du:f32, dv:f32, viscosity:f32, speed:f32, damping:f32, row_stride:u32, pad1:f32 }
@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage,read> src:array<Cell>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage,read_write> dst:array<Cell>;
@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> cfg:Params;
fn index(p:vec3<i32>)->u32 { let n=i32(cfg.n);let a=((p%vec3(n))+vec3(n))%vec3(n);return u32((a.z*n+a.y)*n+a.x); }
fn at(p:vec3<i32>)->Cell {return src[index(p)];}
fn sample_flow(q:vec3<f32>)->vec4<f32> {let lo=vec3<i32>(floor(q));let f=fract(q);var out=vec4(0.);for(var z=0;z<2;z++){for(var y=0;y<2;y++){for(var x=0;x<2;x++){let d=vec3(x,y,z);let w=select(vec3(1.)-f,f,d==vec3(1));out+=at(lo+d).flow*w.x*w.y*w.z;}}}return out;}
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn main(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid:vec3<u32>){let i=gid.x+gid.y*cfg.row_stride;let n=cfg.n;if(i>=n*n*n){return;}let p=vec3<i32>(i32(i%n),i32((i/n)%n),i32(i/(n*n)));let c=src[i];let ex=vec3(1,0,0);let ey=vec3(0,1,0);let ez=vec3(0,0,1);var out=c;
let xp=at(p+ex);let xm=at(p-ex);let yp=at(p+ey);let ym=at(p-ey);let zp=at(p+ez);let zm=at(p-ez);
if(cfg.stage==0u){let lap=xp.chem+xm.chem+yp.chem+ym.chem+zp.chem+zm.chem-6.*c.chem;let reaction=c.chem.x*c.chem.y*c.chem.y;out.chem.x=clamp(c.chem.x+cfg.dt*(cfg.du*lap.x-reaction+cfg.feed*(1.-c.chem.x)),0.,1.);out.chem.y=clamp(c.chem.y+cfg.dt*(cfg.dv*lap.y+reaction-(cfg.feed+cfg.kill)*c.chem.y),0.,1.);out.chem.w=c.chem.w+cfg.dt*(cfg.speed*cfg.speed*lap.z-cfg.damping*c.chem.w);out.chem.z=c.chem.z+cfg.dt*out.chem.w;
}else if(cfg.stage==1u){let advected=sample_flow(vec3<f32>(p)-cfg.dt*c.flow.xyz);let lap=xp.flow+xm.flow+yp.flow+ym.flow+zp.flow+zm.flow-6.*c.flow;out.flow=advected+cfg.dt*cfg.viscosity*lap;
}else if(cfg.stage==2u){out.aux.x=0.;out.aux.y=(c.flow.x-xm.flow.x+c.flow.y-ym.flow.y+c.flow.z-zm.flow.z)/cfg.dt;
}else if(cfg.stage==3u){out.aux.x=(xp.aux.x+xm.aux.x+yp.aux.x+ym.aux.x+zp.aux.x+zm.aux.x-c.aux.y)/6.;
}else if(cfg.stage==4u){out.flow.x-=cfg.dt*(xp.aux.x-c.aux.x);out.flow.y-=cfg.dt*(yp.aux.x-c.aux.x);out.flow.z-=cfg.dt*(zp.aux.x-c.aux.x);}
dst[i]=out;}

//! Native SoA-compatible cell batches; no claim of FPS or power consumption.
use pyo3::{exceptions::PyValueError, prelude::*};
use rayon::prelude::*;
#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Default)]
#[cfg_attr(feature = "webgpu", derive(bytemuck::Pod, bytemuck::Zeroable))]
pub struct Cell {
    pub chem: [f32; 4],
    pub flow: [f32; 4],
    pub aux: [f32; 4],
}

#[pyclass]
pub struct NativeField {
    pub n: usize,
    pub cells: Vec<Cell>,
    pub ticks: u64,
}
#[pymethods]
impl NativeField {
    #[new]
    #[pyo3(signature=(n, chemistry, max_bytes=536870912))]
    fn new(n: usize, chemistry: Vec<f32>, max_bytes: usize) -> PyResult<Self> {
        if !(4..=256).contains(&n)
            || n.checked_pow(3)
                .and_then(|x| x.checked_mul(144))
                .is_none_or(|bytes| bytes > max_bytes)
            || chemistry.len() != 2 * n * n * n
            || chemistry
                .iter()
                .any(|x| !x.is_finite() || *x < 0. || *x > 1.)
        {
            return Err(PyValueError::new_err(
                "invalid field dimensions/state/memory budget",
            ));
        }
        let cells = chemistry
            .chunks_exact(2)
            .map(|x| Cell {
                chem: [x[0], x[1], 0., 0.],
                ..Cell::default()
            })
            .collect();
        Ok(Self { n, cells, ticks: 0 })
    }
    #[pyo3(signature=(steps=1,dt=0.1,feed=0.035,kill=0.065,du=0.16,dv=0.08,viscosity=0.02,speed=1.,damping=0.02,fluid=false,pressure_iterations=32))]
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    fn step(
        &mut self,
        py: Python<'_>,
        steps: usize,
        dt: f32,
        feed: f32,
        kill: f32,
        du: f32,
        dv: f32,
        viscosity: f32,
        speed: f32,
        damping: f32,
        fluid: bool,
        pressure_iterations: usize,
    ) -> PyResult<()> {
        validate(
            steps,
            dt,
            &[feed, kill, du, dv, viscosity, speed, damping],
            pressure_iterations,
        )?;
        py.allow_threads(|| {
            for _ in 0..steps {
                self.cells = cpu_stage(
                    &self.cells,
                    self.n,
                    0,
                    dt,
                    [feed, kill, du, dv, viscosity, speed, damping],
                );
                if fluid {
                    for stage in [1, 2] {
                        self.cells = cpu_stage(
                            &self.cells,
                            self.n,
                            stage,
                            dt,
                            [feed, kill, du, dv, viscosity, speed, damping],
                        );
                    }
                    for _ in 0..pressure_iterations {
                        self.cells = cpu_stage(
                            &self.cells,
                            self.n,
                            3,
                            dt,
                            [feed, kill, du, dv, viscosity, speed, damping],
                        );
                    }
                    self.cells = cpu_stage(
                        &self.cells,
                        self.n,
                        4,
                        dt,
                        [feed, kill, du, dv, viscosity, speed, damping],
                    );
                }
                self.ticks += 1;
            }
        });
        Ok(())
    }
    fn snapshot(&self) -> (Vec<f32>, Vec<f32>, Vec<f32>, u64) {
        snapshot(&self.cells, self.ticks)
    }
    fn inject(&mut self, index: usize, velocity: [f32; 3], signal: f32) -> PyResult<()> {
        if index >= self.cells.len()
            || velocity.iter().any(|v| !v.is_finite() || v.abs() > 10.)
            || !signal.is_finite()
            || signal.abs() > 1.
        {
            return Err(PyValueError::new_err("invalid injection"));
        }
        self.cells[index].flow[..3].copy_from_slice(&velocity);
        self.cells[index].chem[3] += signal;
        Ok(())
    }
}
pub fn snapshot(cells: &[Cell], tick: u64) -> (Vec<f32>, Vec<f32>, Vec<f32>, u64) {
    (
        cells
            .iter()
            .flat_map(|c| c.chem[..2].iter().copied())
            .collect(),
        cells
            .iter()
            .flat_map(|c| c.flow[..3].iter().copied())
            .collect(),
        cells.iter().map(|c| c.chem[2]).collect(),
        tick,
    )
}
pub fn validate(steps: usize, dt: f32, params: &[f32], pressure: usize) -> PyResult<()> {
    if !(1..=1024).contains(&steps)
        || !dt.is_finite()
        || dt <= 0.
        || dt > 0.1
        || params.iter().any(|v| !v.is_finite() || *v < 0. || *v > 1.)
        || 6. * params[2].max(params[3]).max(params[4]) * dt > 0.5
        || dt * params[5] * 3_f32.sqrt() > 0.5
        || !(1..=256).contains(&pressure)
    {
        return Err(PyValueError::new_err(
            "field stability/iteration budget exceeded",
        ));
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn cpu_stage(src: &[Cell], n: usize, stage: u32, dt: f32, v: [f32; 7]) -> Vec<Cell> {
    (0..src.len())
        .into_par_iter()
        .map(|i| {
            let p = [i % n, (i / n) % n, i / (n * n)];
            let idx = |q: [isize; 3]| {
                let x = q[0].rem_euclid(n as isize) as usize;
                let y = q[1].rem_euclid(n as isize) as usize;
                let z = q[2].rem_euclid(n as isize) as usize;
                (z * n + y) * n + x
            };
            let p = p.map(|x| x as isize);
            let c = src[i];
            let mut out = c;
            let mut plus = [Cell::default(); 3];
            let mut minus = plus;
            for a in 0..3 {
                let mut q = p;
                q[a] += 1;
                plus[a] = src[idx(q)];
                q[a] -= 2;
                minus[a] = src[idx(q)];
            }
            match stage {
                0 => {
                    let mut lap = [0.; 4];
                    for (j, l) in lap.iter_mut().enumerate() {
                        *l = (0..3)
                            .map(|a| plus[a].chem[j] + minus[a].chem[j])
                            .sum::<f32>()
                            - 6. * c.chem[j];
                    }
                    let r = c.chem[0] * c.chem[1] * c.chem[1];
                    out.chem[0] = (c.chem[0] + dt * (v[2] * lap[0] - r + v[0] * (1. - c.chem[0])))
                        .clamp(0., 1.);
                    out.chem[1] = (c.chem[1]
                        + dt * (v[3] * lap[1] + r - (v[0] + v[1]) * c.chem[1]))
                        .clamp(0., 1.);
                    out.chem[3] += dt * (v[5] * v[5] * lap[2] - v[6] * c.chem[3]);
                    out.chem[2] += dt * out.chem[3];
                }
                1 => {
                    let q = [
                        p[0] as f32 - dt * c.flow[0],
                        p[1] as f32 - dt * c.flow[1],
                        p[2] as f32 - dt * c.flow[2],
                    ];
                    let lo = q.map(|x| x.floor() as isize);
                    let f = q.map(|x| x - x.floor());
                    out.flow = [0.; 4];
                    for z in 0..2 {
                        for y in 0..2 {
                            for x in 0..2 {
                                let d = [x, y, z];
                                let w = (0..3)
                                    .map(|a| if d[a] == 1 { f[a] } else { 1. - f[a] })
                                    .product::<f32>();
                                let cell = src[idx([lo[0] + x, lo[1] + y, lo[2] + z])];
                                for j in 0..4 {
                                    out.flow[j] += w * cell.flow[j];
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    for j in 0..4 {
                        out.flow[j] += dt
                            * v[4]
                            * ((0..3)
                                .map(|a| plus[a].flow[j] + minus[a].flow[j])
                                .sum::<f32>()
                                - 6. * c.flow[j]);
                    }
                }
                2 => {
                    out.aux[0] = 0.;
                    out.aux[1] = (0..3).map(|a| c.flow[a] - minus[a].flow[a]).sum::<f32>() / dt;
                }
                3 => {
                    out.aux[0] = ((0..3)
                        .map(|a| plus[a].aux[0] + minus[a].aux[0])
                        .sum::<f32>()
                        - c.aux[1])
                        / 6.
                }
                4 => {
                    for a in 0..3 {
                        out.flow[a] -= dt * (plus[a].aux[0] - c.aux[0]);
                    }
                }
                _ => (),
            };
            out
        })
        .collect()
}

//! Optional wgpu 0.19 compute backend. Buffers stay resident until snapshot.
use crate::fields::{snapshot, validate, Cell};
use pyo3::{exceptions::PyRuntimeError, prelude::*};
use wgpu::util::DeviceExt;
#[pyclass]
pub struct GpuField {
    device: wgpu::Device,
    queue: wgpu::Queue,
    pipeline: wgpu::ComputePipeline,
    buffers: [wgpu::Buffer; 2],
    layout: wgpu::BindGroupLayout,
    n: usize,
    current: usize,
    tick: u64,
    adapter: String,
}
#[pymethods]
impl GpuField {
    #[new]
    #[pyo3(signature=(n,chemistry,max_bytes=536870912))]
    fn new(n: usize, chemistry: Vec<f32>, max_bytes: usize) -> PyResult<Self> {
        if !(4..=256).contains(&n)
            || n.checked_pow(3)
                .and_then(|v| v.checked_mul(144))
                .is_none_or(|v| v > max_bytes)
            || chemistry.len() != 2 * n * n * n
            || chemistry
                .iter()
                .any(|v| !v.is_finite() || *v < 0. || *v > 1.)
        {
            return Err(PyRuntimeError::new_err(
                "GPU memory/dimension/state budget exceeded",
            ));
        }
        pollster::block_on(async {
            let instance = wgpu::Instance::default();
            let adapter = instance
                .request_adapter(&wgpu::RequestAdapterOptions::default())
                .await
                .ok_or_else(|| PyRuntimeError::new_err("no WebGPU adapter"))?;
            let info = format!("{:?}", adapter.get_info());
            let bytes = (n * n * n * 48) as u64;
            let limits = adapter.limits();
            if bytes > limits.max_storage_buffer_binding_size as u64
                || bytes > limits.max_buffer_size
            {
                return Err(PyRuntimeError::new_err(
                    "adapter storage/dispatch limit exceeded",
                ));
            }
            let (device, queue) = adapter
                .request_device(
                    &wgpu::DeviceDescriptor {
                        label: Some("nova-fields"),
                        required_features: wgpu::Features::empty(),
                        required_limits: limits,
                    },
                    None,
                )
                .await
                .map_err(|e| PyRuntimeError::new_err(e.to_string()))?;
            let cells: Vec<Cell> = chemistry
                .chunks_exact(2)
                .map(|x| Cell {
                    chem: [x[0], x[1], 0., 0.],
                    ..Cell::default()
                })
                .collect();
            let buffers = [0, 1].map(|_| {
                device.create_buffer_init(&wgpu::util::BufferInitDescriptor {
                    label: Some("nova-volume"),
                    contents: bytemuck::cast_slice(&cells),
                    usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE
                        | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_SRC
                        | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST,
                })
            });
            let layout = device.create_bind_group_layout(&wgpu::BindGroupLayoutDescriptor {
                label: None,
                entries: &[
                    entry(0, true),
                    entry(1, false),
                    wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry {
                        binding: 2,
                        visibility: wgpu::ShaderStages::COMPUTE,
                        ty: wgpu::BindingType::Buffer {
                            ty: wgpu::BufferBindingType::Uniform,
                            has_dynamic_offset: false,
                            min_binding_size: None,
                        },
                        count: None,
                    },
                ],
            });
            let shader = device.create_shader_module(wgpu::ShaderModuleDescriptor {
                label: Some("nova-fields"),
                source: wgpu::ShaderSource::Wgsl(
                    include_str!("compute_shaders/fields.wgsl").into(),
                ),
            });
            let pl = device.create_pipeline_layout(&wgpu::PipelineLayoutDescriptor {
                label: None,
                bind_group_layouts: &[&layout],
                push_constant_ranges: &[],
            });
            let pipeline = device.create_compute_pipeline(&wgpu::ComputePipelineDescriptor {
                label: None,
                layout: Some(&pl),
                module: &shader,
                entry_point: "main",
            });
            Ok(Self {
                device,
                queue,
                pipeline,
                buffers,
                layout,
                n,
                current: 0,
                tick: 0,
                adapter: info,
            })
        })
    }
    fn inject(&mut self, index: usize, velocity: [f32; 3], signal: f32) -> PyResult<()> {
        if index >= self.n * self.n * self.n
            || velocity.iter().any(|v| !v.is_finite() || v.abs() > 10.)
            || !signal.is_finite()
            || signal.abs() > 1.
        {
            return Err(PyRuntimeError::new_err("invalid GPU impulse"));
        }
        self.queue.write_buffer(
            &self.buffers[self.current],
            (index * 48 + 12) as u64,
            bytemuck::cast_slice(&[signal]),
        );
        self.queue.write_buffer(
            &self.buffers[self.current],
            (index * 48 + 16) as u64,
            bytemuck::cast_slice(&velocity),
        );
        Ok(())
    }
    #[getter]
    fn adapter(&self) -> String {
        self.adapter.clone()
    }
    #[pyo3(signature=(steps=1,dt=0.1,feed=0.035,kill=0.065,du=0.16,dv=0.08,viscosity=0.02,speed=1.,damping=0.02,fluid=false,pressure_iterations=32))]
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    fn step(
        &mut self,
        py: Python<'_>,
        steps: usize,
        dt: f32,
        feed: f32,
        kill: f32,
        du: f32,
        dv: f32,
        viscosity: f32,
        speed: f32,
        damping: f32,
        fluid: bool,
        pressure_iterations: usize,
    ) -> PyResult<()> {
        validate(
            steps,
            dt,
            &[feed, kill, du, dv, viscosity, speed, damping],
            pressure_iterations,
        )?;
        py.allow_threads(|| {
            let mut encoder = self
                .device
                .create_command_encoder(&wgpu::CommandEncoderDescriptor { label: None });
            for _ in 0..steps {
                let mut stages = vec![0];
                if fluid {
                    stages.extend([1, 2]);
                    stages.extend(std::iter::repeat(3).take(pressure_iterations));
                    stages.push(4);
                }
                for stage in stages {
                    let groups = (self.n * self.n * self.n).div_ceil(64);
                    let columns = groups
                        .min(self.device.limits().max_compute_workgroups_per_dimension as usize);
                    let mut params = vec![self.n as u32, stage];
                    params.extend(
                        [dt, feed, kill, du, dv, viscosity, speed, damping].map(f32::to_bits),
                    );
                    params.extend([(columns * 64) as u32, 0]);
                    let uniform =
                        self.device
                            .create_buffer_init(&wgpu::util::BufferInitDescriptor {
                                label: None,
                                contents: bytemuck::cast_slice(&params),
                                usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::UNIFORM,
                            });
                    let group = self.device.create_bind_group(&wgpu::BindGroupDescriptor {
                        label: None,
                        layout: &self.layout,
                        entries: &[
                            wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                                binding: 0,
                                resource: self.buffers[self.current].as_entire_binding(),
                            },
                            wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                                binding: 1,
                                resource: self.buffers[1 - self.current].as_entire_binding(),
                            },
                            wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                                binding: 2,
                                resource: uniform.as_entire_binding(),
                            },
                        ],
                    });
                    {
                        let mut pass = encoder.begin_compute_pass(&wgpu::ComputePassDescriptor {
                            label: None,
                            timestamp_writes: None,
                        });
                        pass.set_pipeline(&self.pipeline);
                        pass.set_bind_group(0, &group, &[]);
                        pass.dispatch_workgroups(
                            columns as u32,
                            groups.div_ceil(columns) as u32,
                            1,
                        );
                    }
                    self.current = 1 - self.current;
                }
                self.tick += 1;
            }
            self.queue.submit(Some(encoder.finish()));
        });
        Ok(())
    }
    fn snapshot(&self, py: Python<'_>) -> PyResult<(Vec<f32>, Vec<f32>, Vec<f32>, u64)> {
        py.allow_threads(|| {
            let size = (self.n * self.n * self.n * 48) as u64;
            let read = self.device.create_buffer(&wgpu::BufferDescriptor {
                label: None,
                size,
                usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::MAP_READ | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST,
                mapped_at_creation: false,
            });
            let mut encoder = self
                .device
                .create_command_encoder(&wgpu::CommandEncoderDescriptor { label: None });
            encoder.copy_buffer_to_buffer(&self.buffers[self.current], 0, &read, 0, size);
            self.queue.submit(Some(encoder.finish()));
            let slice = read.slice(..);
            let (tx, rx) = std::sync::mpsc::sync_channel(1);
            slice.map_async(wgpu::MapMode::Read, move |r| {
                let _ = tx.send(r);
            });
            self.device.poll(wgpu::Maintain::Wait);
            rx.recv_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(30))
                .map_err(|e| PyRuntimeError::new_err(e.to_string()))?
                .map_err(|e| PyRuntimeError::new_err(e.to_string()))?;
            let bytes = slice.get_mapped_range();
            let data = snapshot(bytemuck::cast_slice(&bytes), self.tick);
            drop(bytes);
            read.unmap();
            Ok(data)
        })
    }
}
fn entry(binding: u32, read_only: bool) -> wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry {
    wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry {
        binding,
        visibility: wgpu::ShaderStages::COMPUTE,
        ty: wgpu::BindingType::Buffer {
            ty: wgpu::BufferBindingType::Storage { read_only },
            has_dynamic_offset: false,
            min_binding_size: None,
        },
        count: None,
    }
}

ARQUIVO: nova-core/src/lib.rs

//! Native CPU research kernels. Ternary coefficients use branches and add/subtract.
mod fields;
mod biology;
#[cfg(feature="webgpu")]
mod gpu;
use numpy::{ndarray::Array2, PyArray2, PyReadonlyArray2};
use pyo3::{exceptions::PyValueError, prelude::*};
use rayon::prelude::*;

#[pyclass]
struct TernaryBlock {
    rows: usize,
    cols: usize,
    packed: Vec<u8>,
}

fn pack(weights: &[i8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, &'static str> {
    let mut packed = vec![0u8; weights.len().div_ceil(4)];
    for (i, &w) in weights.iter().enumerate() {
        let code = match w { 0 => 0, 1 => 1, -1 => 2, _ => return Err("weights must be -1, 0, 1") };
        packed[i / 4] |= code << (2 * (i % 4));
    }
    Ok(packed)
}

#[pymethods]
impl TernaryBlock {
    #[new]
    fn new(weights: Vec<i8>, rows: usize, cols: usize) -> PyResult<Self> {
        if rows == 0 || cols == 0 || rows.checked_mul(cols) != Some(weights.len()) {
            return Err(PyValueError::new_err("invalid dimensions"));
        }
        Ok(Self { rows, cols, packed: pack(&weights).map_err(PyValueError::new_err)? })
    }
    #[getter]
    fn nbytes(&self) -> usize { self.packed.len() }
    fn packed_bytes(&self) -> Vec<u8> { self.packed.clone() }
    fn matmul<'py>(&self, py: Python<'py>, x: PyReadonlyArray2<'py, f64>) -> PyResult<Bound<'py, PyArray2<f64>>> {
        let a = x.as_array();
        if a.ncols() != self.rows { return Err(PyValueError::new_err("input dimension mismatch")); }
        let batch = a.nrows();
        let input: Vec<f64> = a.iter().copied().collect();
        let mut output = vec![0.; batch * self.cols];
        py.allow_threads(|| {
            output.par_chunks_mut(self.cols).enumerate().for_each(|(n, out)| {
                for k in 0..self.rows {
                    let value = input[n*self.rows+k];
                    for (j, result) in out.iter_mut().enumerate() {
                        let i = k*self.cols+j;
                        match (self.packed[i/4] >> (2*(i%4))) & 3 {
                            1 => *result += value,
                            2 => *result -= value,
                            _ => (),
                        }
                    }
                }
            });
        });
        let result = Array2::from_shape_vec((batch,self.cols),output).map_err(|e|PyValueError::new_err(e.to_string()))?;
        Ok(PyArray2::from_owned_array(py,result))
    }
}

fn hadamard(values: &mut [f64]) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
    if !values.len().is_power_of_two() { return Err("length must be a nonzero power of two"); }
    let mut width=1;
    while width<values.len() {
        for base in (0..values.len()).step_by(2*width) {
            for j in 0..width {
                let a=values[base+j]; let b=values[base+j+width];
                values[base+j]=a+b; values[base+j+width]=a-b;
            }
        }
        width*=2;
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[pyfunction]
fn fast_hadamard_transform(mut values: Vec<f64>, normalize: bool) -> PyResult<Vec<f64>> {
    hadamard(&mut values).map_err(PyValueError::new_err)?;
    if normalize { let scale=(values.len() as f64).sqrt(); for x in &mut values { *x/=scale; } }
    Ok(values)
}

#[pyfunction]
fn kuramoto_step(py: Python<'_>, theta: Vec<f64>, omega: Vec<f64>, adjacency: Vec<f64>, lag: Vec<f64>, coupling: f64, dt: f64) -> PyResult<Vec<f64>> {
    let n=theta.len();
    if n==0 || omega.len()!=n || adjacency.len()!=n*n || lag.len()!=n*n || dt<=0. {
        return Err(PyValueError::new_err("invalid Kuramoto dimensions/dt"));
    }
    Ok(py.allow_threads(|| (0..n).into_par_iter().map(|i| {
        let sum: f64=(0..n).map(|j|adjacency[i*n+j]*(theta[j]-theta[i]-lag[i*n+j]).sin()).sum();
        (theta[i]+dt*(omega[i]+coupling*sum/n as f64)).rem_euclid(std::f64::consts::TAU)
    }).collect()))
}


#[pyfunction]
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
fn adex_step(py: Python<'_>, v: Vec<f64>, w: Vec<f64>, dendrite: Vec<f64>, refractory: Vec<f64>, basal: Vec<f64>, apical: Vec<f64>, adapt_a: Vec<f64>, adapt_b: Vec<f64>, tau: Vec<f64>, dt: f64, nmda: bool) -> PyResult<(Vec<f64>,Vec<f64>,Vec<f64>,Vec<f64>,Vec<bool>)> {
    let n=v.len();
    if n==0 || [w.len(),dendrite.len(),refractory.len(),basal.len(),apical.len(),adapt_a.len(),adapt_b.len(),tau.len()].iter().any(|&len|len!=n) || dt<=0. || dt>1. || tau.iter().any(|&t|t<=0.) {
        return Err(PyValueError::new_err("invalid AdEx arrays/dt (0 < dt <= 1 ms)"));
    }
    let results: Vec<(f64,f64,f64,f64,bool)>=py.allow_threads(|| (0..n).into_par_iter().map(|i| {
        let nmda_current=if nmda { 30./(1.+(-(dendrite[i]+40.)/5.).exp()) } else { 0. };
        let d=dendrite[i]+dt*(-5.*(dendrite[i]+70.)+2.*(v[i]-dendrite[i])+apical[i]+nmda_current)/100.;
        let mut new_w=w[i]+dt*(adapt_a[i]*(v[i]+70.)-w[i])/tau[i];
        let mut r=(refractory[i]-dt).max(0.);
        let mut new_v=if refractory[i]>0. { -58. } else {
            v[i]+dt*(-10.*(v[i]+70.)+20.*((v[i]+50.)/2.).min(10.).exp()-w[i]+2.*(dendrite[i]-v[i])+basal[i])/200.
        };
        let spike=new_v>=20.;
        if spike { new_v=-58.;new_w+=adapt_b[i];r=2.; }
        (new_v,new_w,d,r,spike)
    }).collect());
    Ok((results.iter().map(|r|r.0).collect(),results.iter().map(|r|r.1).collect(),results.iter().map(|r|r.2).collect(),results.iter().map(|r|r.3).collect(),results.iter().map(|r|r.4).collect()))
}

#[pyfunction]
fn cascade_rk4(py: Python<'_>, state: Vec<f64>, matrix: Vec<f64>, drive: Vec<f64>, reservoirs: usize, dt: f64) -> PyResult<Vec<f64>> {
    if reservoirs==0 || state.len()%reservoirs!=0 || drive.len()!=state.len()/reservoirs || matrix.len()!=reservoirs*reservoirs || dt<=0. {
        return Err(PyValueError::new_err("invalid cascade shapes/dt"));
    }
    Ok(py.allow_threads(|| {
        let mut result=state.clone();
        result.par_chunks_mut(reservoirs).enumerate().for_each(|(index,out)| {
            let s=&state[index*reservoirs..(index+1)*reservoirs];
            let f=|v:&[f64]| -> Vec<f64> {(0..reservoirs).map(|i| {
                let value:f64=(0..reservoirs).map(|j|matrix[i*reservoirs+j]*v[j]).sum();
                value+if i==0 {drive[index]} else {0.}
            }).collect()};
            let advance=|k:&[f64],factor:f64| -> Vec<f64> {s.iter().zip(k).map(|(v,d)|v+dt*factor*d).collect()};
            let k1=f(s);let k2=f(&advance(&k1,0.5));let k3=f(&advance(&k2,0.5));let k4=f(&advance(&k3,1.));
            for i in 0..reservoirs {out[i]=s[i]+dt*(k1[i]+2.*k2[i]+2.*k3[i]+k4[i])/6.;}
        });
        result
    }))
}

#[pymodule]
fn _native(m: &Bound<'_, PyModule>) -> PyResult<()> {
    m.add_class::<TernaryBlock>()?;
    m.add_class::<fields::NativeField>()?;
    m.add_function(wrap_pyfunction!(biology::cppn_batch,m)?)?;
    m.add_function(wrap_pyfunction!(biology::hh_batch,m)?)?;
    #[cfg(feature="webgpu")]
    m.add_class::<gpu::GpuField>()?;
    m.add_function(wrap_pyfunction!(adex_step,m)?)?;
    m.add_function(wrap_pyfunction!(cascade_rk4,m)?)?;
    m.add_function(wrap_pyfunction!(fast_hadamard_transform,m)?)?;
    m.add_function(wrap_pyfunction!(kuramoto_step,m)?)?;
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn packing_and_invalid() { assert_eq!(pack(&[0,1,-1,0,1]).unwrap(),vec![36,1]); assert!(pack(&[2]).is_err()); }
    #[test]
    fn hadamard_involution() {
        let mut x=vec![1.,2.,3.,4.]; hadamard(&mut x).unwrap(); hadamard(&mut x).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(x,vec![4.,8.,12.,16.]); assert!(hadamard(&mut [1.,2.,3.]).is_err());
    }
}

ARQUIVO: nova/bioma/acoustics.py

"""Delayed finite-bandwidth acoustic packets with range/attenuation, not full acoustics."""

from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
import numpy as np


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AcousticConfig:
    vocabulary: int = 8
    speed: float = 4.0
    max_range: float = 12.0
    decay: float = 0.08
    max_pending: int = 2048
    max_delivered: int = 4096
    emission_period: int = 4

    def __post_init__(self):
        if (
            not 2 <= self.vocabulary <= 32
            or not 0.01 <= self.speed <= 100
            or not 0 < self.max_range <= 128
            or not 0 <= self.decay <= 1
            or not 1 <= self.max_pending <= 8192
            or not 1 <= self.emission_period <= 100
        ):
            raise ValueError("acoustic budget exceeded")


class AcousticChannel:
    def __init__(self, shape, config=None):
        self.shape = np.asarray(shape, dtype=float)
        self.config = config or AcousticConfig()
        self.pending = []
        self.log = []
        self.last_emit = {}
        self.spent = 0.0

    def emit(self, sender, position, token, recipients, tick):
        c = self.config
        if not isinstance(token, int) or not 0 <= token < c.vocabulary:
            raise ValueError("unknown acoustic token")
        if tick - self.last_emit.get(sender, -c.emission_period) < c.emission_period:
            return 0.0
        self.last_emit[sender] = tick
        self.spent += 0.001
        for receiver, location in sorted(recipients, key=lambda x: x[0]):
            if receiver == sender:
                continue
            delta = np.abs(np.asarray(location) - position)
            distance = float(np.linalg.norm(np.minimum(delta, self.shape - delta)))
            if distance > c.max_range or len(self.pending) >= c.max_pending:
                continue
            self.pending.append(
                {
                    "sender": sender,
                    "receiver": receiver,
                    "token": token,
                    "arrival": tick + max(1, int(np.ceil(distance / c.speed))),
                    "emitted": tick,
                    "amplitude": float(np.exp(-c.decay * distance)),
                }
            )
        return 0.001

    def receive(self, tick, alive):
        delivered = [
            p
            for p in self.pending
            if p["arrival"] <= tick and p["receiver"] in alive and p["sender"] in alive
        ]
        self.pending = [
            p
            for p in self.pending
            if p["arrival"] > tick and p["receiver"] in alive and p["sender"] in alive
        ]
        self.last_emit = {k: v for k, v in self.last_emit.items() if k in alive}
        self.log = (self.log + delivered)[-self.config.max_delivered :]
        return delivered

    def state_dict(self):
        return {
            "shape": self.shape.tolist(),
            "config": asdict(self.config),
            "pending": self.pending,
            "log": self.log,
            "last_emit": self.last_emit,
            "spent": self.spent,
        }

    @classmethod
    def restore(cls, state):
        obj = cls(state["shape"], AcousticConfig(**state["config"]))
        for key in ("pending", "log", "last_emit", "spent"):
            setattr(obj, key, state[key])
        if (
            len(obj.pending) > obj.config.max_pending
            or len(obj.log) > obj.config.max_delivered
        ):
            raise ValueError("acoustic state exceeds budget")
        return obj


class CochlearBank:
    """Streaming digital band-pass bank with filter state and RMS energies.

    This transducer is available for sampled audio experiments; the ecosystem's
    packet channel remains an abstract delayed/attenuated signaling medium.
    """

    def __init__(self, sample_rate=8000, bands=8):
        from scipy.signal import butter

        if not 2000 <= sample_rate <= 48000 or not 2 <= bands <= 32:
            raise ValueError("cochlear filter budget exceeded")
        self.sample_rate = sample_rate
        self.edges = np.geomspace(80, sample_rate * 0.45, bands + 1)
        self.filters = [
            butter(2, [lo, hi], btype="bandpass", fs=sample_rate, output="sos")
            for lo, hi in zip(self.edges[:-1], self.edges[1:])
        ]
        self.state = [np.zeros((len(sos), 2)) for sos in self.filters]

    def process(self, samples):
        from scipy.signal import sosfilt

        samples = np.asarray(samples, dtype=float)
        if (
            samples.ndim != 1
            or not 1 <= len(samples) <= 65536
            or not np.isfinite(samples).all()
        ):
            raise ValueError("audio frame budget exceeded")
        energies = []
        for index, sos in enumerate(self.filters):
            filtered, self.state[index] = sosfilt(sos, samples, zi=self.state[index])
            energies.append(float(np.mean(filtered**2)))
        return np.asarray(energies)


def image_source_echoes(source, receiver, box, speed=343.0, reflection=0.5):
    """Direct path and first-order wall images in a rectangular reflective box.

    Separate from the periodic simulation boundary; returns impulse delays/gains,
    not a full diffraction or reverberation solution.
    """
    source, receiver, box = map(
        lambda x: np.asarray(x, dtype=float), (source, receiver, box)
    )
    if (
        source.shape != receiver.shape
        or source.shape != box.shape
        or source.ndim != 1
        or not len(box) in (2, 3)
        or not all(np.isfinite(x).all() for x in (source, receiver, box))
        or np.any(box <= 0)
        or np.any(source < 0)
        or np.any(receiver < 0)
        or np.any(source > box)
        or np.any(receiver > box)
        or not np.isfinite(speed)
        or speed <= 0
        or not 0 <= reflection <= 1
    ):
        raise ValueError("invalid reflective acoustic domain")
    paths = [(source, 1.0)]
    for axis, length in enumerate(box):
        for value in (-source[axis], 2 * length - source[axis]):
            image = source.copy()
            image[axis] = value
            paths.append((image, reflection))
    return [
        {
            "delay_seconds": float(np.linalg.norm(image - receiver) / speed),
            "gain": float(gain / (1 + np.linalg.norm(image - receiver))),
        }
        for image, gain in paths
    ]


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/bioma/brains.py
========================================================================================
"""Developed mixed neural graph: rates, AdEx, HH and Boolean units actually step."""

from __future__ import annotations
import numpy as np
from nova.neuron import AdExPopulation, HodgkinHuxleyCompartments
from nova.jepa.equilibrium import EquilibriumWorldModel
from nova.continual import AdaptiveSubspaceLinear, AdaptiveConfig
from .genetics import MorphogeneticCPPN
from .determinism import SplitMix64


class DevelopedBrain:
    def __init__(self, genome: MorphogeneticCPPN, sensors=12, actions=3):
        self.topology = genome.develop_brain()
        self.n = len(self.topology["types"])
        self.sensors, self.actions = sensors, actions
        rng = SplitMix64(genome.seed)
        self.sensory_projection = rng.uniform((sensors, self.n), -0.5, 0.5)
        self.motor_projection = rng.uniform((self.n, actions), -0.5, 0.5)
        self.latent_projection = rng.uniform((self.n, 8), -0.25, 0.25)
        self.activation = np.zeros(self.n)
        self.adex = AdExPopulation(self.n, seed=genome.seed)
        self.adex.types = self.topology["types"].copy()
        self.adex.tau = np.choose(self.adex.types, [200.0, 30.0, 250.0, 150.0])
        self.adex.a = np.choose(self.adex.types, [2.0, 0.0, 4.0, 2.0])
        self.adex.b = np.choose(self.adex.types, [40.0, 0.0, 80.0, 20.0])
        self.hh = {
            int(i): HodgkinHuxleyCompartments(1)
            for i in np.flatnonzero(self.topology["families"] == 2)
        }
        self.world = EquilibriumWorldModel(sensors, 8, actions, seed=genome.seed)
        self.world.freeze_encoder()
        self.memory = AdaptiveSubspaceLinear(
            8,
            8,
            seed=genome.seed,
            config=AdaptiveConfig(
                max_features=32, max_state_bytes=1024 * 1024, growth_step=8
            ),
        )
        self.previous_latent = None
        self.previous_action = np.zeros(actions)
        self.ticks = 0
        self.last_error = 0.0
        self.resource_readout = np.zeros(9)
        self.planning_weight = 0.0

    def step(self, sensory, modulator=0.0):
        sensory = np.asarray(sensory, dtype=float)
        if sensory.shape != (self.sensors,) or not np.isfinite(sensory).all():
            raise ValueError("invalid organism sensors")
        current = (
            self.activation @ self.topology["weights"]
            + sensory @ self.sensory_projection
        )
        rate = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.tanh(current)
        spikes, _ = self.adex.step((300 + 50 * np.tanh(current), 0.0), dt=0.1)
        output = rate.copy()
        families = self.topology["families"]
        output[families == 1] = spikes[families == 1]
        for index, neuron in self.hh.items():
            fired = False
            for _ in range(10):
                fired = (
                    bool(
                        neuron.step(
                            (10 + float(np.clip(current[index], -20, 20)), 0.0), dt=0.01
                        )[0][0]
                    )
                    or fired
                )
            output[index] = float(fired)
        for index in np.flatnonzero(families == 3):
            inputs = self.activation[self.topology["weights"][:, index] != 0] > 0.5
            gate = int(index) % 4
            output[index] = float(
                inputs.all()
                if gate == 0 and len(inputs)
                else inputs.any()
                if gate == 1
                else np.count_nonzero(inputs) % 2
                if gate == 2
                else not inputs.any()
            )
        self.activation = np.clip(0.9 * self.activation + 0.1 * output, 0, 1)
        latent = np.tanh(self.activation @ self.latent_projection)
        action = np.tanh((self.activation - 0.5) @ self.motor_projection)
        if self.previous_latent is not None:
            prediction = self.world.predict(
                self.previous_latent[None], self.previous_action[None]
            )[0]
            self.last_error = float(np.mean((prediction - latent) ** 2))
            self.world.learn_latent(
                self.previous_latent[None],
                self.previous_action[None],
                latent[None],
                lr=0.001,
            )
            self.memory.step(
                self.memory.encode(self.previous_latent[None]), latent[None], lr=0.001
            )
        resource_features = np.r_[latent, 1.0]
        residual = float(sensory[-1] - resource_features @ self.resource_readout)
        self.resource_readout += (
            0.01
            * np.clip(residual, -1, 1)
            * resource_features
            / (1 + resource_features @ resource_features)
        )
        # Learned equilibrium forecasts participate in the action path. This is
        # a bounded one-step heuristic, not a demonstrated optimal controller.
        if self.ticks >= 8:
            candidates = np.clip(
                np.vstack(
                    (
                        action,
                        np.zeros(self.actions),
                        action + 0.25 * np.eye(self.actions),
                        action - 0.25 * np.eye(self.actions),
                    )
                ),
                -1,
                1,
            )
            future = self.world.predict(
                np.repeat(latent[None], len(candidates), axis=0), candidates
            )
            value = (
                future @ self.resource_readout[:8]
                + self.resource_readout[8]
                - 0.01 * np.sum(candidates * candidates, axis=1)
            )
            self.planning_weight = 0.25 / (1 + 100 * self.last_error)
            action = (
                1 - self.planning_weight
            ) * action + self.planning_weight * candidates[int(np.argmax(value))]
        self.ticks += 1
        self.memory.age(1.0, consolidation=float(np.clip(modulator, 0, 1)) * 0.01)
        if self.ticks % 32 == 0:
            self.memory.consolidate(self.memory.encode(latent[None]))
            self.memory.manage_capacity()
        self.previous_latent = latent
        self.previous_action = action.copy()
        return action, latent

    def state_dict(self):
        return {
            "topology": self.topology,
            "activation": self.activation.copy(),
            "adex": self.adex.state_dict(),
            "hh": {
                str(i): {"v": n.v.copy(), "gates": n.gates.copy()}
                for i, n in self.hh.items()
            },
            "world": self.world.state_dict(),
            "memory": self.memory.state_dict(),
            "previous_latent": self.previous_latent,
            "previous_action": self.previous_action,
            "ticks": self.ticks,
            "last_error": self.last_error,
            "resource_readout": self.resource_readout.copy(),
            "planning_weight": self.planning_weight,
        }

    def restore(self, state):
        self.topology = {
            k: np.asarray(v) if k != "active_synapses" else v
            for k, v in state["topology"].items()
        }
        self.activation = np.asarray(state["activation"]).copy()
        self.adex.load_state_dict(state["adex"])
        for key, value in state["hh"].items():
            self.hh[int(key)].v = np.asarray(value["v"]).copy()
            self.hh[int(key)].gates = np.asarray(value["gates"]).copy()
        self.world = EquilibriumWorldModel.from_state_dict(state["world"])
        self.memory = AdaptiveSubspaceLinear.from_state_dict(state["memory"])
        self.previous_latent = state["previous_latent"]
        self.previous_action = np.asarray(state["previous_action"]).copy()
        self.ticks = state["ticks"]
        self.last_error = state["last_error"]
        self.resource_readout = np.asarray(state["resource_readout"]).copy()
        self.planning_weight = state["planning_weight"]

"""Explicit entry points for local Biocosm experiments and opt-in peer exchange."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import asyncio
from pathlib import Path

from .determinism import canonical_json, strict_json
from .presets import PRESETS, create_ecosystem


def main(argv=None):
    from nova.chromatic_cli import COMMANDS as CHROMATIC
    from nova.chromatic_cli import main as chromatic_main

    if argv and argv[0] in CHROMATIC:
        return chromatic_main(argv)
    from nova.planetary_cli import COMMANDS
    from nova.planetary_cli import main as planetary_main

    if argv and argv[0] in COMMANDS:
        return planetary_main(argv)
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog="nova cosm",
        description="Experimental fields, organisms, reproducible seeds and scoped discovery",
    )
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    for name in ("run", "serve", "render"):
        p = sub.add_parser(name)
        p.add_argument("--preset", choices=PRESETS, default="organisms")
        p.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
        p.add_argument("--size", type=int, default=32)
        p.add_argument("--dimensions", type=int, choices=(2, 3), default=3)
        p.add_argument("--population", type=int)
        p.add_argument(
            "--genome",
            help="local genome or evolution champion JSON for custom genesis",
        )
        p.add_argument(
            "--resume", help="local ecosystem checkpoint; not a remote executable"
        )
        if name == "run":
            p.add_argument("--ticks", type=int, default=100)
            p.add_argument("--output", default="runs/cosm")
            p.add_argument(
                "--tier",
                choices=("same_environment", "strict_integer"),
                default="same_environment",
            )
        if name == "serve":
            p.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8765)
        if name == "render":
            p.add_argument("--output", default="runs/cosm.html")
            p.add_argument(
                "--kind", choices=("html", "volume", "slice"), default="html"
            )
            p.add_argument("--resolution", type=int, default=384)
    p = sub.add_parser("reproduce")
    p.add_argument("packet")
    p.add_argument("--output")
    p.add_argument("--max-ticks", type=int, default=10000)
    p.add_argument("--require-signature", action="store_true")
    p = sub.add_parser("discover")
    p.add_argument("--dataset", required=True)
    p.add_argument("--output", default="runs/candidate.json")
    p.add_argument("--target", choices=("du_dt", "dv_dt"), default="dv_dt")
    p.add_argument("--degree", type=int, default=3)
    p.add_argument("--max-terms", type=int, default=8)
    p = sub.add_parser("verify")
    p.add_argument("--variables", nargs="+", required=True)
    p.add_argument("--lhs", required=True)
    p.add_argument("--rhs", required=True)
    p.add_argument("--backend", choices=("exact", "z3", "lean"), default="exact")
    p.add_argument("--lean-project")
    p = sub.add_parser("evolve")
    p.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    p.add_argument("--population", type=int, default=4)
    p.add_argument("--generations", type=int, default=4)
    p.add_argument("--evaluations", type=int, default=16)
    p.add_argument("--ticks", type=int, default=32)
    p.add_argument("--output", default="runs/evolution.json")
    for name in ("node", "sync", "peer-id"):
        p = sub.add_parser(name)
        p.add_argument("--directory", default="runs/peer")
        p.add_argument(
            "--peers", help="local JSON list of pinned {peer_id,endpoint} objects"
        )
        if name == "node":
            p.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1")
            p.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8766)
            p.add_argument("--transport", choices=("tcp", "websocket"), default="tcp")
            p.add_argument("--certfile")
            p.add_argument("--keyfile")
        if name == "sync":
            p.add_argument("--peer-id", required=True)
    for command in sorted(COMMANDS | CHROMATIC):
        sub.add_parser(command, help="v0.4 planetary workflow")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.command in ("run", "serve", "render"):
        from .ecosystem import Ecosystem

        genomes = None
        if args.genome:
            if args.resume:
                parser.error("--genome sets genesis and cannot accompany --resume")
            content = strict_json(Path(args.genome).read_bytes())
            genomes = [content.get("champion", content)]
        eco = (
            Ecosystem.load(args.resume)
            if args.resume
            else create_ecosystem(
                args.preset,
                seed=args.seed,
                size=args.size,
                dimensions=args.dimensions,
                population=args.population,
                genesis_genomes=genomes,
            )
        )
        if args.command == "run":
            from nova.visuals import FieldRenderer

            from .pipeline import run_and_export

            result = run_and_export(eco, args.output, args.ticks, tier=args.tier)
            FieldRenderer.export_html(eco, Path(args.output) / "viewer.html")
            FieldRenderer.render(eco, Path(args.output) / "volume.png")
        elif args.command == "serve":
            from nova.visuals.server import serve

            serve(eco, port=args.port)
            return
        else:
            from nova.visuals import FieldRenderer

            result = (
                FieldRenderer.export_html(eco, args.output)
                if args.kind == "html"
                else FieldRenderer.render(
                    eco, args.output, kind=args.kind, resolution=args.resolution
                )
            )
    elif args.command == "reproduce":
        from nova.swarm.holoseed import HoloSeed

        eco, result = HoloSeed.reproduce(
            strict_json(Path(args.packet).read_bytes()),
            max_ticks=args.max_ticks,
            require_signature=args.require_signature,
        )
        if args.output:
            eco.save(args.output)
        if result["status"] != "matched":
            print(canonical_json(result).decode())
            raise SystemExit(2)
    elif args.command == "discover":
        from .pipeline import discover_dataset

        result = discover_dataset(
            args.dataset,
            args.output,
            target=args.target,
            degree=args.degree,
            max_terms=args.max_terms,
        )
    elif args.command == "verify":
        from nova.scientific.polynomial import Polynomial
        from nova.scientific.verification import PolynomialVerifier, Z3Verifier

        lhs = Polynomial.parse(args.lhs, args.variables)
        rhs = Polynomial.parse(args.rhs, args.variables)
        if args.backend == "lean":
            from nova.scientific.verifier_lean import LeanVerifier

            if not args.lean_project:
                parser.error("--lean-project is required for Lean")
            verifier = LeanVerifier(args.lean_project)
        else:
            verifier = PolynomialVerifier() if args.backend == "exact" else Z3Verifier()
        result = verifier.identity(lhs, rhs).to_dict()
    elif args.command == "evolve":
        from .evolution import ArchitectureSearch

        if not 1 <= args.ticks <= 512:
            parser.error("--ticks must be in [1,512]")

        def evaluate(genome, seed):
            eco = create_ecosystem(
                "organisms", seed=seed, size=16, genesis_genomes=[genome.state_dict()]
            )
            eco.step(args.ticks)
            energy = sum(o.energy for o in eco.organisms)
            return {
                "fitness": energy,
                "survivors": len(eco.organisms),
                "world_seed": seed,
                "ticks": args.ticks,
                "metric": "remaining synthetic resource energy",
            }

        result = ArchitectureSearch(args.seed, args.population, args.evaluations).run(
            evaluate, args.generations
        )
        out = Path(args.output)
        out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        out.write_bytes(canonical_json(result))
        result = {k: v for k, v in result.items() if k != "champion"} | {
            "champion_artifact": str(out),
            "validation_used_for_selection": False,
        }
    else:
        from nova.swarm.artifacts import ArtifactStore, SigningIdentity
        from nova.swarm.p2p_node import Peer, PeerNode

        directory = Path(args.directory)
        identity = SigningIdentity.load_or_create(directory / "private" / "signing.key")
        peers = (
            []
            if not args.peers
            else [Peer(**p) for p in strict_json(Path(args.peers).read_bytes())]
        )
        node = PeerNode(identity, ArtifactStore(directory / "artifacts"), peers)
        if args.command == "peer-id":
            result = {"peer_id": identity.peer_id, "directory": str(directory)}
        elif args.command == "sync":
            result = asyncio.run(node.synchronize(args.peer_id))
        else:
            print(
                canonical_json(
                    {
                        "peer_id": identity.peer_id,
                        "host": args.host,
                        "port": args.port,
                        "transport": args.transport,
                    }
                ).decode(),
                flush=True,
            )
            try:
                asyncio.run(
                    node.serve(
                        args.host,
                        args.port,
                        certfile=args.certfile,
                        keyfile=args.keyfile,
                        transport=args.transport,
                    )
                )
            except KeyboardInterrupt:
                pass
            return
    print(canonical_json(result).decode())

ARQUIVO: nova/bioma/determinism.py
========================================================================================
"""Specified PRNG and strict JSON/array encodings for replayable artifacts."""

from __future__ import annotations
import hashlib
import json
import platform
import sys
import numpy as np

MASK = (1 << 64) - 1


class SplitMix64:
    """SplitMix64 with explicit unsigned 64-bit wrapping; no NumPy RNG dependency."""

    def __init__(self, seed: int):
        if not isinstance(seed, int) or not 0 <= seed <= MASK:
            raise ValueError("seed must be uint64")
        self.state = seed

    def next_u64(self) -> int:
        self.state = (self.state + 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15) & MASK
        z = self.state
        z = ((z ^ (z >> 30)) * 0xBF58476D1CE4E5B9) & MASK
        z = ((z ^ (z >> 27)) * 0x94D049BB133111EB) & MASK
        return (z ^ (z >> 31)) & MASK

    def uniform(self, shape, low=0.0, high=1.0):
        count = int(np.prod(shape))
        values = np.fromiter(
            ((self.next_u64() >> 11) * 2.0**-53 for _ in range(count)), float, count
        )
        return (low + (high - low) * values).reshape(shape)

    def integers(self, low: int, high: int, shape):
        if high <= low:
            raise ValueError("empty integer range")
        width = high - low
        limit = (1 << 64) - ((1 << 64) % width)
        result = []
        for _ in range(int(np.prod(shape))):
            value = self.next_u64()
            while value >= limit:
                value = self.next_u64()
            result.append(low + value % width)
        return np.asarray(result, dtype=np.int64).reshape(shape)


def canonical_json(value) -> bytes:
    return json.dumps(
        value,
        sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"),
        ensure_ascii=False,
        allow_nan=False,
    ).encode("utf-8")


def strict_json(raw: bytes | str, max_bytes=262144):
    if len(raw) > max_bytes:
        raise ValueError("JSON exceeds byte budget")

    def pairs(items):
        result = {}
        for key, value in items:
            if key in result:
                raise ValueError("duplicate JSON key")
            result[key] = value
        return result

    def constant(value):
        raise ValueError("nonfinite JSON number")

    try:
        return json.loads(raw, object_pairs_hook=pairs, parse_constant=constant)
    except RecursionError as error:
        raise ValueError("JSON nesting exceeds parser budget") from error


def digest_arrays(arrays: dict[str, np.ndarray], metadata=None) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(metadata or {}))
    for name, value in sorted(arrays.items()):
        value = np.asarray(value)
        if value.dtype.hasobject:
            raise ValueError("object arrays not serializable")
        dtype = value.dtype.newbyteorder("<")
        data = np.asarray(value, dtype=dtype, order="C")
        digest.update(
            canonical_json(
                {"name": name, "shape": list(data.shape), "dtype": dtype.str}
            )
        )
        digest.update(data.tobytes(order="C"))
    return digest.hexdigest()


def environment_fingerprint():
    import scipy

    return {
        "python": platform.python_version(),
        "numpy": np.__version__,
        "scipy": scipy.__version__,
        "machine": platform.machine(),
        "byteorder": sys.byteorder,
        "float_format": "IEEE754-binary64",
        "note": "Fingerprint is diagnostic; float kernels still require replay comparison.",
    }


def digest_state(state):
    """Hash every nested scalar and array with unambiguous structural paths."""
    arrays = {}

    def visit(value, path):
        if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
            name = canonical_json(path).decode()
            arrays[name] = value
            return {"array": name}
        if isinstance(value, np.generic):
            return value.item()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {
                str(k): visit(v, path + [str(k)])
                for k, v in sorted(value.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]))
            }
        if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
            return [visit(v, path + [i]) for i, v in enumerate(value)]
        return value

    metadata = visit(state, [])
    return digest_arrays(arrays, metadata)
