"""Lazy backend exports: NumPy equilibrium inference does not import PyTorch."""

from importlib import import_module

__all__ = [
    "JEPA",
    "ContextEncoder",
    "TargetEncoder",
    "LatentPredictor",
    "EquilibriumNetwork",
    "EquilibriumWorldModel",
    "EquilibriumConfig",
]


def __getattr__(name):
    if name in ("JEPA", "ContextEncoder", "TargetEncoder", "LatentPredictor"):
        return getattr(import_module(".torch_backend", __name__), name)
    if name in ("EquilibriumNetwork", "EquilibriumWorldModel", "EquilibriumConfig"):
        return getattr(import_module(".equilibrium", __name__), name)
    raise AttributeError(name)


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/jepa/equilibrium.py
========================================================================================
"""Contractive rate-network latent learning via two-phase equilibrium correlations.

This backend uses NumPy and explicit local equations, with no autograd calls.
It is a separate hypothesis from the PyTorch JEPA baseline, not a claim that
latent prediction is automatically variational free-energy inference.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
import copy
from time import perf_counter
import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import ArrayLike


def batch(x: ArrayLike, width: int) -> np.ndarray:
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.ndim == 1:
        x = x[None]
    if x.ndim != 2 or x.shape[1] != width or not len(x) or not np.isfinite(x).all():
        raise ValueError(f"expected finite nonempty [batch, {width}] array")
    return x


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EquilibriumConfig:
    state_dim: int = 32
    recurrent_radius: float = 0.5
    damping: float = 0.5
    beta: float = 0.02
    tolerance: float = 1e-8
    max_steps: int = 200
    update_clip: float = 1.0

    def __post_init__(self):
        if min(self.state_dim, self.max_steps) < 1 or not 0 < self.damping <= 1:
            raise ValueError("invalid relaxation configuration")
        if (
            not 0 <= self.recurrent_radius < 1
            or not 0 < self.beta < 1 - self.recurrent_radius
        ):
            raise ValueError("require recurrent_radius + beta < 1 for both phases")
        if (
            not np.isfinite(self.tolerance)
            or self.tolerance <= 0
            or not np.isfinite(self.update_clip)
            or self.update_clip <= 0
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid tolerance or update clip")


@dataclass
class EquilibriumResult:
    state: np.ndarray
    output: np.ndarray
    iterations: int
    residual: float
    converged: bool


class EquilibriumNetwork:
    """E(s,u)=sum integral_0^s atanh(v)dv - s.T W s/2 - s.T(B.T u+b).

    State is in (-1,1). Hessian diag(1/(1-s²))-W is positive definite
    when symmetric ||W||_2<1. Free equilibrium solves s=tanh(sW+uB+b).
    Nudging adds beta/2 ||P s-y||², or beta*g.T P s for local messages.
    Central +/- beta phases estimate energy-parameter derivatives. This is a
    bounded nonlinear rate model; its symmetric weights are not a Dale circuit.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        inputs: int,
        outputs: int,
        *,
        seed: int = 0,
        config: EquilibriumConfig | None = None,
    ):
        self.config = config or EquilibriumConfig(state_dim=max(32, outputs * 2))
        if inputs < 1 or not 1 <= outputs <= self.config.state_dim:
            raise ValueError("invalid network dimensions")
        self.inputs, self.outputs = inputs, outputs
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        n = self.config.state_dim
        matrix = rng.normal(0, 0.1, (n, n))
        self.W = (matrix + matrix.T) / 2
        self.B = rng.normal(0, 1 / np.sqrt(inputs), (inputs, n))
        self.bias = np.zeros(n)
        self._contract()
        self.last_diagnostics = {}
        self.updates = 0

    def _contract(self):
        values, vectors = np.linalg.eigh((self.W + self.W.T) / 2)
        values = np.clip(
            values, -self.config.recurrent_radius, self.config.recurrent_radius
        )
        self.W = (vectors * values) @ vectors.T

    def energy(self, state: ArrayLike, inputs: ArrayLike) -> np.ndarray:
        state = batch(state, self.config.state_dim)
        u = batch(inputs, self.inputs)
        if len(state) != len(u):
            raise ValueError("batch mismatch")
        s = np.clip(state, -1 + 1e-12, 1 - 1e-12)
        primitive = s * np.arctanh(s) + 0.5 * np.log1p(-s * s)
        return (
            primitive.sum(1)
            - 0.5 * np.sum((s @ self.W) * s, axis=1)
            - np.sum(s * (u @ self.B + self.bias), axis=1)
        )

    def relax(
        self,
        inputs: ArrayLike,
        *,
        targets: ArrayLike | None = None,
        beta: float = 0.0,
        output_gradient: ArrayLike | None = None,
        initial: ArrayLike | None = None,
        deadline: float | None = None,
    ) -> EquilibriumResult:
        u = batch(inputs, self.inputs)
        c = self.config
        if abs(beta) + c.recurrent_radius >= 1:
            raise ValueError("nudge violates contraction bound")
        if targets is not None and output_gradient is not None:
            raise ValueError("choose targets or output_gradient")
        y = None if targets is None else batch(targets, self.outputs)
        gradient = (
            None if output_gradient is None else batch(output_gradient, self.outputs)
        )
        if (y is not None and len(y) != len(u)) or (
            gradient is not None and len(gradient) != len(u)
        ):
            raise ValueError("batch mismatch")
        s = (
            np.zeros((len(u), c.state_dim))
            if initial is None
            else batch(initial, c.state_dim).copy()
        )
        if len(s) != len(u):
            raise ValueError("initial batch mismatch")
        drive = u @ self.B + self.bias
        residual = float("inf")
        completed = 0
        for step in range(c.max_steps):
            if deadline is not None and perf_counter() >= deadline:
                break
            current = s @ self.W + drive
            if y is not None:
                current[:, -self.outputs :] -= beta * (s[:, -self.outputs :] - y)
            if gradient is not None:
                current[:, -self.outputs :] -= beta * gradient
            fixed = np.tanh(current)
            residual = float(np.max(abs(fixed - s)))
            completed = step + 1
            s += (fixed - s) * c.damping
            if residual <= c.tolerance:
                break
        # If no iteration fit the budget, report a finite bound, not a success.
        if not completed:
            residual = float(np.max(abs(np.tanh(drive) - s)))
        result = EquilibriumResult(
            s,
            s[:, -self.outputs :].copy(),
            completed,
            residual,
            bool(completed and residual <= c.tolerance),
        )
        self.last_diagnostics = {
            "iterations": completed,
            "residual": residual,
            "converged": result.converged,
        }
        return result

    def predict(self, inputs: ArrayLike) -> np.ndarray:
        result = self.relax(inputs)
        if not result.converged:
            raise RuntimeError("equilibrium inference did not converge")
        return result.output

    def learn(
        self,
        inputs: ArrayLike,
        targets: ArrayLike | None = None,
        *,
        output_gradient: ArrayLike | None = None,
        lr: float = 0.01,
    ) -> dict:
        u = batch(inputs, self.inputs)
        if (targets is None) == (output_gradient is None):
            raise ValueError("supply targets or a local output gradient")
        if not np.isfinite(lr) or lr < 0:
            raise ValueError("invalid learning rate")
        free = self.relax(u)
        beta = self.config.beta
        plus = self.relax(
            u,
            targets=targets,
            output_gradient=output_gradient,
            beta=beta,
            initial=free.state,
        )
        minus = self.relax(
            u,
            targets=targets,
            output_gradient=output_gradient,
            beta=-beta,
            initial=free.state,
        )
        if not all(r.converged for r in (free, plus, minus)):
            return {
                "updated": False,
                "reason": "relaxation_not_converged",
                "residual": max(r.residual for r in (free, plus, minus)),
                "input_gradient": np.zeros_like(u),
                "prediction": free.output,
            }
        delta = (plus.state - minus.state) / (2 * beta)
        # This input message is evaluated before modifying B.
        input_gradient = -delta @ self.B.T
        dB = u.T @ delta / len(u)
        dW = (plus.state.T @ plus.state - minus.state.T @ minus.state) / (
            4 * beta * len(u)
        )
        db = delta.mean(0)
        magnitude = np.sqrt(np.sum(dB * dB) + np.sum(dW * dW) + np.sum(db * db))
        scale = min(1.0, self.config.update_clip / max(magnitude, 1e-12))
        self.B += lr * scale * dB
        self.W += lr * scale * dW
        self.bias += lr * scale * db
        self._contract()
        self.updates += 1
        loss = (
            None
            if targets is None
            else float(
                0.5
                * np.mean(
                    np.sum((free.output - batch(targets, self.outputs)) ** 2, axis=1)
                )
            )
        )
        return {
            "updated": True,
            "loss": loss,
            "input_gradient": input_gradient,
            "prediction": free.output,
            "gradient_norm": float(magnitude),
            "clip_scale": float(scale),
            "residual": max(r.residual for r in (free, plus, minus)),
        }

    def linearize(self, inputs: ArrayLike) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
        """Implicit ds/du = (diag(1/(1-s²))-W)^(-1) B.T, not finite differences."""
        result = self.relax(inputs)
        if not result.converged:
            raise RuntimeError("cannot linearize a nonconverged equilibrium")
        jacobian = []
        for s in result.state:
            hessian = np.diag(1 / np.maximum(1 - s * s, 1e-12)) - self.W
            jacobian.append(np.linalg.solve(hessian, self.B.T)[-self.outputs :])
        return result.output, np.stack(jacobian)

    def state_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "inputs": self.inputs,
            "outputs": self.outputs,
            "config": asdict(self.config),
            "W": self.W.copy(),
            "B": self.B.copy(),
            "bias": self.bias.copy(),
            "updates": self.updates,
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_state_dict(cls, state: dict) -> "EquilibriumNetwork":
        result = cls(
            state["inputs"],
            state["outputs"],
            config=EquilibriumConfig(**state["config"]),
        )
        for name in ("W", "B", "bias"):
            setattr(result, name, np.asarray(state[name], dtype=float).copy())
        if (
            not np.allclose(result.W, result.W.T, atol=1e-10)
            or np.linalg.norm(result.W, 2) > result.config.recurrent_radius + 1e-10
        ):
            raise ValueError("checkpoint violates symmetric contraction constraint")
        result.updates = state["updates"]
        return result


class EquilibriumWorldModel:
    """EMA-target latent prediction with an actual equilibrium encoder/predictor.

    During offline learning, local EqProp messages can also train the context
    encoder, with explicit variance/covariance output forces. Online runtime
    learning freezes the encoder to preserve protected coordinates. No lifetime
    or performance advantage follows merely from avoiding autograd.
    """

    kind = "equilibrium"

    def __init__(
        self,
        observation_dim: int,
        latent_dim: int,
        action_dim: int,
        *,
        seed: int = 0,
        config: EquilibriumConfig | None = None,
    ):
        self.observation_dim, self.latent_dim, self.action_dim = (
            observation_dim,
            latent_dim,
            action_dim,
        )
        self.config = config or EquilibriumConfig(state_dim=max(32, latent_dim * 2))
        self.context = EquilibriumNetwork(
            observation_dim, latent_dim, seed=seed, config=self.config
        )
        self.target = copy.deepcopy(self.context)
        self.predictor = EquilibriumNetwork(
            latent_dim + action_dim, latent_dim, seed=seed + 1, config=self.config
        )
        self.mean = np.zeros(observation_dim)
        self.scale = np.ones(observation_dim)
        self.encoder_frozen = False
        self.last_diagnostics = {}

    def fit_normalizer(self, observations: ArrayLike):
        if self.encoder_frozen:
            raise ValueError("normalizer is frozen with encoder coordinates")
        x = batch(observations, self.observation_dim)
        self.mean = x.mean(0)
        self.scale = np.maximum(x.std(0), 0.05)

    def _normalize(self, x):
        return (batch(x, self.observation_dim) - self.mean) / self.scale

    def encode(self, x: ArrayLike) -> np.ndarray:
        output = self.context.predict(self._normalize(x))
        self.last_diagnostics = self.context.last_diagnostics.copy()
        return output

    def predict(self, z: ArrayLike, action: ArrayLike) -> np.ndarray:
        z = batch(z, self.latent_dim)
        a = batch(action, self.action_dim)
        if len(z) != len(a):
            raise ValueError("latent/action batch mismatch")
        return self.predictor.predict(np.c_[z, a])

    def linearize(
        self, z: ArrayLike, action: ArrayLike
    ) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
        z = batch(z, self.latent_dim)
        a = batch(action, self.action_dim)
        output, jacobian = self.predictor.linearize(np.c_[z, a])
        return (
            output,
            jacobian[:, :, : self.latent_dim],
            jacobian[:, :, self.latent_dim :],
        )

    def learn_latent(self, z, action, future_z, lr=0.01) -> dict:
        return self.predictor.learn(
            np.c_[batch(z, self.latent_dim), batch(action, self.action_dim)],
            batch(future_z, self.latent_dim),
            lr=lr,
        )

    def learn_transition(
        self,
        x,
        action,
        future,
        *,
        lr=0.01,
        train_encoder=False,
        variance_weight=1.0,
        covariance_weight=0.01,
        ema=0.99,
    ) -> dict:
        if train_encoder and self.encoder_frozen:
            raise ValueError("encoder coordinates are frozen")
        normalized = self._normalize(x)
        z = self.context.predict(normalized)
        target = self.target.predict(self._normalize(future))
        result = self.learn_latent(z, action, target, lr)
        if train_encoder and result["updated"]:
            gradient = result["input_gradient"][:, : self.latent_dim]
            centered = z - z.mean(0)
            n, d = z.shape
            std = np.sqrt(np.mean(centered**2, axis=0) + 1e-4)
            if n > 1:
                # Output-gradient arrays are per-example cost gradients; network
                # learn performs its own batch mean on parameter correlations.
                gradient += (
                    variance_weight
                    * (-centered / np.maximum(std, 1e-8))
                    * (std < 0.5)
                    / d
                )
                cov = centered.T @ centered / (n - 1)
                off = cov - np.diag(np.diag(cov))
                gradient += (
                    covariance_weight * 4 * n / (d * d * (n - 1)) * (centered @ off)
                )
            result["encoder_updated"] = self.context.learn(
                normalized, output_gradient=gradient, lr=lr
            )["updated"]
            for name in ("W", "B", "bias"):
                setattr(
                    self.target,
                    name,
                    ema * getattr(self.target, name)
                    + (1 - ema) * getattr(self.context, name),
                )
        result["latent_std"] = float(z.std(0).mean())
        return result

    def freeze_encoder(self):
        self.encoder_frozen = True

    def state_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "kind": self.kind,
            "observation_dim": self.observation_dim,
            "latent_dim": self.latent_dim,
            "action_dim": self.action_dim,
            "config": asdict(self.config),
            "context": self.context.state_dict(),
            "target": self.target.state_dict(),
            "predictor": self.predictor.state_dict(),
            "mean": self.mean.copy(),
            "scale": self.scale.copy(),
            "encoder_frozen": self.encoder_frozen,
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_state_dict(cls, state: dict) -> "EquilibriumWorldModel":
        obj = cls(
            state["observation_dim"],
            state["latent_dim"],
            state["action_dim"],
            config=EquilibriumConfig(**state["config"]),
        )
        for name in ("context", "target", "predictor"):
            setattr(obj, name, EquilibriumNetwork.from_state_dict(state[name]))
        obj.mean = np.asarray(state["mean"]).copy()
        obj.scale = np.asarray(state["scale"]).copy()
        obj.encoder_frozen = state["encoder_frozen"]
        return obj


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/jepa/torch_backend.py
========================================================================================
"""Latent prediction with a stopped, exponential-moving-average target."""

from copy import deepcopy
import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F


class ContextEncoder(nn.Sequential):
    def __init__(self, observation_dim: int, latent_dim: int, hidden: int = 64):
        super().__init__(
            nn.Linear(observation_dim, hidden), nn.SiLU(), nn.Linear(hidden, latent_dim)
        )


class TargetEncoder(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, encoder: nn.Module):
        super().__init__()
        self.encoder = deepcopy(encoder).requires_grad_(False)

    def forward(self, x):
        with torch.no_grad():
            return self.encoder(x)

    @torch.no_grad()
    def update(self, encoder: nn.Module, decay: float = 0.99):
        for target, source in zip(
            self.encoder.parameters(), encoder.parameters(), strict=True
        ):
            target.lerp_(source, 1 - decay)
        for target, source in zip(
            self.encoder.buffers(), encoder.buffers(), strict=True
        ):
            target.copy_(source)


class LatentPredictor(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, latent_dim: int, action_dim: int, hidden: int = 64):
        super().__init__()
        self.net = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(latent_dim + action_dim, hidden),
            nn.SiLU(),
            nn.Linear(hidden, latent_dim),
        )

    def forward(self, z, action):
        return self.net(torch.cat((z, action), dim=-1))


class JEPA(nn.Module):
    def __init__(
        self,
        observation_dim: int,
        latent_dim: int,
        action_dim: int,
        *,
        variance_weight: float = 1.0,
        covariance_weight: float = 0.01,
    ):
        super().__init__()
        self.context = ContextEncoder(observation_dim, latent_dim)
        self.target = TargetEncoder(self.context)
        self.predictor = LatentPredictor(latent_dim, action_dim)
        self.variance_weight, self.covariance_weight = (
            variance_weight,
            covariance_weight,
        )

    def loss(self, x, action, future):
        z = self.context(x)
        target = self.target(future)
        pred = self.predictor(z, action)
        prediction = F.smooth_l1_loss(pred, target)
        std = torch.sqrt(z.var(0, unbiased=False) + 1e-4)
        variance = F.relu(0.5 - std).mean()
        centered = z - z.mean(0)
        covariance = centered.T @ centered / max(len(z) - 1, 1)
        off_diagonal = covariance - torch.diag(torch.diag(covariance))
        loss = (
            prediction
            + self.variance_weight * variance
            + self.covariance_weight * off_diagonal.square().mean()
        )
        return loss, {
            "prediction": prediction.detach().item(),
            "latent_std": z.std(0).mean().detach().item(),
        }

    @torch.no_grad()
    def assert_noncollapsed(self, x, threshold=0.05):
        std = self.context(x).std(0).mean().item()
        if std < threshold:
            raise AssertionError(f"latent collapse: mean std {std:.6g} < {threshold}")
        return std


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/kernels/__init__.py
========================================================================================
"""Optional packed kernels; CPU implementation lives in nova.core."""

from nova.core import TernaryLinear

__all__ = ["TernaryLinear"]


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/kernels/triton_ternary.py
========================================================================================
"""Optional GPU reference kernel: 2-bit weights, signed additions, no dense GEMM.

Requires Triton and CUDA. This intentionally simple kernel needs benchmarking;
compaction alone is not evidence of lower latency or energy.
"""

import torch


def pack_ternary(weights):
    if (
        weights.ndim != 2
        or not torch.isin(
            weights, torch.tensor([-1, 0, 1], device=weights.device)
        ).all()
    ):
        raise ValueError("expected ternary matrix")
    flat = weights.flatten()
    codes = torch.where(flat == -1, 2, flat).to(torch.uint8)
    padded = torch.zeros(
        ((len(codes) + 3) // 4) * 4, dtype=torch.uint8, device=weights.device
    )
    padded[: len(codes)] = codes
    padded = padded.reshape(-1, 4)
    return (
        padded[:, 0] | (padded[:, 1] << 2) | (padded[:, 2] << 4) | (padded[:, 3] << 6)
    )


def matmul(x, packed, out_features):
    if not x.is_cuda or not packed.is_cuda:
        raise ValueError("CUDA tensors required")
    if (
        x.ndim != 2
        or not x.is_contiguous()
        or packed.dtype != torch.uint8
        or out_features < 1
    ):
        raise ValueError("invalid tensor layout")
    if packed.numel() != (x.shape[1] * out_features + 3) // 4:
        raise ValueError("packed dimensions mismatch")
    import triton
    import triton.language as tl

    @triton.jit
    def kernel(X, W, Y, D: tl.constexpr, N: tl.constexpr, BLOCK: tl.constexpr):
        row = tl.program_id(0)
        cols = tl.program_id(1) * BLOCK + tl.arange(0, BLOCK)
        acc = tl.full((BLOCK,), 0, tl.float32)
        for k in range(D):
            index = k * N + cols
            bits = tl.load(W + index // 4, cols < N, other=0)
            code = (bits >> (2 * (index % 4))) & 3
            value = tl.load(X + row * D + k).to(tl.float32)
            acc += tl.where(code == 1, value, tl.where(code == 2, -value, 0.0))
        tl.store(Y + row * N + cols, acc, cols < N)

    y = torch.empty((len(x), out_features), device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)
    kernel[(len(x), triton.cdiv(out_features, 64))](
        x, packed, y, x.shape[1], out_features, 64
    )
    return y


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/language/__init__.py
========================================================================================
"""Grounded user-facing verbalization; optional neural generations remain drafts."""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from nova.values import OutputPolicy, SIMULATION_LABEL


@dataclass
class Interlocutor:
    beliefs: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    goals: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    estimated_valence: float = 0.0
    turns: int = 0

    def update(self, *, beliefs=None, goals=None, valence=0.0):
        self.beliefs.update(beliefs or {})
        self.goals = list(goals or self.goals)
        self.estimated_valence = max(-1.0, min(1.0, valence))
        self.turns += 1


class GroundedVerbalizer:
    def render(self, claims, identity_mode=False, consented=False, style=None):
        # Style only changes formatting; it cannot create additional factual content.
        answer = OutputPolicy.render_claims(
            claims, identity_mode=identity_mode, consented=consented
        )
        if style and style.get("compact"):
            answer = answer.replace("\n", " ")
        return answer

    def stream(self, *args, interrupted=None, **kwargs):
        answer = self.render(*args, **kwargs)
        # Keep the permanent identity label intact as the first streaming chunk.
        if answer.startswith(SIMULATION_LABEL):
            yield SIMULATION_LABEL + "\n"
            answer = answer[len(SIMULATION_LABEL) :].lstrip()
        for word in answer.split():
            if interrupted is not None and interrupted():
                break
            yield word + " "


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class UnverifiedDraft:
    text: str
    source_ids: tuple[str, ...]
    requires_review: bool = True


class LocalLanguageAdapter:
    """Optional frozen local LLM with learned latent-prefix input (not auto-published).

    Requires separately installed transformers and local model weights. Generated
    text is an unverified draft; the grounded renderer remains the output gate.
    """

    def __init__(self, model_directory, latent_dim=8):
        import torch
        from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM

        self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
            model_directory, local_files_only=True
        )
        self.model = (
            AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_directory, local_files_only=True)
            .eval()
            .requires_grad_(False)
        )
        self.prefix = torch.nn.Linear(
            latent_dim, self.model.get_input_embeddings().embedding_dim
        )

    def draft(self, latent, claims, style=None, max_new_tokens=80):
        import json
        import torch

        prompt = json.dumps(
            {
                "facts": claims,
                "style": style or {},
                "instruction": "Verbalize only supplied facts and keep sources.",
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
        )
        tokens = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors="pt")
        with torch.no_grad():
            embeddings = self.model.get_input_embeddings()(tokens["input_ids"])
            prefix = self.prefix(torch.as_tensor(latent, dtype=embeddings.dtype))[
                None, None
            ]
            generated = self.model.generate(
                inputs_embeds=torch.cat((prefix, embeddings), 1),
                max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,
                do_sample=False,
            )
        return UnverifiedDraft(
            self.tokenizer.decode(generated[0], skip_special_tokens=True),
            tuple(c["source"] for c in claims),
        )

"""Finite particle/continuum reference models with explicit numerical diagnostics."""


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/matter/chemistry.py
========================================================================================
"""Colloidal/thermodynamic reference equations; quantities require declared units."""

import numpy as np

from nova.bioma.determinism import SplitMix64


def dlvo_potential(
    gap, *, radius=1e-6, hamaker=1e-20, permittivity=7e-10, zeta=0.025, kappa=1e8
):
    gap = np.asarray(gap, dtype=float)
    if (
        np.any(gap <= 0)
        or not np.isfinite(gap).all()
        or min(radius, hamaker, permittivity, kappa) <= 0
        or not np.isfinite(zeta)
    ):
        raise ValueError("positive finite separated-sphere parameters required")
    # Derjaguin small-gap approximation for equal spheres, not a molecular model.
    return -hamaker * radius / (
        12 * gap
    ) + 2 * np.pi * permittivity * radius * zeta * zeta * np.log1p(np.exp(-kappa * gap))


def regular_solution(fraction, temperature, chi, gas_constant=8.314462618):
    fraction = np.asarray(fraction, dtype=float)
    if (
        not np.isfinite(fraction).all()
        or np.any((fraction <= 0) | (fraction >= 1))
        or not temperature > 0
        or not np.isfinite([temperature, chi]).all()
    ):
        raise ValueError("mixture fractions must be interior and temperature positive")
    free = (
        gas_constant
        * temperature
        * (
            fraction * np.log(fraction)
            + (1 - fraction) * np.log1p(-fraction)
            + chi * fraction * (1 - fraction)
        )
    )
    chemical = (
        gas_constant
        * temperature
        * (np.log(fraction / (1 - fraction)) + chi * (1 - 2 * fraction))
    )
    return free, chemical


def onsager_flux(forces, mobility):
    forces = np.asarray(forces, dtype=float)
    matrix = np.asarray(mobility, dtype=float)
    if (
        matrix.shape != (forces.shape[-1], forces.shape[-1])
        or not np.isfinite(matrix).all()
        or not np.isfinite(forces).all()
        or not np.allclose(matrix, matrix.T, atol=1e-12)
        or np.linalg.eigvalsh(matrix).min() < -1e-12
    ):
        raise ValueError("symmetric positive-semidefinite mobility required")
    flux = forces @ matrix.T
    production = np.sum(flux * forces, axis=-1)
    return flux, production


class GillespieReactor:
    """Exact SSA for a finite, well-mixed mass-action reaction network."""

    def __init__(self, reactants, products, rates, counts, seed=0, max_events=100000):
        self.reactants = np.asarray(reactants, dtype=np.int64)
        self.products = np.asarray(products, dtype=np.int64)
        self.rates = np.asarray(rates, dtype=float)
        self.counts = np.asarray(counts, dtype=np.int64)
        if (
            self.reactants.ndim != 2
            or self.reactants.shape != self.products.shape
            or self.reactants.shape[1] != len(self.counts)
            or self.rates.shape != (len(self.reactants),)
            or max(self.reactants.shape) > 32
            or np.any(self.reactants < 0)
            or np.any(self.products < 0)
            or np.any(self.counts < 0)
            or np.any(self.reactants > 4)
            or np.any(self.reactants.sum(axis=1) > 4)
            or np.any(self.products > 8)
            or np.any(self.counts > 1_000_000_000)
            or not np.isfinite(self.rates).all()
            or np.any(self.rates < 0)
            or not 1 <= max_events <= 100000
        ):
            raise ValueError("reaction network budget/domain exceeded")
        self.rng = SplitMix64(seed)
        self.time = 0.0
        self.events = 0
        self.max_events = max_events

    def step(self, until):
        import math

        if not np.isfinite(until) or until < self.time:
            raise ValueError("invalid reaction clock")
        while self.events < self.max_events:
            prop = self.rates.copy()
            for r in range(len(prop)):
                for species, order in enumerate(self.reactants[r]):
                    prop[r] *= (
                        math.comb(int(self.counts[species]), int(order))
                        if self.counts[species] >= order
                        else 0
                    )
            total = prop.sum()
            if not np.isfinite(prop).all() or not np.isfinite(total):
                raise FloatingPointError("reaction propensities overflow")
            if not total > 0:
                return {"time": self.time, "events": self.events, "status": "absorbing"}
            wait = -np.log(max(float(self.rng.uniform((1,))[0]), 2.0**-53)) / total
            if self.time + wait > until:
                # Finite-interval API restarts the exponential clock; memorylessness
                # preserves its distribution, not a single RNG trajectory partition.
                self.time = until
                return {"time": self.time, "events": self.events, "status": "horizon"}
            choice = int(
                np.searchsorted(
                    np.cumsum(prop), self.rng.uniform((1,))[0] * total, side="right"
                )
            )
            self.counts += self.products[choice] - self.reactants[choice]
            if np.any(self.counts > 1_000_000_000):
                raise ValueError("molecule count budget exceeded")
            self.time += wait
            self.events += 1
        return {"time": self.time, "events": self.events, "status": "budget_exhausted"}


def regular_solution_binodal(chi):
    """Coexistence of a symmetric mean-field regular solution, not measured ink."""
    from scipy.optimize import brentq
    from scipy.special import expit

    if not np.isfinite(chi) or not 0 <= chi <= 100:
        raise ValueError("regular-solution parameter budget exceeded")
    if chi <= 2:
        return {"phases": 1, "fractions": [0.5], "critical_chi": 2.0}
    if chi - 2 < 1e-6:
        c = 0.5 - np.sqrt(3 * (chi - 2) / 8)
    else:
        root = brentq(
            lambda z: z + chi * (1 - 2 * expit(z)), -chi - 1, -1e-6, xtol=1e-12
        )
        c = float(expit(root))
    return {
        "phases": 2,
        "fractions": [c, 1 - c],
        "critical_chi": 2.0,
        "scope": "symmetric mean-field common tangent",
    }


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/matter/constitutive.py
========================================================================================
"""Constitutive laws: regularized yield stress, thixotropy and finite strain."""

import numpy as np


def herschel_bulkley(
    shear_rate,
    *,
    yield_stress=0.1,
    consistency=0.02,
    exponent=0.6,
    regularization=100.0,
    structure=1.0,
):
    rate = np.asarray(shear_rate, dtype=float)
    if (
        not np.isfinite(rate).all()
        or np.any(rate < 0)
        or min(yield_stress, consistency) < 0
        or not 0.1 <= exponent <= 2
        or not 0 < regularization <= 1e4
        or not np.all((np.asarray(structure) >= 0) & (np.asarray(structure) <= 1))
    ):
        raise ValueError("invalid rheology/domain")
    safe = np.maximum(rate, 1e-8)
    plastic = (
        yield_stress
        * np.asarray(structure)
        * (-np.expm1(-regularization * safe))
        / safe
    )
    return plastic + consistency * safe ** (exponent - 1)


def thixotropic_structure(
    previous, shear_rate, dt, *, rebuild_time=2.0, break_rate=1.0
):
    previous, shear_rate = np.broadcast_arrays(previous, shear_rate)
    if (
        not 0 < dt <= 0.1
        or rebuild_time <= 0
        or break_rate < 0
        or not np.isfinite(previous).all()
        or not np.isfinite(shear_rate).all()
        or np.any((previous < 0) | (previous > 1) | (shear_rate < 0))
    ):
        raise ValueError("invalid thixotropic state/time")
    # Exact solution while shear is held fixed; remains in [0,1].
    rate = 1 / rebuild_time + break_rate * shear_rate
    equilibrium = (1 / rebuild_time) / rate
    return equilibrium + (previous - equilibrium) * np.exp(-rate * dt)


def saint_venant_kirchhoff(F, mu, lam):
    F = np.asarray(F, dtype=float)
    d = F.shape[-1]
    if (
        F.shape[-2:] != (d, d)
        or d not in (2, 3)
        or not np.isfinite(F).all()
        or mu < 0
        or lam < 0
    ):
        raise ValueError("invalid elastic state/moduli")
    E = 0.5 * (np.swapaxes(F, -1, -2) @ F - np.eye(d))
    trace = np.trace(E, axis1=-2, axis2=-1)
    second = 2 * mu * E + lam * trace[..., None, None] * np.eye(d)
    return F @ second, mu * np.sum(E * E, axis=(-2, -1)) + 0.5 * lam * trace**2


def marangoni_tangent(
    normal, temperature_gradient, concentration_gradient, dsigma_dT, dsigma_dc
):
    normal = np.asarray(normal, dtype=float)
    g = dsigma_dT * np.asarray(temperature_gradient) + dsigma_dc * np.asarray(
        concentration_gradient
    )
    norm = np.linalg.norm(normal, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    if np.any(norm < 1e-12) or not np.isfinite(g).all():
        raise ValueError("finite nonzero interface normals required")
    n = normal / norm
    return g - n * np.sum(g * n, axis=-1, keepdims=True)


def immersed_drag(body_velocity, fluid_velocity, weights, drag=1.0):
    weights = np.asarray(weights, dtype=float)
    fluid_velocity = np.asarray(fluid_velocity, dtype=float)
    body_velocity = np.asarray(body_velocity, dtype=float)
    if (
        weights.ndim != 1
        or fluid_velocity.shape != (len(weights), len(body_velocity))
        or np.any(weights < 0)
        or weights.sum() <= 0
        or drag < 0
        or not all(
            np.isfinite(x).all() for x in (weights, fluid_velocity, body_velocity)
        )
    ):
        raise ValueError("invalid immersed coupling")
    weights = weights / weights.sum()
    fluid_force = drag * weights[:, None] * (body_velocity - fluid_velocity)
    return -fluid_force.sum(axis=0), fluid_force


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/matter/mpm.py
========================================================================================
"""Small CPU MLS-MPM/APIC/FLIP reference with explicit constitutive choices.

Not the real-time browser solver. Free-surface incompressibility, exact angular
momentum under walls/clipping and fully resolved FSI are not asserted.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from itertools import product

import numpy as np

from nova.bioma.determinism import SplitMix64

from .constitutive import (
    herschel_bulkley,
    saint_venant_kirchhoff,
    thixotropic_structure,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MPMConfig:
    particles: int = 512
    grid: int = 24
    dt: float = 0.001
    density: float = 1.0
    mu: float = 10.0
    lam: float = 10.0
    bulk: float = 20.0
    gravity: float = -1.0
    material: str = "liquid"
    flip: float = 0.9
    seed: int = 42

    def __post_init__(self):
        if (
            not 8 <= self.particles <= 8192
            or not 8 <= self.grid <= 48
            or not 0 < self.dt <= 0.005
            or self.material not in ("liquid", "stvk")
            or min(self.mu, self.lam, self.bulk) < 0
            or not self.density > 0
            or not 0 <= self.flip <= 1
            or not np.isfinite(
                [self.dt, self.density, self.mu, self.lam, self.bulk, self.gravity]
            ).all()
        ):
            raise ValueError("MPM resource/material budget exceeded")


class MaterialPointSystem:
    def __init__(self, config=None):
        self.config = config or MPMConfig()
        c = self.config
        rng = SplitMix64(c.seed)
        self.x = rng.uniform((c.particles, 3), 0.3, 0.7)
        self.v = np.zeros_like(self.x)
        self.C = np.zeros((c.particles, 3, 3))
        self.F = np.repeat(np.eye(3)[None], c.particles, axis=0)
        self.structure = np.ones(c.particles)
        self.tick = 0
        self.projected_singular_values = 0
        self.wall_impulse = np.zeros(3)
        self.mass = c.density * 0.4**3 / c.particles
        self.volume = self.mass / c.density

    def step(self, steps=1):
        c = self.config
        dx = 1 / c.grid
        dt = c.dt
        if not 1 <= steps <= 1000:
            raise ValueError("MPM step budget exceeded")
        for _ in range(steps):
            wave_speed = np.sqrt((c.lam + 2 * c.mu + c.bulk) / c.density)
            if dt * (wave_speed + np.linalg.norm(self.v, axis=1).max()) / dx > 0.4:
                raise ValueError("MPM explicit CFL condition exceeded")
            mass = np.zeros((c.grid,) * 3)
            momentum = np.zeros((*mass.shape, 3))
            initial_momentum = np.zeros_like(momentum)
            base = np.floor(self.x / dx - 0.5).astype(int)
            fx = self.x / dx - base
            weights = np.stack(
                (0.5 * (1.5 - fx) ** 2, 0.75 - (fx - 1) ** 2, 0.5 * (fx - 0.5) ** 2),
                axis=1,
            )
            D = 0.5 * (self.C + np.swapaxes(self.C, 1, 2))
            shear = np.sqrt(2 * np.sum(D * D, axis=(1, 2)))
            self.structure = thixotropic_structure(self.structure, shear, dt)
            if c.material == "stvk":
                P, _ = saint_venant_kirchhoff(self.F, c.mu, c.lam)
                stress = P @ np.swapaxes(self.F, 1, 2)
            else:
                J = np.linalg.det(self.F)
                pressure = c.bulk * (np.clip(J, 0.5, 2.0) ** -4 - 1)
                eta = herschel_bulkley(shear, structure=self.structure)
                stress = (
                    -pressure[:, None, None] * np.eye(3) + 2 * eta[:, None, None] * D
                )
            affine = self.mass * self.C - dt * self.volume * 4 / dx**2 * stress
            records = []
            for offset in product(range(3), repeat=3):
                node = base + offset
                if np.any(node < 0) or np.any(node >= c.grid):
                    raise ValueError(
                        "MPM support crossed grid; reduce step or maintain boundary margin"
                    )
                w = (
                    weights[:, offset[0], 0]
                    * weights[:, offset[1], 1]
                    * weights[:, offset[2], 2]
                )
                dpos = (np.asarray(offset) - fx) * dx
                index = tuple(node.T)
                np.add.at(mass, index, w * self.mass)
                carrier = self.mass * self.v + np.einsum(
                    "nij,nj->ni", self.mass * self.C, dpos
                )
                np.add.at(initial_momentum, index, w[:, None] * carrier)
                np.add.at(
                    momentum,
                    index,
                    w[:, None]
                    * (self.mass * self.v + np.einsum("nij,nj->ni", affine, dpos)),
                )
                records.append((index, w, dpos))
            occupied = mass > 0
            old = np.zeros_like(momentum)
            new = np.zeros_like(momentum)
            old[occupied] = initial_momentum[occupied] / mass[occupied, None]
            new[occupied] = momentum[occupied] / mass[occupied, None]
            new[..., 1][occupied] += dt * c.gravity
            coordinates = np.indices(mass.shape)
            for axis in range(3):
                hit = occupied & (
                    ((coordinates[axis] < 2) & (new[..., axis] < 0))
                    | ((coordinates[axis] > c.grid - 3) & (new[..., axis] > 0))
                )
                self.wall_impulse[axis] -= np.sum(mass[hit] * new[..., axis][hit])
                new[..., axis][hit] = 0
            pic = np.zeros_like(self.v)
            delta = np.zeros_like(self.v)
            C = np.zeros_like(self.C)
            for index, w, dpos in records:
                vg = new[index]
                pic += w[:, None] * vg
                delta += w[:, None] * (vg - old[index])
                C += 4 / dx**2 * w[:, None, None] * vg[:, :, None] * dpos[:, None, :]
            self.v = (1 - c.flip) * pic + c.flip * (self.v + delta)
            self.C = C
            self.x += dt * self.v
            self.x = np.clip(self.x, 1.6 * dx, 1 - 2.6 * dx)
            trial = (np.eye(3) + dt * self.C) @ self.F
            if np.any(np.linalg.det(trial) <= 0):
                raise ValueError("orientation-reversing MPM step; reduce dt")
            U, s, Vt = np.linalg.svd(trial)
            clipped = np.clip(s, 0.6, 1.4)
            self.projected_singular_values += int(np.count_nonzero(clipped != s))
            self.F = (U * clipped[:, None, :]) @ Vt
            if not all(np.isfinite(v).all() for v in (self.x, self.v, self.F)):
                raise FloatingPointError("nonfinite MPM state")
            self.tick += 1
        return self.metrics()

    def metrics(self):
        return {
            "tick": self.tick,
            "mass": self.mass * len(self.x),
            "momentum": (self.mass * self.v.sum(0)).tolist(),
            "angular_momentum": (self.mass * np.cross(self.x, self.v).sum(0)).tolist(),
            "kinetic": float(0.5 * self.mass * np.sum(self.v**2)),
            "mean_structure": float(self.structure.mean()),
            "projected_singular_values": self.projected_singular_values,
            "wall_impulse": self.wall_impulse.tolist(),
            "incompressibility": "equation of state; no exact pressure projection",
        }


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/matter/particle_life.py
========================================================================================
"""Actual directed species affinity forces with periodic spatial cell lists."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass

import numpy as np

from nova.bioma.determinism import SplitMix64, digest_state


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ParticleLifeConfig:
    count: int = 2048
    species: int = 8
    seed: int = 42
    cutoff: float = 0.18
    repulsion_radius: float = 0.25
    strength: float = 4.0
    damping: float = 1.8
    dt: float = 1 / 120
    max_speed: float = 1.5
    normalize_neighbors: bool = True
    max_neighbors: int = 2048

    def __post_init__(self):
        if (
            not 2 <= self.count <= 100000
            or not 1 <= self.species <= 8
            or not 0.06 <= self.cutoff <= 0.4
            or not 0.05 <= self.repulsion_radius <= 0.6
            or not 0 < self.strength <= 30
            or not 0 <= self.damping <= 20
            or not 0 < self.dt <= 1 / 30
            or not 0 < self.max_speed <= 5
            or not 16 <= self.max_neighbors <= 4096
        ):
            raise ValueError("particle law/resource budget exceeded")
        SplitMix64(self.seed)


def affinity_matrix(seed, species=8, symmetric=False):
    matrix = SplitMix64(seed ^ 0xAFF1717).uniform((8, 8), -1, 1)
    matrix[np.diag_indices(8)] = 0.7
    if symmetric:
        matrix = (matrix + matrix.T) * 0.5
    return matrix


def radial_force(q, affinity, beta=0.25):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=float)
    return np.where(
        q < beta, q / beta - 1, affinity * (1 - np.abs(2 * q - 1 - beta) / (1 - beta))
    ) * (q < 1)


class ParticleLife:
    """CPU reference, not the 100k browser backend.

    Directed affinities are active/nonconservative; damping exports energy.
    Neighbor truncation is counted, never described as an exact full solve.
    """

    def __init__(self, config=None, matrix=None):
        self.config = config or ParticleLifeConfig()
        c = self.config
        rng = SplitMix64(c.seed)
        self.position = rng.uniform((c.count, 3), -1, 1)
        self.velocity = np.zeros_like(self.position)
        self.types = rng.integers(0, c.species, (c.count,))
        self.matrix = (
            affinity_matrix(c.seed, c.species)
            if matrix is None
            else np.asarray(matrix, dtype=float)
        )
        if (
            self.matrix.shape != (8, 8)
            or not np.isfinite(self.matrix).all()
            or np.any(abs(self.matrix) > 1)
        ):
            raise ValueError("8x8 affinity matrix in [-1,1] required")
        self.tick = 0
        self.truncated = 0
        self.speed_clamps = 0

    def step(self, steps=1):
        if not 1 <= steps <= 4096 or self.config.count > 8192:
            raise ValueError(
                "CPU reference work budget exceeded; use browser compute for larger scenes"
            )
        c = self.config
        dim = max(1, int(2 / c.cutoff))
        cell_width = 2 / dim
        for _ in range(steps):
            buckets = {}
            cell = np.floor((self.position + 1) / cell_width).astype(int) % dim
            for i, key in enumerate(map(tuple, cell)):
                buckets.setdefault(key, []).append(i)
            acceleration = np.zeros_like(self.position)
            for i in range(c.count):
                candidates = []
                for dx in (-1, 0, 1):
                    for dy in (-1, 0, 1):
                        for dz in (-1, 0, 1):
                            candidates.extend(
                                buckets.get(tuple((cell[i] + [dx, dy, dz]) % dim), ())
                            )
                if len(candidates) > c.max_neighbors:
                    self.truncated += 1
                    candidates = candidates[: c.max_neighbors]
                ids = np.asarray(candidates, dtype=int)
                delta = self.position[ids] - self.position[i]
                delta -= 2 * np.round(delta / 2)
                distance = np.linalg.norm(delta, axis=1)
                keep = (distance > 1e-9) & (distance < c.cutoff)
                ids = ids[keep]
                delta = delta[keep]
                distance = distance[keep]
                if len(ids):
                    weights = radial_force(
                        distance / c.cutoff,
                        self.matrix[self.types[i], self.types[ids]],
                        c.repulsion_radius,
                    )
                    acceleration[i] = (
                        c.strength
                        * np.sum(delta / distance[:, None] * weights[:, None], axis=0)
                        / (max(1, len(ids)) if c.normalize_neighbors else 1)
                    )
            self.velocity = (self.velocity + c.dt * acceleration) * np.exp(
                -c.damping * c.dt
            )
            speed = np.linalg.norm(self.velocity, axis=1)
            self.speed_clamps += int(np.count_nonzero(speed > c.max_speed))
            self.velocity *= np.minimum(1, c.max_speed / np.maximum(speed, 1e-12))[
                :, None
            ]
            self.position = (self.position + c.dt * self.velocity + 1) % 2 - 1
            self.tick += 1
        return self.metrics()

    def metrics(self):
        return {
            "tick": self.tick,
            "particles": self.config.count,
            "kinetic_proxy": float(0.5 * np.sum(self.velocity**2)),
            "truncated_queries": self.truncated,
            "speed_clamps": self.speed_clamps,
            "conservative": False,
        }

    def save(self, path):
        from nova.checkpoint import save_state

        state = {
            "kind": "NOVA-PARTICLE-LIFE-CPU-v1",
            "config": asdict(self.config),
            "position": self.position,
            "velocity": self.velocity,
            "types": self.types,
            "matrix": self.matrix,
            "tick": self.tick,
            "truncated": self.truncated,
            "speed_clamps": self.speed_clamps,
        }
        save_state(path, state)
        return {
            "checkpoint": str(path),
            "state_sha256": digest_state(state),
        }


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/memory/__init__.py

"""Associative retrieval, bounded working state, and source-preserving episodes."""

from __future__ import annotations
from collections import deque
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import numpy as np
from scipy.special import softmax, logsumexp


def kwta(x, k):
    """Keep k largest magnitudes per row; ties are deterministic."""
    x = np.asarray(x)
    n = x.shape[-1]
    k = max(1, round(k * n)) if isinstance(k, float) else k
    if not 1 <= k <= n:
        raise ValueError("k outside [1, dimensions]")
    indices = np.argsort(np.abs(x), axis=-1, kind="stable")[..., -k:]
    out = np.zeros_like(x)
    np.put_along_axis(out, indices, np.take_along_axis(x, indices, -1), -1)
    return out


class ModernHopfield:
    """Recall softmax(beta * q X.T) X; E(q)=||q||²/2-logsumexp(beta Xq)/beta."""

    def __init__(self, patterns, beta=10.0):
        self.patterns = np.asarray(patterns, dtype=float)
        if self.patterns.ndim != 2 or not len(self.patterns) or beta <= 0:
            raise ValueError("invalid patterns/beta")
        self.beta = beta

    def retrieve(self, query, iterations=1):
        q = np.asarray(query, dtype=float)
        for _ in range(iterations):
            q = softmax(self.beta * (q @ self.patterns.T), axis=-1) @ self.patterns
        return q

    def energy(self, query):
        q = np.asarray(query, dtype=float)
        return (
            np.sum(q * q, axis=-1) / 2
            - logsumexp(self.beta * (q @ self.patterns.T), axis=-1) / self.beta
        )


@dataclass
class Episode:
    id: str
    embedding: np.ndarray
    content: str
    source: str
    timestamp: str
    location: str = ""
    participants: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    valence: float = 0.0
    surprise: float = 0.0
    anchor: bool = False
    revisions: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    feature_version: int = 0


class EpisodicMemory:
    def __init__(self, capacity=1024, threshold=0.5):
        self.capacity, self.threshold = capacity, threshold
        self.episodes: list[Episode] = []

    def write(self, episode: Episode, force=False):
        if not episode.source:
            raise ValueError("episode requires source")
        if any(e.id == episode.id for e in self.episodes):
            raise ValueError("duplicate episode id")
        if not force and episode.surprise < self.threshold:
            return False
        if len(self.episodes) >= self.capacity:
            candidates = [e for e in self.episodes if not e.anchor]
            if not candidates:
                return False
            victim = min(candidates, key=lambda e: e.surprise + abs(e.valence))
            self.episodes = [e for e in self.episodes if e.id != victim.id]
        self.episodes.append(episode)
        return True

    def retrieve(self, query, limit=3, *, participant=None, location=None, before=None):
        candidates = [
            e
            for e in self.episodes
            if (participant is None or participant in e.participants)
            and (location is None or location == e.location)
            and (before is None or e.timestamp < before)
        ]
        q = np.asarray(query)
        norm = max(np.linalg.norm(q), 1e-12)
        return sorted(
            candidates,
            key=lambda e: float(
                q @ e.embedding / (norm * max(np.linalg.norm(e.embedding), 1e-12))
            ),
            reverse=True,
        )[:limit]

    def reconsolidate(self, episode_id, content):
        e = next(e for e in self.episodes if e.id == episode_id)
        e.revisions.append(e.content)
        e.content = content

    def sleep(self, learn, limit=32):
        chosen = sorted(
            self.episodes, key=lambda e: e.surprise + abs(e.valence), reverse=True
        )[:limit]
        for e in chosen:
            learn(e)
        # Exact duplicates only: conflicting or similar episodes must not be merged.
        seen = set()
        kept = []
        for e in self.episodes:
            key = (e.content, e.source, e.timestamp)
            if e.anchor or key not in seen:
                kept.append(e)
            seen.add(key)
        self.episodes = kept
        return len(chosen)


class WorkingMemory:
    def __init__(self, slots=7, dimensions=8, decay=0.95):
        if not 1 <= slots <= 10 or not 0 <= decay <= 1:
            raise ValueError("invalid working-memory configuration")
        self.state = np.zeros((slots, dimensions))
        self.decay = decay
        self.cursor = 0

    def update(self, value=None, gate=1.0):
        if not 0 <= gate <= 1:
            raise ValueError("gate must be in [0,1]")
        self.state *= self.decay
        if value is not None:
            self.state[self.cursor] = (1 - gate) * self.state[
                self.cursor
            ] + gate * np.asarray(value)
            self.cursor = (self.cursor + 1) % len(self.state)
        return self.state.copy()


class SensoryBuffer(deque):
    def __init__(self, capacity=32):
        super().__init__(maxlen=capacity)


class SemanticGraph:
    """Retain contradictory facts with distinct sources; never silently merge."""

    def __init__(self, capacity=None):
        if capacity is not None and capacity < 1:
            raise ValueError("capacity must be positive")
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.evicted = 0
        self.facts = []

    def add(
        self,
        subject,
        predicate,
        value,
        source,
        confidence=1.0,
        timestamp="",
        anchor=False,
    ):
        if not source or not 0 <= confidence <= 1:
            raise ValueError("source/confidence required")
        fact = dict(
            subject=subject,
            predicate=predicate,
            value=value,
            source=source,
            confidence=confidence,
            timestamp=timestamp,
        )
        if anchor:
            fact["anchor"] = True
        if fact in self.facts:
            return False
        if self.capacity is not None and len(self.facts) >= self.capacity:
            index = next(
                (i for i, f in enumerate(self.facts) if not f.get("anchor")), None
            )
            if index is None:
                return False
            self.facts.pop(index)
            self.evicted += 1
        self.facts.append(fact)
        return True

    def query(self, subject, predicate):
        return [
            f.copy()
            for f in self.facts
            if f["subject"] == subject and f["predicate"] == predicate
        ]


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/neuromod/__init__.py
========================================================================================
"""Bounded functional control signals, independently ablatable."""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import numpy as np


@dataclass
class Neuromodulator:
    enabled: frozenset[str] = field(
        default_factory=lambda: frozenset({"NE", "DA", "ACh", "5HT"})
    )
    error_scale: float = 1.0
    surprise: float = 0.0
    reward_pe: float = 0.0
    uncertainty: float = 0.0
    patience: float = 0.5

    def update(self, error, reward=0.0, value=0.0, next_value=0.0, uncertainty=0.0):
        error = max(0.0, float(error))
        self.surprise = float(np.clip(error / max(self.error_scale, 1e-8), 0, 10))
        self.error_scale = 0.99 * self.error_scale + 0.01 * error
        self.reward_pe = float(np.clip(reward + 0.99 * next_value - value, -10, 10))
        self.uncertainty = float(np.clip(uncertainty, 0, 1))
        self.patience = float(
            np.clip(0.99 * self.patience + 0.01 * (1 - self.uncertainty), 0, 1)
        )
        return self

    def learning_rate(self, base):
        return base * (1 + min(self.surprise, 2)) if "NE" in self.enabled else base

    def write_threshold(self, base):
        return base / (1 + self.surprise) if "NE" in self.enabled else base

    def sensory_weight(self):
        return 0.5 + 0.5 * self.uncertainty if "ACh" in self.enabled else 0.5

    def consolidation(self):
        return float(1 / (1 + np.exp(-self.reward_pe))) if "DA" in self.enabled else 0.5

    def discount(self):
        return 0.9 + 0.099 * self.patience if "5HT" in self.enabled else 0.95

"""Interchangeable N0, N1 and small-compartment N2 research models."""

from .models import RateNeuron, AdExPopulation, HodgkinHuxleyCompartments, make_neuron

__all__ = ["RateNeuron", "AdExPopulation", "HodgkinHuxleyCompartments", "make_neuron"]


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/neuron/models.py
========================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations
import numpy as np
from scipy.special import expit
from nova.continual import ProtectedLinear
from nova.memory import kwta


class RateNeuron:
    """N0: y=ReLU(v_b*(1+tanh(v_a))); k-WTA after apical gain."""

    def __init__(self, inputs=8, units=32, seed=0, k=0.1, apical_gain=True):
        self.basal = ProtectedLinear(inputs, units, seed=seed)
        self.apical = ProtectedLinear(inputs, units, seed=seed + 1)
        self.k, self.apical_gain = k, apical_gain
        self.state = np.zeros(units)

    def step(self, inputs, modulators=None, dt=0.1):
        if dt <= 0:
            raise ValueError("dt must be positive")
        basal, apical = inputs
        vb = self.basal(np.asarray(basal)[None])[0]
        va = self.apical(np.asarray(apical)[None])[0]
        output = vb * (1 + np.tanh(va)) if self.apical_gain else vb + va
        self.state = kwta(np.maximum(output, 0), self.k)
        return self.state.copy(), {"rate": self.state.copy()}


class AdExPopulation:
    """N1, ms/mV/pA/pF: C dv/dt=-gL(v-EL)+gL*Delta*exp((v-VT)/Delta)-w+gc(d-v)+I.

    tau_w dw/dt=a(v-EL)-w; dendritic conductance coupling, optional NMDA sigmoid.
    Euler dt <= 1 ms. Types: 0=E, 1=PV, 2=SST, 3=VIP. Sign belongs to outgoing
    synapses and is enforced by Microcircuit. Parameters are research defaults.
    """

    def __init__(self, units=100, seed=0, backend="numpy", nmda=False):
        if units < 1:
            raise ValueError("units must be positive")
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.types = np.zeros(units, dtype=int)
        self.types[int(0.8 * units) : int(0.9 * units)] = 1
        self.types[int(0.9 * units) : int(0.95 * units)] = 2
        self.types[int(0.95 * units) :] = 3
        self.a = np.choose(self.types, [2.0, 0.0, 4.0, 2.0])
        self.b = np.choose(self.types, [40.0, 0.0, 80.0, 20.0])
        self.tau = np.choose(self.types, [200.0, 30.0, 250.0, 150.0]) * rng.lognormal(
            0, 0.1, units
        )
        self.v = np.full(units, -70.0)
        self.w = np.zeros(units)
        self.dendrite = self.v.copy()
        self.refractory = np.zeros(units)
        self.backend, self.nmda = backend, nmda

    def step(self, inputs, modulators=None, dt=0.1):
        if not 0 < dt <= 1:
            raise ValueError("N1 requires 0 < dt <= 1 ms")
        basal, apical = inputs
        basal = np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(basal, dtype=float), self.v.shape)
        apical = np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(apical, dtype=float), self.v.shape)
        if not np.isfinite(basal).all() or not np.isfinite(apical).all():
            raise ValueError("nonfinite currents")
        if self.backend == "rust":
            from nova.core import require_native

            args = [
                a.tolist()
                for a in (
                    self.v,
                    self.w,
                    self.dendrite,
                    self.refractory,
                    basal,
                    apical,
                    self.a,
                    self.b,
                    self.tau,
                )
            ]
            result = require_native().adex_step(*args, dt, self.nmda)
            self.v, self.w, self.dendrite, self.refractory, spikes = map(
                np.asarray, result
            )
        elif self.backend == "numpy":
            v, w, d = self.v, self.w, self.dendrite
            self.dendrite = (
                d
                + dt
                * (
                    -5 * (d + 70)
                    + 2 * (v - d)
                    + apical
                    + (30 * expit((d + 40) / 5) if self.nmda else 0)
                )
                / 100
            )
            self.w = w + dt * (self.a * (v + 70) - w) / self.tau
            updated = (
                v
                + dt
                * (
                    -10 * (v + 70)
                    + 20 * np.exp(np.minimum((v + 50) / 2, 10))
                    - w
                    + 2 * (d - v)
                    + basal
                )
                / 200
            )
            self.v = np.where(self.refractory > 0, -58.0, updated)
            self.refractory = np.maximum(self.refractory - dt, 0)
            spikes = self.v >= 20
            self.v[spikes] = -58
            self.w[spikes] += self.b[spikes]
            self.refractory[spikes] = 2
        else:
            raise ValueError("backend must be numpy or rust")
        return spikes.copy(), self.state_dict()

    def state_dict(self):
        return {
            key: getattr(self, key).copy()
            for key in ("v", "w", "dendrite", "refractory", "types", "a", "b", "tau")
        }

    def load_state_dict(self, state):
        for key, value in state.items():
            if key not in self.state_dict() or np.shape(value) != self.v.shape:
                raise ValueError("invalid neuron checkpoint")
            setattr(self, key, np.asarray(value).copy())


class HodgkinHuxleyCompartments:
    """N2 small branched cable: Na/K HH gates plus phenomenological Ca and Ih.

    C dV/dt=I-gNa*m³h(V-50)-gK*n⁴(V+77)-gCa*c²(V-120)-gh*q(V+20)
             -gL(V+54.4)+sum gc(V_neighbor-V). Gates relax to voltage equilibria.
    Explicit RK4, dt <= .05 ms; not a fitted cellular morphology.
    """

    def __init__(self, compartments=4):
        if compartments < 1:
            raise ValueError("compartments must be positive")
        self.v = np.full(compartments, -65.0)
        self.gates = self._steady(self.v)[0]
        self.coupling = np.zeros((compartments, compartments))
        for i in range(1, compartments):
            self.coupling[i, (i - 1) // 2] = self.coupling[(i - 1) // 2, i] = 0.1

    @staticmethod
    def _ratio(x):
        x = np.asarray(x)
        out = np.ones_like(x)
        np.divide(x, -np.expm1(-x), out=out, where=np.abs(x) > 1e-7)
        return out

    def _steady(self, v):
        am = self._ratio((v + 40) / 10)
        bm = 4 * np.exp(-(v + 65) / 18)
        ah = 0.07 * np.exp(-(v + 65) / 20)
        bh = expit((v + 35) / 10)
        an = 0.1 * self._ratio((v + 55) / 10)
        bn = 0.125 * np.exp(-(v + 65) / 80)
        steady = np.stack(
            (
                am / (am + bm),
                ah / (ah + bh),
                an / (an + bn),
                expit((v + 20) / 6),
                expit(-(v + 80) / 8),
            )
        )
        tau = np.stack(
            (
                1 / (am + bm),
                1 / (ah + bh),
                1 / (an + bn),
                np.full_like(v, 5),
                np.full_like(v, 100),
            )
        )
        return steady, tau

    def step(self, inputs, modulators=None, dt=0.01):
        if not 0 < dt <= 0.05:
            raise ValueError("N2 requires 0 < dt <= .05 ms")
        basal, apical = inputs
        current = np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(basal, dtype=float), self.v.shape).copy()
        current[1:] += np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(apical, dtype=float), self.v.shape)[
            1:
        ]
        before = self.v.copy()
        state = np.vstack((self.v, self.gates))

        def f(s):
            v = s[0]
            m, h, n, c, q = s[1:]
            steady, tau = self._steady(v)
            ionic = (
                120 * m**3 * h * (v - 50)
                + 36 * n**4 * (v + 77)
                + 0.05 * c * c * (v - 120)
                + 0.02 * q * (v + 20)
                + 0.3 * (v + 54.4)
            )
            dv = current - ionic + self.coupling @ v - self.coupling.sum(1) * v
            return np.vstack((dv, (steady - s[1:]) / tau))

        k1 = f(state)
        k2 = f(state + dt * k1 / 2)
        k3 = f(state + dt * k2 / 2)
        k4 = f(state + dt * k3)
        state += dt * (k1 + 2 * k2 + 2 * k3 + k4) / 6
        self.v, self.gates = state[0], state[1:]
        return (before < 0) & (self.v >= 0), {
            "v": self.v.copy(),
            "gates": self.gates.copy(),
        }


def make_neuron(level="N0", **kwargs):
    return {"N0": RateNeuron, "N1": AdExPopulation, "N2": HodgkinHuxleyCompartments}[
        level
    ](**kwargs)

"""Offline, self-contained metric explorer; no server or external assets."""

from pathlib import Path
import json


def dashboard(metrics_path, output):
    rows = [
        json.loads(line)
        for line in Path(metrics_path).read_text().splitlines()
        if line.strip()
    ]
    payload = json.dumps(rows).replace("<", "\\u003c")
    html = """<!doctype html><html lang="pt-BR"><meta charset="utf-8"><title>NOVA — métricas</title>

<label>Métrica <select id="metric"></select></label><canvas id="chart" width="950" height="350"></canvas><table id="summary"></table>
<script>const data=PAYLOAD;const select=document.getElementById('metric');
const keys=[...new Set(data.flatMap(row=>Object.keys(row).filter(k=>typeof row[k]==='number'&&Number.isFinite(row[k])&&k!=='tick')))];
for(const key of keys){const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=o.textContent=key;select.append(o)}
function draw(){const key=select.value,values=data.map(d=>d[key]),c=document.getElementById('chart'),ctx=c.getContext('2d');ctx.clearRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);if(!values.length)return;
const finite=values.filter(v=>typeof v==='number'&&Number.isFinite(v));if(!finite.length)return;const low=Math.min(...finite),high=Math.max(...finite),span=high-low||1;ctx.strokeStyle='#38bdf8';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();let pen=false;values.forEach((v,i)=>{if(typeof v!=='number'||!Number.isFinite(v)){pen=false;return;}const x=40+i*870/Math.max(values.length-1,1),y=310-(v-low)*270/span;pen?ctx.lineTo(x,y):ctx.moveTo(x,y);pen=true});ctx.stroke();ctx.fillStyle='#e2e8f0';ctx.fillText(high.toPrecision(5),5,25);ctx.fillText(low.toPrecision(5),5,325);ctx.fillText('ticks: '+values.length,400,345);

        "PAYLOAD", payload
    )
    Path(output).write_text(html)

========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/optics/path_tracer.py
