"""Bounded spectral Monte Carlo reference with NEE/MIS, dielectric spheres and HG.

Independent CPU calibration scene; not a production BDPT/ReSTIR pipeline or the
browser particle scene. Wavelength-to-XYZ uses analytic CIE fits, not a measured
spectral camera. No rendered result is asserted without an explicit execution.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np

from nova.bioma.determinism import SplitMix64

from .transport import aces_fitted, henyey_greenstein, thin_film_reflectance


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Sphere:
    center: tuple
    radius: float
    pigment: tuple = (0.5, 0.5, 0.5)
    material: str = "diffuse"
    emission: float = 0.0
    ior: float = 1.45
    film_nm: float = 0.0
    scattering: float = 2.0


def unit(v):
    return v / max(np.linalg.norm(v), 1e-15)


def xyz_fit(w):
    def gaussian(mu, left, right):
        return np.exp(-0.5 * ((w - mu) * (left if w < mu else right)) ** 2)

    return np.array(
        [
            0.362 * gaussian(442.0, 0.0624, 0.0374)
            + 1.056 * gaussian(599.8, 0.0264, 0.0323)
            - 0.065 * gaussian(501.1, 0.049, 0.0382),
            0.821 * gaussian(568.8, 0.0213, 0.0247)
            + 0.286 * gaussian(530.9, 0.0613, 0.0322),
            1.217 * gaussian(437.0, 0.0845, 0.0278)
            + 0.681 * gaussian(459.0, 0.0385, 0.0725),
        ]
    )


def spectrum(rgb, wavelength):
    weights = np.exp(-0.5 * ((wavelength - np.array([620.0, 535.0, 450.0])) / 45) ** 2)
    weights /= max(weights.sum(), 1e-12)
    return float(np.clip(np.asarray(rgb) @ weights, 0.005, 0.99))


def basis(direction):
    w = unit(direction)
    u = unit(np.cross([0, 1, 0] if abs(w[1]) < 0.9 else [1, 0, 0], w))
    return u, np.cross(w, u), w


def cosine_sample(normal, rng):
    u, v, w = basis(normal)
    a = 2 * np.pi * rng.uniform((1,))[0]
    r = np.sqrt(rng.uniform((1,))[0])
    return unit(u * r * np.cos(a) + v * r * np.sin(a) + w * np.sqrt(max(0, 1 - r * r)))


def hg_sample(direction, g, rng):
    r = float(rng.uniform((1,))[0])
    cosine = (
        1 - 2 * r
        if abs(g) < 1e-6
        else (1 + g * g - ((1 - g * g) / (1 - g + 2 * g * r)) ** 2) / (2 * g)
    )
    sine = np.sqrt(max(0, 1 - cosine * cosine))
    angle = 2 * np.pi * rng.uniform((1,))[0]
    u, v, w = basis(direction)
    return unit(sine * (np.cos(angle) * u + np.sin(angle) * v) + cosine * w)


class SpectralPathTracer:
    def __init__(
        self,
        spheres=None,
        *,
        seed=42,
        max_bounces=8,
        scattering=0.05,
        absorption=0.01,
        g=0.25,
    ):
        self.spheres = spheres or [
            Sphere((0, -1001, 0), 1000, (0.25, 0.26, 0.3)),
            Sphere((-0.65, 0, 0), 0.68, (0.9, 0.05, 0.2), "dielectric", film_nm=350),
            Sphere((0.65, 0, -0.1), 0.65, (0.03, 0.35, 0.9), "dielectric", film_nm=620),
            Sphere((0, 2.8, 1.0), 0.65, (1, 1, 1), "diffuse", emission=20.0),
        ]
        if (
            len(self.spheres) > 64
            or not 1 <= max_bounces <= 16
            or not 0 <= scattering <= 10
            or not 0 <= absorption <= 10
            or not -0.95 <= g <= 0.95
        ):
            raise ValueError("optical scene/path budget exceeded")
        for s in self.spheres:
            if (
                len(s.center) != 3
                or len(s.pigment) != 3
                or not np.isfinite(
                    [*s.center, *s.pigment, s.radius, s.emission, s.ior, s.film_nm]
                ).all()
                or s.radius <= 0
                or s.material not in ("diffuse", "dielectric")
                or not 1 <= s.ior <= 3
                or s.emission < 0
                or s.film_nm < 0
                or not 0 <= s.scattering <= 100
                or any(not 0 < c <= 1 for c in s.pigment)
            ):
                raise ValueError("invalid optical sphere")
        self.rng = SplitMix64(seed)
        self.max_bounces = max_bounces
        self.scattering = scattering
        self.absorption = absorption
        self.g = g
        self.lights = [i for i, s in enumerate(self.spheres) if s.emission > 0]

    def hit(self, origin, direction, limit=np.inf):
        best = limit
        selected = None
        for i, s in enumerate(self.spheres):
            offset = origin - np.asarray(s.center)
            b = offset @ direction
            c = offset @ offset - s.radius * s.radius
            d = b * b - c
            if d < 0:
                continue
            root = np.sqrt(d)
            near = -b - root
            far = -b + root
            t = near if near > 1e-5 else far
            if 1e-5 < t < best:
                best = t
                selected = i
        return best, selected

    def direct(self, p, normal, incoming, wavelength, phase=False):
        if not self.lights:
            return 0.0
        light_id = self.lights[int(self.rng.integers(0, len(self.lights), (1,))[0])]
        light = self.spheres[light_id]
        z = self.rng.uniform((1,), -1, 1)[0]
        angle = self.rng.uniform((1,), 0, 2 * np.pi)[0]
        n = np.array(
            [np.sqrt(1 - z * z) * np.cos(angle), z, np.sqrt(1 - z * z) * np.sin(angle)]
        )
        point = np.asarray(light.center) + light.radius * n
        d = point - p
        distance = np.linalg.norm(d)
        direction = d / max(distance, 1e-12)
        cos_light = max(0, -n @ direction)
        if cos_light <= 0:
            return 0.0
        hit_distance, hit_id = self.hit(
            p + direction * 1e-4, direction, distance + 1e-3
        )
        if hit_id != light_id or abs(hit_distance - distance) > 2e-3:
            return 0.0
        light_pdf = (
            distance
            * distance
            / (4 * np.pi * light.radius**2 * cos_light * len(self.lights))
        )
        bsdf_pdf = (
            float(henyey_greenstein(np.clip(incoming @ direction, -1, 1), self.g))
            if phase
            else max(0, normal @ direction) / np.pi
        )
        mis = light_pdf**2 / (light_pdf**2 + bsdf_pdf**2)
        return (
            light.emission
            * np.exp(-(self.absorption + self.scattering) * distance)
            * bsdf_pdf
            / light_pdf
            * mis
        )

    def trace(self, origin, direction, wavelength):
        throughput = 1.0
        radiance = 0.0
        inside = None
        previous_pdf = 0.0
        previous_point = origin
        previous_nee = False
        for bounce in range(self.max_bounces):
            distance, index = self.hit(origin, direction)
            if distance > 20.0:
                index = None
                distance = 20.0
            max_distance = min(distance, 20.0)
            sigma_a = self.absorption
            sigma_s = self.scattering
            if inside is not None:
                sigma_s += self.spheres[inside].scattering
                sigma_a += (
                    -np.log(spectrum(self.spheres[inside].pigment, wavelength)) * 1.5
                )
            sigma_t = sigma_a + sigma_s
            free = (
                -np.log(max(float(self.rng.uniform((1,))[0]), 2.0**-53)) / sigma_t
                if sigma_t > 0
                else np.inf
            )
            if free < max_distance:
                origin = origin + direction * free
                throughput *= sigma_s / max(sigma_t, 1e-20)
                if throughput <= 0:
                    break
                if inside is None:
                    radiance += throughput * self.direct(
                        origin, None, direction, wavelength, phase=True
                    )
                old_direction = direction
                direction = hg_sample(direction, self.g, self.rng)
                previous_pdf = float(
                    henyey_greenstein(np.clip(old_direction @ direction, -1, 1), self.g)
                )
                previous_point = origin.copy()
                previous_nee = inside is None
                origin += direction * 1e-4
            else:
                if index is None:
                    radiance += throughput * 0.025
                    break
                p = origin + distance * direction
                s = self.spheres[index]
                outward = unit(p - np.asarray(s.center))
                front = direction @ outward < 0
                normal = outward if front else -outward
                if s.emission:
                    weight = 1.0
                    if previous_nee and previous_pdf > 0:
                        cos_light = max(0, -outward @ direction)
                        light_pdf = np.linalg.norm(p - previous_point) ** 2 / max(
                            4 * np.pi * s.radius**2 * cos_light * len(self.lights),
                            1e-20,
                        )
                        weight = previous_pdf**2 / (previous_pdf**2 + light_pdf**2)
                    radiance += throughput * s.emission * weight
                    break
                previous_point = p.copy()
                if s.material == "diffuse":
                    albedo = spectrum(s.pigment, wavelength)
                    radiance += (
                        throughput
                        * albedo
                        * self.direct(p, normal, direction, wavelength)
                    )
                    direction = cosine_sample(normal, self.rng)
                    throughput *= albedo
                    previous_pdf = max(normal @ direction, 0) / np.pi
                    previous_nee = True
                else:
                    eta_i, eta_t = (1.0, s.ior) if front else (s.ior, 1.0)
                    eta = eta_i / eta_t
                    cosine = max(0, -direction @ normal)
                    sin2 = eta * eta * (1 - cosine * cosine)
                    if sin2 >= 1:
                        fresnel = 1.0
                    else:
                        c2 = np.sqrt(1 - sin2)
                        rs = (eta_i * cosine - eta_t * c2) / (
                            eta_i * cosine + eta_t * c2
                        )
                        rp = (eta_t * cosine - eta_i * c2) / (
                            eta_t * cosine + eta_i * c2
                        )
                        fresnel = 0.5 * (rs * rs + rp * rp)
                        if front and s.film_nm:
                            fresnel = float(
                                thin_film_reflectance(
                                    wavelength,
                                    s.film_nm,
                                    s.scattering,
                                    max(cosine, 1e-6),
                                    n0=eta_i,
                                    n1=1.33,
                                    n2=eta_t,
                                )
                            )
                    if self.rng.uniform((1,))[0] < fresnel:
                        direction = unit(direction + 2 * cosine * normal)
                    else:
                        direction = unit(
                            eta * direction
                            + (eta * cosine - np.sqrt(max(0, 1 - sin2))) * normal
                        )
                        throughput *= eta * eta
                        inside = index if front else None
                    previous_pdf = 0.0
                    previous_nee = False
                origin = p + direction * 1e-4
            if bounce >= 3:
                survival = float(np.clip(throughput, 0.05, 0.95))
                if self.rng.uniform((1,))[0] > survival:
                    break
                throughput /= survival
        return radiance

    def render(self, width=128, height=96, samples=16, *, aperture=0.015, focus=3.6):
        if (
            not 16 <= width <= 512
            or not 16 <= height <= 512
            or not 1 <= samples <= 256
            or width * height * samples > 8_000_000
            or not 0 <= aperture <= 0.2
            or not 1 <= focus <= 10
        ):
            raise ValueError("CPU spectral render budget exceeded")
        eye = np.array([0.0, 0.65, 3.5])
        forward = unit(np.array([0.0, 0.0, 0.0]) - eye)
        right = unit(np.cross(forward, [0, 1, 0]))
        up = np.cross(right, forward)
        xyz = np.zeros((height, width, 3))
        for y in range(height):
            for x in range(width):
                for _ in range(samples):
                    jitter = self.rng.uniform((2,))
                    u = (2 * (x + jitter[0]) / width - 1) * width / height * 0.35
                    v = (1 - 2 * (y + jitter[1]) / height) * 0.35
                    direction = unit(forward + u * right + v * up)
                    focus_point = eye + direction * focus / (direction @ forward)
                    radius = aperture * np.sqrt(self.rng.uniform((1,))[0])
                    angle = 2 * np.pi * self.rng.uniform((1,))[0]
                    origin = eye + radius * (np.cos(angle) * right + np.sin(angle) * up)
                    direction = unit(focus_point - origin)
                    wavelength = 380 + 400 * self.rng.uniform((1,))[0]
                    xyz[y, x] += (
                        self.trace(origin, direction, wavelength)
                        * xyz_fit(wavelength)
                        * 400
                        / (106.856895 * samples)
                    )
        matrix = np.array(
            [
                [3.2406, -1.5372, -0.4986],
                [-0.9689, 1.8758, 0.0415],
                [0.0557, -0.204, 1.057],
            ]
        )
        hdr = np.maximum(xyz @ matrix.T, 0)
        display = aces_fitted(hdr) ** (1 / 2.2)
        return hdr, np.rint(display * 255).astype(np.uint8)

"""Weighted reservoir / RIS primitive; not a complete ReSTIR DI/GI renderer."""

from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np

from nova.bioma.determinism import SplitMix64


@dataclass
class Reservoir:
    seed: int = 0

    def __post_init__(self):
        self.rng = SplitMix64(self.seed)
        self.sample = None
        self.target = 0.0
        self.weight_sum = 0.0
        self.candidates = 0

    def update(self, sample, target, proposal_pdf):
        if (
            not np.isfinite([target, proposal_pdf]).all()
            or target < 0
            or proposal_pdf <= 0
        ):
            raise ValueError("finite target and positive proposal density required")
        weight = target / proposal_pdf
        if not np.isfinite(weight) or not np.isfinite(self.weight_sum + weight):
            raise ValueError("reservoir weights overflow")
        self.candidates += 1
        self.weight_sum += weight
        if weight and self.rng.uniform((1,))[0] < weight / self.weight_sum:
            self.sample = sample
            self.target = target

    def estimate(self, integrand_at_sample):
        if self.sample is None:
            return 0.0
        return (
            np.asarray(integrand_at_sample)
            * self.weight_sum
            / (self.candidates * self.target)
        )

    def merge_reweighted(self, other, target_at_new_receiver):
        if other.sample is None:
            return
        if not np.isfinite(target_at_new_receiver) or target_at_new_receiver < 0:
            raise ValueError("invalid receiver target")
        weight = target_at_new_receiver * other.weight_sum / max(other.target, 1e-30)
        total = self.weight_sum + weight
        if not np.isfinite(total):
            raise ValueError("reused reservoir overflow")
        self.candidates += other.candidates
        if weight and self.rng.uniform((1,))[0] < weight / max(total, 1e-30):
            self.sample = other.sample
            self.target = target_at_new_receiver
        self.weight_sum = total

"""Beer-Lambert, HG phase, diffuse BSSRDF profile and complex thin-film optics."""

import numpy as np


def beer_lambert(absorption, scattering, distance):
    a, s, d = np.broadcast_arrays(absorption, scattering, distance)
    if not all(np.isfinite(x).all() for x in (a, s, d)) or np.any(
        (a < 0) | (s < 0) | (d < 0)
    ):
        raise ValueError("finite nonnegative extinction and distance required")
    return np.exp(-(a + s) * d)


def henyey_greenstein(cosine, g):
    c = np.asarray(cosine, dtype=float)
    if not np.isfinite(c).all() or np.any(abs(c) > 1) or not -0.99 <= g <= 0.99:
        raise ValueError("phase domain exceeded")
    return (1 - g * g) / (4 * np.pi * (1 + g * g - 2 * g * c) ** 1.5)


def burley_profile(radius, diffusion_length):
    r, d = np.broadcast_arrays(radius, diffusion_length)
    if (
        not np.isfinite(r).all()
        or not np.isfinite(d).all()
        or np.any(r <= 0)
        or np.any(d <= 0)
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            "positive radius/diffusion length required; profile singularity is integrable at zero"
        )
    return (np.exp(-r / d) + np.exp(-r / (3 * d))) / (8 * np.pi * d * r)


def thin_film_reflectance(
    wavelength_nm, thickness_nm, cos_incident=1.0, n0=1.0, n1=1.33, n2=1.5
):
    wavelength = np.asarray(wavelength_nm, dtype=float)
    if (
        np.any(wavelength <= 0)
        or not np.isfinite(wavelength).all()
        or not np.isfinite(thickness_nm)
        or thickness_nm < 0
        or not 0 < cos_incident <= 1
    ):
        raise ValueError("invalid optical geometry/wavelength")
    # Complex refractive indices permit absorbing film/substrate. Branch chosen
    # with nonnegative forward attenuation; wavelengths/thickness share units.
    n0, n1, n2 = complex(n0), complex(n1), complex(n2)
    if any(
        n.real <= 0 or n.imag < 0 or not np.isfinite([n.real, n.imag]).all()
        for n in (n0, n1, n2)
    ):
        raise ValueError("passive refractive indices required")
    sin2 = 1 - cos_incident * cos_incident
    c1 = np.sqrt(1 - (n0 / n1) ** 2 * sin2 + 0j)
    c2 = np.sqrt(1 - (n0 / n2) ** 2 * sin2 + 0j)
    if (n1 * c1).imag < 0:
        c1 = -c1
    if (n2 * c2).imag < 0:
        c2 = -c2
    r01s = (n0 * cos_incident - n1 * c1) / (n0 * cos_incident + n1 * c1)
    r12s = (n1 * c1 - n2 * c2) / (n1 * c1 + n2 * c2)
    r01p = (n1 * cos_incident - n0 * c1) / (n1 * cos_incident + n0 * c1)
    r12p = (n2 * c1 - n1 * c2) / (n2 * c1 + n1 * c2)
    propagation = np.exp(4j * np.pi * n1 * c1 * thickness_nm / wavelength)
    s = (r01s + r12s * propagation) / (1 + r01s * r12s * propagation)
    p = (r01p + r12p * propagation) / (1 + r01p * r12p * propagation)
    return np.clip(0.5 * (np.abs(s) ** 2 + np.abs(p) ** 2), 0, 1)


def aces_fitted(linear_rgb, exposure=1.0):
    x = np.asarray(linear_rgb, dtype=float) * exposure
    if not np.isfinite(x).all() or np.any(x < 0):
        raise ValueError("finite nonnegative HDR radiance required")
    return np.clip(x * (2.51 * x + 0.03) / (x * (2.43 * x + 0.59) + 0.14), 0, 1)


def diffraction_psf(size=128, aperture_radius=0.4, blades=0):
    if (
        not 32 <= size <= 512
        or not 0.05 <= aperture_radius <= 0.49
        or not 0 <= blades <= 12
        or blades in (1, 2)
    ):
        raise ValueError("pupil discretization budget exceeded")
    axis = (np.arange(size) - size / 2) / size
    x, y = np.meshgrid(axis, axis)
    radius = np.sqrt(x * x + y * y)
    boundary = np.full_like(radius, aperture_radius)
    if blades:
        theta = np.arctan2(y, x)
        sector = (theta + np.pi / blades) % (2 * np.pi / blades) - np.pi / blades
        boundary = aperture_radius * np.cos(np.pi / blades) / np.cos(sector)
    pupil = (radius <= boundary).astype(float)
    field = np.fft.fftshift(np.fft.fft2(np.fft.ifftshift(pupil)))
    intensity = abs(field) ** 2
    return intensity / intensity.sum()

ARQUIVO: nova/perception/__init__.py

"""Modality encoders, streaming log-mel audio, slots and predictive inference."""

from __future__ import annotations
import math
import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F


class VisionEncoder(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, latent_dim=16, channels=3):
        super().__init__()
        self.net = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv2d(channels, 16, 3, padding=1),
            nn.SiLU(),
            nn.Conv2d(16, 32, 3, stride=2, padding=1),
            nn.SiLU(),
            nn.AdaptiveAvgPool2d(1),
            nn.Flatten(),
            nn.Linear(32, latent_dim),
        )

    def foveate(self, image, gaze=(0.0, 0.0), radius=0.3):
        if radius <= 0:
            raise ValueError("radius must be positive")
        h, w = image.shape[-2:]
        yy, xx = torch.meshgrid(
            torch.linspace(-1, 1, h, device=image.device),
            torch.linspace(-1, 1, w, device=image.device),
            indexing="ij",
        )
        mask = torch.exp(-((xx - gaze[0]) ** 2 + (yy - gaze[1]) ** 2) / (2 * radius**2))
        low = F.interpolate(
            F.interpolate(image, size=(max(1, h // 8), max(1, w // 8)), mode="area"),
            size=(h, w),
            mode="bilinear",
            align_corners=False,
        )
        return image * mask + low * (1 - mask)

    def forward(self, image, gaze=(0.0, 0.0)):
        return self.net(self.foveate(image, gaze))


def mel_filters(sample_rate, n_fft, bands):
    hz_to_mel = lambda hz: 2595 * math.log10(1 + hz / 700)
    points = 700 * (
        10 ** (torch.linspace(0, hz_to_mel(sample_rate / 2), bands + 2) / 2595) - 1
    )
    freq = torch.linspace(0, sample_rate / 2, n_fft // 2 + 1)
    rising = (freq[None] - points[:-2, None]) / (points[1:-1, None] - points[:-2, None])
    falling = (points[2:, None] - freq[None]) / (points[2:, None] - points[1:-1, None])
    return torch.minimum(rising, falling).clamp_min(0)


class StreamingAudioEncoder(nn.Module):
    """Uncentered STFT -> log-mel -> causal temporal convolution; chunk-invariant."""

    def __init__(self, latent_dim=16, sample_rate=16000, n_fft=256, hop=128, bands=24):
        super().__init__()
        if not 0 < hop <= n_fft:
            raise ValueError("hop must be in (0,n_fft]")
        self.n_fft, self.hop, self.bands = n_fft, hop, bands
        self.register_buffer("filters", mel_filters(sample_rate, n_fft, bands))
        self.register_buffer("window", torch.hann_window(n_fft))
        self.register_buffer("samples", torch.empty(0))
        self.register_buffer("history", torch.zeros(1, bands, 2))
        self.conv = nn.Conv1d(bands, latent_dim, 3)

    def reset(self):
        self.samples = self.samples.new_empty(0)
        self.history.zero_()

    def forward(self, chunk):
        if chunk.ndim != 1:
            raise ValueError("expected mono one-dimensional samples")
        samples = torch.cat((self.samples, chunk))
        if len(samples) < self.n_fft:
            self.samples = samples.detach()
            return samples.new_empty((0, self.conv.out_channels))
        frames = samples.unfold(0, self.n_fft, self.hop)
        spectrum = torch.fft.rfft(frames * self.window).abs().square()
        mel = torch.log1p(spectrum @ self.filters.T).T[None]
        sequence = torch.cat((self.history, mel), dim=2)
        output = self.conv(sequence).squeeze(0).T
        self.history = sequence[:, :, -2:].detach()
        self.samples = samples[len(frames) * self.hop :].detach()
        return output


class TextEncoder(nn.Module):
    """UTF-8 byte tokens (padding=256) and a small recurrent sensory encoder."""

    def __init__(self, latent_dim=16):
        super().__init__()
        self.embedding = nn.Embedding(257, 32, padding_idx=256)
        self.gru = nn.GRU(32, latent_dim, batch_first=True)

    def forward(self, texts):
        encoded = [list(t.encode("utf-8")) or [256] for t in texts]
        if not encoded:
            raise ValueError("empty batch")
        lengths = torch.tensor([len(t) for t in encoded])
        tokens = torch.full(
            (len(encoded), int(lengths.max())),
            256,
            dtype=torch.long,
            device=self.embedding.weight.device,
        )
        for i, tokens_i in enumerate(encoded):
            tokens[i, : len(tokens_i)] = torch.tensor(tokens_i, device=tokens.device)
        packed = nn.utils.rnn.pack_padded_sequence(
            self.embedding(tokens), lengths, batch_first=True, enforce_sorted=False
        )
        _, state = self.gru(packed)
        return state[-1]


class SlotAttention(nn.Module):
    """Iterative slot competition; previous slots provide temporal object context."""

    def __init__(self, dimension=16, slots=4, iterations=3):
        super().__init__()
        self.initial = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(1, slots, dimension) * 0.1)
        self.key = nn.Linear(dimension, dimension)
        self.query = nn.Linear(dimension, dimension)
        self.value = nn.Linear(dimension, dimension)
        self.update = nn.GRUCell(dimension, dimension)
        self.iterations = iterations

    def forward(self, tokens, previous=None):
        slots = (
            self.initial.expand(len(tokens), -1, -1) if previous is None else previous
        )
        k, v = self.key(tokens), self.value(tokens)
        for _ in range(self.iterations):
            score = (
                self.query(slots) @ k.transpose(-1, -2) / math.sqrt(tokens.shape[-1])
            )
            attention = score.softmax(dim=1) + 1e-8
            attention = attention / attention.sum(-1, keepdim=True)
            updates = attention @ v
            slots = self.update(
                updates.reshape(-1, tokens.shape[-1]),
                slots.reshape(-1, tokens.shape[-1]),
            ).reshape_as(slots)
        return slots


class MultimodalFusion(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, dimension=16):
        super().__init__()
        self.cross = nn.Linear(dimension, dimension, bias=False)

    def cross_prediction_loss(self, a, b):
        return F.smooth_l1_loss(self.cross(a), b.detach()) + F.smooth_l1_loss(
            self.cross(b), a.detach()
        )

    def forward(self, modalities, precision=None):
        stack = torch.stack(modalities, dim=1)
        weights = (
            torch.ones(stack.shape[:2], device=stack.device)
            if precision is None
            else precision
        )
        if torch.any(weights < 0) or torch.any(weights.sum(1) <= 0):
            raise ValueError("precision must be nonnegative with positive sum")
        return (stack * weights[:, :, None]).sum(1) / weights.sum(1, keepdim=True)


class PredictiveHierarchy:
    """E=1/2 sum_l pi_l ||r_l-W_l r_(l+1)||²; relax representations by gradient descent."""

    def __init__(self, dimensions=(8, 4, 2), seed=0):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.weights = [
            rng.normal(0, 1 / np.sqrt(b), (a, b))
            for a, b in zip(dimensions[:-1], dimensions[1:])
        ]
        self.dimensions = dimensions

    def infer(self, observation, precision=None, steps=100, lr=0.03):
        x = np.asarray(observation, dtype=float)
        states = [x.copy()] + [
            np.zeros((*x.shape[:-1], d)) for d in self.dimensions[1:]
        ]
        precision = [1.0] * len(self.weights) if precision is None else precision
        if len(precision) != len(self.weights) or any(
            np.any(np.asarray(p) < 0) for p in precision
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid precision")
        history = []
        for _ in range(steps):
            errors = [
                states[i] - states[i + 1] @ w.T for i, w in enumerate(self.weights)
            ]
            history.append(
                float(
                    sum(
                        np.sum(np.asarray(p) * e * e) / 2
                        for p, e in zip(precision, errors)
                    )
                )
            )
            gradients = []
            for i in range(1, len(states)):
                grad = -(errors[i - 1] * precision[i - 1]) @ self.weights[i - 1]
                if i < len(errors):
                    grad += errors[i] * precision[i]
                gradients.append(grad)
            for i, grad in enumerate(gradients, 1):
                states[i] -= lr * grad
        return states, history

    def learn(self, states, lr=0.001):
        for i, w in enumerate(self.weights):
            error = states[i] - states[i + 1] @ w.T
            w += (
                lr
                * np.atleast_2d(error).T
                @ np.atleast_2d(states[i + 1])
                / len(np.atleast_2d(error))
            )


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/planetary.py
========================================================================================
"""Opt-in bounded orchestration of a local biosphere, proof spool and peer mesh."""

from __future__ import annotations
import asyncio
from collections import deque
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
import threading
import time
from .bioma.determinism import canonical_json, strict_json
from .bioma.macro_evolution import LineageArchive
from .swarm.artifacts import ArtifactStore, SigningIdentity, ScientificLedger, atomic
from .swarm.dht_discovery import DiscoveryTable, descriptor, LanDiscovery
from .swarm.p2p_node import PeerNode
from .swarm.spore_migration import SporeMigration, make_spore
from .swarm.cross_validation import CrossValidationWorker
from .scientific.auto_lean import ProofDaemon


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PlanetaryConfig:
    directory: str = "runs/planetary"
    host: str = "127.0.0.1"
    port: int = 8766
    advertise: str = "tcp://127.0.0.1:8766"
    bootstrap: str | None = None
    allow_lan: bool = False
    mdns_ip: str | None = None
    certfile: str | None = None
    keyfile: str | None = None
    transport: str = "tcp"
    migration: bool = False
    validate_challenges: bool = False
    ice_file: str | None = None
    lean_project: str | None = None
    science: bool = True
    max_ticks: int = 10000
    tick_interval: float = 0.05

    def __post_init__(self):
        if (
            not 1 <= self.port <= 65535
            or not 1 <= self.max_ticks <= 100000
            or not 0.01 <= self.tick_interval <= 60
        ):
            raise ValueError("planetary service clock/port budget exceeded")
        if self.mdns_ip and not self.allow_lan:
            raise ValueError("mDNS requires explicit LAN admission")


class PlanetaryService:
    def __init__(self, ecosystem, config=PlanetaryConfig()):
        self.ecosystem = ecosystem
        self.config = config
        self.directory = Path(config.directory)
        self.directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.identity = SigningIdentity.load_or_create(
            self.directory / "private" / "signing.key"
        )
        self.store = ArtifactStore(self.directory / "artifacts")
        self.discovery = DiscoveryTable(
            self.identity,
            descriptor(self.identity, config.advertise),
            allow_lan=config.allow_lan,
        )
        self.node = PeerNode(self.identity, self.store, discovery=self.discovery)
        self.node.migration = SporeMigration(self.identity, enabled=config.migration)
        self.node.cross_validation = (
            CrossValidationWorker(self.identity) if config.validate_challenges else None
        )
        self.proofs = ProofDaemon(
            self.directory / "science", lean_project=config.lean_project
        )
        self.archive = LineageArchive()
        self.lock = threading.RLock()
        self.shutdown = threading.Event()
        self.paused = False
        self.thread = None
        self.jobs = {}
        self._loop = None
        self._task = None
        self.probes = deque(maxlen=512)
        self.state = {
            "status": "created",
            "peer_id": self.identity.peer_id,
            "routing_peers": 0,
            "events": [],
            "discoveries": [],
            "migrations": [],
            "proofs": [],
        }
        self.recent_spores = set()
        self.ledger = None
        atomic(self.directory / "descriptor.json", canonical_json(self.discovery.own))

    def event(self, kind, body):
        record = {"kind": kind, **body}
        self.state["events"] = (self.state["events"] + [record])[-128:]
        if self.ledger:
            self.ledger.append(
                kind
                if kind
                in (
                    "discovery",
                    "lineage",
                    "peer_claim",
                    "verification",
                    "reproduction",
                )
                else "peer_claim",
                record,
            )

    def control(self, paused):
        if type(paused) is not bool:
            raise ValueError("paused must be boolean")
        self.paused = paused

    def atlas(self):
        with self.lock:
            return {
                **self.state,
                "routing_peers": len(self.node.routing.peers),
                "paused": self.paused,
                "simulation": self.ecosystem.metrics() if self.ecosystem else None,
                "communications": self.ecosystem.interpreter.report()
                if self.ecosystem and hasattr(self.ecosystem, "interpreter")
                else None,
                "ancestry": self.archive.snapshot(),
                "network_scope": "bounded self-signed peer discovery; no trusted global consensus",
                "attestations": list(
                    self.node.cross_validation.receipts.copy().values()
                )[-32:]
                if self.node.cross_validation
                else [],
            }

    def advance(self):
        with self.lock:
            eco = self.ecosystem
            if self.paused or eco is None or eco.world.tick >= self.config.max_ticks:
                return None
            world = eco.world
            before = None
            if hasattr(world, "laplacian") and world.config.mode in (
                "gray_scott",
                "hybrid",
            ):
                index = tuple(n // 2 for n in world.shape)
                before = (
                    float(world.u[index]),
                    float(world.v[index]),
                    float(world.laplacian(world.u)[index]),
                    float(world.laplacian(world.v)[index]),
                )
            eco.step()
            if before is not None:
                self.probes.append(
                    {
                        "features": before,
                        "target": float((world.v[index] - before[1]) / world.config.dt),
                        "tick": world.tick,
                    }
                )
            if hasattr(eco, "genesis_genomes"):
                if world.tick % 16 == 0:
                    self.archive.observe(eco)
                migration = self.node.migration.admit(eco)
            else:
                migration = None
            return migration

    def discover(self):
        if (
            len(self.probes) < 24
            or not self.ecosystem
            or not hasattr(self.ecosystem, "genesis_genomes")
        ):
            return None
        import numpy as np
        from .scientific.non_polynomial import NonPolynomialRegressor
        from .scientific.polynomial import Polynomial
        from .swarm.holoseed import HoloSeed
        from .scientific.dataset_synthesizer import DatasetSynthesizer

        with self.lock:
            rows = list(self.probes)
            packet = HoloSeed.pack(self.ecosystem, identity=self.identity)
            physics = self.ecosystem.world.config
        seed_digest = self.store.put_json(packet)
        dataset = DatasetSynthesizer(self.directory / "science" / "dataset")
        for row in rows[-64:]:
            dataset.append(
                row,
                seed_digest=seed_digest,
                units={"time": "nondimensional", "space": "grid cells"},
            )
        dataset.manifest()
        candidate = NonPolynomialRegressor().fit(
            np.asarray([r["features"] for r in rows]),
            np.asarray([r["target"] for r in rows]),
            ["u", "v", "lap_u", "lap_v"],
        )
        candidate["seed_artifact"] = seed_digest
        candidate["tick"] = rows[-1]["tick"]
        candidate["finite_difference_coupling_warning"] = True
        digest = self.store.put_json(self.identity.sign(candidate))
        atomic(
            self.directory / "science" / "latest_candidate.json",
            canonical_json(candidate),
        )
        # Only a candidate independently equal to the declared polynomial term can
        # become a proved identity. Transcendental candidates remain unproved.
        expected = (
            f"({physics.diffusion_v})*lap_v+u*v*v-({physics.feed}+{physics.kill})*v"
        )
        try:
            Polynomial.parse(candidate["expression"], candidate["variables"])
            job = self.proofs.submit(
                candidate["expression"],
                expected,
                candidate["variables"],
                model={
                    "physics_term": "continuous Gray-Scott dv; excludes coupling/integration"
                },
            )
            candidate["proof_job"] = job
        except (ValueError, SyntaxError):
            candidate["proof_status"] = "outside_polynomial_Lean_template"
        return {
            "artifact": digest,
            "expression": candidate["expression"],
            "heldout_mse": candidate["heldout_mse"],
            "formal_proof": False,
        }

    def spawn(self, name, operation):
        if name in self.jobs and not self.jobs[name].done():
            return False

        async def runner():
            try:
                await operation()
            except (
                ValueError,
                OSError,
                ImportError,
                KeyError,
                TypeError,
                asyncio.TimeoutError,
            ) as error:
                self.state[name + "_status"] = {
                    "status": "inconclusive",
                    "reason": type(error).__name__,
                }

        self.jobs[name] = asyncio.create_task(runner())
        return True

    async def mesh_job(self, seeds):
        self.discovery.own = descriptor(self.identity, self.config.advertise)
        atomic(self.directory / "descriptor.json", canonical_json(self.discovery.own))
        self.state["discovery"] = await self.discovery.discover(self.node, seeds)
        if self.node.webrtc:
            for peer in list(self.node.routing.peers)[:2]:
                if peer not in self.node.webrtc.connections:
                    try:
                        await self.node.webrtc.connect(peer)
                    except (
                        ValueError,
                        KeyError,
                        TypeError,
                        OSError,
                        asyncio.TimeoutError,
                    ):
                        pass
        atomic(
            self.directory / "peers.json",
            canonical_json(
                [
                    {"peer_id": p.peer_id, "endpoint": p.endpoint}
                    for p in self.node.routing.peers.values()
                ]
            ),
        )

    async def science_job(self):
        candidate = await asyncio.to_thread(self.discover)
        if candidate:
            self.state["discoveries"] = (self.state["discoveries"] + [candidate])[-128:]
            self.event("discovery", candidate)

    async def proof_job(self):
        proofs = await asyncio.to_thread(self.proofs.process_once)
        for proof in proofs:
            self.event("verification", proof)
        self.state["proofs"] = (self.state["proofs"] + proofs)[-128:]

    async def spore_job(self, tick):
        with self.lock:
            organisms = [
                o
                for o in self.ecosystem.organisms
                if o.age >= 64
                and o.energy >= 3
                and o.genome.hash() not in self.recent_spores
            ]
            organism = max(organisms, key=lambda o: o.energy, default=None)
            spore = (
                make_spore(
                    self.identity,
                    organism.genome,
                    {"tick": tick, "peer": self.identity.peer_id},
                )
                if organism
                else None
            )
        if spore:
            self.store.put_json(spore)
            self.recent_spores.add(spore["payload"]["genome_sha256"])
            for peer in list(self.node.routing.peers)[:3]:
                try:
                    await self.node.request(peer, "spore", spore)
                except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError, OSError, asyncio.TimeoutError):
                    pass

    async def run(self, duration=None):
        self._loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
        self._task = asyncio.current_task()
        config = self.config
        self.ledger = ScientificLedger(self.directory / "ledger.sqlite", self.identity)
        server = None
        lan = None
        started = time.monotonic()
        last_tick = -1
        next_proof = 0.0
        next_discovery = 0.0
        last_spore = -1
        try:
            if config.ice_file:
                from .swarm.webrtc_transport import WebRTCTransport

                ice = strict_json(Path(config.ice_file).read_bytes())
                self.node.webrtc = WebRTCTransport(self.node, ice)
            server = asyncio.create_task(
                self.node.serve(
                    config.host,
                    config.port,
                    certfile=config.certfile,
                    keyfile=config.keyfile,
                    transport=config.transport,
                )
            )
            await asyncio.sleep(0)
            if config.mdns_ip:
                lan = LanDiscovery(self.discovery.own)
                await lan.start(config.mdns_ip, config.port)
            bootstraps = (
                []
                if not config.bootstrap
                else strict_json(Path(config.bootstrap).read_bytes())
            )
            self.state["status"] = "running"
            print(
                canonical_json(
                    {
                        "NOVA": "planetary_experimental",
                        "peer_id": self.identity.peer_id,
                        "endpoint": config.advertise,
                    }
                ).decode(),
                flush=True,
            )
            while not self.shutdown.is_set() and (
                duration is None or time.monotonic() - started < duration
            ):
                if server.done():
                    await server
                now = time.monotonic()
                if now >= next_discovery:
                    seeds = bootstraps + ([] if not lan else lan.found[:32])
                    if self.spawn(
                        "mesh", lambda seeds=seeds: self.mesh_job(seeds[:32])
                    ):
                        bootstraps = []
                        if lan:
                            lan.found.clear()
                    next_discovery = now + 30
                migration = await asyncio.to_thread(self.advance)
                if migration:
                    self.state["migrations"] = (self.state["migrations"] + [migration])[
                        -64:
                    ]
                    self.event("lineage", migration)
                tick = self.ecosystem.world.tick if self.ecosystem else 0
                if tick != last_tick and tick % 64 == 0 and tick > 0:
                    if config.science:
                        self.spawn("science", self.science_job)
                    last_tick = tick
                if now >= next_proof:
                    self.spawn("proof", self.proof_job)
                    next_proof = now + 2
                if self.node.cross_validation:
                    for receipt in self.node.cross_validation.process_once():
                        self.store.put_json(receipt)
                        self.event("verification", {"attestation": receipt})
                if config.migration and self.ecosystem and tick - last_spore >= 256:
                    self.spawn("spore", lambda tick=tick: self.spore_job(tick))
                    last_spore = tick
                await asyncio.sleep(config.tick_interval)
        finally:
            self.state["status"] = "stopped"
            for task in self.jobs.values():
                task.cancel()
            await asyncio.gather(*self.jobs.values(), return_exceptions=True)
            if server:
                server.cancel()
                await asyncio.gather(server, return_exceptions=True)
            if lan:
                await lan.close()
            if self.node.webrtc:
                await self.node.webrtc.close()
            atomic(self.directory / "atlas.json", canonical_json(self.atlas()))
            self.ledger.close()
            self.ledger = None

    def start(self):
        def target():
            try:
                asyncio.run(self.run())
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                pass
            except (ValueError, OSError, ImportError) as error:
                self.state["status"] = "error"
                self.state["error"] = str(error)[:512]

        self.thread = threading.Thread(
            target=target, daemon=True, name="nova-planetary"
        )
        self.thread.start()

    def close(self):
        self.shutdown.set()
        if self.thread:
            if self._loop and self._task and not self._task.done():
                self._loop.call_soon_threadsafe(self._task.cancel)
            self.thread.join(timeout=10)

ARQUIVO: nova/planetary_cli.py
========================================================================================
"""v0.4 commands; network services, proof workers and GPU execution are explicit."""

from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import asyncio
from pathlib import Path
import zipfile
import numpy as np
from nova.bioma.determinism import canonical_json, strict_json

COMMANDS = {
    "start",
    "join",
    "native",
    "nonlinear",
    "hamiltonian",
    "proof-daemon",
    "proof-submit",
    "challenge",
    "aggregate",
    "spore",
    "descriptor",
}


def read_arrays(path, names):
    with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as archive:
        if (
            len(archive.infolist()) > 8
            or sum(entry.file_size for entry in archive.infolist()) > 64 * 1024 * 1024
        ):
            raise ValueError("numerical input archive exceeds budget")
    with np.load(path, allow_pickle=False) as data:
        return [data[name].copy() for name in names]


def main(argv):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog="nova cosm",
        description="Experimental planetary orchestration; no performance or universal-truth guarantee",
    )
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    for name in ("start", "join"):
        p = sub.add_parser(name)
        p.add_argument(
            "--preset",
            default="organisms",
            choices=(
                "organisms",
                "ink",
                "lenia",
                "conway",
                "light",
                "particles",
                "fluid",
                "nca",
                "fixed",
            ),
        )
        p.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
        p.add_argument("--size", type=int, default=32)
        p.add_argument("--resume")
        p.add_argument("--crossover-rate", type=float, default=0.1)
        p.add_argument(
            "--signaling", action=argparse.BooleanOptionalAction, default=True
        )
        p.add_argument("--directory", default="runs/planetary")
        p.add_argument("--bootstrap", help="JSON list of signed peer descriptors")
        p.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1")
        p.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8766)
        p.add_argument(
            "--advertise",
            help="reachable signed endpoint; defaults to listener address/port",
        )
        p.add_argument("--allow-lan", action="store_true")
        p.add_argument("--mdns-ip")
        p.add_argument("--certfile")
        p.add_argument("--keyfile")
        p.add_argument("--transport", choices=("tcp", "websocket"), default="tcp")
        p.add_argument("--migration", action="store_true")
        p.add_argument("--validate-challenges", action="store_true")
        p.add_argument(
            "--ice-file",
            help="local STUN/TURN JSON; may contain private credentials, never shared",
        )
        p.add_argument("--lean-project")
        p.add_argument("--science", action=argparse.BooleanOptionalAction, default=True)
        p.add_argument("--max-ticks", type=int, default=10000)
        if name == "start":
            p.add_argument(
                "--backend", choices=("python", "rust", "webgpu"), default="python"
            )
            p.add_argument("--renderer", choices=("webgl", "webgpu"), default="webgpu")
            p.add_argument("--viewer-port", type=int, default=8765)
            p.add_argument("--open-browser", action="store_true")
        else:
            p.add_argument("--duration", type=int, default=3600)
    p = sub.add_parser("native")
    p.add_argument("--backend", choices=("rust", "webgpu"), default="rust")
    p.add_argument("--size", type=int, default=32)
    p.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    p.add_argument("--ticks", type=int, default=100)
    p.add_argument("--fluid", action="store_true")
    p.add_argument("--memory-mib", type=int, default=512)
    p.add_argument("--output", default="runs/native.npz")
    for name in ("nonlinear", "hamiltonian"):
        p = sub.add_parser(name)
        p.add_argument(
            "--input",
            required=True,
            help="bounded local NPZ with x,y or states,derivatives arrays",
        )
        p.add_argument("--variables", nargs="+", required=True)
        p.add_argument("--output", default=f"runs/{name}.json")
        if name == "hamiltonian":
            p.add_argument("--basis", nargs="+", required=True)
    for name in ("proof-daemon", "proof-submit"):
        p = sub.add_parser(name)
        p.add_argument("--directory", default="runs/planetary/science")
        p.add_argument("--lean-project")
        if name == "proof-daemon":
            p.add_argument("--once", action="store_true")
        else:
            p.add_argument("--lhs", required=True)
            p.add_argument("--rhs", required=True)
            p.add_argument("--variables", nargs="+", required=True)
    for name in ("challenge", "spore", "descriptor"):
        p = sub.add_parser(name)
        p.add_argument("--directory", default="runs/planetary")
        p.add_argument("--output", default=f"runs/{name}.json")
        if name == "descriptor":
            p.add_argument("--endpoint", required=True)
        if name == "spore":
            p.add_argument("--genome", required=True)
        if name == "challenge":
            p.add_argument("--lhs", required=True)
            p.add_argument("--rhs", default="0")
            p.add_argument("--variables", nargs="+", required=True)
            p.add_argument("--bounds", required=True, help="JSON [[lo,hi],...]")
            p.add_argument(
                "--kind",
                choices=("identity", "oscillator_invariant"),
                default="identity",
            )
            p.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
            p.add_argument("--samples", type=int, default=512)
            p.add_argument("--local", action="store_true")
        if name in ("challenge", "spore"):
            p.add_argument(
                "--send-peers",
                help="explicit bounded JSON peer list; up to 50 recipients",
            )
    p = sub.add_parser("aggregate")
    p.add_argument("--receipts", required=True)
    p.add_argument("--challenge", required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.command in ("start", "join"):
        from .bioma.presets import create_ecosystem
        from .bioma.ecosystem import Ecosystem
        from .planetary import PlanetaryConfig, PlanetaryService

        if args.command == "start" and args.backend != "python":
            from .core.resident import ResidentSimulation, ResidentConfig

            if args.resume:
                parser.error(
                    "native snapshots are exports, not resumable ecosystem checkpoints"
                )
            eco = ResidentSimulation(ResidentConfig(args.size, args.seed, args.backend))
        else:
            eco = (
                Ecosystem.load(args.resume)
                if args.resume
                else create_ecosystem(
                    args.preset,
                    seed=args.seed,
                    size=args.size,
                    signaling=args.signaling,
                    crossover_rate=args.crossover_rate,
                )
            )
        if args.advertise is None:
            if args.host in ("0.0.0.0", "::"):
                parser.error(
                    "a wildcard listener requires an explicit reachable --advertise endpoint"
                )
            host = f"[{args.host}]" if ":" in args.host else args.host
            scheme = (
                ("wss" if args.certfile else "ws")
                if args.transport == "websocket"
                else ("tls" if args.certfile else "tcp")
            )
            args.advertise = f"{scheme}://{host}:{args.port}"
        config = PlanetaryConfig(
            **{
                name: getattr(args, name)
                for name in (
                    "directory",
                    "host",
                    "port",
                    "advertise",
                    "bootstrap",
                    "allow_lan",
                    "mdns_ip",
                    "certfile",
                    "keyfile",
                    "transport",
                    "migration",
                    "validate_challenges",
                    "ice_file",
                    "lean_project",
                    "science",
                    "max_ticks",
                )
            }
        )
        service = PlanetaryService(eco, config)
        try:
            if args.command == "start":
                from .visuals.holographic_canvas import start

                start(
                    eco,
                    port=args.viewer_port,
                    renderer=args.renderer,
                    planetary=service,
                    open_browser=args.open_browser,
                )
            else:
                if not 1 <= args.duration <= 86400:
                    parser.error("--duration must be in [1,86400]")
                try:
                    asyncio.run(service.run(args.duration))
                except KeyboardInterrupt:
                    pass
        finally:
            service.close()
            if hasattr(eco, "genesis_genomes"):
                with service.lock:
                    eco.save(Path(args.directory) / "ecosystem.npz")
            from .visuals.holographic_canvas import export_atlas

            export_atlas(service.atlas(), Path(args.directory) / "science_atlas.html")
        return
    if args.command == "native":
        from .core.resident import ResidentSimulation, ResidentConfig

        if not 1 <= args.ticks <= 1024 or not 1 <= args.memory_mib <= 8192:
            parser.error("native steps/memory budget exceeded")
        simulation = ResidentSimulation(
            ResidentConfig(
                args.size,
                args.seed,
                args.backend,
                args.fluid,
                args.memory_mib * 1024**2,
            )
        )
        simulation.step(args.ticks)
        result = {**simulation.save(args.output), "metrics": simulation.metrics()}
    elif args.command in ("nonlinear", "hamiltonian"):
        if args.command == "nonlinear":
            from .scientific.non_polynomial import NonPolynomialRegressor

            x, y = read_arrays(args.input, ["x", "y"])
            result = NonPolynomialRegressor().fit(x, y, args.variables)
        else:
            from .scientific.hamiltonian_search import HamiltonianSearch

            x, y = read_arrays(args.input, ["states", "derivatives"])
            result = HamiltonianSearch(args.variables, args.basis).fit(x, y)
        output = Path(args.output)
        output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        output.write_bytes(canonical_json(result))
    elif args.command in ("proof-daemon", "proof-submit"):
        from .scientific.auto_lean import ProofDaemon

        worker = ProofDaemon(args.directory, lean_project=args.lean_project)
        if args.command == "proof-submit":
            result = {
                "job": worker.submit(args.lhs, args.rhs, args.variables),
                "status": "queued",
            }
        elif args.once:
            result = {"receipts": worker.process_once()}
        else:
            try:
                worker.run()
            except KeyboardInterrupt:
                worker.stop.set()
            return
    elif args.command == "aggregate":
        from .swarm.cross_validation import aggregate_attestations

        result = aggregate_attestations(
            strict_json(Path(args.receipts).read_bytes(), 4 * 1024 * 1024),
            args.challenge,
        )
    else:
        from .swarm.artifacts import SigningIdentity, ArtifactStore

        identity = SigningIdentity.load_or_create(
            Path(args.directory) / "private" / "signing.key"
        )
        store = ArtifactStore(Path(args.directory) / "artifacts")
        if args.command == "descriptor":
            from .swarm.dht_discovery import descriptor

            packet = descriptor(identity, args.endpoint)
        elif args.command == "spore":
            from .swarm.spore_migration import make_spore
            from .bioma.genetics import MorphogeneticCPPN

            content = strict_json(Path(args.genome).read_bytes())
            genome = MorphogeneticCPPN.from_state_dict(content.get("champion", content))
            packet = make_spore(identity, genome, {"peer": identity.peer_id})
        else:
            from .swarm.cross_validation import make_challenge, CrossValidationWorker

            packet = make_challenge(
                identity,
                args.lhs,
                args.rhs,
                args.variables,
                strict_json(args.bounds),
                kind=args.kind,
                seed=args.seed,
                samples=args.samples,
            )
        output = Path(args.output)
        output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        output.write_bytes(canonical_json(packet))
        result = {"artifact": store.put_json(packet), "output": str(output)}
        if args.command == "challenge" and args.local:
            worker = CrossValidationWorker(identity)
            worker.enqueue(packet, identity.peer_id)
            result["local_evidence"] = worker.process_once()
        if args.command in ("challenge", "spore") and args.send_peers:
            from .swarm.p2p_node import Peer, PeerNode

            peers = [Peer(**p) for p in strict_json(Path(args.send_peers).read_bytes())]
            if not 1 <= len(peers) <= 50:
                raise ValueError("recipient budget is [1,50]")

            async def distribute():
                node = PeerNode(identity, store, peers)
                gate = asyncio.Semaphore(3)

                async def send(peer):
                    async with gate:
                        try:
                            return {
                                "peer": peer.peer_id,
                                "result": await node.request(
                                    peer.peer_id, args.command, packet
                                ),
                            }
                        except (
                            ValueError,
                            KeyError,
                            TypeError,
                            OSError,
                            asyncio.TimeoutError,
                        ) as error:
                            return {
                                "peer": peer.peer_id,
                                "status": "failed",
                                "error": type(error).__name__,
                            }

                return await asyncio.gather(*(send(peer) for peer in peers))

            result["recipients"] = asyncio.run(distribute())
    print(canonical_json(result).decode())
