"""Receding-horizon CEM baseline, explicit feasibility and warm-start state."""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from time import perf_counter
import numpy as np


@dataclass
class Plan:
    action: np.ndarray
    cost: float | None
    iterations: int
    rollouts: int
    diagnostics: dict = field(default_factory=dict)


class LatentPlanner:
    """CEM searches H*action_dim variables; latent_dim affects rollout cost.

    Reuses the shifted previous plan, retains a feasible incumbent, and rejects
    invalid trajectories. The time budget is soft and checked between model
    calls. A zero stop command is not certified safe when every candidate fails.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        predictor,
        cost,
        action_dim=1,
        horizon=12,
        rollouts=128,
        iterations=4,
        elite_fraction=0.1,
        seed=0,
        discount=0.99,
        constraint=None,
    ):
        if (
            min(action_dim, horizon, rollouts, iterations) < 1
            or not 0 < elite_fraction <= 1
            or not 0 < discount <= 1
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid search configuration")
        self.predictor, self.cost = predictor, cost
        self.action_dim, self.horizon, self.rollouts, self.iterations = (
            action_dim,
            horizon,
            rollouts,
            iterations,
        )
        self.elite_fraction, self.discount, self.constraint = (
            elite_fraction,
            discount,
            constraint,
        )
        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.warm_actions = np.zeros((horizon, action_dim))
        self.last_diagnostics = {}

    def plan(self, z, budget_ms=None):
        if budget_ms is not None and (not np.isfinite(budget_ms) or budget_ms <= 0):
            raise ValueError("positive time budget required")
        z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
        if z.ndim != 1 or not np.isfinite(z).all():
            raise ValueError("finite latent vector required")
        start = perf_counter()
        mean = self.warm_actions.copy()
        std = np.ones_like(mean)
        best_cost = float("inf")
        best = None
        completed = 0
        calls = 0
        reason = "iterations"
        for iteration in range(self.iterations):
            if budget_ms is not None and (perf_counter() - start) * 1000 >= budget_ms:
                reason = "deadline"
                break
            actions = np.clip(
                self.rng.normal(mean, std, (self.rollouts, *mean.shape)), -1, 1
            )
            actions[0] = self.warm_actions if best is None else best
            states = np.broadcast_to(z, (self.rollouts, len(z))).copy()
            costs = np.zeros(self.rollouts)
            valid = np.ones(self.rollouts, dtype=bool)
            aborted = False
            for t in range(self.horizon):
                if (
                    budget_ms is not None
                    and (perf_counter() - start) * 1000 >= budget_ms
                ):
                    reason = "deadline"
                    aborted = True
                    break
                states = np.asarray(self.predictor(states, actions[:, t]))
                calls += self.rollouts
                if states.shape != (self.rollouts, len(z)):
                    raise ValueError("invalid model shape")
                valid &= np.isfinite(states).all(1)
                safe_states = np.where(np.isfinite(states), states, 0.0)
                step_cost = np.asarray(self.cost(safe_states), dtype=float)
                if step_cost.shape != (self.rollouts,):
                    raise ValueError("invalid cost shape")
                valid &= np.isfinite(step_cost)
                costs += self.discount**t * np.where(
                    np.isfinite(step_cost), step_cost, 0
                )
                if self.constraint is not None:
                    valid &= np.all(
                        safe_states
                        >= self.constraint.lower - self.constraint.tolerance,
                        axis=1,
                    )
                    valid &= np.all(
                        safe_states
                        <= self.constraint.upper + self.constraint.tolerance,
                        axis=1,
                    )
                states = safe_states
            if aborted:
                break
            eligible = np.flatnonzero(valid)
            if not len(eligible):
                reason = "no_feasible_rollout"
                completed = iteration + 1
                continue
            elite = eligible[
                np.argsort(costs[eligible])[
                    : max(1, int(self.rollouts * self.elite_fraction))
                ]
            ]
            if costs[elite[0]] < best_cost:
                best_cost = float(costs[elite[0]])
                best = actions[elite[0]].copy()
            mean = actions[elite].mean(0)
            std = np.maximum(actions[elite].std(0), 0.05)
            completed = iteration + 1
        if best is None:
            action = np.zeros(self.action_dim)
            self.warm_actions.fill(0)
        else:
            action = best[0].copy()
            self.warm_actions = np.vstack((best[1:], best[-1]))
        self.last_diagnostics = {
            "solver": "cem",
            "reason": reason,
            "feasible": best is not None,
            "model_calls": calls,
            "search_variables": self.horizon * self.action_dim,
            "iterations": completed,
            "latency_ms": (perf_counter() - start) * 1000,
            "fallback": None if best is not None else "no_verified_feasible_trajectory",
        }
        return Plan(
            action,
            None if best is None else best_cost,
            completed,
            completed * self.rollouts,
            self.last_diagnostics.copy(),
        )

    def state_dict(self):
        return {
            "warm_actions": self.warm_actions.copy(),
            "rng": self.rng.bit_generator.state,
        }

    def load_state_dict(self, state):
        if "warm_actions" in state:
            actions = np.asarray(state["warm_actions"], dtype=float)
            if actions.shape != self.warm_actions.shape:
                raise ValueError("invalid CEM checkpoint shape")
            self.warm_actions = actions.copy()
        self.rng.bit_generator.state = state["rng"]


def drift_diffusion(
    evidence, *, threshold=1.0, noise=0.1, dt=0.01, max_steps=1000, seed=0, urgency=0.0
):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    evidence = np.asarray(evidence, dtype=float)
    state = np.zeros_like(evidence)
    for step in range(max_steps):
        state += evidence * dt + noise * np.sqrt(dt) * rng.normal(size=state.shape)
        if state.max() >= max(0.1, threshold - urgency * step * dt):
            return int(state.argmax()), (step + 1) * dt
    return int(state.argmax()), max_steps * dt


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/planning/energy.py
========================================================================================
"""Action-space energy optimization with exact dynamics derivatives and budgets."""

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
from time import perf_counter
import numpy as np
from . import Plan


class TemporalSheaf:
    """Matrix-free temporal coboundary: D_e z = R_left z_e - R_right z_(e+1).

    E=weight/2 ||Dz||², gradient=weight D.T Dz. Default restrictions are the
    identity; nontrivial restrictions must be supplied explicitly. A consistency
    penalty is not a replacement for learned dynamics or a safety certificate.
    """

    def __init__(self, dimension: int, weight: float = 0.0, left=None, right=None):
        if dimension < 1 or not np.isfinite(weight) or weight < 0:
            raise ValueError("invalid sheaf regularizer")
        self.dimension, self.weight = dimension, weight
        self.identity = left is None and right is None
        self.left = np.eye(dimension) if left is None else np.asarray(left, dtype=float)
        self.right = (
            np.eye(dimension) if right is None else np.asarray(right, dtype=float)
        )
        if self.left.shape != (dimension, dimension) or self.right.shape != (
            dimension,
            dimension,
        ):
            raise ValueError("restriction shape mismatch")

    def energy_gradient(self, states: np.ndarray) -> tuple[float, np.ndarray]:
        states = np.asarray(states, dtype=float)
        if states.ndim != 2 or states.shape[1] != self.dimension:
            raise ValueError("invalid trajectory shape")
        gradient = np.zeros_like(states)
        if len(states) < 2 or not self.weight:
            return 0.0, gradient
        if self.identity:
            error = states[:-1] - states[1:]
            gradient[:-1] += self.weight * error
            gradient[1:] -= self.weight * error
            return float(0.5 * self.weight * np.sum(error * error)), gradient
        error = states[:-1] @ self.left.T - states[1:] @ self.right.T
        gradient[:-1] += self.weight * error @ self.left
        gradient[1:] -= self.weight * error @ self.right
        return float(0.5 * self.weight * np.sum(error * error)), gradient


class BoxStateConstraint:
    """Soft squared violation plus an explicit hard feasibility predicate."""

    def __init__(self, lower, upper, penalty=100.0, tolerance=1e-8):
        self.lower = np.asarray(lower, dtype=float)
        self.upper = np.asarray(upper, dtype=float)
        if (
            not np.isfinite(self.lower).all()
            or not np.isfinite(self.upper).all()
            or self.lower.shape != self.upper.shape
            or np.any(self.lower > self.upper)
            or penalty < 0
            or tolerance < 0
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid state bounds")
        self.penalty, self.tolerance = penalty, tolerance

    def energy_gradient(self, states):
        low = np.minimum(states - self.lower, 0)
        high = np.maximum(states - self.upper, 0)
        return float(
            self.penalty * np.sum(low * low + high * high)
        ), 2 * self.penalty * (low + high)

    def feasible(self, states):
        return bool(
            np.all(states >= self.lower - self.tolerance)
            and np.all(states <= self.upper + self.tolerance)
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EnergyPlannerConfig:
    horizon: int = 12
    iterations: int = 20
    learning_rate: float = 0.3
    discount: float = 0.99
    action_penalty: float = 0.001
    slew_penalty: float = 0.01
    sheaf_weight: float = 0.0
    max_model_calls: int = 4096
    line_search_steps: int = 8
    gradient_tolerance: float = 1e-5

    def __post_init__(self):
        if (
            min(self.horizon, self.iterations, self.line_search_steps) < 1
            or self.max_model_calls < self.horizon
        ):
            raise ValueError("insufficient solver budget")
        if (
            not 0 < self.discount <= 1
            or self.learning_rate <= 0
            or self.gradient_tolerance <= 0
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid solver scales")
        if any(
            not np.isfinite(x) or x < 0
            for x in (self.action_penalty, self.slew_penalty, self.sheaf_weight)
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid energy weights")


class SolverBudgetExceeded(RuntimeError):
    pass


class EnergyPlanner:
    """Projected action descent through differentiable dynamics, with Armijo search.

    The search variables are H*action_dim, also for CEM. Increasing latent_dim
    increases model/Jacobian cost; it does not change that search dimension.
    Gradients use an explicit backward costate recursion, never torch.autograd.
    A model must supply predict(z,a) and linearize(z,a)->(next,F_z,F_a).
    Nonconvex trajectory optimization can stop at a local optimum.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model,
        cost_gradient,
        action_dim: int,
        latent_dim: int,
        *,
        config: EnergyPlannerConfig | None = None,
        constraint=None,
    ):
        if min(action_dim, latent_dim) < 1:
            raise ValueError("invalid model dimensions")
        self.model, self.cost_gradient, self.action_dim = (
            model,
            cost_gradient,
            action_dim,
        )
        self.config = config or EnergyPlannerConfig()
        self.constraint = constraint
        self.sheaf = TemporalSheaf(latent_dim, self.config.sheaf_weight)
        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(
            0
        )  # common checkpoint interface; solver itself is deterministic
        self.warm_actions = np.zeros((self.config.horizon, action_dim))
        self.previous_action = np.zeros(action_dim)
        self.model_calls = 0
        self._deadline = None
        self.last_diagnostics = {}

    def _consume(self):
        if self.model_calls >= self.config.max_model_calls:
            raise SolverBudgetExceeded("model_call_budget")
        if self._deadline is not None and perf_counter() >= self._deadline:
            raise SolverBudgetExceeded("deadline")
        self.model_calls += 1

    def _rollout(self, z, actions, derivatives=False):
        states = []
        fz = []
        fa = []
        current = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)[None]
        for action in actions:
            self._consume()
            if derivatives:
                current, jz, ja = self.model.linearize(current, action[None])
                fz.append(jz[0])
                fa.append(ja[0])
            else:
                current = self.model.predict(current, action[None])
            current = np.asarray(current, dtype=float)
            if (
                current.shape != (1, self.sheaf.dimension)
                or not np.isfinite(current).all()
            ):
                raise ValueError("nonfinite/invalid model rollout")
            states.append(current[0].copy())
        return np.stack(states), fz, fa

    def _objective(self, states, actions):
        c = self.config
        cost, gradient = self.cost_gradient(states)
        cost = np.asarray(cost, dtype=float)
        gradient = np.asarray(gradient, dtype=float)
        if (
            cost.shape != (len(states),)
            or gradient.shape != states.shape
            or not np.isfinite(cost).all()
            or not np.isfinite(gradient).all()
        ):
            raise ValueError("cost_gradient returned invalid values")
        discount = c.discount ** np.arange(len(states))
        value = float(discount @ cost)
        gz = discount[:, None] * gradient
        sheaf_value, sheaf_gradient = self.sheaf.energy_gradient(states)
        value += sheaf_value
        gz += sheaf_gradient
        if self.constraint is not None:
            extra, grad = self.constraint.energy_gradient(states)
            value += extra
            gz += grad
        value += c.action_penalty * float(np.sum(actions * actions))
        ga = 2 * c.action_penalty * actions
        difference = actions - np.vstack((self.previous_action, actions[:-1]))
        value += c.slew_penalty * float(np.sum(difference * difference))
        ga += 2 * c.slew_penalty * difference
        ga[:-1] -= 2 * c.slew_penalty * difference[1:]
        return value, gz, ga

    def plan(self, z, budget_ms=None, initial_actions=None):
        z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
        if z.shape != (self.sheaf.dimension,) or not np.isfinite(z).all():
            raise ValueError("invalid initial state")
        if budget_ms is not None and (not np.isfinite(budget_ms) or budget_ms <= 0):
            raise ValueError("budget_ms must be positive")
        self.model_calls = 0
        start = perf_counter()
        self._deadline = None if budget_ms is None else start + budget_ms / 1000
        actions = (
            self.warm_actions.copy()
            if initial_actions is None
            else np.asarray(initial_actions, dtype=float).copy()
        )
        if actions.shape != self.warm_actions.shape or not np.isfinite(actions).all():
            raise ValueError("invalid initial action sequence")
        actions = np.clip(actions, -1, 1)
        best = None
        best_value = float("inf")
        completed = 0
        reason = "iterations"
        gradient_norm = None
        try:
            states, _, _ = self._rollout(z, actions)
            value, _, _ = self._objective(states, actions)
            if self.constraint is None or self.constraint.feasible(states):
                best = actions.copy()
                best_value = value
            for iteration in range(self.config.iterations):
                states, fz, fa = self._rollout(z, actions, derivatives=True)
                value, gz, grad = self._objective(states, actions)
                costate = np.zeros(len(z))
                for t in range(self.config.horizon - 1, -1, -1):
                    costate += gz[t]
                    grad[t] += fa[t].T @ costate
                    costate = fz[t].T @ costate
                # Projected stationarity, not raw gradient, handles active box constraints.
                projected = actions - np.clip(actions - grad, -1, 1)
                gradient_norm = float(np.linalg.norm(projected))
                if gradient_norm <= self.config.gradient_tolerance:
                    reason = "projected_stationary"
                    break
                accepted = False
                step = self.config.learning_rate
                for _ in range(self.config.line_search_steps):
                    candidate = np.clip(actions - step * grad, -1, 1)
                    trajectory, _, _ = self._rollout(z, candidate)
                    candidate_value, _, _ = self._objective(trajectory, candidate)
                    feasible = self.constraint is None or self.constraint.feasible(
                        trajectory
                    )
                    if feasible and candidate_value < best_value:
                        best = candidate.copy()
                        best_value = candidate_value
                    if candidate_value <= value + 1e-4 * float(
                        np.sum(grad * (candidate - actions))
                    ):
                        actions = candidate
                        accepted = True
                        break
                    step *= 0.5
                completed = iteration + 1
                if not accepted:
                    reason = "line_search_stalled"
                    break
        except SolverBudgetExceeded as error:
            reason = str(error)
        feasible = best is not None
        if feasible:
            action = best[0].copy()
            self.warm_actions = np.vstack((best[1:], best[-1]))
        else:
            # This is a stop command, NOT a claim that zero is safe in every world.
            action = np.zeros(self.action_dim)
            self.warm_actions.fill(0)
        self.previous_action = action.copy()
        self.last_diagnostics = {
            "solver": "energy",
            "reason": reason,
            "feasible": feasible,
            "model_calls": self.model_calls,
            "search_variables": self.config.horizon * self.action_dim,
            "iterations": completed,
            "projected_gradient_norm": gradient_norm,
            "latency_ms": (perf_counter() - start) * 1000,
            "fallback": None if feasible else "no_verified_feasible_trajectory",
        }
        return Plan(
            action,
            float(best_value) if feasible else None,
            completed,
            0,
            self.last_diagnostics.copy(),
        )

    def state_dict(self):
        return {
            "warm_actions": self.warm_actions.copy(),
            "previous_action": self.previous_action.copy(),
            "rng": self.rng.bit_generator.state,
        }

    def load_state_dict(self, state):
        warm = np.asarray(state["warm_actions"], dtype=float)
        previous = np.asarray(state["previous_action"], dtype=float)
        if (
            warm.shape != self.warm_actions.shape
            or previous.shape != self.previous_action.shape
        ):
            raise ValueError("planner checkpoint dimension mismatch")
        self.warm_actions = warm.copy()
        self.previous_action = previous.copy()
        self.rng.bit_generator.state = state["rng"]

"""Explicit opt-in research profiles; choosing a profile is not an evidence claim."""

from dataclasses import asdict
from .runtime import RuntimeConfig


def runtime_config(profile="baseline", overrides=None):
    options = {
        "baseline": {},
        "adaptive": dict(
            world_backend="equilibrium",
            adaptive_memory=True,
            planning=True,
            planner_backend="energy",
            world_online_learning=True,
            neuromodulation=True,
            planner_iterations=12,
            sleep_every=32,
        ),
        "brain": dict(
            world_backend="adex",
            adaptive_memory=True,
            planning=True,
            planner_backend="energy",
            world_online_learning=True,
            neuromodulation=True,
            planner_iterations=12,
            sleep_every=32,
        ),
    }
    if profile not in options:
        raise ValueError("unknown runtime profile")
    values = {**options[profile], **(overrides or {})}
    # Validate all names/types through the dataclass instead of silently ignoring typos.
    return RuntimeConfig(**values)


def describe(config: RuntimeConfig):
    return {
        "config": asdict(config),
        "status": "experimental; no new performance measurements in this revision",
        "autograd_used": config.world_backend == "torch",
        "retention_scope": "versioned retained activation span; released directions may change",
        "finite_capacity": True,
        "time_budget": "soft wall budget or deterministic operation budget",
    }

ARQUIVO: nova/reasoning/__init__.py

"""Budgeted search, exact arithmetic tools, and held-out confidence calibration."""

from __future__ import annotations
import ast
import operator
from collections import deque
from dataclasses import dataclass
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize_scalar
from scipy.special import softmax


class Calculator:
    """Evaluate bounded arithmetic ASTs; no names, calls, attributes or code execution."""

    operations = {
        ast.Add: operator.add,
        ast.Sub: operator.sub,
        ast.Mult: operator.mul,
        ast.Div: operator.truediv,
        ast.Pow: operator.pow,
        ast.Mod: operator.mod,
    }

    def __call__(self, expression):
        if len(expression) > 512:
            raise ValueError("expression too long")
        tree = ast.parse(expression, mode="eval")
        if len(list(ast.walk(tree))) > 128:
            raise ValueError("expression too complex")

        def visit(node):
            if isinstance(node, ast.Constant) and type(node.value) in (int, float):
                value = node.value
            elif isinstance(node, ast.UnaryOp) and isinstance(
                node.op, (ast.UAdd, ast.USub)
            ):
                value = visit(node.operand) * (
                    1 if isinstance(node.op, ast.UAdd) else -1
                )
            elif isinstance(node, ast.BinOp) and type(node.op) in self.operations:
                a, b = visit(node.left), visit(node.right)
                if isinstance(node.op, ast.Pow) and abs(b) > 16:
                    raise ValueError("exponent budget exceeded")
                value = self.operations[type(node.op)](a, b)
            else:
                raise ValueError("only arithmetic is allowed")
            if (
                isinstance(value, complex)
                or not np.isfinite(value)
                or abs(value) > 1e100
            ):
                raise ValueError("result outside numeric budget")
            return value

        return visit(tree.body)


class ToolRegistry:
    def __init__(self):
        self.tools = {"calculator": Calculator()}

    def register(self, name, callback):
        if name in self.tools:
            raise ValueError("duplicate tool")
        self.tools[name] = callback

    def call(self, name, *args, **kwargs):
        if name not in self.tools:
            raise ValueError("tool not registered")
        return self.tools[name](*args, **kwargs)


@dataclass
class SearchResult:
    path: list[tuple[int, int]]
    expanded: int
    verified: bool


def grid_search(grid, start, goal, budget=1000):
    """Exact BFS verifier, bounded by expansions; more budget preserves found solutions."""
    grid = np.asarray(grid, dtype=bool)
    if grid.ndim != 2 or budget < 0:
        raise ValueError("invalid grid/budget")
    for p in (start, goal):
        if not 0 <= p[0] < grid.shape[0] or not 0 <= p[1] < grid.shape[1] or grid[p]:
            raise ValueError("invalid endpoint")
    start, goal = tuple(start), tuple(goal)
    queue = deque([start])
    parent = {start: None}
    expanded = 0
    while queue and expanded < budget:
        node = queue.popleft()
        expanded += 1
        if node == goal:
            path = []
            while node is not None:
                path.append(node)
                node = parent[node]
            return SearchResult(path[::-1], expanded, True)
        for delta in ((0, 1), (1, 0), (-1, 0), (0, -1)):
            nxt = (node[0] + delta[0], node[1] + delta[1])
            if (
                0 <= nxt[0] < grid.shape[0]
                and 0 <= nxt[1] < grid.shape[1]
                and not grid[nxt]
                and nxt not in parent
            ):
                parent[nxt] = node
                queue.append(nxt)
    return SearchResult([], expanded, False)


def hierarchical_route(grid, start, subgoals, budget=1000):
    path = [tuple(start)]
    used = 0
    for goal in subgoals:
        result = grid_search(grid, path[-1], tuple(goal), max(0, budget - used))
        used += result.expanded
        if not result.verified:
            return SearchResult([], used, False)
        path.extend(result.path[1:])
    return SearchResult(path, used, True)


def expected_calibration_error(probabilities, labels, bins=10):
    p = np.asarray(probabilities)
    labels = np.asarray(labels)
    if (
        p.ndim != 2
        or len(p) == 0
        or len(labels) != len(p)
        or np.any(p < 0)
        or not np.allclose(p.sum(1), 1)
    ):
        raise ValueError("invalid probability distribution")
    confidence = p.max(1)
    correct = p.argmax(1) == labels
    value = 0.0
    index = np.minimum((confidence * bins).astype(int), bins - 1)
    for i in range(bins):
        selected = index == i
        if selected.any():
            value += selected.mean() * abs(
                confidence[selected].mean() - correct[selected].mean()
            )
    return float(value)


class TemperatureCalibrator:
    def __init__(self):
        self.temperature = 1.0

    def fit(self, logits, labels):
        logits = np.asarray(logits)
        labels = np.asarray(labels)

        def nll(log_temperature):
            p = softmax(logits / np.exp(log_temperature), axis=1)
            return -np.log(np.maximum(p[np.arange(len(p)), labels], 1e-12)).mean()

        result = minimize_scalar(nll, bounds=(-4, 4), method="bounded")
        if not result.success:
            raise RuntimeError("calibration did not converge")
        self.temperature = float(np.exp(result.x))
        return self

    def predict(self, logits):
        return softmax(np.asarray(logits) / self.temperature, axis=1)


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/reasoning/local_policy.py
========================================================================================
"""Amortized control with a fixed random feature map and local output delta rule."""

import numpy as np


class LocalPolicy:
    """a=tanh(tanh(zR+b)W+c); only the readout W,c adapts to a teacher action.

    This is a local supervised readout, not a replacement for all policy-learning
    algorithms. Hidden random features are frozen; no hidden-layer backward pass.
    """

    def __init__(self, inputs, outputs, seed=0, hidden=64):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.inputs, self.outputs = inputs, outputs
        self.projection = rng.normal(0, 1 / np.sqrt(inputs), (inputs, hidden))
        self.bias = rng.normal(0, 0.1, hidden)
        self.weights = np.zeros((hidden, outputs))
        self.output_bias = np.zeros(outputs)

    def features(self, x):
        return np.tanh(np.asarray(x) @ self.projection + self.bias)

    def predict(self, x):
        return np.tanh(self.features(x) @ self.weights + self.output_bias)

    def learn(self, x, target, lr=0.01):
        x = np.atleast_2d(x)
        target = np.atleast_2d(target)
        features = self.features(x)
        pred = np.tanh(features @ self.weights + self.output_bias)
        if target.shape != pred.shape or not np.isfinite(target).all() or lr < 0:
            raise ValueError("invalid policy target")
        delta = np.clip((pred - target) * (1 - pred * pred), -1, 1)
        self.weights -= lr * features.T @ delta / len(x)
        self.output_bias -= lr * delta.mean(0)
        return float(np.mean((pred - target) ** 2))

    def state_dict(self):
        return {
            "inputs": self.inputs,
            "outputs": self.outputs,
            **{
                key: getattr(self, key).copy()
                for key in ("projection", "bias", "weights", "output_bias")
            },
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_state_dict(cls, state):
        obj = cls(
            state["inputs"], state["outputs"], hidden=np.shape(state["weights"])[0]
        )
        for key in ("projection", "bias", "weights", "output_bias"):
            setattr(obj, key, np.asarray(state[key]).copy())
        return obj

"""Opt-in hypotheses: mathematical checks do not establish task-level benefit."""

import numpy as np


class PhaseOscillatorLayer:
    """dtheta_i/dt=omega_i+K/N sum_j A_ij sin(theta_j-theta_i-alpha_ij)."""

    def __init__(
        self, size=32, coupling=2.0, seed=0, adjacency=None, lag=None, backend="numpy"
    ):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.theta = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size)
        self.omega = rng.normal(0, 0.1, size)
        self.coupling = coupling
        self.adjacency = (
            np.ones((size, size))
            if adjacency is None
            else np.asarray(adjacency, dtype=float)
        )
        self.lag = (
            np.zeros((size, size)) if lag is None else np.asarray(lag, dtype=float)
        )
        if self.adjacency.shape != (size, size) or self.lag.shape != (size, size):
            raise ValueError("invalid coupling shape")
        self.backend = backend

    def step(self, dt=0.01):
        if dt <= 0:
            raise ValueError("dt must be positive")
        if self.backend == "rust":
            from nova.core import require_native

            self.theta = np.asarray(
                require_native().kuramoto_step(
                    self.theta.tolist(),
                    self.omega.tolist(),
                    self.adjacency.ravel().tolist(),
                    self.lag.ravel().tolist(),
                    self.coupling,
                    dt,
                )
            )
        elif self.backend == "numpy":
            derivative = self.omega + self.coupling * np.mean(
                self.adjacency
                * np.sin(self.theta[None, :] - self.theta[:, None] - self.lag),
                axis=1,
            )
            self.theta = (self.theta + dt * derivative) % (2 * np.pi)
        else:
            raise ValueError("backend must be numpy or rust")
        return self.theta.copy()

    @property
    def order(self):
        return float(abs(np.exp(1j * self.theta).mean()))


class QuadraticEquilibriumPropagation:
    """E(s)=1/2 s.T A s-s.T Bx; C=1/2 ||P s-y||².

    Free and nudged equilibria are reached by actual relaxation. Parameter
    gradient is -(s_beta-s_free)x.T/beta, compared to implicit backprop.
    A is symmetric positive definite; this is a small quadratic validation model.
    """

    def __init__(self, inputs=3, state=5, outputs=2, seed=0):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        m = rng.normal(0, 0.1, (state, state))
        self.A = np.eye(state) + m.T @ m
        self.B = rng.normal(0, 0.2, (state, inputs))
        self.P = np.eye(state)[-outputs:]
        self.last_iterations = (0, 0)

    def relax(
        self, x, y=None, beta=0.0, initial=None, tolerance=1e-11, max_steps=10000
    ):
        if beta < 0:
            raise ValueError("beta must be nonnegative")
        s = np.zeros(len(self.A)) if initial is None else initial.copy()
        matrix = self.A + beta * self.P.T @ self.P
        drive = self.B @ x + (beta * self.P.T @ y if y is not None else 0)
        dt = 0.8 / np.linalg.eigvalsh(matrix).max()
        for i in range(max_steps):
            gradient = matrix @ s - drive
            if np.linalg.norm(gradient) < tolerance:
                return s, i
            s -= dt * gradient
        raise RuntimeError("equilibrium did not converge")

    def gradient(self, x, y, beta=1e-4):
        if beta <= 0:
            raise ValueError("beta must be positive")
        free, n0 = self.relax(x)
        nudged, nb = self.relax(x, y, beta, free)
        self.last_iterations = (n0, nb)
        return -np.outer(nudged - free, x) / beta


class SheafCoherence:
    """Cellular sheaf coboundary D_e x = R_eu x_u - R_ev x_v; L=D.T D."""

    def __init__(self, nodes, stalk_dim, edges, restrictions=None):
        self.D = np.zeros((len(edges) * stalk_dim, nodes * stalk_dim))
        for i, (u, v) in enumerate(edges):
            if not 0 <= u < nodes or not 0 <= v < nodes or u == v:
                raise ValueError("invalid edge")
            ru, rv = (
                (np.eye(stalk_dim), np.eye(stalk_dim))
                if restrictions is None
                else restrictions[i]
            )
            self.D[
                i * stalk_dim : (i + 1) * stalk_dim, u * stalk_dim : (u + 1) * stalk_dim
            ] = ru
            self.D[
                i * stalk_dim : (i + 1) * stalk_dim, v * stalk_dim : (v + 1) * stalk_dim
            ] = -rv
        self.L = self.D.T @ self.D

    def energy(self, x):
        return float(np.linalg.norm(self.D @ np.asarray(x).ravel()) ** 2 / 2)

    def diffuse(self, x, steps=20, dt=None):
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
        shape = x.shape
        x = x.ravel().copy()
        maximum = np.linalg.eigvalsh(self.L).max()
        if maximum == 0:
            return x.reshape(shape)
        dt = 0.9 / maximum if dt is None else dt
        if dt <= 0 or dt * maximum >= 2:
            raise ValueError("unstable diffusion step")
        for _ in range(steps):
            x -= dt * self.L @ x
        return x.reshape(shape)

"""Integrated cognitive cycle with optional adaptive memory and physical latent inference."""

from __future__ import annotations
from collections import deque
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict, replace
import json
from pathlib import Path
from time import perf_counter
import numpy as np
from nova.continual import ProtectedLinear
from nova.memory import (
    SensoryBuffer,
    WorkingMemory,
    EpisodicMemory,
    Episode,
    SemanticGraph,
)
from nova.synapse import DiffusiveCascade
from nova.neuromod import Neuromodulator
from nova.planning import LatentPlanner
from nova.values import InternalState
from nova.values.progress import LearningProgress
from nova.checkpoint import save_state, load_state, CheckpointFormatError


@dataclass
class RuntimeConfig:
    seed: int = 0
    observation_dim: int = 12
    latent_dim: int = 8
    action_dim: int = 2
    memory_capacity: int = 128
    sleep_every: int = 100
    tick_budget_ms: float = 100.0
    planning: bool = False
    neuromodulation: bool = False
    deterministic: bool = True
    world_backend: str = "torch"
    world_online_learning: bool = False
    adaptive_memory: bool = False
    memory_policy: str = "hybrid"
    memory_growth: int = 8
    max_memory_features: int = 128
    memory_reserve: float = 0.2
    max_memory_bytes: int = 32 * 1024 * 1024
    planner_backend: str = "cem"
    planner_horizon: int = 6
    planner_iterations: int = 3
    planner_rollouts: int = 64
    solver_max_calls: int = 512
    sheaf_weight: float = 0.0
    spiking_units: int = 100
    spiking_steps: int = 100
    spiking_backend: str = "numpy"
    spiking_plasticity: bool = False
    history_limit: int = 2048
    goal_latent: list[float] | None = None
    state_lower: list[float] | None = None
    state_upper: list[float] | None = None

    def __post_init__(self):
        if (
            min(
                self.observation_dim,
                self.latent_dim,
                self.action_dim,
                self.memory_capacity,
                self.sleep_every,
                self.history_limit,
                self.planner_horizon,
                self.planner_iterations,
                self.planner_rollouts,
                self.spiking_units,
                self.spiking_steps,
            )
            < 1
        ):
            raise ValueError("dimensions and budgets must be positive")
        if not np.isfinite(self.tick_budget_ms) or self.tick_budget_ms <= 0:
            raise ValueError("invalid time budget")
        if self.world_backend not in (
            "torch",
            "equilibrium",
            "adex",
        ) or self.planner_backend not in ("cem", "energy"):
            raise ValueError("unknown world/planner backend")
        if self.spiking_plasticity and self.world_backend != "adex":
            raise ValueError("spiking plasticity requires AdEx backend")
        if self.world_online_learning and self.world_backend == "torch":
            raise ValueError(
                "online world learning is provided by the local EqProp backends"
            )


class _Dynamics:
    def __init__(self, runtime):
        self.runtime = runtime

    def predict(self, z, a):
        return self.runtime.predict(z, a)

    def linearize(self, z, a):
        return self.runtime.linearize(z, a)


class CognitiveRuntime:
    """Versioned memory + real latent backend + amortized/energy-based control.

    Baseline behavior remains selectable. Adaptive and physical backends are
    experimental and unbenchmarked in this revision. Encoders are frozen for
    online rate-model training. A stateful AdEx representation has history
    dependence; stored-activation protection is not raw-observation retention.
    """

    def __init__(self, config: RuntimeConfig | None = None):
        self.config = config or RuntimeConfig()
        c = self.config
        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(c.seed)
        if c.world_backend == "torch":
            import torch
            from torch import nn
            from nova.jepa import JEPA

            with torch.random.fork_rng():
                torch.manual_seed(c.seed)
                self.world = JEPA(c.observation_dim, c.latent_dim, c.action_dim)
                self.policy = nn.Sequential(
                    nn.Linear(c.latent_dim, 32),
                    nn.Tanh(),
                    nn.Linear(32, c.action_dim),
                    nn.Tanh(),
                )
            self.world.context.requires_grad_(False)
            self.world.target.requires_grad_(False)
        else:
            from nova.jepa.equilibrium import EquilibriumWorldModel
            from nova.brain.latent import SpikingWorldModel
            from nova.reasoning.local_policy import LocalPolicy

            if c.world_backend == "adex":
                self.world = SpikingWorldModel(
                    c.observation_dim,
                    c.latent_dim,
                    c.action_dim,
                    seed=c.seed,
                    spiking_units=c.spiking_units,
                    spiking_steps=c.spiking_steps,
                    spiking_backend=c.spiking_backend,
                )
            else:
                self.world = EquilibriumWorldModel(
                    c.observation_dim, c.latent_dim, c.action_dim, seed=c.seed
                )
            self.world.freeze_encoder()
            self.policy = LocalPolicy(c.latent_dim, c.action_dim, seed=c.seed)
        if c.adaptive_memory:
            from nova.continual.adaptive import AdaptiveSubspaceLinear, AdaptiveConfig

            cfg = AdaptiveConfig(
                policy=c.memory_policy,
                growth_step=c.memory_growth,
                max_features=c.max_memory_features,
                max_state_bytes=c.max_memory_bytes,
                reserve_fraction=c.memory_reserve,
            )
            self.continual = AdaptiveSubspaceLinear(
                c.latent_dim, c.latent_dim, seed=c.seed, config=cfg
            )
        else:
            self.continual = ProtectedLinear(c.latent_dim, c.latent_dim, seed=c.seed)
        self.cascade = DiffusiveCascade(c.latent_dim)
        self.mod = (
            Neuromodulator() if c.neuromodulation else Neuromodulator(frozenset())
        )
        self.internal = InternalState()
        self.progress = LearningProgress()
        self.sensory = SensoryBuffer()
        self.working = WorkingMemory(dimensions=c.latent_dim)
        self.episodic = EpisodicMemory(c.memory_capacity)
        self.semantic = SemanticGraph(capacity=4 * c.memory_capacity)
        self.rest = self.rng.normal(0, 0.03, c.latent_dim)
        self.z = self.rest.copy()
        self.prediction = self.rest.copy()
        self.last_action = np.zeros(c.action_dim)
        self.z_version = 0
        self.tick_index = 0
        self.previous_observation = None
        self.metrics = deque(maxlen=c.history_limit)
        self.external_state = {}
        self.last_solver = {}
        self.last_world_update = {}
        self.goal = (
            np.zeros(c.latent_dim)
            if c.goal_latent is None
            else np.asarray(c.goal_latent, dtype=float)
        )
        if self.goal.shape != (c.latent_dim,) or not np.isfinite(self.goal).all():
            raise ValueError("goal shape mismatch")
        self._create_planner()

    def _create_planner(self):
        c = self.config
        constraint = None
        if c.state_lower is not None or c.state_upper is not None:
            from nova.planning.energy import BoxStateConstraint

            if c.state_lower is None or c.state_upper is None:
                raise ValueError("both state bounds required")
            constraint = BoxStateConstraint(c.state_lower, c.state_upper)
            if constraint.lower.shape != (c.latent_dim,):
                raise ValueError("state constraint dimension mismatch")
        if c.planner_backend == "energy":
            from nova.planning.energy import EnergyPlanner, EnergyPlannerConfig

            cfg = EnergyPlannerConfig(
                horizon=c.planner_horizon,
                iterations=c.planner_iterations,
                max_model_calls=c.solver_max_calls,
                sheaf_weight=c.sheaf_weight,
            )
            self.planner = EnergyPlanner(
                _Dynamics(self),
                self.planning_cost_gradient,
                c.action_dim,
                c.latent_dim,
                config=cfg,
                constraint=constraint,
            )
        else:
            self.planner = LatentPlanner(
                self.predict,
                self.planning_cost,
                c.action_dim,
                seed=c.seed,
                rollouts=c.planner_rollouts,
                horizon=c.planner_horizon,
                iterations=c.planner_iterations,
                constraint=constraint,
            )

    def _encode(self, observation):
        if self.config.world_backend != "torch":
            return self.world.encode(observation)[0]
        import torch

        with torch.no_grad():
            return (
                self.world.context(torch.as_tensor(observation, dtype=torch.float32))
                .numpy()
                .astype(float)
            )

    def predict(self, z, a):
        if self.config.world_backend != "torch":
            return self.world.predict(z, a)
        import torch

        with torch.no_grad():
            return self.world.predictor(
                torch.as_tensor(z, dtype=torch.float32),
                torch.as_tensor(a, dtype=torch.float32),
            ).numpy()

    def linearize(self, z, a):
        if self.config.world_backend != "torch":
            return self.world.linearize(z, a)
        # Explicit derivative of the baseline Linear -> SiLU -> Linear predictor.
        z, a = np.asarray(z, dtype=float), np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
        network = self.world.predictor.net
        w1, b1 = network[0].weight.detach().numpy(), network[0].bias.detach().numpy()
        w2, b2 = network[2].weight.detach().numpy(), network[2].bias.detach().numpy()
        pre = np.c_[z, a] @ w1.T + b1
        from scipy.special import expit

        sigmoid = expit(pre)
        derivative = sigmoid * (1 + pre * (1 - sigmoid))
        jacobian = np.einsum("oh,bh,hi->boi", w2, derivative, w1)
        output = (pre * sigmoid) @ w2.T + b2
        return (
            output,
            jacobian[:, :, : self.config.latent_dim],
            jacobian[:, :, self.config.latent_dim :],
        )

    @staticmethod
    def cost(z):
        return np.sum(np.asarray(z) ** 2, axis=-1)

    @staticmethod
    def cost_gradient(z):
        return CognitiveRuntime.cost(z), 2 * np.asarray(z)

    def planning_cost(self, z):
        return np.sum((np.asarray(z) - self.goal) ** 2, axis=-1)

    def planning_cost_gradient(self, z):
        return self.planning_cost(z), 2 * (np.asarray(z) - self.goal)

    def set_goal(self, latent):
        goal = np.asarray(latent, dtype=float)
        if goal.shape != self.goal.shape or not np.isfinite(goal).all():
            raise ValueError("invalid goal")
        self.goal = goal.copy()
        self.planner.warm_actions.fill(0)

    def _policy_predict(self, z):
        if self.config.world_backend != "torch":
            return self.policy.predict(z)
        import torch

        with torch.no_grad():
            return self.policy(torch.as_tensor(z, dtype=torch.float32)).numpy()

    def _policy_learn(self, z, action):
        if self.config.world_backend != "torch":
            return self.policy.learn(z, action, lr=0.001)
        import torch

        optimizer = torch.optim.SGD(self.policy.parameters(), lr=0.001)
        loss = (
            (
                self.policy(torch.as_tensor(z, dtype=torch.float32))
                - torch.as_tensor(action, dtype=torch.float32)
            )
            .square()
            .mean()
        )
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        loss.backward()
        optimizer.step()
        return float(loss.detach())

    def _memory_input(self, z, version=None):
        x = np.atleast_2d(z)
        return self.continual.encode(x, version) if self.config.adaptive_memory else x

    @property
    def feature_version(self):
        return self.continual.feature_version if self.config.adaptive_memory else 0

    def tick(self, observation=None, reward=0.0):
        start = perf_counter()
        idle = observation is None
        if not np.isfinite(reward):
            raise ValueError("nonfinite reward")
        if not idle:
            observation = np.asarray(observation, dtype=float)
            if (
                observation.shape != (self.config.observation_dim,)
                or not np.isfinite(observation).all()
            ):
                raise ValueError("invalid observation")
        previous = self.z.copy()
        previous_version = self.z_version
        previous_action = self.last_action.copy()
        self.tick_index += 1
        if idle:
            self.z = 0.99 * self.z + 0.01 * self.rest
        else:
            self.sensory.append(observation.copy())
            self.z = np.asarray(self._encode(observation), dtype=float)
        self.z_version = self.feature_version
        error = float(np.mean((self.z - self.prediction) ** 2))
        uncertainty = float(np.clip(error, 0, 1))
        self.mod.update(
            error, reward, -self.cost(previous), -self.cost(self.z), uncertainty
        )
        recalled = self.episodic.retrieve(self.z, limit=1)
        sensory_weight = (
            self.mod.sensory_weight() if self.config.neuromodulation else 0.9
        )
        context = (
            self.z
            if not recalled
            else sensory_weight * self.z + (1 - sensory_weight) * recalled[0].embedding
        )
        self.working.update(context)
        slow = (
            not idle
            and self.config.planning
            and (uncertainty > 0.1 or self.mod.surprise > 0.5)
            and self.internal.energy > 0.1
        )
        self.last_solver = {}
        if idle:
            action = np.zeros(self.config.action_dim)
            mode = "idle"
        elif slow:
            if self.config.neuromodulation:
                if self.config.planner_backend == "energy":
                    self.planner.config = replace(
                        self.planner.config, discount=self.mod.discount()
                    )
                else:
                    self.planner.discount = self.mod.discount()
            result = self.planner.plan(
                context,
                None if self.config.deterministic else self.config.tick_budget_ms * 0.7,
            )
            action = result.action
            mode = "deliberate"
            self.last_solver = result.diagnostics
            if result.cost is not None:
                self._policy_learn(context, action)
        else:
            action = self._policy_predict(context)
            mode = "fast"
        world_loss = None
        if not idle:
            memory_input = self._memory_input(previous, previous_version)
            self.continual.step(
                memory_input, self.z[None], self.mod.learning_rate(0.001)
            )
            if self.config.adaptive_memory:
                self.continual.observe(memory_input)
                self.continual.age(1.0, consolidation=0.01 * self.mod.consolidation())
            self.cascade.step(np.full(self.config.latent_dim, error), dt=0.1)
            self.episodic.threshold = self.mod.write_threshold(0.05)
            self.episodic.write(
                Episode(
                    str(self.tick_index),
                    self.z.copy(),
                    f"observation at tick {self.tick_index}",
                    f"sensor:{self.tick_index}",
                    f"{self.tick_index:012d}",
                    surprise=error,
                    valence=self.internal.valence,
                    feature_version=self.z_version,
                )
            )
            if (
                self.config.world_online_learning
                and self.previous_observation is not None
            ):
                update = self.world.learn_latent(
                    previous[None],
                    previous_action[None],
                    self.z[None],
                    lr=self.mod.learning_rate(0.001),
                )
                world_loss = update.get("loss")
                self.last_world_update = {
                    key: value
                    for key, value in update.items()
                    if not isinstance(value, np.ndarray)
                }
            if self.config.spiking_plasticity:
                self.world.spiking.learn_local(self.mod.reward_pe)
            self.previous_observation = observation.copy()
        self.last_action = np.asarray(action, dtype=float).copy()
        self.prediction = self.predict(self.z[None], self.last_action[None])[0]
        self.internal.update(
            error,
            0.8 if slow else 0.1,
            len(self.episodic.episodes) / self.config.memory_capacity,
            reward,
        )
        self.internal.curiosity = 0.0 if idle else self.progress.update(error)
        if self.tick_index % self.config.sleep_every == 0:
            self.sleep()
        elapsed = (perf_counter() - start) * 1000
        metric = {
            "tick": self.tick_index,
            "mode": mode,
            "error": error,
            "free_dims": self.continual.free_dims,
            "memory": len(self.episodic.episodes),
            "latency_ms": elapsed,
            "budget_exceeded": elapsed > self.config.tick_budget_ms,
            "energy": self.internal.energy,
            "curiosity": self.internal.curiosity,
            "progress_standard_error": self.progress.standard_error,
            "world_backend": self.config.world_backend,
            "planner_backend": self.config.planner_backend,
            "world_loss": world_loss,
            "semantic_facts": len(self.semantic.facts),
            "semantic_evicted": self.semantic.evicted,
            "feature_version": self.feature_version,
            "memory_width": self.continual.in_features,
            "solver_model_calls": self.last_solver.get("model_calls", 0),
            "solver_feasible": self.last_solver.get("feasible"),
            "world_converged": getattr(self.world, "last_diagnostics", {}).get(
                "converged"
            ),
        }
        if self.config.adaptive_memory:
            metric.update(self.continual.capacity_metrics())
        self.metrics.append(metric)
        return {
            "action": self.last_action.copy(),
            "mode": mode,
            "latent": self.z.copy(),
            "metrics": metric,
            "solver": self.last_solver.copy(),
        }

    def remember_anchor(self, episode: Episode):
        if not episode.anchor:
            raise ValueError("anchor must be explicit")
        if episode.embedding.shape != (self.config.latent_dim,):
            raise ValueError("anchor latent shape mismatch")
        x = self._memory_input(episode.embedding, episode.feature_version)
        if not self.episodic.write(episode, force=True):
            raise ValueError("episode capacity contains only anchors")
        if self.config.adaptive_memory:
            self.continual.consolidate(x, anchor=True)
        else:
            self.continual.consolidate(x)

    def sleep(self):
        self.episodic.sleep(
            lambda e: self.semantic.add(
                e.id,
                "observed",
                e.content,
                e.source,
                timestamp=e.timestamp,
                anchor=e.anchor,
            )
        )
        if self.episodic.episodes:
            x = np.stack([e.embedding for e in self.episodic.episodes])
            versions = np.array([e.feature_version for e in self.episodic.episodes])
            self.continual.consolidate(self._memory_input(x, versions))
        if self.config.adaptive_memory:
            self.continual.manage_capacity()
        self.cascade.consolidate(self.mod.consolidation() * 0.01)
        self.internal.sleep()

    def state_dict(self):
        if self.config.world_backend == "torch":
            world = {k: v.detach().numpy() for k, v in self.world.state_dict().items()}
            policy = {
                k: v.detach().numpy() for k, v in self.policy.state_dict().items()
            }
        else:
            world = self.world.state_dict()
            policy = self.policy.state_dict()
        memory = (
            self.continual.state_dict()
            if self.config.adaptive_memory
            else {
                **{
                    k: getattr(self.continual, k).copy()
                    for k in ("W_base", "dW", "U", "singular_values", "last_update")
                },
                "rank_max": self.continual.rank_max,
                "energy_threshold": self.continual.energy_threshold,
            }
        )
        return {
            "kind": "nova-runtime",
            "config": asdict(self.config),
            "world": world,
            "policy": policy,
            "memory": memory,
            "cascade": self.cascade.state.copy(),
            "working": self.working.state.copy(),
            "working_cursor": self.working.cursor,
            "z": self.z.copy(),
            "prediction": self.prediction.copy(),
            "rest": self.rest.copy(),
            "last_action": self.last_action.copy(),
            "goal": self.goal.copy(),
            "z_version": self.z_version,
            "tick": self.tick_index,
            "rng": self.rng.bit_generator.state,
            "planner": self.planner.state_dict()
            if hasattr(self.planner, "state_dict")
            else {"rng": self.planner.rng.bit_generator.state},
            "mod": {**asdict(self.mod), "enabled": sorted(self.mod.enabled)},
            "internal": asdict(self.internal),
            "progress": self.progress.state_dict(),
            "episodes": [asdict(e) for e in self.episodic.episodes],
            "facts": self.semantic.facts,
            "semantic_evicted": self.semantic.evicted,
            "sensory": list(self.sensory),
            "previous_observation": self.previous_observation,
            "episodic_threshold": self.episodic.threshold,
            "metrics": list(self.metrics),
            "external_state": self.external_state,
            "last_solver": self.last_solver,
            "last_world_update": self.last_world_update,
        }

    def save(self, path):
        save_state(path, self.state_dict())

    @classmethod
    def from_state_dict(cls, state):
        if state.get("kind") != "nova-runtime":
            raise ValueError("not a runtime checkpoint")
        obj = cls(RuntimeConfig(**state["config"]))
        if obj.config.world_backend == "torch":
            import torch

            for name in ("world", "policy"):
                getattr(obj, name).load_state_dict(
                    {
                        k: torch.from_numpy(np.asarray(v).copy())
                        for k, v in state[name].items()
                    }
                )
        else:
            from nova.jepa.equilibrium import EquilibriumWorldModel
            from nova.brain.latent import SpikingWorldModel
            from nova.reasoning.local_policy import LocalPolicy

            model_class = (
                SpikingWorldModel
                if obj.config.world_backend == "adex"
                else EquilibriumWorldModel
            )
            obj.world = model_class.from_state_dict(state["world"])
            obj.policy = LocalPolicy.from_state_dict(state["policy"])
        if obj.config.adaptive_memory:
            from nova.continual.adaptive import AdaptiveSubspaceLinear

            obj.continual = AdaptiveSubspaceLinear.from_state_dict(state["memory"])
        else:
            for name in ("W_base", "dW", "U", "singular_values", "last_update"):
                setattr(obj.continual, name, np.asarray(state["memory"][name]).copy())
            obj.continual.W_base.flags.writeable = False
            obj.continual.rank_max = state["memory"]["rank_max"]
            obj.continual.energy_threshold = state["memory"]["energy_threshold"]
        for name in ("z", "prediction", "rest", "last_action"):
            setattr(obj, name, np.asarray(state[name]).copy())
        obj.goal = np.asarray(state.get("goal", obj.goal)).copy()
        obj.z_version = state["z_version"]
        obj.tick_index = state["tick"]
        obj.rng.bit_generator.state = state["rng"]
        obj.cascade.state = np.asarray(state["cascade"]).copy()
        obj.working.state = np.asarray(state["working"]).copy()
        obj.working.cursor = state["working_cursor"]
        mod = state["mod"].copy()
        mod["enabled"] = frozenset(mod["enabled"])
        obj.mod = Neuromodulator(**mod)
        obj.internal = InternalState(**state["internal"])
        obj.progress = LearningProgress.from_state_dict(state["progress"])
        obj.episodic.episodes = [
            Episode(**{**e, "participants": tuple(e["participants"])})
            for e in state["episodes"]
        ]
        obj.episodic.threshold = state["episodic_threshold"]
        obj.semantic.facts = state["facts"]
        obj.semantic.evicted = state.get("semantic_evicted", 0)
        obj.sensory.extend(state["sensory"])
        obj.previous_observation = state["previous_observation"]
        obj.metrics = deque(state["metrics"], maxlen=obj.config.history_limit)
        obj.external_state = state["external_state"]
        obj.last_solver = state.get("last_solver", {})
        obj.last_world_update = state.get("last_world_update", {})
        obj._create_planner()
        if hasattr(obj.planner, "load_state_dict"):
            obj.planner.load_state_dict(state["planner"])
        else:
            obj.planner.rng.bit_generator.state = state["planner"]["rng"]
        return obj

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, path):
        try:
            return cls.from_state_dict(load_state(path))
        except CheckpointFormatError as error:
            if str(error) != "not a v2 state archive":
                raise
        # Explicit migration of the published v1 runtime format.
        with np.load(path, allow_pickle=False) as arrays:
            if "metadata" not in arrays.files:
                raise ValueError("unknown checkpoint format")
            meta = json.loads(str(arrays["metadata"]))
            if meta.get("version") != 1:
                raise ValueError("unsupported legacy version")
            obj = cls(RuntimeConfig(**meta["config"]))
            state = obj.state_dict()
            for name, module in [("world", obj.world), ("policy", obj.policy)]:
                state[name] = {
                    k: arrays[f"{name}.{k}"].copy() for k in module.state_dict()
                }
            for name in ("W_base", "dW", "U", "singular_values", "last_update"):
                state["memory"][name] = arrays[f"continual.{name}"].copy()
            for name in ("z", "prediction", "rest", "last_action"):
                state[name] = arrays[name].copy()
            state.update(
                cascade=arrays["cascade"].copy(),
                working=arrays["working"].copy(),
                working_cursor=meta["working_cursor"],
                tick=meta["tick"],
                rng=meta["rng"],
                mod=meta["mod"],
                internal=meta["internal"],
                episodes=[
                    {**e, "embedding": np.asarray(e["embedding"])}
                    for e in meta["episodes"]
                ],
                facts=meta["facts"],
                sensory=[np.asarray(x) for x in meta["sensory"]],
                previous_observation=None
                if meta["previous_observation"] is None
                else np.asarray(meta["previous_observation"]),
                episodic_threshold=meta["episodic_threshold"],
                metrics=meta["metrics"],
                external_state=meta.get("external_state", {}),
            )
            state["planner"] = {"rng": meta["planner_rng"]}
            return cls.from_state_dict(state)

    def write_metrics(self, path):
        Path(path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        Path(path).write_text(
            "".join(json.dumps(m, allow_nan=False) + "\n" for m in self.metrics)
        )

ARQUIVO: nova/scientific/__init__.py
