"""Candidate discovery and scoped verification; empirical fit is not a theorem."""


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/scientific/auto_lean.py
========================================================================================
"""Opt-in proof spool worker. Only bounded regenerated identities enter Lean."""

from __future__ import annotations
import hashlib
import os
from pathlib import Path
import threading
from .polynomial import Polynomial
from .verification import PolynomialVerifier
from .verifier_lean import LeanVerifier
from nova.bioma.determinism import canonical_json, strict_json
from nova.swarm.artifacts import atomic


class ProofDaemon:
    def __init__(self, directory, *, lean_project=None, max_jobs=128, poll_seconds=2):
        self.directory = Path(directory)
        self.queue = self.directory / "pending"
        self.receipts = self.directory / "receipts"
        self.theorems = self.directory / "theorems"
        for path in (self.queue, self.receipts, self.theorems):
            path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        if not 1 <= max_jobs <= 1024 or not 0.1 <= poll_seconds <= 60:
            raise ValueError("proof worker budget exceeded")
        self.max_jobs = max_jobs
        self.poll_seconds = poll_seconds
        self.lean = LeanVerifier(lean_project) if lean_project else None
        self.stop = threading.Event()

    def submit(self, lhs, rhs, variables, *, model=None):
        left, right = Polynomial.parse(lhs, variables), Polynomial.parse(rhs, variables)
        task = {
            "schema": "NOVA-PROOF-TASK-v1",
            "lhs": left.expression(),
            "rhs": right.expression(),
            "variables": list(variables),
            "model": model or {},
        }
        raw = canonical_json(task)
        if len(raw) > 32768 or len(list(self.queue.glob("*.json"))) >= self.max_jobs:
            raise ValueError("proof queue budget exceeded")
        key = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        atomic(self.queue / (key + ".json"), raw)
        return key

    def process_once(self):
        with (self.directory / ".worker.lock").open("a+b") as lock:
            try:
                if os.name == "nt":
                    import msvcrt

                    lock.seek(0)
                    lock.write(b"0")
                    lock.flush()
                    lock.seek(0)
                    msvcrt.locking(lock.fileno(), msvcrt.LK_NBLCK, 1)
                else:
                    import fcntl

                    fcntl.flock(lock.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
            except (BlockingIOError, OSError):
                return []
            try:
                return self._process_unlocked()
            finally:
                if os.name == "nt":
                    lock.seek(0)
                    msvcrt.locking(lock.fileno(), msvcrt.LK_UNLCK, 1)
                else:
                    fcntl.flock(lock.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_UN)

    def _process_unlocked(self):
        results = []
        for path in sorted(self.queue.glob("*.json"))[: self.max_jobs]:
            key = path.stem
            if len(key) != 64:
                continue
            if (self.receipts / (key + ".json")).exists():
                path.unlink()
                continue
            try:
                if path.stat().st_size > 32768:
                    raise ValueError("proof task too large")
                raw = path.read_bytes()
                if hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() != key:
                    raise ValueError("task content address mismatch")
                task = strict_json(raw, 32768)
                if task["schema"] != "NOVA-PROOF-TASK-v1":
                    raise ValueError("unknown task schema")
                lhs = Polynomial.parse(task["lhs"], task["variables"])
                rhs = Polynomial.parse(task["rhs"], task["variables"])
                exact = (
                    PolynomialVerifier()
                    .identity(lhs, rhs, model=task.get("model"))
                    .to_dict()
                )
                result = {
                    "job": key,
                    "exact": exact,
                    "lean": {"status": "unavailable"},
                    "physical_truth_proved": False,
                }
                if exact["status"] == "proved" and self.lean:
                    receipt = self.lean.identity(lhs, rhs).to_dict()
                    result["lean"] = receipt
                    if receipt["status"] == "proved":
                        # Same safe generator as the adapter; peer-supplied Lean source is never used.
                        source = self.lean.source(lhs, rhs)
                        if (
                            hashlib.sha256(source.encode()).hexdigest()
                            != receipt["details"]["source_sha256"]
                        ):
                            raise ValueError(
                                "proof source differs from compiled source"
                            )
                        file = self.theorems / ("thm_" + key + ".lean")
                        atomic(file, source.encode())
                        result["lean_file"] = str(file)
                atomic(self.receipts / (key + ".json"), canonical_json(result))
                results.append(result)
                path.unlink()
            except (
                ValueError,
                TypeError,
                KeyError,
                SyntaxError,
                RecursionError,
                OSError,
            ) as error:
                result = {"job": key, "status": "rejected", "reason": str(error)[:512]}
                atomic(self.receipts / (key + ".json"), canonical_json(result))
                path.unlink()
                results.append(result)
        return results

    def run(self):
        while not self.stop.is_set():
            self.process_once()
            self.stop.wait(self.poll_seconds)


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/scientific/dataset_synthesizer.py
========================================================================================
"""Append-only scientific rows with explicit evidence classes and provenance."""

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import os
import re
from .polynomial import Polynomial
from .verification import PolynomialVerifier
from nova.bioma.determinism import canonical_json, strict_json


class DatasetSynthesizer:
    """Generated observations are not upgraded to facts by a remote signature.

    Optional formal claims are locally rechecked using the exact polynomial
    checker. A proof covers the encoded model identity, not every numeric row.
    """

    def __init__(self, directory, *, max_rows=100000, max_row_bytes=262144):
        self.directory = Path(directory)
        self.directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.path = self.directory / "observations.jsonl"
        self.max_rows = max_rows
        self.max_row_bytes = max_row_bytes
        self.count = 0
        self.head = "0" * 64
        if self.path.exists():
            with self.path.open("rb") as stream:
                for line in stream:
                    if len(line) > max_row_bytes:
                        raise ValueError("existing row exceeds budget")
                    row = strict_json(line, max_row_bytes)
                    digest = row.pop("row_sha256")
                    if (
                        row["previous"] != self.head
                        or hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(row)).hexdigest() != digest
                    ):
                        raise ValueError("dataset chain corrupted")
                    self.head = digest
                    self.count += 1
            if self.count > max_rows:
                raise ValueError("dataset row budget exceeded")

    def append(self, observation, *, seed_digest, units, claim=None):
        if self.count >= self.max_rows:
            raise ValueError("dataset row budget exceeded")
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", seed_digest):
            raise ValueError("source seed artifact digest required")
        verification = None
        label = "simulation_observation"
        if claim:
            variables = claim["variables"]
            lhs = Polynomial.parse(claim["lhs"], variables)
            rhs = Polynomial.parse(claim["rhs"], variables)
            verification = (
                PolynomialVerifier()
                .identity(lhs, rhs, model=claim.get("model"))
                .to_dict()
            )
            label = (
                "observation_with_model_identity"
                if verification["status"] == "proved"
                else "observation_with_unproved_candidate"
            )
        row = {
            "schema": "NOVA-DATASET-ROW-v1",
            "index": self.count,
            "previous": self.head,
            "seed_digest": seed_digest,
            "observation": observation,
            "units": dict(units),
            "evidence_class": label,
            "verification": verification,
        }
        raw = canonical_json(row)
        digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        row["row_sha256"] = digest
        encoded = canonical_json(row) + b"\n"
        if len(encoded) > self.max_row_bytes:
            raise ValueError("scientific row exceeds budget")
        with self.path.open("ab") as stream:
            stream.write(encoded)
            stream.flush()
            os.fsync(stream.fileno())
        self.head = digest
        self.count += 1
        return digest

    def manifest(self):
        result = {
            "schema": "NOVA-DATASET-v1",
            "rows": self.count,
            "head": self.head,
            "file": "observations.jsonl",
            "evidence_policy": "simulation observations and scoped model identities; no universal real-world truth guarantee",
        }
        (self.directory / "manifest.json").write_bytes(canonical_json(result))
        return result


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/scientific/hamiltonian_search.py
========================================================================================
"""Fit canonical Hamiltonians from measured vector fields, with explicit assumptions."""

import numpy as np
from .non_polynomial import Expression
from .polynomial import Polynomial
from .verification import PolynomialVerifier


class HamiltonianSearch:
    def __init__(self, variables, basis):
        if (
            not len(variables) % 2 == 0
            or not 2 <= len(variables) <= 8
            or not 1 <= len(basis) <= 64
        ):
            raise ValueError("canonical variables/basis budget exceeded")
        self.variables = list(variables)
        self.basis = [Expression.parse(source, variables) for source in basis]

    def fit(self, states, derivatives):
        x = np.asarray(states, dtype=float)
        dx = np.asarray(derivatives, dtype=float)
        d = len(self.variables)
        half = d // 2
        if (
            x.shape != dx.shape
            or x.ndim != 2
            or x.shape[1] != d
            or not 16 <= len(x) <= 50000
            or not np.isfinite(x).all()
            or not np.isfinite(dx).all()
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid canonical observations")
        columns = []
        for expression in self.basis:
            gradient = np.stack(
                [
                    expression.derivative(name).evaluate(x, self.variables)
                    for name in self.variables
                ],
                axis=1,
            )
            columns.append(np.c_[gradient[:, half:], -gradient[:, :half]])
        design = np.stack(columns, axis=-1)
        cut = int(0.75 * len(x))
        matrix = design[:cut].reshape(-1, len(self.basis))
        target = dx[:cut].ravel()
        coefficients = np.linalg.lstsq(matrix, target, rcond=1e-10)[0]
        h = Expression.constant(0)
        for c, expression in zip(coefficients, self.basis):
            h = Expression(
                "add",
                (h, Expression("mul", (Expression.constant(float(c)), expression))),
            )
        prediction = np.einsum("ndk,k->nd", design, coefficients)
        energy = h.evaluate(x, self.variables)
        # This generic algebraic cancellation assumes differentiability and the
        # canonical time-independent equations. It does not prove data obey them.
        names = ["dq", "dp"]
        lhs = Polynomial.parse("dq*dp-dp*dq", names)
        identity = (
            PolynomialVerifier()
            .identity(
                lhs,
                Polynomial.parse("0", names),
                model={
                    "assumptions": "time-independent differentiable canonical Hamiltonian model"
                },
            )
            .to_dict()
        )
        return {
            "status": "empirical_hamiltonian_candidate",
            "expression": h.simplify().text(),
            "tree": h.to_dict(),
            "variables": self.variables,
            "coefficients": coefficients.tolist(),
            "train_vector_mse": float(np.mean((prediction[:cut] - dx[:cut]) ** 2)),
            "heldout_vector_mse": float(np.mean((prediction[cut:] - dx[cut:]) ** 2)),
            "observed_energy_range": float(np.ptp(energy)),
            "canonical_algebra_identity": identity,
            "scope": "energy cancellation conditional on autonomous canonical model; no Noether symmetry discovery or integrator conservation claim",
        }


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/scientific/non_polynomial.py
========================================================================================
"""Bounded transcendental expression trees; strict domains, no eval or peer code."""

from __future__ import annotations
import ast
from dataclasses import dataclass
from fractions import Fraction
import numpy as np


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Expression:
    op: str
    args: tuple = ()
    value: str = ""

    def size(self):
        return 1 + sum(a.size() for a in self.args)

    @classmethod
    def constant(cls, value):
        return cls("const", (), str(Fraction(str(value))))

    def evaluate(self, x, variables):
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
        if self.op == "const":
            return np.full(x.shape[:-1], float(Fraction(self.value)))
        if self.op == "var":
            return x[..., variables.index(self.value)]
        a = [v.evaluate(x, variables) for v in self.args]
        with np.errstate(all="ignore"):
            if self.op == "add":
                out = a[0] + a[1]
            elif self.op == "sub":
                out = a[0] - a[1]
            elif self.op == "mul":
                out = a[0] * a[1]
            elif self.op == "div":
                out = np.divide(a[0], a[1])
            elif self.op == "sin":
                out = np.sin(a[0])
            elif self.op == "cos":
                out = np.cos(a[0])
            elif self.op == "exp":
                out = np.exp(a[0])
            elif self.op == "log":
                out = np.log(a[0])
            elif self.op == "sqrt":
                out = np.sqrt(a[0])
            else:
                raise ValueError("unknown expression operator")
        if not np.isfinite(out).all():
            raise ValueError("expression outside its real/finite domain")
        return out

    def derivative(self, variable):
        C = Expression.constant
        E = Expression
        if self.op == "const":
            return C(0)
        if self.op == "var":
            return C(int(self.value == variable))
        a = self.args[0]
        da = a.derivative(variable)
        if self.op in ("add", "sub"):
            return E(self.op, (da, self.args[1].derivative(variable))).simplify()
        if self.op == "mul":
            b = self.args[1]
            return E(
                "add", (E("mul", (da, b)), E("mul", (a, b.derivative(variable))))
            ).simplify()
        if self.op == "div":
            b = self.args[1]
            return E(
                "div",
                (
                    E(
                        "sub",
                        (E("mul", (da, b)), E("mul", (a, b.derivative(variable)))),
                    ),
                    E("mul", (b, b)),
                ),
            ).simplify()
        if self.op == "sin":
            outer = E("cos", (a,))
        elif self.op == "cos":
            outer = E("mul", (C(-1), E("sin", (a,))))
        elif self.op == "exp":
            outer = E("exp", (a,))
        elif self.op == "log":
            outer = E("div", (C(1), a))
        elif self.op == "sqrt":
            outer = E("div", (C(1), E("mul", (C(2), E("sqrt", (a,))))))
        else:
            raise ValueError("unknown derivative operator")
        return E("mul", (outer, da)).simplify()

    def simplify(self):
        if not self.args:
            return self
        args = tuple(a.simplify() for a in self.args)
        a = args[0]
        b = args[1] if len(args) > 1 else None
        zero = lambda v: v.op == "const" and Fraction(v.value) == 0
        one = lambda v: v.op == "const" and Fraction(v.value) == 1
        # Preserve undefined domains: 0*log(x) must not erase log's domain.
        if self.op == "mul" and (
            (zero(a) and b.op in ("const", "var"))
            or (zero(b) and a.op in ("const", "var"))
        ):
            return self.constant(0)
        if self.op in ("add", "sub") and zero(b):
            return a
        if self.op == "add" and zero(a):
            return b
        if self.op == "mul" and one(a):
            return b
        if self.op == "mul" and one(b):
            return a
        return Expression(self.op, args, self.value)

    def text(self):
        if self.op in ("const", "var"):
            return f"({self.value})" if self.op == "const" else self.value
        if self.op in ("sin", "cos", "exp", "log", "sqrt"):
            return f"{self.op}({self.args[0].text()})"
        symbol = {"add": "+", "sub": "-", "mul": "*", "div": "/"}[self.op]
        return f"({self.args[0].text()}{symbol}{self.args[1].text()})"

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "op": self.op,
            "value": self.value,
            "args": [a.to_dict() for a in self.args],
        }

    @classmethod
    def parse(cls, source, variables):
        if not isinstance(source, str) or len(source) > 4096:
            raise ValueError("expression size exceeded")
        if (
            len(set(variables)) != len(variables)
            or not 1 <= len(variables) <= 8
            or any(
                not name.isidentifier() or not name.isascii() or name.startswith("_")
                for name in variables
            )
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid variables")
        tree = ast.parse(source, mode="eval")
        if len(list(ast.walk(tree))) > 128:
            raise ValueError("expression tree budget exceeded")

        def visit(node, depth=0):
            if depth > 12:
                raise ValueError("expression depth exceeded")
            if isinstance(node, ast.Name) and node.id in variables:
                return cls("var", (), node.id)
            if isinstance(node, ast.Constant) and type(node.value) in (int, float):
                value = Fraction(str(node.value))
                if (
                    max(
                        abs(value.numerator).bit_length(),
                        value.denominator.bit_length(),
                    )
                    > 256
                ):
                    raise ValueError("constant too large")
                return cls.constant(value)
            if isinstance(node, ast.UnaryOp) and isinstance(
                node.op, (ast.USub, ast.UAdd)
            ):
                a = visit(node.operand, depth + 1)
                return (
                    cls("mul", (cls.constant(-1), a))
                    if isinstance(node.op, ast.USub)
                    else a
                )
            if isinstance(node, ast.BinOp):
                a = visit(node.left, depth + 1)
                if isinstance(node.op, ast.Pow):
                    if (
                        not isinstance(node.right, ast.Constant)
                        or type(node.right.value) != int
                        or not 0 <= node.right.value <= 4
                    ):
                        raise ValueError("power budget exceeded")
                    out = cls.constant(1)
                    for _ in range(node.right.value):
                        out = cls("mul", (out, a))
                    return out.simplify()
                kinds = {
                    ast.Add: "add",
                    ast.Sub: "sub",
                    ast.Mult: "mul",
                    ast.Div: "div",
                }
                if type(node.op) not in kinds:
                    raise ValueError("operator not allowed")
                return cls(kinds[type(node.op)], (a, visit(node.right, depth + 1)))
            if (
                isinstance(node, ast.Call)
                and isinstance(node.func, ast.Name)
                and node.func.id in ("sin", "cos", "exp", "log", "sqrt")
                and len(node.args) == 1
                and not node.keywords
            ):
                return cls(node.func.id, (visit(node.args[0], depth + 1),))
            raise ValueError(
                "only declared arithmetic operators/functions are accepted"
            )

        result = visit(tree.body).simplify()
        if result.size() > 128:
            raise ValueError("expanded expression budget exceeded")
        return result


class NonPolynomialRegressor:
    """Sparse measured search over finite operator combinations with domain rejection."""

    def __init__(self, max_features=128, max_terms=8):
        if not 4 <= max_features <= 256 or not 1 <= max_terms <= 16:
            raise ValueError("symbolic search budget exceeded")
        self.max_features, self.max_terms = max_features, max_terms

    def fit(self, x, y, variables, *, groups=None):
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
        y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
        if (
            x.ndim != 2
            or not 12 <= len(x) <= 50000
            or not 1 <= x.shape[1] <= 8
            or x.shape[1] != len(variables)
            or y.shape != (len(x),)
            or not np.isfinite(x).all()
            or not np.isfinite(y).all()
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid discovery observations")
        sources = ["1"] + list(variables)
        for name in variables:
            sources.extend(
                [f"{op}({name})" for op in ("sin", "cos", "exp", "log", "sqrt")]
                + [f"{name}**2", f"{name}**3"]
            )
        for i, a in enumerate(variables):
            for b in variables[i:]:
                sources.extend([f"{a}*{b}", f"{a}*sin({b})", f"{a}/(1+{b}**2)"])
        expressions = []
        columns = []
        rejected = []
        for source in dict.fromkeys(sources):
            if len(expressions) >= self.max_features:
                break
            expression = Expression.parse(source, variables)
            try:
                column = expression.evaluate(x, variables)
            except ValueError:
                rejected.append(source)
                continue
            expressions.append(expression)
            columns.append(column)
        design = np.stack(columns, axis=1)
        if groups is None:
            train = np.arange(len(x)) < int(0.75 * len(x))
            split = "ordered_block"
        else:
            groups = np.asarray(groups)
            unique = list(dict.fromkeys(groups.tolist()))
            if len(unique) < 2 or groups.shape != (len(x),):
                raise ValueError("multiple trajectory groups required")
            train = np.isin(groups, unique[: max(1, int(0.75 * len(unique)))])
            split = "trajectory_groups"
        if train.sum() < 4 or (~train).sum() < 2:
            raise ValueError("insufficient holdout")
        chosen = []
        available = list(range(design.shape[1]))
        residual = y[train].copy()
        best = float("inf")
        for _ in range(min(self.max_terms, len(available))):
            index = max(
                available,
                key=lambda i: (
                    abs(design[train, i] @ residual)
                    / max(np.linalg.norm(design[train, i]), 1e-12)
                ),
            )
            trial = chosen + [index]
            coef = np.linalg.lstsq(design[train][:, trial], y[train], rcond=1e-10)[0]
            error = y[train] - design[train][:, trial] @ coef
            objective = float(np.mean(error**2)) + len(trial) * 1e-6
            if chosen and objective >= best:
                break
            best = objective
            chosen = trial
            available.remove(index)
            residual = error
        coef = np.linalg.lstsq(design[train][:, chosen], y[train], rcond=1e-10)[0]
        formula = Expression.constant(0)
        for index, c in zip(chosen, coef):
            formula = Expression(
                "add",
                (
                    formula,
                    Expression(
                        "mul",
                        (
                            Expression.constant(
                                Fraction(float(c)).limit_denominator(100000)
                            ),
                            expressions[index],
                        ),
                    ),
                ),
            )
        formula = formula.simplify()
        prediction = formula.evaluate(x, variables)
        return {
            "status": "empirical_candidate",
            "expression": formula.text(),
            "tree": formula.to_dict(),
            "variables": list(variables),
            "train_mse": float(np.mean((prediction[train] - y[train]) ** 2)),
            "heldout_mse": float(np.mean((prediction[~train] - y[~train]) ** 2)),
            "baseline_mse": float(np.mean((y[~train] - y[train].mean()) ** 2)),
            "split": split,
            "domain_rejected": rejected,
            "features": len(expressions),
            "formal_proof": False,
        }


def grunwald_letnikov(values, alpha, dt, *, memory=256):
    """Causal finite-memory Grünwald–Letnikov derivative, specified zero prehistory.

    This is a numerical operator with truncation/boundary bias, not a symbolic
    universal fractional calculus theorem.
    """
    values = np.asarray(values, dtype=float)
    if (
        values.ndim != 1
        or not 1 <= len(values) <= 50000
        or not np.isfinite(values).all()
        or not 0 < alpha < 2
        or not np.isfinite(dt)
        or not dt > 0
        or not 1 <= memory <= 2048
    ):
        raise ValueError("fractional derivative budget/domain exceeded")
    weights = np.ones(min(memory, len(values)))
    for k in range(1, len(weights)):
        weights[k] = weights[k - 1] * (1 - (alpha + 1) / k)
    return np.convolve(values, weights, mode="full")[: len(values)] / dt**alpha


def vector_cross(left, right):
    left, right = np.asarray(left, dtype=float), np.asarray(right, dtype=float)
    if (
        left.ndim < 1
        or left.shape != right.shape
        or left.shape[-1] != 3
        or not np.isfinite(left).all()
        or not np.isfinite(right).all()
    ):
        raise ValueError("finite matching 3-vectors required")
    return np.cross(left, right)


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/scientific/polynomial.py
========================================================================================
"""Bounded exact rational polynomials parsed from an arithmetic-only AST."""

from __future__ import annotations
import ast
from fractions import Fraction
import re
import numpy as np


class Polynomial:
    max_terms = 512
    max_degree = 8

    def __init__(self, variables, terms=None):
        self.variables = tuple(variables)
        if (
            not 1 <= len(self.variables) <= 16
            or len(set(self.variables)) != len(self.variables)
            or any(
                not re.fullmatch(r"[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_]{0,31}", v)
                for v in self.variables
            )
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid polynomial variables")
        self.terms = {tuple(m): Fraction(c) for m, c in (terms or {}).items() if c}
        if len(self.terms) > self.max_terms:
            raise ValueError("polynomial term budget exceeded")
        for m, c in self.terms.items():
            if (
                len(m) != len(self.variables)
                or any(not isinstance(p, int) or p < 0 for p in m)
                or sum(m) > self.max_degree
                or max(abs(c.numerator).bit_length(), c.denominator.bit_length()) > 512
            ):
                raise ValueError("polynomial degree/coefficient budget exceeded")

    def constant(self, value):
        return Polynomial(self.variables, {(0,) * len(self.variables): Fraction(value)})

    def _coerce(self, other):
        if not isinstance(other, Polynomial):
            other = self.constant(other)
        if other.variables != self.variables:
            raise ValueError("variable context mismatch")
        return other

    def __add__(self, other):
        other = self._coerce(other)
        terms = self.terms.copy()
        for m, c in other.terms.items():
            terms[m] = terms.get(m, Fraction(0)) + c
        return Polynomial(self.variables, terms)

    def __neg__(self):
        return Polynomial(self.variables, {m: -c for m, c in self.terms.items()})

    def __sub__(self, other):
        return self + -self._coerce(other)

    def __mul__(self, other):
        other = self._coerce(other)
        terms = {}
        if len(self.terms) * len(other.terms) > 65536:
            raise ValueError("polynomial expansion budget exceeded")
        for a, c in self.terms.items():
            for b, d in other.terms.items():
                m = tuple(x + y for x, y in zip(a, b))
                terms[m] = terms.get(m, Fraction(0)) + c * d
        return Polynomial(self.variables, terms)

    def derivative(self, index):
        result = {}
        for m, c in self.terms.items():
            if m[index]:
                n = list(m)
                n[index] -= 1
                result[tuple(n)] = c * m[index]
        return Polynomial(self.variables, result)

    def evaluate(self, x):
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
        if x.shape[-1] != len(self.variables):
            raise ValueError("polynomial input dimension mismatch")
        out = np.zeros(x.shape[:-1])
        for powers, c in self.terms.items():
            out += float(c) * np.prod(x ** np.asarray(powers), axis=-1)
        return out

    def expression(self):
        if not self.terms:
            return "0"
        terms = []
        for powers, c in sorted(self.terms.items()):
            factors = [f"({c.numerator}/{c.denominator})"]
            factors.extend(
                name if power == 1 else f"({name}**{power})"
                for name, power in zip(self.variables, powers)
                if power
            )
            terms.append("*".join(factors))
        return "(" + "+".join(terms) + ")"

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "variables": list(self.variables),
            "terms": [[list(m), str(c)] for m, c in sorted(self.terms.items())],
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data):
        return cls(data["variables"], {tuple(m): Fraction(c) for m, c in data["terms"]})

    @classmethod
    def parse(cls, expression, variables):
        if not isinstance(expression, str) or len(expression) > 16384:
            raise ValueError("expression too large")
        tree = ast.parse(expression, mode="eval")
        if len(list(ast.walk(tree))) > 2048:
            raise ValueError("expression tree budget exceeded")
        base = cls(variables)
        names = {
            name: cls(variables, {tuple(int(i == j) for i in range(len(variables))): 1})
            for j, name in enumerate(variables)
        }

        def visit(node):
            if isinstance(node, ast.Name) and node.id in names:
                return names[node.id]
            if isinstance(node, ast.Constant) and type(node.value) in (int, float):
                return base.constant(Fraction(str(node.value)))
            if isinstance(node, ast.UnaryOp) and isinstance(
                node.op, (ast.USub, ast.UAdd)
            ):
                value = visit(node.operand)
                return -value if isinstance(node.op, ast.USub) else value
            if isinstance(node, ast.BinOp):
                left = visit(node.left)
                if isinstance(node.op, ast.Pow):
                    if (
                        not isinstance(node.right, ast.Constant)
                        or type(node.right.value) != int
                        or not 0 <= node.right.value <= cls.max_degree
                    ):
                        raise ValueError("invalid exponent")
                    value = base.constant(1)
                    for _ in range(node.right.value):
                        value = value * left
                    return value
                right = visit(node.right)
                if isinstance(node.op, ast.Add):
                    return left + right
                if isinstance(node.op, ast.Sub):
                    return left - right
                if isinstance(node.op, ast.Mult):
                    return left * right
                if isinstance(node.op, ast.Div):
                    zero = (0,) * len(variables)
                    if set(right.terms) != {zero}:
                        raise ValueError("division requires nonzero rational constant")
                    return left * base.constant(1 / right.terms[zero])
            raise ValueError("only bounded polynomial arithmetic is supported")

        return visit(tree.body)


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/scientific/symbolic_regression.py
========================================================================================
"""Sparse polynomial discovery with held-out groups and rationalized coefficients."""

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
from fractions import Fraction
from itertools import combinations_with_replacement
import hashlib
import numpy as np
from .polynomial import Polynomial


@dataclass
class SymbolicCandidate:
    polynomial: Polynomial
    metrics: dict
    provenance: dict

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            "polynomial": self.polynomial.to_dict(),
            "expression": self.polynomial.expression(),
            "metrics": self.metrics,
            "provenance": self.provenance,
            "status": "empirical_candidate",
        }


class SymbolicRegressor:
    """Greedy sparse least squares over a declared polynomial library.

    Train/holdout splitting is by trajectory groups when supplied, otherwise by
    ordered blocks. A formula fitting a latent model is not automatically a law
    in physical coordinates. Rationalization is checked on held-out data.
    """

    def __init__(
        self, degree=3, max_terms=8, max_features=256, complexity_penalty=1e-5
    ):
        if (
            not 1 <= degree <= 5
            or not 1 <= max_terms <= 32
            or not 1 <= max_features <= 512
        ):
            raise ValueError("symbolic search budget exceeded")
        self.degree, self.max_terms, self.max_features, self.penalty = (
            degree,
            max_terms,
            max_features,
            complexity_penalty,
        )

    def fit(self, x, y, variables, groups=None, provenance=None):
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
        y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
        if (
            x.ndim != 2
            or x.shape[1] != len(variables)
            or not 1 <= x.shape[1] <= 8
            or y.shape != (len(x),)
            or len(x) < 12
            or not np.isfinite(x).all()
            or not np.isfinite(y).all()
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid symbolic observations")
        if groups is None:
            train = np.arange(len(x)) < int(0.75 * len(x))
            split = "ordered_block"
        else:
            groups = np.asarray(groups)
            unique = list(dict.fromkeys(groups.tolist()))
            if len(unique) < 2:
                raise ValueError("held-out trajectory groups required")
            train = np.isin(groups, unique[: max(1, int(0.75 * len(unique)))])
            split = "trajectory_groups"
        test = ~train
        if train.sum() < 4 or test.sum() < 2:
            raise ValueError("insufficient held-out observations")
        powers = [(0,) * x.shape[1]]
        for degree in range(1, self.degree + 1):
            for indices in combinations_with_replacement(range(x.shape[1]), degree):
                p = [0] * x.shape[1]
                for i in indices:
                    p[i] += 1
                powers.append(tuple(p))
                if len(powers) > self.max_features:
                    raise ValueError(
                        "feature library exceeds budget; lower degree/dimension"
                    )
        design = np.stack([np.prod(x ** np.asarray(p), axis=1) for p in powers], axis=1)
        if not np.isfinite(design).all():
            raise ValueError("overflow in symbolic library")
        selected = []
        residual = y[train].copy()
        scale = np.maximum(np.linalg.norm(design[train], axis=0), 1e-12)
        available = set(range(len(powers)))
        best = float(np.mean(residual**2))
        for _ in range(min(self.max_terms, len(powers))):
            index = max(
                sorted(available),
                key=lambda i: abs(float(design[train, i] @ residual)) / scale[i],
            )
            trial = selected + [index]
            coef = np.linalg.lstsq(design[train][:, trial], y[train], rcond=1e-10)[0]
            error = y[train] - design[train][:, trial] @ coef
            objective = float(np.mean(error**2)) + self.penalty * len(trial)
            if selected and objective >= best:
                break
            selected = trial
            available.remove(index)
            residual = error
            best = objective
        coef = np.linalg.lstsq(design[train][:, selected], y[train], rcond=1e-10)[0]
        polynomial = Polynomial(
            variables,
            {
                powers[i]: Fraction(float(c)).limit_denominator(10000)
                for i, c in zip(selected, coef)
            },
        )
        prediction = polynomial.evaluate(x)
        baseline = float(np.mean((y[test] - y[train].mean()) ** 2))
        condition = float(np.linalg.cond(design[train][:, selected]))
        condition = condition if np.isfinite(condition) else None
        return SymbolicCandidate(
            polynomial,
            {
                "train_mse": float(np.mean((prediction[train] - y[train]) ** 2)),
                "heldout_mse": float(np.mean((prediction[test] - y[test]) ** 2)),
                "mean_baseline_mse": baseline,
                "terms": len(polynomial.terms),
                "train_samples": int(train.sum()),
                "heldout_samples": int(test.sum()),
                "split": split,
                "design_condition": condition,
            },
            {
                **(provenance or {}),
                "feature_variables": list(variables),
                "data_sha256": hashlib.sha256(x.tobytes() + y.tobytes()).hexdigest(),
            },
        )

    def distill_latent(self, world, z, actions, coordinates, dt=1.0):
        z = np.asarray(z)
        actions = np.asarray(actions)
        if dt <= 0 or len(coordinates) + actions.shape[1] > 8:
            raise ValueError("explicit low-dimensional observable selection required")
        future = world.predict(z, actions)
        x = np.c_[z[:, coordinates], actions]
        names = [f"z{i}" for i in coordinates] + [
            f"a{i}" for i in range(actions.shape[1])
        ]
        return [
            self.fit(
                x,
                (future[:, index] - z[:, index]) / dt,
                names,
                provenance={
                    "scope": "selected_latent_coordinates",
                    "coordinate": index,
                    "dt": dt,
                    "warning": "unobserved latent coordinates may invalidate closure; not a physical-coordinate theorem",
                },
            )
            for index in coordinates
        ]

ARQUIVO: nova/scientific/verification.py

"""Exact model-relative proofs and optional SMT; no truth-by-checksum shortcut."""

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
import hashlib
from .polynomial import Polynomial
from nova.bioma.determinism import canonical_json


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VerificationReceipt:
    status: str
    backend: str
    claim_sha256: str
    model_sha256: str
    assumptions: tuple[str, ...]
    details: dict

    def to_dict(self):
        return asdict(self)


def receipt(status, backend, claim, model=None, assumptions=(), details=None):
    return VerificationReceipt(
        status,
        backend,
        hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(claim)).hexdigest(),
        hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(model or {})).hexdigest(),
        tuple(assumptions),
        details or {},
    )


class PolynomialVerifier:
    def identity(self, lhs: Polynomial, rhs: Polynomial, *, model=None):
        difference = lhs - rhs
        claim = {
            "kind": "polynomial_identity",
            "lhs": lhs.to_dict(),
            "rhs": rhs.to_dict(),
        }
        return receipt(
            "proved" if not difference.terms else "refuted",
            "exact-rational-normalization-v1",
            claim,
            model,
            ("over rational/real variables in the encoded polynomial model",),
            {"residual": difference.to_dict()},
        )

    def invariant(self, invariant: Polynomial, vector_field: list[Polynomial]):
        if len(vector_field) != len(invariant.variables):
            raise ValueError("vector field dimension mismatch")
        derivative = invariant.constant(0)
        for i, component in enumerate(vector_field):
            derivative = derivative + invariant.derivative(i) * component
        model = {
            "variables": list(invariant.variables),
            "vector_field": [p.to_dict() for p in vector_field],
        }
        claim = {"kind": "continuous_ode_invariant", "invariant": invariant.to_dict()}
        return receipt(
            "proved" if not derivative.terms else "refuted",
            "exact-rational-lie-derivative-v1",
            claim,
            model,
            (
                "continuous polynomial ODE exactly as encoded; no claim about floating integrator or real world",
            ),
            {"lie_derivative": derivative.to_dict()},
        )


class Z3Verifier:
    def __init__(self, timeout_ms=5000):
        if not 1 <= timeout_ms <= 30000:
            raise ValueError("SMT timeout out of range")
        self.timeout = timeout_ms

    def identity(self, lhs: Polynomial, rhs: Polynomial, assumptions=()):
        if lhs.variables != rhs.variables or any(
            p.variables != lhs.variables for p in assumptions
        ):
            raise ValueError("variable context mismatch")
        claim = {
            "kind": "polynomial_identity",
            "lhs": lhs.to_dict(),
            "rhs": rhs.to_dict(),
        }
        model = {"domain": [p.to_dict() for p in assumptions]}
        try:
            import z3
        except ImportError:
            return receipt(
                "unavailable",
                "z3",
                claim,
                model,
                details={"reason": "install nova-research[science]"},
            )
        variables = {name: z3.Real(name) for name in lhs.variables}

        def encode(p):
            value = z3.RealVal(0)
            for powers, c in p.terms.items():
                term = z3.RealVal(f"{c.numerator}/{c.denominator}")
                for name, power in zip(p.variables, powers):
                    term *= variables[name] ** power
                value += term
            return value

        solver = z3.Solver()
        solver.set(timeout=self.timeout)
        for assumption in assumptions:
            solver.add(encode(assumption) >= 0)
        # Distinguish an empty assumption domain from a nonvacuous theorem.
        domain = solver.check()
        if domain != z3.sat:
            return receipt(
                "unknown" if domain == z3.unknown else "vacuous",
                "z3",
                claim,
                model,
                details={"domain": str(domain)},
            )
        solver.add(encode(lhs) != encode(rhs))
        result = solver.check()
        details = {"solver_version": z3.get_version_string(), "result": str(result)}
        if result == z3.sat:
            details["counterexample"] = {
                name: str(solver.model().eval(v, model_completion=True))
                for name, v in variables.items()
            }
        return receipt(
            "proved"
            if result == z3.unsat
            else "refuted"
            if result == z3.sat
            else "unknown",
            "z3",
            claim,
            model,
            ("only within the encoded nonempty real-polynomial domain",),
            details,
        )


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/scientific/verifier_lean.py
========================================================================================
"""Lean adapter for internally generated polynomial identities, never peer code."""

from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
from .polynomial import Polynomial
from .verification import receipt


class LeanVerifier:
    """Requires a user-provisioned Lean4 + Mathlib project. No network downloads.

    Only bounded expressions regenerated from Polynomial objects are compiled.
    Remote .lean files, tactics, #eval and arbitrary imports are not accepted.
    """

    def __init__(self, project, timeout=30):
        self.project = Path(project).resolve()
        self.timeout = timeout
        if not 1 <= timeout <= 120:
            raise ValueError("Lean timeout out of range")

    @staticmethod
    def _expression(p):
        pieces = []
        for powers, c in sorted(p.terms.items()):
            factors = [f"(({c.numerator} : ℝ) / {c.denominator})"]
            factors.extend(
                f"({name} ^ {power})"
                for name, power in zip(p.variables, powers)
                if power
            )
            pieces.append(" * ".join(factors))
        return " + ".join(pieces) or "(0 : ℝ)"

    def source(self, lhs, rhs):
        if lhs.variables != rhs.variables:
            raise ValueError("variable context mismatch")
        names = " ".join(lhs.variables)
        return f"import Mathlib\nset_option maxHeartbeats 200000\nexample ({names} : ℝ) : {self._expression(lhs)} = {self._expression(rhs)} := by\n  ring\n"

    def identity(self, lhs: Polynomial, rhs: Polynomial):
        if lhs.variables != rhs.variables:
            raise ValueError("variable context mismatch")
        claim = {
            "kind": "polynomial_identity",
            "lhs": lhs.to_dict(),
            "rhs": rhs.to_dict(),
        }
        if not self.project.is_dir() or not shutil.which("lake"):
            return receipt(
                "unavailable",
                "lean4-mathlib",
                claim,
                details={"reason": "local lake/Mathlib project required"},
            )
        source = self.source(lhs, rhs)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(
            prefix=".nova-proof-", dir=self.project
        ) as directory:
            path = Path(directory) / "Claim.lean"
            path.write_text(source)
            try:
                result = subprocess.run(
                    ["lake", "env", "lean", str(path)],
                    cwd=self.project,
                    capture_output=True,
                    text=True,
                    timeout=self.timeout,
                    check=False,
                )
                version = subprocess.run(
                    ["lake", "env", "lean", "--version"],
                    cwd=self.project,
                    capture_output=True,
                    text=True,
                    timeout=10,
                    check=False,
                )
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                return receipt(
                    "unknown", "lean4-mathlib", claim, details={"reason": "timeout"}
                )
        return receipt(
            "proved" if result.returncode == 0 else "not_proved",
            "lean4-mathlib",
            claim,
            assumptions=(
                "generated real-polynomial identity, Mathlib ring tactic and local toolchain",
            ),
            details={
                "returncode": result.returncode,
                "source_sha256": hashlib.sha256(source.encode()).hexdigest(),
                "lean_version": version.stdout.strip()[:512],
                "diagnostics": (result.stdout + result.stderr)[-8192:],
            },
        )

========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/swarm/artifacts.py
