"""Content-addressed blobs, Ed25519 identities and a locally append-only ledger."""

from __future__ import annotations
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import base64
import hashlib
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import sqlite3
import tempfile
import threading
from cryptography.exceptions import InvalidSignature
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric.ed25519 import (
    Ed25519PrivateKey,
    Ed25519PublicKey,
)
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import serialization
from nova.bioma.determinism import canonical_json, strict_json


HEX = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{64}$")


def atomic(path, data):
    path = Path(path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    fd, name = tempfile.mkstemp(dir=path.parent, prefix=".nova-artifact-")
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as stream:
            stream.write(data)
            stream.flush()
            os.fsync(stream.fileno())
        os.replace(name, path)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(name):
            os.unlink(name)


class SigningIdentity:
    def __init__(self, key):
        self.key = key

    @classmethod
    def load_or_create(cls, path):
        path = Path(path)
        if path.exists():
            return cls(Ed25519PrivateKey.from_private_bytes(path.read_bytes()))
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        key = Ed25519PrivateKey.generate()
        fd = os.open(path, os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_WRONLY, 0o600)
        with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as stream:
            stream.write(
                key.private_bytes(
                    serialization.Encoding.Raw,
                    serialization.PrivateFormat.Raw,
                    serialization.NoEncryption(),
                )
            )
        return cls(key)

    @property
    def public_key(self):
        return self.key.public_key().public_bytes(
            serialization.Encoding.Raw, serialization.PublicFormat.Raw
        )

    @property
    def peer_id(self):
        return hashlib.sha256(self.public_key).hexdigest()

    def sign(self, payload):
        raw = canonical_json(payload)
        return {
            "payload": payload,
            "public_key": base64.b64encode(self.public_key).decode(),
            "signature": base64.b64encode(self.key.sign(raw)).decode(),
        }

    @staticmethod
    def verify(envelope, expected_peer=None):
        key = base64.b64decode(envelope["public_key"], validate=True)
        signature = base64.b64decode(envelope["signature"], validate=True)
        peer = hashlib.sha256(key).hexdigest()
        if expected_peer is not None and peer != expected_peer:
            raise ValueError("signer identity mismatch")
        try:
            Ed25519PublicKey.from_public_bytes(key).verify(
                signature, canonical_json(envelope["payload"])
            )
        except InvalidSignature as error:
            raise ValueError("invalid envelope signature") from error
        return peer, envelope["payload"]


class ArtifactStore:
    def __init__(
        self, directory, max_bytes=8 * 1024 * 1024, max_total_bytes=256 * 1024 * 1024
    ):
        self.lock = threading.RLock()
        self.directory = Path(directory)
        self.directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.max_bytes = max_bytes
        self.max_total = max_total_bytes

    def put(self, data: bytes):
        with self.lock:
            return self._put(data)

    def _put(self, data: bytes):
        if len(data) > self.max_bytes:
            raise ValueError("artifact exceeds size budget")
        digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        path = self.directory / digest
        if not path.exists():
            total = sum(
                p.stat().st_size
                for p in self.directory.iterdir()
                if p.is_file() and HEX.fullmatch(p.name)
            )
            if total + len(data) > self.max_total:
                raise ValueError("artifact store quota exceeded")
            atomic(path, data)
        return digest

    def get(self, digest):
        if not HEX.fullmatch(digest):
            raise ValueError("invalid content address")
        path = self.directory / digest
        if path.stat().st_size > self.max_bytes:
            raise ValueError("stored artifact exceeds size budget")
        data = path.read_bytes()
        if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != digest:
            raise ValueError("artifact integrity mismatch")
        return data

    def put_json(self, data):
        return self.put(canonical_json(data))

    def get_json(self, digest):
        return strict_json(self.get(digest), self.max_bytes)

    def inventory(self, limit=128):
        return sorted(
            p.name
            for p in self.directory.iterdir()
            if p.is_file() and HEX.fullmatch(p.name)
        )[:limit]


class ScientificLedger:
    """Signed local hash chain. A trusted external head is needed to detect rollback.

    This is not a global blockchain, Byzantine consensus, or immutable storage.
    Foreign discovery receipts are claims until locally reverified.
    """

    def __init__(self, path, identity: SigningIdentity):
        self.identity = identity
        Path(path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.db = sqlite3.connect(path, timeout=10)
        self.db.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
        self.db.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS entries (sequence INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, digest TEXT UNIQUE, envelope BLOB NOT NULL)"
        )
        self.db.commit()

    def append(self, kind, body):
        if kind not in (
            "discovery",
            "lineage",
            "dataset",
            "peer_claim",
            "verification",
            "reproduction",
        ):
            raise ValueError("unknown ledger entry kind")
        if len(canonical_json(body)) > 262144:
            raise ValueError("ledger entry exceeds budget")
        try:
            self.db.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            last = self.db.execute(
                "SELECT sequence,digest FROM entries ORDER BY sequence DESC LIMIT 1"
            ).fetchone()
            payload = {
                "protocol": "NOVA-LEDGER-v1",
                "sequence": 0 if last is None else last[0] + 1,
                "previous": "0" * 64 if last is None else last[1],
                "origin": self.identity.peer_id,
                "kind": kind,
                "body": body,
                "recorded_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
            }
            envelope = self.identity.sign(payload)
            raw = canonical_json(envelope)
            digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
            self.db.execute(
                "INSERT INTO entries VALUES (?,?,?)", (payload["sequence"], digest, raw)
            )
            self.db.commit()
            return digest
        except BaseException:
            self.db.rollback()
            raise

    def verify(self, expected_head=None):
        previous = "0" * 64
        count = 0
        for sequence, digest, raw in self.db.execute(
            "SELECT sequence,digest,envelope FROM entries ORDER BY sequence"
        ):
            if len(raw) > 524288:
                raise ValueError("ledger entry size exceeded")
            _, payload = SigningIdentity.verify(
                strict_json(raw, 524288), self.identity.peer_id
            )
            if (
                sequence != count
                or payload["sequence"] != sequence
                or payload["previous"] != previous
                or hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() != digest
            ):
                raise ValueError("ledger chain mismatch")
            previous = digest
            count += 1
        if expected_head is not None and expected_head != previous:
            raise ValueError("ledger rollback/head mismatch")
        return {"entries": count, "head": previous, "scope": "local signed chain only"}

    def close(self):
        self.db.close()


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/swarm/cross_validation.py
========================================================================================
"""Signed reproducible counterexample searches. Finite survival is never a theorem."""

from __future__ import annotations
import hashlib
import time
import numpy as np
from nova.bioma.determinism import canonical_json, SplitMix64
from nova.scientific.non_polynomial import Expression
from .artifacts import SigningIdentity


def make_challenge(
    identity,
    lhs,
    rhs,
    variables,
    bounds,
    *,
    kind="identity",
    seed=0,
    samples=512,
    ttl=86400,
):
    Expression.parse(lhs, variables)
    Expression.parse(rhs, variables)
    return identity.sign(
        {
            "schema": "NOVA-CHALLENGE-v1",
            "kind": kind,
            "lhs": lhs,
            "rhs": rhs,
            "variables": list(variables),
            "bounds": bounds,
            "seed": seed,
            "samples": samples,
            "issued": int(time.time()),
            "deadline": int(time.time()) + ttl,
            "scope": "declared finite-domain model only; no universal physical-law certification",
        }
    )


class CrossValidationWorker:
    def __init__(self, identity, *, max_jobs=128, max_samples=4096):
        if not 1 <= max_jobs <= 256 or not 16 <= max_samples <= 8192:
            raise ValueError("validation worker budget exceeded")
        self.identity = identity
        self.max_jobs = max_jobs
        self.max_samples = max_samples
        self.pending = {}
        self.receipts = {}
        self.completed = 0
        self.max_completed = 4096

    def enqueue(self, envelope, expected_peer):
        peer, claim = SigningIdentity.verify(envelope, expected_peer)
        raw = canonical_json(envelope)
        if (
            len(raw) > 32768
            or len(self.pending) >= self.max_jobs
            or self.completed >= self.max_completed
        ):
            raise ValueError("challenge queue size exceeded")
        if (
            claim.get("schema") != "NOVA-CHALLENGE-v1"
            or claim["kind"] not in ("identity", "oscillator_invariant")
            or not time.time() < claim["deadline"] <= claim["issued"] + 86400
            or claim["issued"] > time.time() + 120
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid challenge kind/deadline")
        variables = claim["variables"]
        left = Expression.parse(claim["lhs"], variables)
        right = Expression.parse(claim["rhs"], variables)
        bounds = np.asarray(claim["bounds"], dtype=float)
        if (
            bounds.shape != (len(variables), 2)
            or not np.isfinite(bounds).all()
            or np.any(bounds[:, 0] >= bounds[:, 1])
            or np.any(abs(bounds) > 1e4)
            or type(claim["samples"]) is not int
            or not 16 <= claim["samples"] <= self.max_samples
        ):
            raise ValueError("challenge sampling budget/domain exceeded")
        SplitMix64(claim["seed"])
        if claim["kind"] == "oscillator_invariant" and variables != ["q", "p"]:
            raise ValueError("oscillator model requires q,p coordinates")
        key = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        if key not in self.receipts:
            self.pending[key] = (envelope, left, right)
        return {
            "challenge": key,
            "status": "queued" if key in self.pending else "already_evaluated",
            "truth_certified": False,
        }

    def process_once(self, limit=2):
        results = []
        for key in list(self.pending)[: min(limit, 8)]:
            envelope, left, right = self.pending.pop(key)
            claim = envelope["payload"]
            bounds = np.asarray(claim["bounds"])
            rng = SplitMix64(claim["seed"] ^ int(self.identity.peer_id[:16], 16))
            points = bounds[:, 0] + rng.uniform((claim["samples"], len(bounds))) * (
                bounds[:, 1] - bounds[:, 0]
            )
            # Explicit extremes plus independent per-peer random samples.
            points[0] = bounds[:, 0]
            points[1] = bounds[:, 1]
            status = "no_counterexample_within_budget"
            counterexample = None
            try:
                if time.time() > claim["deadline"]:
                    raise TimeoutError("challenge expired")
                if claim["kind"] == "identity":
                    a = left.evaluate(points, claim["variables"])
                    b = right.evaluate(points, claim["variables"])
                else:
                    a = (
                        left.derivative("q").evaluate(points, ["q", "p"]) * points[:, 1]
                        - left.derivative("p").evaluate(points, ["q", "p"])
                        * points[:, 0]
                    )
                    b = right.evaluate(points, ["q", "p"])
                scale = 1 + np.maximum(abs(a), abs(b))
                residual = np.abs(a / scale - b / scale)
                bad = np.flatnonzero(residual > 1e-8)
                if len(bad):
                    i = int(bad[0])
                    status = "counterexample_found"
                    counterexample = {
                        "point": points[i].tolist(),
                        "lhs": float(a[i]),
                        "rhs": float(b[i]),
                        "relative_residual": float(residual[i]),
                    }
            except TimeoutError:
                status = "expired"
            except ValueError:
                status = "domain_inconclusive"
            payload = {
                "schema": "NOVA-ATTESTATION-v1",
                "challenge": key,
                "status": status,
                "counterexample": counterexample,
                "samples": len(points),
                "effective_seed": claim["seed"] ^ int(self.identity.peer_id[:16], 16),
                "sampling": "SplitMix64 uniform plus two extremes",
                "scope": claim["scope"],
                "formal_proof": False,
                "finished": int(time.time()),
            }
            receipt = self.identity.sign(payload)
            self.receipts[key] = receipt
            self.completed += 1
            results.append(receipt)
            while len(self.receipts) > 1024:
                self.receipts.pop(next(iter(self.receipts)))
        return results


def aggregate_attestations(receipts, challenge):
    if len(receipts) > 256:
        raise ValueError("attestation aggregation budget exceeded")
    unique = {}
    for envelope in receipts:
        peer, payload = SigningIdentity.verify(envelope)
        if (
            payload.get("schema") != "NOVA-ATTESTATION-v1"
            or payload["challenge"] != challenge
        ):
            raise ValueError("attestation context mismatch")
        if payload["status"] not in (
            "counterexample_found",
            "no_counterexample_within_budget",
            "domain_inconclusive",
            "expired",
        ):
            raise ValueError("unknown evidence status")
        unique[peer] = payload
    refutations = [p for p in unique.values() if p["status"] == "counterexample_found"]
    return {
        "challenge": challenge,
        "independent_keys": len(unique),
        "status": "reported_counterexample"
        if refutations
        else "finite_unrefuted_evidence"
        if any(
            p["status"] == "no_counterexample_within_budget" for p in unique.values()
        )
        else "inconclusive",
        "counterexamples": [p["counterexample"] for p in refutations],
        "formal_proof": False,
        "scope": "signatures attest reports; independent keys do not establish independent people, honest execution or universal truth",
    }


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/swarm/dht_discovery.py
========================================================================================
"""Bounded iterative XOR discovery with signed expiring endpoint descriptors.

Kademlia-style routing, not a claim to implement libp2p's wire protocol. Global
bootstrap requires at least one reachable deployment; mDNS is optional LAN only.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import asyncio
from collections import OrderedDict
import ipaddress
import socket
import time
from urllib.parse import urlsplit
from .artifacts import SigningIdentity, HEX
from .p2p_node import Peer
from nova.bioma.determinism import canonical_json, strict_json


def descriptor(identity, endpoint, ttl=3600):
    Peer(identity.peer_id, endpoint)
    if not 30 <= ttl <= 86400:
        raise ValueError("descriptor TTL out of range")
    return identity.sign(
        {
            "schema": "NOVA-PEER-v1",
            "peer_id": identity.peer_id,
            "endpoint": endpoint,
            "issued": int(time.time()),
            "expires": int(time.time()) + ttl,
        }
    )


class DiscoveryTable:
    def __init__(self, identity, own, *, allow_lan=False, capacity=64):
        if not 1 <= capacity <= 256:
            raise ValueError("discovery capacity exceeded")
        self.identity = identity
        self.own = own
        self.allow_lan = allow_lan
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.descriptors = OrderedDict()
        self.addresses = {}
        self.buckets = {}
        self.verify(own)

    def verify(self, envelope):
        if len(canonical_json(envelope)) > 4096:
            raise ValueError("descriptor byte budget exceeded")
        signer, payload = SigningIdentity.verify(envelope)
        now = time.time()
        if (
            payload.get("schema") != "NOVA-PEER-v1"
            or payload["peer_id"] != signer
            or not now - 86400 <= payload["issued"] <= now + 120
            or not now < payload["expires"] <= payload["issued"] + 86400
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid/expired descriptor")
        Peer(signer, payload["endpoint"])
        return signer, payload

    def prune(self):
        expired = [
            peer
            for peer, envelope in self.descriptors.items()
            if envelope["payload"]["expires"] <= time.time()
        ]
        for peer in expired:
            self.descriptors.pop(peer, None)
            self.addresses.pop(peer, None)
        for bucket in list(self.buckets):
            self.buckets[bucket] = [p for p in self.buckets[bucket] if p not in expired]

    async def admit(self, envelope):
        self.prune()
        signer, payload = self.verify(envelope)
        if signer == self.identity.peer_id:
            return None
        url = urlsplit(payload["endpoint"])
        addresses = await asyncio.wait_for(
            asyncio.get_running_loop().getaddrinfo(
                url.hostname, url.port, type=socket.SOCK_STREAM
            ),
            5,
        )
        ips = sorted({record[4][0] for record in addresses})
        if not ips or (
            not self.allow_lan
            and any(not ipaddress.ip_address(ip).is_global for ip in ips)
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "discovered endpoint is not public; opt in to LAN separately"
            )
        # Pin a resolved address for the dial, keeping TLS hostname verification.
        peer = Peer(signer, payload["endpoint"], connect_ip=ips[0])
        distance = int(self.identity.peer_id, 16) ^ int(signer, 16)
        bucket = distance.bit_length() - 1
        members = self.buckets.setdefault(bucket, [])
        if signer not in self.descriptors and (
            len(self.descriptors) >= self.capacity or len(members) >= 8
        ):
            return None
        previous = self.descriptors.get(signer)
        if previous and previous["payload"]["issued"] > payload["issued"]:
            return None
        if signer not in members:
            members.append(signer)
        self.descriptors[signer] = envelope
        self.addresses[signer] = peer
        return peer

    def nearest(self, key, limit=8):
        if not HEX.fullmatch(key):
            raise ValueError("invalid lookup key")
        now = time.time()
        peers = [
            key
            for key, envelope in self.descriptors.items()
            if envelope["payload"]["expires"] > now
        ]
        peers.sort(key=lambda peer: int(peer, 16) ^ int(key, 16))
        return [self.descriptors[peer] for peer in peers[: min(limit, 8)]]

    async def discover(self, node, bootstraps=(), *, rounds=4):
        if not 1 <= rounds <= 8 or len(bootstraps) > 32:
            raise ValueError("lookup work budget exceeded")
        for envelope in bootstraps:
            peer = await self.admit(envelope)
            if peer:
                node.admit_discovered(peer)
        queried = set()
        failures = []
        for _ in range(rounds):
            shortlist = [
                p
                for p in node.routing.closest(self.identity.peer_id)
                if p.peer_id not in queried
            ][:3]
            if not shortlist:
                break

            async def visit(peer):
                queried.add(peer.peer_id)
                try:
                    hello = await node.request(
                        peer.peer_id, "hello", {"descriptor": self.own}
                    )
                    candidate = await self.admit(hello["descriptor"])
                    if candidate:
                        node.admit_discovered(candidate)
                    reply = await node.request(
                        peer.peer_id, "lookup", {"key": self.identity.peer_id}
                    )
                    if len(reply["descriptors"]) > 8:
                        raise ValueError("lookup fanout exceeded")
                    for item in reply["descriptors"]:
                        candidate = await self.admit(item)
                        if candidate:
                            node.admit_discovered(candidate)
                except (
                    ValueError,
                    KeyError,
                    TypeError,
                    OSError,
                    asyncio.TimeoutError,
                ) as error:
                    failures.append(
                        {"peer": peer.peer_id, "error": type(error).__name__}
                    )

            await asyncio.gather(*(visit(peer) for peer in shortlist))
        return {
            "routing_peers": len(node.routing.peers),
            "queried": len(queried),
            "failures": failures,
            "global_connectivity_proved": False,
            "bootstrap_required": not bool(queried),
        }


class LanDiscovery:
    """Optional DNS-SD/mDNS on the local network; no WAN NAT traversal claim."""

    service = "_nova-cosm._tcp.local."

    def __init__(self, own):
        self.own = own
        self.found = []
        self.registration = None
        self.browser = None
        self.zeroconf = None
        self.tasks = set()

    async def start(self, advertise_ip, port):
        from zeroconf import ServiceInfo
        from zeroconf.asyncio import (
            AsyncZeroconf,
            AsyncServiceBrowser,
            AsyncServiceInfo,
        )

        address = ipaddress.ip_address(advertise_ip)
        if address.is_loopback or address.is_unspecified:
            raise ValueError("mDNS requires an explicit LAN interface address")
        raw = canonical_json(self.own)
        if len(raw) > 2048:
            raise ValueError("mDNS descriptor too large")
        properties = {
            f"chunk{i}".encode(): raw[i * 200 : (i + 1) * 200]
            for i in range((len(raw) + 199) // 200)
        }
        peer = self.own["payload"]["peer_id"]
        self.zeroconf = AsyncZeroconf()
        self.registration = ServiceInfo(
            self.service,
            peer[:16] + "." + self.service,
            addresses=[address.packed],
            port=port,
            properties=properties,
        )
        await self.zeroconf.async_register_service(self.registration)

        async def resolve(name):
            info = AsyncServiceInfo(self.service, name)
            if await info.async_request(self.zeroconf.zeroconf, 2000):
                raw = b"".join(
                    info.properties.get(f"chunk{i}".encode(), b"") for i in range(11)
                )
                try:
                    envelope = strict_json(raw, 2048)
                    SigningIdentity.verify(envelope)
                    if len(self.found) < 64:
                        self.found.append(envelope)
                except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
                    pass

        loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()

        def schedule(name):
            if len(self.tasks) >= 32:
                return
            task = asyncio.create_task(resolve(name))
            self.tasks.add(task)
            task.add_done_callback(self.tasks.discard)

        def changed(zeroconf, service_type, name, state_change):
            if name != self.registration.name and state_change.name in (
                "Added",
                "Updated",
            ):
                loop.call_soon_threadsafe(schedule, name)

        self.browser = AsyncServiceBrowser(
            self.zeroconf.zeroconf, self.service, handlers=[changed]
        )

    async def close(self):
        for task in list(self.tasks):
            task.cancel()
        await asyncio.gather(*self.tasks, return_exceptions=True)
        if self.browser:
            await self.browser.async_cancel()
        if self.zeroconf:
            if self.registration:
                await self.zeroconf.async_unregister_service(self.registration)
            await self.zeroconf.async_close()


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/swarm/holoseed.py
========================================================================================
"""Replay manifests bind seeds to algorithms, parameters, genomes and event logs."""

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import asdict
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
from nova import __version__
from nova.bioma.determinism import canonical_json, environment_fingerprint
from nova.bioma.physics_engine import PhysicsConfig
from nova.bioma.ecosystem import Ecosystem, EcosystemConfig
from .artifacts import SigningIdentity


def engine_digest():
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    for file in sorted(root.rglob("*.py")):
        digest.update(file.relative_to(root).as_posix().encode())
        digest.update(file.read_bytes())
    return digest.hexdigest()


class HoloSeed:
    """A full bounded manifest, not a 64-byte encoding of arbitrary state.

    A SHA-256 address is 32 bytes; it locates referenced content but cannot
    reconstruct that content without the matching artifacts and engine.
    Float64 does not imply portable bit-identical execution.
    """

    protocol = "NOVA-HOLOSEED-v1"

    @classmethod
    def pack(
        cls,
        ecosystem: Ecosystem,
        *,
        initial_genomes=None,
        tier="same_environment",
        identity=None,
    ):
        if tier not in ("strict_integer", "same_environment"):
            raise ValueError("unsupported replay tier")
        world = ecosystem.world
        if tier == "strict_integer" and (
            world.config.mode not in ("conway", "fixed_gray_scott")
            or ecosystem.config.initial_population != 0
            or ecosystem.organisms
            or ecosystem.events
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "strict integer replay requires a field-only integer mode and no external events"
            )
        if initial_genomes is not None:
            raise ValueError(
                "set custom genomes at Ecosystem genesis, not after simulation"
            )
        expected = (
            world.state_hash() if tier == "strict_integer" else ecosystem.state_hash()
        )
        payload = {
            "protocol": cls.protocol,
            "engine_version": __version__,
            "engine_sha256": engine_digest(),
            "physics": asdict(world.config),
            "ecosystem": asdict(ecosystem.config),
            "seed": world.config.seed,
            "ticks": world.tick,
            "events": ecosystem.events,
            "reproducibility": tier,
            "environment": environment_fingerprint(),
            "expected_state_sha256": expected,
            "genesis": "seeded_engine_v1",
            "initial_genomes": ecosystem.genesis_genomes,
            "note": "Digest equality verifies this encoded replay, not a scientific theorem.",
        }
        if len(canonical_json(payload)) > 260000:
            raise ValueError(
                "genesis manifest exceeds packet budget; use a checkpoint artifact"
            )
        return (
            identity.sign(payload)
            if identity
            else {"payload": payload, "unsigned": True}
        )

    @classmethod
    def validate(
        cls,
        envelope,
        *,
        max_ticks=10000,
        max_cells=262144,
        max_population=32,
        require_signature=False,
        max_cell_updates=67108864,
        max_agent_ticks=32768,
    ):
        if len(canonical_json(envelope)) > 262144:
            raise ValueError("manifest exceeds packet budget")
        if require_signature or not envelope.get("unsigned"):
            SigningIdentity.verify(envelope)
        payload = envelope["payload"]
        if (
            payload.get("protocol") != cls.protocol
            or payload.get("genesis") != "seeded_engine_v1"
        ):
            raise ValueError("unknown seed protocol/genesis")
        if (
            not isinstance(payload["ticks"], int)
            or not 0 <= payload["ticks"] <= max_ticks
        ):
            raise ValueError("replay tick budget exceeded")
        physics = payload["physics"].copy()
        physics["shape"] = tuple(physics["shape"])
        physics["birth"] = tuple(physics["birth"])
        physics["survive"] = tuple(physics["survive"])
        config = PhysicsConfig(**physics)
        eco = EcosystemConfig(**payload["ecosystem"])
        if (
            int(np.prod(config.shape)) > max_cells
            or eco.max_population > max_population
            or len(payload["events"]) > 10000
            or int(np.prod(config.shape)) * payload["ticks"] > max_cell_updates
            or eco.max_population * payload["ticks"] > max_agent_ticks
        ):
            raise ValueError("replay resource budget exceeded")
        if payload["seed"] != config.seed:
            raise ValueError("seed mismatch")
        if payload["reproducibility"] == "strict_integer" and (
            config.mode not in ("conway", "fixed_gray_scott")
            or eco.initial_population
            or payload["events"]
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid strict-integer replay declaration")
        if payload["reproducibility"] not in ("strict_integer", "same_environment"):
            raise ValueError("unknown reproducibility tier")
        genomes = payload.get("initial_genomes")
        if genomes is not None:
            from nova.bioma.genetics import MorphogeneticCPPN

            if not isinstance(genomes, list) or len(genomes) != eco.initial_population:
                raise ValueError("genesis genome population mismatch")
            for genome in genomes:
                MorphogeneticCPPN.from_state_dict(genome)
        return payload, config, eco

    @classmethod
    def reproduce(cls, envelope, **budgets):
        payload, physics, config = cls.validate(envelope, **budgets)
        if payload["engine_sha256"] != engine_digest():
            raise ValueError("engine digest differs; replay is not claimed equivalent")
        if payload["engine_version"] != __version__:
            raise ValueError("engine version mismatch")
        if (
            payload["reproducibility"] == "same_environment"
            and payload["environment"] != environment_fingerprint()
        ):
            raise ValueError("floating environment fingerprint mismatch")
        ecosystem = Ecosystem(
            physics, config, genesis_genomes=payload.get("initial_genomes")
        )
        events = {}
        for event in payload["events"]:
            events.setdefault(event["tick"], []).append(event)
        if any(
            not isinstance(t, int) or not 0 <= t <= payload["ticks"] for t in events
        ):
            raise ValueError("event log clock out of range")
        for tick in range(payload["ticks"]):
            for event in events.get(tick, []):
                ecosystem.apply_event(event)
            ecosystem.step()
        for event in events.get(payload["ticks"], []):
            ecosystem.apply_event(event)
        digest = (
            ecosystem.world.state_hash()
            if payload["reproducibility"] == "strict_integer"
            else ecosystem.state_hash()
        )
        return ecosystem, {
            "status": "matched"
            if digest == payload["expected_state_sha256"]
            else "mismatch",
            "expected": payload["expected_state_sha256"],
            "observed": digest,
            "tier": payload["reproducibility"],
            "scientific_truth_proved": False,
        }


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/swarm/p2p_node.py
========================================================================================
"""Opt-in pinned-peer RPC and XOR routing over framed TLS or WebSockets.

No unauthenticated peer code, automatic simulation, dynamic imports, arbitrary
URLs, or received proof scripts are executed. Received claims remain unverified.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import asyncio
import base64
from collections import OrderedDict
from dataclasses import dataclass
import hashlib
import ssl
import struct
import time
from urllib.parse import urlsplit
from nova.bioma.determinism import canonical_json, strict_json
from .artifacts import ArtifactStore, SigningIdentity, HEX


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Peer:
    peer_id: str
    endpoint: str
    connect_ip: str | None = None

    def __post_init__(self):
        u = urlsplit(self.endpoint)
        if (
            not HEX.fullmatch(self.peer_id)
            or u.scheme not in ("tls", "tcp", "wss", "ws")
            or not u.hostname
            or not u.port
            or u.username
            or u.password
            or u.query
            or u.fragment
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid pinned peer endpoint")
        if u.scheme in ("tcp", "ws") and u.hostname not in (
            "127.0.0.1",
            "localhost",
            "::1",
        ):
            raise ValueError("remote peers require TLS/wss")


class RoutingTable:
    """Bounded XOR-distance routing; manually pinned peers, not a global DHT claim."""

    def __init__(self, identity, peers=(), capacity=64):
        if len(peers) > capacity:
            raise ValueError("routing budget exceeded")
        self.identity = identity
        self.peers = {p.peer_id: p for p in peers}
        self.capacity = capacity

    def closest(self, key, limit=8):
        if not HEX.fullmatch(key):
            raise ValueError("invalid routing key")
        return sorted(
            self.peers.values(), key=lambda p: int(p.peer_id, 16) ^ int(key, 16)
        )[:limit]


class PeerNode:
    max_frame = 1024 * 1024

    def __init__(
        self,
        identity: SigningIdentity,
        store: ArtifactStore,
        peers=(),
        *,
        max_connections=8,
        clock_skew=120,
        discovery=None,
    ):
        self.identity, self.store = identity, store
        self.routing = RoutingTable(identity.peer_id, peers)
        self.static_peers = set(self.routing.peers)
        self.allowed = set(self.routing.peers) | {identity.peer_id}
        self.semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(max_connections)
        self.seen = OrderedDict()
        self.rates = {}
        self.clock_skew = clock_skew
        self.received = []
        self.discovery = discovery
        self.webrtc = None
        self.migration = None
        self.cross_validation = None
        self.sequence = 0
        self.consensus = {}
        self.subspaces = {}

    def admit_discovered(self, peer):
        if self.discovery is None:
            raise ValueError("dynamic discovery is disabled")
        for stale in list(self.routing.peers):
            if (
                stale not in self.static_peers
                and stale not in self.discovery.descriptors
            ):
                self.routing.peers.pop(stale, None)
                self.allowed.discard(stale)
        if (
            peer.peer_id not in self.routing.peers
            and len(self.routing.peers) >= self.routing.capacity
        ):
            return False
        self.routing.peers[peer.peer_id] = peer
        self.allowed.add(peer.peer_id)
        return True

    def register_consensus(self, gossip):
        """Opt in to a shared, locally declared model coordinate system."""
        self.consensus[gossip.model_id] = gossip

    def register_subspace(self, coordinate_id, basis, rank_budget):
        from .subspace_exchange import package_subspace

        packet = package_subspace(basis, coordinate_id)
        self.subspaces[coordinate_id] = {"packet": packet, "rank_budget": rank_budget}

    def message(self, method, body):
        if method not in (
            "inventory",
            "get",
            "put",
            "find",
            "announce",
            "belief",
            "subspace",
            "hello",
            "lookup",
            "spore",
            "challenge",
            "attestations",
            "rtc-offer",
        ):
            raise ValueError("unsupported RPC method")
        self.sequence += 1
        payload = {
            "protocol": "NOVA-SWARM-v1",
            "origin": self.identity.peer_id,
            "method": method,
            "body": body,
            "time": int(time.time()),
            "nonce": hashlib.sha256(
                self.identity.public_key
                + struct.pack(">QQ", time.time_ns(), self.sequence)
            ).hexdigest(),
        }
        return self.identity.sign(payload)

    def validate(self, envelope):
        peer, payload = SigningIdentity.verify(envelope)
        if (
            (
                peer not in self.allowed
                and not (
                    self.discovery is not None and payload.get("method") == "hello"
                )
            )
            or payload.get("origin") != peer
            or payload.get("protocol") != "NOVA-SWARM-v1"
        ):
            raise ValueError("peer is not admitted")
        now = time.time()
        if abs(now - payload["time"]) > self.clock_skew or not HEX.fullmatch(
            payload["nonce"]
        ):
            raise ValueError("expired/invalid envelope")
        key = (peer, payload["nonce"])
        if key in self.seen:
            raise ValueError("replayed envelope")
        self.rates = {
            key: bucket
            for key, bucket in self.rates.items()
            if bucket and now - bucket[-1] < 60
        }
        if peer not in self.rates and len(self.rates) >= 256:
            raise ValueError("admission rate table full")
        bucket = [t for t in self.rates.get(peer, []) if now - t < 60]
        if len(bucket) >= 120:
            raise ValueError("peer rate budget exceeded")
        bucket.append(now)
        self.rates[peer] = bucket
        self.seen[key] = now
        while len(self.seen) > 4096:
            self.seen.popitem(last=False)
        return peer, payload

    async def handle(self, raw):
        if isinstance(raw, str):
            raw = raw.encode()
        peer, payload = self.validate(strict_json(raw, self.max_frame))
        method = payload["method"]
        body = payload["body"]
        if method == "hello":
            if self.discovery is None:
                raise ValueError("dynamic discovery is disabled")
            signer, _ = self.discovery.verify(body["descriptor"])
            if signer != peer:
                raise ValueError("descriptor and RPC origin differ")
            admitted = await self.discovery.admit(body["descriptor"])
            if admitted:
                self.admit_discovered(admitted)
            result = {"descriptor": self.discovery.own}
        elif method == "rtc-offer":
            if self.webrtc is None:
                raise ValueError("WebRTC is disabled")
            result = await self.webrtc.accept(body, peer)
        elif method == "lookup":
            if self.discovery is None:
                raise ValueError("dynamic discovery is disabled")
            result = {"descriptors": self.discovery.nearest(body["key"])}
        elif method == "spore":
            if self.migration is None:
                raise ValueError("migration policy is disabled")
            result = self.migration.receive(body, expected_peer=peer)
            result["artifact"] = self.store.put_json(body)
        elif method == "challenge":
            if self.cross_validation is None:
                raise ValueError("cross validation is disabled")
            result = self.cross_validation.enqueue(body, peer)
        elif method == "attestations":
            if self.cross_validation is None:
                raise ValueError("cross validation is disabled")
            result = {"receipts": list(self.cross_validation.receipts.values())[-16:]}
        elif method == "inventory":
            result = {"hashes": self.store.inventory()}
        elif method == "get":
            data = self.store.get(body["digest"])
            if len(data) > 512 * 1024:
                raise ValueError("network object transfer budget exceeded")
            result = {"digest": body["digest"], "data": base64.b64encode(data).decode()}
        elif method == "put":
            if len(body["data"]) > 700000:
                raise ValueError("network object too large")
            data = base64.b64decode(body["data"], validate=True)
            if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != body["digest"]:
                raise ValueError("object digest mismatch")
            # A blob is inert content, never an imported module or runnable script.
            digest = self.store.put(data)
            result = {"stored": digest, "verification": "not_scientifically_verified"}
        elif method == "find":
            result = {
                "peers": [
                    {"peer_id": p.peer_id, "endpoint": p.endpoint}
                    for p in self.routing.closest(body["key"])
                ]
            }
        elif method == "belief":
            model = self.consensus.get(body.get("model_id"))
            if model is None:
                result = {"status": "model_not_registered", "truth_proved": False}
            else:
                accepted = model.accept(peer, body)
                result = {
                    "accepted": accepted,
                    "belief": model.step() if accepted else model.packet(),
                    "truth_proved": False,
                }
        elif method == "subspace":
            from .subspace_exchange import merge_subspaces, package_subspace
            import numpy as np

            coordinate_id = body.get("coordinate_id")
            local = self.subspaces.get(coordinate_id)
            if local is None:
                result = {"status": "coordinate_system_not_registered"}
            else:
                merged = merge_subspaces(
                    np.asarray(local["packet"]["basis"]),
                    body,
                    coordinate_id,
                    local["rank_budget"],
                )
                result = {k: v for k, v in merged.items() if k != "basis"}
                if merged["basis"] is not None:
                    local["packet"] = package_subspace(merged["basis"], coordinate_id)
                    result["packet"] = local["packet"]
        elif method == "announce":
            if not HEX.fullmatch(body["digest"]):
                raise ValueError("invalid announcement")
            self.received.append(
                {"peer": peer, "digest": body["digest"], "status": "quarantined_claim"}
            )
            self.received = self.received[-128:]
            result = {"accepted": "inventory_only", "executed": False}
        else:
            raise ValueError("unknown RPC method")
        return canonical_json(
            self.identity.sign(
                {
                    "protocol": "NOVA-SWARM-REPLY-v1",
                    "request_nonce": payload["nonce"],
                    "body": result,
                }
            )
        )

    async def _read(self, reader):
        length = struct.unpack(">I", await asyncio.wait_for(reader.readexactly(4), 10))[
            0
        ]
        if length > self.max_frame:
            raise ValueError("frame size exceeded")
        return await asyncio.wait_for(reader.readexactly(length), 10)

    async def _serve_connection(self, reader, writer):
        # Reject excess accepted connections instead of accumulating waiters.
        if self.semaphore.locked():
            writer.close()
            return
        async with self.semaphore:
            try:
                reply = await self.handle(await self._read(reader))
                writer.write(struct.pack(">I", len(reply)) + reply)
                await writer.drain()
            except (
                ValueError,
                KeyError,
                TypeError,
                OSError,
                asyncio.IncompleteReadError,
                asyncio.TimeoutError,
            ):
                pass
            finally:
                writer.close()
                await writer.wait_closed()

    async def serve(
        self,
        host="127.0.0.1",
        port=8766,
        *,
        certfile=None,
        keyfile=None,
        transport="tcp",
    ):
        local = host in ("127.0.0.1", "localhost", "::1")
        if not local and not (certfile and keyfile):
            raise ValueError("remote listening requires TLS certificate/key")
        context = None
        if certfile:
            context = ssl.SSLContext(ssl.PROTOCOL_TLS_SERVER)
            context.minimum_version = ssl.TLSVersion.TLSv1_2
            context.load_cert_chain(certfile, keyfile)
        if transport == "websocket":
            from websockets.asyncio.server import serve

            async def handler(socket):
                if self.semaphore.locked():
                    await socket.close(code=1013, reason="connection budget exceeded")
                    return
                async with self.semaphore:
                    try:
                        raw = await asyncio.wait_for(socket.recv(), 10)
                        await socket.send(await self.handle(raw))
                    except (
                        ValueError,
                        KeyError,
                        TypeError,
                        OSError,
                        asyncio.TimeoutError,
                    ):
                        await socket.close(code=1008, reason="request rejected")

            async with serve(
                handler,
                host,
                port,
                ssl=context,
                max_size=self.max_frame,
                max_queue=4,
                open_timeout=10,
            ):
                await asyncio.Future()
        elif transport == "tcp":
            server = await asyncio.start_server(
                self._serve_connection,
                host,
                port,
                ssl=context,
                limit=self.max_frame + 4,
                backlog=16,
            )
            async with server:
                await server.serve_forever()
        else:
            raise ValueError("unknown transport")

    async def request(self, peer_id, method, body):
        if peer_id not in self.routing.peers:
            raise ValueError("peer endpoint must be manually pinned")
        peer = self.routing.peers[peer_id]
        url = urlsplit(peer.endpoint)
        envelope = self.message(method, body)
        raw = canonical_json(envelope)
        if len(raw) > self.max_frame:
            raise ValueError("request frame exceeded")
        context = ssl.create_default_context() if url.scheme in ("tls", "wss") else None
        if url.scheme in ("ws", "wss"):
            from websockets.asyncio.client import connect

            kwargs = (
                {"host": peer.connect_ip, "server_hostname": url.hostname}
                if peer.connect_ip and context
                else ({"host": peer.connect_ip} if peer.connect_ip else {})
            )
            async with connect(
                peer.endpoint,
                ssl=context,
                max_size=self.max_frame,
                open_timeout=10,
                **kwargs,
            ) as socket:
                await socket.send(raw)
                reply = await asyncio.wait_for(socket.recv(), 10)
        else:
            reader, writer = await asyncio.wait_for(
                asyncio.open_connection(
                    peer.connect_ip or url.hostname,
                    url.port,
                    ssl=context,
                    server_hostname=url.hostname if context else None,
                ),
                10,
            )
            try:
                writer.write(struct.pack(">I", len(raw)) + raw)
                await writer.drain()
                reply = await self._read(reader)
            finally:
                writer.close()
                await writer.wait_closed()
        _, payload = SigningIdentity.verify(strict_json(reply, self.max_frame), peer_id)
        if (
            payload.get("protocol") != "NOVA-SWARM-REPLY-v1"
            or payload.get("request_nonce") != envelope["payload"]["nonce"]
        ):
            raise ValueError("reply/request mismatch")
        return payload["body"]

    async def synchronize(self, peer_id, limit=8):
        inventory = await self.request(peer_id, "inventory", {})
        downloaded = []
        for digest in inventory["hashes"][:limit]:
            if not HEX.fullmatch(digest):
                continue
            try:
                self.store.get(digest)
                continue
            except FileNotFoundError:
                pass
            packet = await self.request(peer_id, "get", {"digest": digest})
            data = base64.b64decode(packet["data"], validate=True)
            if len(data) > 512 * 1024 or hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != digest:
                raise ValueError("received object digest/size mismatch")
            self.store.put(data)
            downloaded.append(digest)
        return {
            "downloaded": downloaded,
            "scientific_verification": "pending_local_reverification",
            "automatic_execution": False,
        }


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/swarm/sheaf_consensus.py
========================================================================================
"""Local sheaf discrepancy descent; no Byzantine agreement or truth oracle."""

from __future__ import annotations
import numpy as np


class SheafGossip:
    def __init__(
        self, dimension, model_id, initial=None, *, max_neighbors=32, huber_delta=1.0
    ):
        if (
            not 1 <= dimension <= 128
            or not 1 <= max_neighbors <= 256
            or huber_delta <= 0
        ):
            raise ValueError("gossip size/scale budget exceeded")
        self.dimension, self.model_id, self.max_neighbors = (
            dimension,
            model_id,
            max_neighbors,
        )
        self.value = (
            np.zeros(dimension)
            if initial is None
            else np.asarray(initial, dtype=float).copy()
        )
        if self.value.shape != (dimension,) or not np.isfinite(self.value).all():
            raise ValueError("invalid belief")
        self.neighbors = {}
        self.huber_delta = huber_delta
        self.sequence = 0

    def accept(self, peer, payload, left=None, right=None):
        if payload.get("model_id") != self.model_id:
            return False
        value = np.asarray(payload["value"], dtype=float)
        sequence = payload["sequence"]
        if (
            value.shape != self.value.shape
            or not np.isfinite(value).all()
            or not isinstance(sequence, int)
            or sequence < 0
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid belief packet")
        if peer not in self.neighbors and len(self.neighbors) >= self.max_neighbors:
            return False
        old = self.neighbors.get(peer)
        if old and sequence <= old["sequence"]:
            return False
        left = np.eye(self.dimension) if left is None else np.asarray(left, dtype=float)
        right = (
            np.eye(self.dimension) if right is None else np.asarray(right, dtype=float)
        )
        if (
            left.shape != (self.dimension, self.dimension)
            or right.shape != left.shape
            or not np.isfinite(left).all()
            or not np.isfinite(right).all()
        ):
            raise ValueError("restriction mismatch")
        self.neighbors[peer] = {
            "sequence": sequence,
            "value": value.copy(),
            "left": left,
            "right": right,
        }
        return True

    def step(self, step_size=0.1):
        if not 0 < step_size <= 1:
            raise ValueError("invalid gossip step")
        gradient = np.zeros_like(self.value)
        energy = 0.0
        bound = 0.0
        for neighbor in self.neighbors.values():
            left, right = neighbor["left"], neighbor["right"]
            difference = left @ self.value - right @ neighbor["value"]
            norm = np.linalg.norm(difference)
            scale = min(1.0, self.huber_delta / max(norm, 1e-12))
            gradient += left.T @ (difference * scale)
            energy += (
                0.5 * norm * norm
                if norm <= self.huber_delta
                else self.huber_delta * (norm - 0.5 * self.huber_delta)
            )
            bound += np.linalg.norm(left, 2) ** 2
        self.value -= min(step_size, 0.45 / max(bound, 1.0)) * gradient
        self.sequence += 1
        return {
            "model_id": self.model_id,
            "sequence": self.sequence,
            "value": self.value.tolist(),
            "discrepancy_before": float(energy),
            "scope": "local numerical consistency only; agreement is not a proof of truth",
        }

    def packet(self):
        return {
            "model_id": self.model_id,
            "sequence": self.sequence,
            "value": self.value.tolist(),
        }


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/swarm/spore_migration.py
========================================================================================
"""Signed bounded genome migration with quarantine, provenance and local admission."""

import hashlib
import time
import numpy as np
from nova.bioma.determinism import canonical_json, SplitMix64
from nova.bioma.genetics import MorphogeneticCPPN
from .artifacts import SigningIdentity


def make_spore(identity, genome, origin, *, ttl=3600):
    if not 30 <= ttl <= 86400:
        raise ValueError("spore TTL invalid")
    return identity.sign(
        {
            "schema": "NOVA-SPORE-v1",
            "genome": genome.state_dict(),
            "genome_sha256": genome.hash(),
            "origin": dict(origin),
            "issued": int(time.time()),
            "expires": int(time.time()) + ttl,
            "meaning": "developmental genome; trained neural state and skills are not transferred",
        }
    )


class SporeMigration:
    def __init__(
        self,
        identity,
        *,
        enabled=False,
        max_pending=16,
        max_imports=32,
        cooldown_ticks=64,
    ):
        self.identity = identity
        self.enabled = enabled
        self.max_pending = max_pending
        self.max_imports = max_imports
        self.cooldown = cooldown_ticks
        if (
            not 1 <= max_pending <= 64
            or not 1 <= max_imports <= 256
            or not 1 <= cooldown_ticks <= 4096
        ):
            raise ValueError("migration budget invalid")
        self.pending = {}
        self.seen = set()
        self.imports = 0
        self.last_tick = -cooldown_ticks
        self.records = []

    def receive(self, envelope, expected_peer):
        peer, payload = SigningIdentity.verify(envelope, expected_peer)
        raw = canonical_json(envelope)
        if (
            len(raw) > 131072
            or len(self.pending) >= self.max_pending
            or len(self.seen) >= 4096
        ):
            raise ValueError("spore queue/size/history budget exceeded")
        if (
            payload.get("schema") != "NOVA-SPORE-v1"
            or not time.time() < payload["expires"] <= payload["issued"] + 86400
            or payload["issued"] > time.time() + 120
        ):
            raise ValueError("spore schema/deadline invalid")
        genome = MorphogeneticCPPN.from_state_dict(payload["genome"])
        if genome.hash() != payload["genome_sha256"]:
            raise ValueError("spore genome digest differs")
        if (
            genome.config.neural_nodes > 64
            or genome.config.max_n2 > 2
            or genome.config.body_resolution > 16
        ):
            raise ValueError("immigrant development budget exceeded")
        digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        if digest in self.seen:
            raise ValueError("replayed spore")
        self.seen.add(digest)
        self.pending[digest] = {"envelope": envelope, "peer": peer}
        return {
            "spore": digest,
            "status": "quarantined",
            "automatic_admission_enabled": self.enabled,
        }

    def admit(self, ecosystem):
        if (
            not self.enabled
            or not self.pending
            or self.imports >= self.max_imports
            or ecosystem.world.tick - self.last_tick < self.cooldown
        ):
            return None
        if len(ecosystem.organisms) >= ecosystem.config.max_population:
            return None
        key = next(iter(self.pending))
        item = self.pending.pop(key)
        payload = item["envelope"]["payload"]
        if payload["expires"] <= time.time():
            return {"status": "expired", "spore": key}
        genome = MorphogeneticCPPN.from_state_dict(payload["genome"])
        # Admission is a data-only, recorded simulation event. Replay retains the
        # genome and position; wall-clock signature checks do not enter replay.
        position = SplitMix64(int(key[:16], 16)).uniform(
            (ecosystem.world.ndim,)
        ) * np.asarray(ecosystem.world.shape)
        event = {
            "tick": ecosystem.world.tick,
            "kind": "immigrate",
            "genome": genome.state_dict(),
            "position": position.tolist(),
            "energy": ecosystem.config.initial_energy,
            "spore_sha256": key,
            "source_peer": item["peer"],
        }
        ecosystem.apply_event(event)
        self.imports += 1
        self.last_tick = ecosystem.world.tick
        record = {
            "status": "admitted",
            "spore": key,
            "genome": genome.hash(),
            "source_peer": item["peer"],
            "tick": ecosystem.world.tick,
        }
        self.records.append(record)
        return record


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/swarm/subspace_exchange.py
========================================================================================
"""Coordinate-checked exchange of protection constraints, not instant skill transfer."""

import numpy as np
from nova.bioma.determinism import digest_arrays


def coordinate_fingerprint(encoder_state, feature_schema):
    arrays = {k: np.asarray(v) for k, v in encoder_state.items()}
    return digest_arrays(arrays, {"feature_schema": feature_schema})


def package_subspace(u, coordinate_id, *, max_rank=128):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=float)
    if (
        u.ndim != 2
        or not 1 <= u.shape[0] <= 512
        or u.shape[1] > min(max_rank, u.shape[0])
        or not np.isfinite(u).all()
    ):
        raise ValueError("subspace size/value budget exceeded")
    if not np.allclose(u.T @ u, np.eye(u.shape[1]), atol=1e-9):
        raise ValueError("basis is not orthonormal")
    return {
        "protocol": "NOVA-SUBSPACE-v1",
        "coordinate_id": coordinate_id,
        "dimension": u.shape[0],
        "basis": u.tolist(),
        "meaning": "input directions to protect; does not encode target functions or skills",
    }


def merge_subspaces(local_u, packet, coordinate_id, rank_budget):
    if (
        packet.get("protocol") != "NOVA-SUBSPACE-v1"
        or packet["coordinate_id"] != coordinate_id
    ):
        raise ValueError("encoder/coordinate schema mismatch")
    remote = np.asarray(packet["basis"], dtype=float)
    local = np.asarray(local_u, dtype=float)
    package_subspace(remote, coordinate_id)
    if (
        local.ndim != 2
        or local.shape[0] != remote.shape[0]
        or not np.allclose(local.T @ local, np.eye(local.shape[1]), atol=1e-9)
    ):
        raise ValueError("local coordinate/basis mismatch")
    u, s, _ = np.linalg.svd(
        np.concatenate((local, remote), axis=1), full_matrices=False
    )
    rank = int(np.count_nonzero(s > 1e-12 * s[0])) if len(s) and s[0] else 0
    if rank > rank_budget:
        return {
            "status": "capacity_rejected",
            "required_rank": rank,
            "rank_budget": rank_budget,
            "basis": None,
        }
    return {
        "status": "merged_constraints",
        "basis": u[:, :rank],
        "rank": rank,
        "transferred_skill": False,
    }


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/swarm/webrtc_transport.py
========================================================================================
"""Optional aiortc data channels with signed SDP over admitted-peer RPC.

STUN/TURN servers are explicitly configured locally. NAT traversal depends on
network conditions and relay credentials; no public relay is silently selected.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import asyncio
import hashlib
import ipaddress
import time
from .artifacts import SigningIdentity
from nova.bioma.determinism import canonical_json, strict_json


class WebRTCTransport:
    def __init__(self, node, ice_servers=(), max_connections=4):
        if not 1 <= max_connections <= 8 or len(ice_servers) > 8:
            raise ValueError("WebRTC budget exceeded")
        from aiortc import RTCConfiguration, RTCIceServer

        for server in ice_servers:
            urls = server.get("urls", [])
            urls = [urls] if isinstance(urls, str) else urls
            if not urls or any(
                not u.startswith(("stun:", "stuns:", "turn:", "turns:")) for u in urls
            ):
                raise ValueError("only STUN/TURN ICE servers are supported")
        self.node = node
        self.config = RTCConfiguration(
            iceServers=[RTCIceServer(**s) for s in ice_servers]
        )
        self.max_connections = max_connections
        self.connections = {}
        self.channels = {}
        self.pending = {}
        self.tasks = set()

    def _remote_sdp(self, sdp):
        if self.node.discovery and self.node.discovery.allow_lan:
            return sdp
        lines = []
        for line in sdp.splitlines():
            if line.startswith("a=candidate:"):
                parts = line.split()
                try:
                    allowed = (
                        len(parts) > 5 and ipaddress.ip_address(parts[4]).is_global
                    )
                except ValueError:
                    allowed = False
                if not allowed:
                    continue
            lines.append(line)
        return "\r\n".join(lines) + "\r\n"

    def _connection(self, peer):
        from aiortc import RTCPeerConnection

        if peer in self.connections or len(self.connections) >= self.max_connections:
            raise ValueError("WebRTC connection limit/duplicate")
        pc = RTCPeerConnection(self.config)
        self.connections[peer] = pc

        @pc.on("datachannel")
        def on_channel(channel):
            self._channel(peer, channel)

        @pc.on("connectionstatechange")
        async def state_change():
            if pc.connectionState in ("failed", "closed"):
                if self.connections.get(peer) is pc:
                    self.connections.pop(peer, None)
                    self.channels.pop(peer, None)
                if pc.connectionState != "closed":
                    await pc.close()

        return pc

    def _channel(self, peer, channel):
        self.channels[peer] = channel
        queue = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=8)

        @channel.on("message")
        def on_message(raw):
            if (
                not isinstance(raw, (str, bytes))
                or len(raw) > self.node.max_frame
                or queue.full()
            ):
                channel.close()
                return
            queue.put_nowait(raw)

        async def process():
            try:
                while channel.readyState != "closed":
                    raw = await asyncio.wait_for(queue.get(), 120)
                    envelope = strict_json(raw, self.node.max_frame)
                    _, payload = SigningIdentity.verify(envelope, peer)
                    if payload.get("protocol") == "NOVA-SWARM-REPLY-v1":
                        waiter = self.pending.pop(
                            (peer, payload["request_nonce"]), None
                        )
                        if waiter and not waiter.done():
                            waiter.set_result(payload["body"])
                    else:
                        reply = await self.node.handle(raw)
                        if channel.bufferedAmount > self.node.max_frame:
                            raise ValueError("WebRTC send buffer exceeded")
                        channel.send(reply)
            except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError, OSError, asyncio.TimeoutError):
                channel.close()

        task = asyncio.create_task(process())
        self.tasks.add(task)
        task.add_done_callback(self.tasks.discard)

    async def accept(self, envelope, peer):
        _, offer = SigningIdentity.verify(envelope, peer)
        if (
            offer.get("schema") != "NOVA-RTC-SDP-v1"
            or offer["type"] != "offer"
            or len(offer["sdp"]) > 65536
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid signed SDP")
        from aiortc import RTCSessionDescription

        pc = self._connection(peer)
        try:
            await asyncio.wait_for(
                pc.setRemoteDescription(
                    RTCSessionDescription(self._remote_sdp(offer["sdp"]), "offer")
                ),
                15,
            )
            answer = await pc.createAnswer()
            await asyncio.wait_for(pc.setLocalDescription(answer), 20)
            return self.node.identity.sign(
                {
                    "schema": "NOVA-RTC-SDP-v1",
                    "session": offer["session"],
                    "type": "answer",
                    "sdp": pc.localDescription.sdp,
                }
            )
        except BaseException:
            await pc.close()
            self.connections.pop(peer, None)
            raise

    async def connect(self, peer):
        from aiortc import RTCSessionDescription

        if peer not in self.node.allowed:
            raise ValueError("peer must first be admitted")
        pc = self._connection(peer)
        channel = pc.createDataChannel("nova-signed-rpc", ordered=True)
        self._channel(peer, channel)
        try:
            offer = await pc.createOffer()
            await asyncio.wait_for(pc.setLocalDescription(offer), 20)
            session = hashlib.sha256(
                f"{self.node.identity.peer_id}:{time.time_ns()}".encode()
            ).hexdigest()
            request = self.node.identity.sign(
                {
                    "schema": "NOVA-RTC-SDP-v1",
                    "session": session,
                    "type": "offer",
                    "sdp": pc.localDescription.sdp,
                }
            )
            envelope = await self.node.request(peer, "rtc-offer", request)
            _, answer = SigningIdentity.verify(envelope, peer)
            if (
                answer.get("schema") != "NOVA-RTC-SDP-v1"
                or answer["session"] != session
                or answer["type"] != "answer"
                or len(answer["sdp"]) > 65536
            ):
                raise ValueError("SDP answer context mismatch")
            await asyncio.wait_for(
                pc.setRemoteDescription(
                    RTCSessionDescription(self._remote_sdp(answer["sdp"]), "answer")
                ),
                15,
            )
        except BaseException:
            await pc.close()
            self.connections.pop(peer, None)
            self.channels.pop(peer, None)
            raise

    async def request(self, peer, method, body):
        channel = self.channels.get(peer)
        if channel is None or channel.readyState != "open":
            raise ValueError("WebRTC channel is not open")
        envelope = self.node.message(method, body)
        raw = canonical_json(envelope)
        if (
            len(raw) > self.node.max_frame
            or len(self.pending) >= 32
            or channel.bufferedAmount > self.node.max_frame
        ):
            raise ValueError("WebRTC request/send budget exceeded")
        nonce = envelope["payload"]["nonce"]
        future = asyncio.get_running_loop().create_future()
        self.pending[peer, nonce] = future
        try:
            channel.send(raw)
            return await asyncio.wait_for(future, 15)
        finally:
            self.pending.pop((peer, nonce), None)

    async def close(self):
        for task in list(self.tasks):
            task.cancel()
        await asyncio.gather(*self.tasks, return_exceptions=True)
        await asyncio.gather(
            *(pc.close() for pc in self.connections.values()), return_exceptions=True
        )
        self.connections.clear()
        self.channels.clear()


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/synapse/__init__.py
========================================================================================
"""Explicit diffusion cascade, three-factor plasticity, and homeostasis."""

import numpy as np
from scipy.linalg import expm


class DiffusiveCascade:
    """C_i du_i/dt = g_(i-1)(u_(i-1)-u_i)+g_i(u_(i+1)-u_i)-lambda_i*u_i+I_i.

    C_i=b**i, g_i=kappa*b**(-i), reflecting endpoints and configurable leak.
    This declared diffusion model is not the absent conversation's exact equations.
    """

    def __init__(self, synapses=1, reservoirs=4, b=2.0, kappa=1.0, leak=0.001):
        if min(synapses, reservoirs) < 1 or b <= 1 or kappa < 0 or leak < 0:
            raise ValueError("invalid cascade")
        self.capacity = b ** np.arange(reservoirs)
        self.state = np.zeros((synapses, reservoirs))
        self.matrix = np.diag(-np.full(reservoirs, leak) / self.capacity)
        for i in range(reservoirs - 1):
            g = kappa * b ** (-i)
            self.matrix[i, i] -= g / self.capacity[i]
            self.matrix[i, i + 1] += g / self.capacity[i]
            self.matrix[i + 1, i] += g / self.capacity[i + 1]
            self.matrix[i + 1, i + 1] -= g / self.capacity[i + 1]

    def step(self, drive=0.0, dt=0.1, method="rk4"):
        if dt <= 0:
            raise ValueError("dt must be positive")
        force = np.zeros_like(self.state)
        force[:, 0] = drive
        f = lambda u: u @ self.matrix.T + force
        if method == "euler":
            if dt * max(abs(np.linalg.eigvals(self.matrix))) > 1:
                raise ValueError("unstable Euler step")
            self.state += dt * f(self.state)
        elif method == "rk4":
            if dt * max(abs(np.linalg.eigvals(self.matrix))) > 2.5:
                raise ValueError("unstable RK4 step")
            k1 = f(self.state)
            k2 = f(self.state + dt * k1 / 2)
            k3 = f(self.state + dt * k2 / 2)
            k4 = f(self.state + dt * k3)
            self.state += dt * (k1 + 2 * k2 + 2 * k3 + k4) / 6
        else:
            raise ValueError("method must be euler or rk4")
        return self.state[:, 0].copy()

    def analytic(self, t):
        return self.state @ expm(t * self.matrix).T

    def expressed(self, beta=None):
        return self.state @ (
            np.eye(1, self.state.shape[1])[0] if beta is None else np.asarray(beta)
        )

    def consolidate(self, amount):
        """Conserve capacity-weighted mass while moving fast state to slow."""
        fraction = float(np.clip(amount, 0, 1))
        if self.state.shape[1] > 1:
            mass = self.state[:, 0] * fraction * self.capacity[0]
            self.state[:, 0] -= mass / self.capacity[0]
            self.state[:, -1] += mass / self.capacity[-1]


class ThreeFactorRule:
    """Spike STDP eligibility: pre_trace*post - post_trace*pre, gated by M(t)."""

    def __init__(self, pre, post, tau=20.0):
        self.pre = np.zeros(pre)
        self.post = np.zeros(post)
        self.tau = tau

    def step(self, pre, post, modulator, dt=1.0, lr=0.001):
        self.pre *= np.exp(-dt / self.tau)
        self.post *= np.exp(-dt / self.tau)
        eligibility = np.outer(self.pre, post) - np.outer(pre, self.post)
        self.pre += pre
        self.post += post
        return lr * modulator * eligibility


def oja(weights, pre, post, lr=0.001, modulator=1.0):
    return weights + lr * modulator * (np.outer(pre, post) - weights * np.square(post))


def homeostatic_scale(weights, rates, target=5.0, eta=0.01):
    return weights * np.exp(np.clip(eta * (target - np.asarray(rates)), -0.1, 0.1))


def prune(weights, rigidity, threshold=0.001):
    result = np.array(weights, copy=True)
    result[(abs(result) < threshold) & (np.asarray(rigidity) < threshold)] = 0
    return result


class NativeDiffusiveCascade(DiffusiveCascade):
    """Same declared equations, contiguous Rust/Rayon RK4 implementation."""

    def step(self, drive=0.0, dt=0.1, method="rk4"):
        if method != "rk4":
            return super().step(drive, dt, method)
        if dt <= 0 or dt * max(abs(np.linalg.eigvals(self.matrix))) > 2.5:
            raise ValueError("unstable RK4 step")
        from nova.core import require_native

        force = np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(drive, dtype=float), (len(self.state),))
        result = require_native().cascade_rk4(
            self.state.ravel().tolist(),
            self.matrix.ravel().tolist(),
            force.tolist(),
            self.state.shape[1],
            dt,
        )
        self.state = np.asarray(result).reshape(self.state.shape)
        return self.state[:, 0].copy()
