"""Offline world training with explicit PyTorch or NumPy/local-equilibrium backends."""

from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
from nova.environments import point_transitions
from nova.checkpoint import save_state, load_state


def train_world(output, steps=900, seed=0, *, backend="torch", train_encoder=False):
    if steps < 1:
        raise ValueError("steps must be positive")
    if backend == "torch":
        return _train_torch(output, steps, seed)
    from nova.jepa.equilibrium import EquilibriumWorldModel
    from nova.brain.latent import SpikingWorldModel

    if backend not in ("equilibrium", "adex"):
        raise ValueError("unknown training backend")
    if backend == "adex" and train_encoder:
        raise ValueError(
            "AdEx encoder uses explicit STDP rather than rate-network EqProp"
        )
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x, a, y, _ = point_transitions(seed=seed)
    model_class = SpikingWorldModel if backend == "adex" else EquilibriumWorldModel
    model = model_class(12, 8, 2, seed=seed)
    model.fit_normalizer(x)
    successful = 0
    last = {}
    for _ in range(steps):
        idx = rng.integers(len(x), size=32)
        last = model.learn_transition(
            x[idx], a[idx], y[idx], lr=0.01, train_encoder=train_encoder
        )
        successful += int(last["updated"])
    model.freeze_encoder()
    save_state(
        output, {"kind": "nova-world", "backend": backend, "state": model.state_dict()}
    )
    # Training summary only; scientific acceptance belongs to the explicit held-out runner.
    return {
        "backend": backend,
        "steps": steps,
        "updates": successful,
        "last_training_loss": last.get("loss"),
        "seed": seed,
        "checkpoint": str(output),
        "accepted": None,
        "evaluation": "not performed by training command",
    }


def _train_torch(output, steps, seed):
    import torch
    from nova.jepa import JEPA

    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    torch.set_num_threads(2)
    x, a, y, _ = map(torch.from_numpy, point_transitions(seed=seed))
    model = JEPA(12, 8, 2)
    opt = torch.optim.Adam(model.parameters(), lr=0.002)
    for _ in range(steps):
        idx = torch.randint(len(x), (128,))
        loss, metrics = model.loss(x[idx], a[idx], y[idx])
        opt.zero_grad()
        loss.backward()
        opt.step()
        model.target.update(model.context)
    # Preserve the established training command's report protocol for this baseline.
    tx, ta, ty, _ = map(torch.from_numpy, point_transitions(1024, seed + 1000))
    with torch.no_grad():
        target = model.target(ty)
        mse = (model.predictor(model.context(tx), ta) - target).square().mean().item()
        baseline = (target - model.target(y).mean(0)).square().mean().item()
        std = model.assert_noncollapsed(tx)
    path = Path(output)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with path.open("wb") as stream:
        np.savez_compressed(
            stream, **{k: v.detach().numpy() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}
        )
    return {
        "backend": "torch",
        "latent_mse": mse,
        "mean_baseline_mse": baseline,
        "latent_std": std,
        "steps": steps,
        "seed": seed,
        "accepted": mse < baseline,
    }


def load_world(runtime, path):
    if runtime.tick_index or runtime.continual.U.shape[1]:
        raise ValueError(
            "load encoder before online learning; changing coordinates invalidates protection"
        )
    if runtime.config.world_backend == "torch":
        import torch

        with np.load(path, allow_pickle=False) as arrays:
            runtime.world.load_state_dict(
                {
                    k: torch.from_numpy(arrays[k].copy())
                    for k in runtime.world.state_dict()
                }
            )
        runtime.world.context.requires_grad_(False)
        runtime.world.target.requires_grad_(False)
    else:
        state = load_state(path)
        if (
            state.get("kind") != "nova-world"
            or state["backend"] != runtime.config.world_backend
        ):
            raise ValueError("world checkpoint/backend mismatch")
        from nova.jepa.equilibrium import EquilibriumWorldModel
        from nova.brain.latent import SpikingWorldModel

        cls = SpikingWorldModel if state["backend"] == "adex" else EquilibriumWorldModel
        model = cls.from_state_dict(state["state"])
        if (model.observation_dim, model.latent_dim, model.action_dim) != (
            runtime.config.observation_dim,
            runtime.config.latent_dim,
            runtime.config.action_dim,
        ):
            raise ValueError("world checkpoint dimensions do not match runtime")
        model.freeze_encoder()
        runtime.world = model

"""Functional resource/affect state and hard output constraints outside learning."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
import numpy as np


SIMULATION_LABEL = "Simulação baseada em dados — não é a pessoa real."


@dataclass
class InternalState:
    energy: float = 1.0
    memory_load: float = 0.0
    curiosity: float = 0.0
    coherence: float = 1.0
    engagement: float = 0.0
    valence: float = 0.0
    arousal: float = 0.0
    control: float = 1.0
    previous_error: float = 1.0

    def update(
        self, error, compute_fraction, memory_load, value_delta=0.0, engagement=0.0
    ):
        self.energy = float(np.clip(self.energy + 0.01 - 0.02 * compute_fraction, 0, 1))
        self.memory_load = float(np.clip(memory_load, 0, 1))
        # Legacy one-step heuristic. Runtime overrides it with windowed LearningProgress.
        self.curiosity = float(np.clip(self.previous_error - error, 0, 1))
        self.previous_error = float(error)
        self.valence = float(np.tanh(value_delta - error))
        self.arousal = float(np.clip(error, 0, 1))
        self.control = float(np.clip(1 - error, 0, 1))
        self.engagement = float(np.clip(engagement, 0, 1))
        return self

    def sleep(self):
        self.energy = min(1.0, self.energy + 0.2)


class OutputPolicy:
    """A deterministic gate, not a trainable reward or a mutable network weight."""

    @staticmethod
    def render_claims(claims, *, identity_mode=False, consented=False):
        if identity_mode and not consented:
            raise PermissionError("identity consent required")
        lines = []
        for claim in claims:
            if claim.get("third_party_private"):
                raise PermissionError("third-party data is not releasable")
            if not claim.get("source"):
                raise ValueError("factual claim requires source")
            prefix = "Inferência: " if claim.get("inference") else ""
            lines.append(f"{prefix}{claim['text']} (fonte: {claim['source']})")
        answer = (
            "\n".join(lines)
            if lines
            else "Não sei; preciso de uma fonte para responder."
        )
        return f"{SIMULATION_LABEL}\n{answer}" if identity_mode else answer

"""Confidence-adjusted learning progress; raw surprise is not intrinsic reward."""

from collections import deque
import numpy as np


class LearningProgress:
    """Compare disjoint old/recent error windows, penalizing sampling uncertainty.

    A positive-part one-step error difference has positive expectation under IID
    noise. Windowing and a confidence margin reduce that bias, but do not prove
    zero reward for every stationary process. Inputs should be held-out errors.
    """

    def __init__(self, window=32, confidence=2.0, reward_cap=1.0):
        if window < 2 or confidence < 0 or reward_cap < 0:
            raise ValueError("invalid progress estimator")
        self.window, self.confidence, self.reward_cap = window, confidence, reward_cap
        self.errors = deque(maxlen=2 * window)
        self.reward = 0.0
        self.standard_error = 0.0

    def update(self, error):
        if not np.isfinite(error) or error < 0:
            raise ValueError("prediction error must be finite and nonnegative")
        self.errors.append(float(error))
        self.reward = 0.0
        if len(self.errors) == 2 * self.window:
            old, recent = (
                np.asarray(self.errors)[: self.window],
                np.asarray(self.errors)[self.window :],
            )
            self.standard_error = float(
                np.sqrt(
                    old.var(ddof=1) / self.window + recent.var(ddof=1) / self.window
                )
            )
            self.reward = float(
                np.clip(
                    old.mean() - recent.mean() - self.confidence * self.standard_error,
                    0,
                    self.reward_cap,
                )
            )
        return self.reward

    def state_dict(self):
        return {
            "window": self.window,
            "confidence": self.confidence,
            "reward_cap": self.reward_cap,
            "errors": list(self.errors),
            "reward": self.reward,
            "standard_error": self.standard_error,
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_state_dict(cls, state):
        obj = cls(state["window"], state["confidence"], state["reward_cap"])
        obj.errors.extend(state["errors"])
        obj.reward = state["reward"]
        obj.standard_error = state["standard_error"]
        return obj

const MASK = (1n << 64n) - 1n;
class SplitMix64 {
  constructor(seed) {
    this.state = BigInt(seed);
    if (this.state < 0n || this.state > MASK)
      throw Error("Semente deve ser uint64.");
  }
  next() {
    this.state = (this.state + 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15n) & MASK;
    let z = this.state;
    z = ((z ^ (z >> 30n)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9n) & MASK;
    z = ((z ^ (z >> 27n)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebn) & MASK;
    return (z ^ (z >> 31n)) & MASK;
  }
  uniform() {
    return Number(this.next() >> 11n) / 9007199254740992;
  }
}

function clone(x) {
  return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x));
}
function colorLinear(hex) {
  return [1, 3, 5].map((i) => {
    const c = parseInt(hex.slice(i, i + 2), 16) / 255;
    return c <= 0.04045 ? c / 12.92 : ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4;
  });
}
function normalize(v) {
  const l = Math.hypot(...v) || 1;
  return v.map((x) => x / l);
}
function cross(a, b) {
  return [
    a[1] * b[2] - a[2] * b[1],
    a[2] * b[0] - a[0] * b[2],
    a[0] * b[1] - a[1] * b[0],
  ];
}
function sha(bytes) {
  return crypto.subtle
    .digest("SHA-256", bytes)
    .then((b) =>
      Array.from(new Uint8Array(b), (x) =>
        x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"),
      ).join(""),
    );
}
function download(data, name, type = "application/json") {
  const blob = data instanceof Blob ? data : new Blob([data], { type });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob),
    a = document.createElement("a");
  a.href = url;
  a.download = name;
  a.click();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
}
function toBase64(bytes) {
  let chunks = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 16384)
    chunks.push(String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + 16384)));
  return btoa(chunks.join(""));
}
function validateSettings(s) {
  if (
    !s ||
    !Number.isInteger(s.count) ||
    s.count < 2 ||
    s.count > 100000 ||
    !PRESETS[s.preset] ||
    !["draft", "balanced", "high", "ultra"].includes(s.quality)
  )
    throw Error("Configuração de cena fora dos limites.");
  new SplitMix64(s.seed);
  if (typeof s.planar !== "boolean") throw Error("Dimensão inválida.");
  const bounds = {
    strength: [0.2, 20],
    flow: [0, 2],
    roughness: [0.04, 0.8],
    sss: [0, 4],
    absorption: [0.1, 8],
    film: [0, 1200],
    iridescence: [0, 1],
    radius: [0.008, 0.06],
    exposure: [0.1, 3],
    bloom: [0, 1.5],
    focus: [1, 6],
    aperture: [0, 10],
    damping: [0, 20],
  };
  for (const [k, [lo, hi]] of Object.entries(bounds))
    if (!Number.isFinite(s[k]) || s[k] < lo || s[k] > hi)
      throw Error("Parâmetro inválido: " + k);
  if (
    !Number.isInteger(s.iterations) ||
    s.iterations < 0 ||
    s.iterations > 4 ||
    !Number.isInteger(s.filter) ||
    s.filter < 0 ||
    s.filter > 8
  )
    throw Error("Orçamento de projeção/filtro inválido.");
}
function checkMatrix(matrix) {
  if (
    !Array.isArray(matrix) ||
    matrix.length !== 64 ||
    matrix.some((x) => !Number.isFinite(x) || Math.abs(x) > 1)
  )
    throw Error("Matriz de afinidade inválida.");
}
function matrixFor(seed) {
  const rng = new SplitMix64(BigInt(seed) ^ 0xaff1717n);
  return Array.from({ length: 64 }, (_, i) => {
    const value = rng.uniform() * 2 - 1;
    return i % 9 === 0 ? 0.7 : value;
  });
}
class Chromatic {
  constructor(device, context, format) {
    this.device = device;
    this.context = context;
    this.format = format;
    this.canvas = $("universe");
    this.tick = 0;
    this.running = true;
    this.tail = Promise.resolve();
    this.resources = [];
    this.targets = [];
    this.yaw = 0.32;
    this.pitch = 0.18;
    this.distance = 3.3;
    this.fps = 0;
    this.frameCount = 0;
    this.lastStats = 0;
    this.replay = null;
    this.recording = null;
    this.lastTime = 0;
    this.accumulator = 0;
    this.capture = false;
    this.events = [];
    this.eventCursor = 0;
    this.width = 0;
    this.height = 0;
    this.filterAxis = [];
    this.statusMetrics = {};
  }
  enqueue(operation) {
    const result = this.tail.then(operation);
    this.tail = result.catch((error) => this.error(error));
    return result;
  }
  error(error) {
    $("error").textContent = String(error.message || error);
    console.error(error);
  }
  buffer(size, usage, data = null) {
    const b = this.device.createBuffer({
      size: Math.ceil(size / 4) * 4,
      usage,
      mappedAtCreation: !!data,
    });
    if (data) {
      new Uint8Array(b.getMappedRange()).set(
        new Uint8Array(
          data.buffer || data,
          data.byteOffset || 0,
          data.byteLength,
        ),
      );
      b.unmap();
    }
    this.resources.push(b);
    return b;
  }
  async pipelines() {
    this.modules = {};
    for (const name of SHADER_NAMES) {
      const module = this.device.createShaderModule({
        code: SOURCES[name],
        label: name,
      });
      const info = await module.getCompilationInfo();
      const errors = info.messages.filter((m) => m.type === "error");
      if (errors.length)
        throw Error(name + ": " + errors.map((m) => m.message).join("\n"));
      this.modules[name] = module;
    }
    const storage = (
      binding,
      readOnly,
      visibility = GPUShaderStage.COMPUTE,
    ) => ({
      binding,
      visibility,
      buffer: { type: readOnly ? "read-only-storage" : "storage" },
    });
    this.simLayout = this.device.createBindGroupLayout({
      entries: [
        storage(0, true),
        storage(1, false),
        storage(2, false),
        storage(3, false),
        storage(4, false),
        storage(5, true),
        storage(6, false),
        {
          binding: 7,
          visibility: GPUShaderStage.COMPUTE,
          buffer: { type: "uniform" },
        },
      ],
    });
    const layout = this.device.createPipelineLayout({
      bindGroupLayouts: [this.simLayout],
    });
    this.compute = {};
    for (const entry of [
      "clear_stats",
      "clear_grid",
      "build_grid",
      "predict",
      "solve_density",
      "project",
      "finalize",
    ])
      this.compute[entry] = await this.device.createComputePipelineAsync({
        layout,
        compute: { module: this.modules.particle_life, entryPoint: entry },
      });
    this.surfaceLayout = this.device.createBindGroupLayout({
      entries: [
        storage(0, true, GPUShaderStage.VERTEX),
        {
          binding: 1,
          visibility: GPUShaderStage.VERTEX | GPUShaderStage.FRAGMENT,
          buffer: { type: "uniform" },
        },
        storage(2, true, GPUShaderStage.VERTEX),
      ],
    });
    const sl = this.device.createPipelineLayout({
      bindGroupLayouts: [this.surfaceLayout],
    });
    this.depthPipeline = await this.device.createRenderPipelineAsync({
      layout: sl,
      vertex: { module: this.modules.fluid_surface, entryPoint: "splat" },
      fragment: {
        module: this.modules.fluid_surface,
        entryPoint: "sphere_depth",
        targets: [{ format: "r32float" }],
      },
      primitive: { topology: "triangle-list" },
      depthStencil: {
        format: "depth32float",
        depthWriteEnabled: true,
        depthCompare: "less",
      },
    });
    this.thicknessPipeline = await this.device.createRenderPipelineAsync({
      layout: sl,
      vertex: { module: this.modules.fluid_surface, entryPoint: "splat" },
      fragment: {
        module: this.modules.fluid_surface,
        entryPoint: "optical_thickness",
        targets: [
          {
            format: "rgba16float",
            blend: {
              color: { srcFactor: "one", dstFactor: "one", operation: "add" },
              alpha: { srcFactor: "one", dstFactor: "one", operation: "add" },
            },
          },
        ],
      },
      primitive: { topology: "triangle-list" },
    });
    const textureEntry = (binding, visibility, unfilterable = false) => ({
      binding,
      visibility,
      texture: {
        sampleType: unfilterable ? "unfilterable-float" : "float",
        viewDimension: "2d",
      },
    });
    const uniformEntry = (binding, visibility) => ({
      binding,
      visibility,
      buffer: { type: "uniform" },
    });
    const pipelineLayout = (entries) =>
      this.device.createPipelineLayout({
        bindGroupLayouts: [this.device.createBindGroupLayout({ entries })],
      });
    const cv = GPUShaderStage.COMPUTE,
      fv = GPUShaderStage.FRAGMENT;
    const filterLayout = pipelineLayout([
      textureEntry(0, cv, true),
      textureEntry(1, cv),
      {
        binding: 2,
        visibility: cv,
        storageTexture: {
          access: "write-only",
          format: "r32float",
          viewDimension: "2d",
        },
      },
      {
        binding: 3,
        visibility: cv,
        storageTexture: {
          access: "write-only",
          format: "rgba16float",
          viewDimension: "2d",
        },
      },
      uniformEntry(4, cv),
    ]);
    const lightingLayout = pipelineLayout([
      textureEntry(0, fv, true),
      textureEntry(1, fv),
      uniformEntry(2, fv),
    ]);
    const postLayout = pipelineLayout([
      textureEntry(0, fv),
      textureEntry(1, fv, true),
      { binding: 2, visibility: fv, sampler: { type: "filtering" } },
      uniformEntry(3, fv),
    ]);
    this.filterPipeline = await this.device.createComputePipelineAsync({
      layout: filterLayout,
      compute: {
        module: this.modules.fluid_filter,
        entryPoint: "bilateral_main",
      },
    });
    this.lightingPipeline = await this.device.createRenderPipelineAsync({
      layout: lightingLayout,
      vertex: { module: this.modules.fluid_lighting, entryPoint: "fullscreen" },
      fragment: {
        module: this.modules.fluid_lighting,
        entryPoint: "shade",
        targets: [{ format: "rgba16float" }],
      },
      primitive: { topology: "triangle-list" },
    });
    this.postPipeline = await this.device.createRenderPipelineAsync({
      layout: postLayout,
      vertex: { module: this.modules.fluid_post, entryPoint: "fullscreen" },
      fragment: {
        module: this.modules.fluid_post,
        entryPoint: "post",
        targets: [{ format: this.format }],
      },
      primitive: { topology: "triangle-list" },
    });
    this.sampler = this.device.createSampler({
      magFilter: "linear",
      minFilter: "linear",
      addressModeU: "clamp-to-edge",
      addressModeV: "clamp-to-edge",
    });
  }
  async reset(settings, saved = null) {
    validateSettings(settings);
    await this.device.queue.onSubmittedWorkDone();
    for (const b of this.resources) b.destroy();
    for (const t of this.targets) t.destroy();
    this.resources = [];
    this.targets = [];
    this.width = 0;
    this.height = 0;
    this.particles = null;
    this.settings = clone(settings);
    this.initial = clone(settings);
    this.tick = 0;
    this.events = [];
    this.eventCursor = 0;
    this.accumulator = 0;
    this.replay = null;
    this.matrix = matrixFor(settings.seed);
    this.palette = COLORS.slice();
    const n = settings.count;
    this.gridDim = Math.floor(2 / 0.18);
    this.gridCells = this.gridDim ** 3;
    let data;
    if (saved?.data) {
      data = saved.decoded;
      this.tick = saved.tick;
    } else {
      const genesis = saved && !saved.data ? saved.initial : settings;
      const rng = new SplitMix64(genesis.seed);
      data = new Float32Array(n * 12);
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        let p = [
          rng.uniform() * 2 - 1,
          rng.uniform() * 2 - 1,
          rng.uniform() * 2 - 1,
        ];
        if (settings.preset !== "life") {
          const group = i % 8,
            angle = (group * Math.PI * 2) / 8;
          p = p.map((x) => x * 0.18);
          p[0] += 0.45 * Math.cos(angle);
          p[1] += 0.3 * Math.sin(angle);
          p[2] += 0.23 * Math.sin(angle * 2);
        }
        if (genesis.planar) p[2] = 0;
        const k = i * 12;
        data.set([...p, i % 8, 0, 0, 0, 1, ...p, 0], k);
      }
    }
    if (saved) {
      checkMatrix(saved.matrix);
      this.matrix = saved.matrix.slice();
      if (
        !Array.isArray(saved.palette) ||
        saved.palette.length !== 8 ||
        saved.palette.some((c) => !/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(c))
      )
        throw Error("Paleta inválida.");
      this.palette = saved.palette.slice();
      this.initial = clone(saved.initial);
      this.events = saved.timeline || [];
      if (this.events.length > 4096) throw Error("Histórico excede o limite.");
      validateSettings(this.initial);
      this.validateEvents(this.events);
      if (!saved.data) {
        this.matrix = matrixFor(this.initial.seed);
        this.palette = COLORS.slice();
        this.settings = {
          ...clone(saved.settings),
          strength: this.initial.strength,
          flow: this.initial.flow,
          planar: this.initial.planar,
        };
        this.replay = { target: saved.tick, expected: saved.state_sha256 };
        this.tick = 0;
        this.eventCursor = 0;
      } else this.eventCursor = this.events.length;
    }
    this.particles = [
      this.buffer(
        data.byteLength,
        GPUBufferUsage.STORAGE |
          GPUBufferUsage.COPY_SRC |
          GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
        data,
      ),
      this.buffer(
        data.byteLength,
        GPUBufferUsage.STORAGE |
          GPUBufferUsage.COPY_SRC |
          GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
        data,
      ),
    ];
    this.current = 0;
    this.heads = this.buffer(this.gridCells * 4, GPUBufferUsage.STORAGE);
    this.links = this.buffer(n * 4, GPUBufferUsage.STORAGE);
    this.density = this.buffer(n * 16, GPUBufferUsage.STORAGE);
    this.affinities = this.buffer(
      64 * 4,
      GPUBufferUsage.STORAGE | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
      new Float32Array(this.matrix),
    );
    this.statsBuffer = this.buffer(
      16,
      GPUBufferUsage.STORAGE | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_SRC,
    );
    this.statsRead = this.buffer(
      16,
      GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST | GPUBufferUsage.MAP_READ,
    );
    this.simUniform = this.buffer(
      80,
      GPUBufferUsage.UNIFORM | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
    );
    this.cameraUniform = this.buffer(
      128,
      GPUBufferUsage.UNIFORM | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
    );
    this.paletteBuffer = this.buffer(
      128,
      GPUBufferUsage.STORAGE | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
    );
    this.postUniform = this.buffer(
      48,
      GPUBufferUsage.UNIFORM | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
    );
    this.simGroups = [0, 1].map((i) =>
      this.device.createBindGroup({
        layout: this.simLayout,
        entries: [
          this.particles[i],
          this.particles[1 - i],
          this.heads,
          this.links,
          this.density,
          this.affinities,
          this.statsBuffer,
          this.simUniform,
        ].map((buffer, binding) => ({ binding, resource: { buffer } })),
      }),
    );
    this.surfaceGroups = this.particles.map((buffer) =>
      this.device.createBindGroup({
        layout: this.surfaceLayout,
        entries: [buffer, this.cameraUniform, this.paletteBuffer].map(
          (buffer, binding) => ({ binding, resource: { buffer } }),
        ),
      }),
    );
    this.writePalette();
    this.syncUI();
    this.running = true;
    $("pause").textContent = "Pausar";
    $("error").textContent = "";
  }
  validateEvents(events, initial = this.initial) {
    let last = 0;
    for (const e of events) {
      if (!Number.isInteger(e.tick) || e.tick < last || e.tick > 0xffffffff)
        throw Error("Relógio de eventos inválido.");
      last = e.tick;
      if (e.kind === "matrix") {
        if (
          !Number.isInteger(e.index) ||
          e.index < 0 ||
          e.index >= 64 ||
          !Number.isFinite(e.value) ||
          Math.abs(e.value) > 1
        )
          throw Error("Evento de matriz inválido.");
      } else if (e.kind === "parameter") {
        const copy = { ...initial, [e.name]: e.value };
        if (!["strength", "flow", "planar"].includes(e.name))
          throw Error("Parâmetro de evento não permitido.");
        validateSettings(copy);
      } else if (e.kind === "palette") {
        if (
          !Number.isInteger(e.index) ||
          e.index < 0 ||
          e.index >= 8 ||
          !/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(e.value)
        )
          throw Error("Evento de paleta inválido.");
      } else throw Error("Tipo de evento inválido.");
    }
  }
  writePalette() {
    this.device.queue.writeBuffer(
      this.paletteBuffer,
      0,
      new Float32Array(this.palette.flatMap((c) => [...colorLinear(c), 1])),
    );
  }
  syncUI() {
    $("planar").value = this.settings.planar ? "1" : "0";
    if (
      !Array.from($("count").options).some(
        (o) => o.value === String(this.settings.count),
      )
    ) {
      const option = document.createElement("option");
      option.value = String(this.settings.count);
      option.textContent = String(this.settings.count);
      $("count").append(option);
    }
    for (const name of [
      "preset",
      "count",
      "quality",
      "seed",
      "strength",
      "flow",
      "radius",
      "roughness",
      "absorption",
      "sss",
      "film",
      "iridescence",
      "exposure",
      "bloom",
      "focus",
      "aperture",
    ])
      $(name).value = this.settings[name];
    this.matrixUI();
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) $("color" + i).value = this.palette[i];
    this.labels();
  }
  labels() {
    for (const name of ["strength", "flow", "radius"])
      $(name + "Value").textContent = Number(this.settings[name]).toFixed(
        name === "radius" ? 3 : 2,
      );
  }
  matrixUI() {
    for (let i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
      const b = $("m" + i),
        a = this.matrix[i];
      b.style.background =
        a > 0
          ? `rgba(92,200,167,${0.15 + 0.8 * a})`
          : `rgba(230,91,132,${0.15 - 0.8 * a})`;
      b.title = `${Math.floor(i / 8)} → ${i % 8}: ${a.toFixed(2)}`;
      b.textContent = a.toFixed(1);
    }
  }
  applyEvent(e, record = true) {
    if (record) {
      if (this.events.length >= 4096)
        throw Error("Histórico cheio; exporte um checkpoint e recrie a cena.");
      this.events.push({ ...e, tick: this.tick });
    }
    if (e.kind === "matrix") {
      this.matrix[e.index] = e.value;
      this.device.queue.writeBuffer(
        this.affinities,
        e.index * 4,
        new Float32Array([e.value]),
      );
      this.matrixUI();
    } else if (e.kind === "parameter") {
      this.settings[e.name] = e.value;
      this.labels();
    } else if (e.kind === "palette") {
      this.palette[e.index] = e.value;
      this.writePalette();
    }
  }
  uniforms() {
    const s = this.settings,
      b = new ArrayBuffer(80),
      u = new Uint32Array(b),
      f = new Float32Array(b);
    u.set([
      s.count,
      this.gridDim,
      this.tick >>> 0,
      s.count > 32768 ? 512 : 2048,
    ]);
    f.set([1 / 120, 0.18, s.strength, s.damping], 4);
    f.set([8 / s.count, 3, 0.0001, s.iterations > 0 ? 0.025 : 0], 8);
    f.set(
      [s.flow, 0, s.preset === "life" ? 0 : 2, s.iterations > 0 ? 0.02 : 0],
      12,
    );
    f.set([0.25, 1, 1.5, s.planar ? 1 : 0], 16);
    this.device.queue.writeBuffer(this.simUniform, 0, b);
  }
  dispatch(encoder, entry, count) {
    const pass = encoder.beginComputePass({ label: entry });
    pass.setPipeline(this.compute[entry]);
    pass.setBindGroup(0, this.simGroups[this.current]);
    pass.dispatchWorkgroups(Math.ceil(count / 64));
    pass.end();
  }
  build(encoder) {
    this.dispatch(encoder, "clear_grid", this.gridCells);
    this.dispatch(encoder, "build_grid", this.settings.count);
  }
  step(encoder) {
    while (
      this.replay &&
      this.eventCursor < this.events.length &&
      this.events[this.eventCursor].tick === this.tick
    )
      this.applyEvent(this.events[this.eventCursor++], false);
    this.uniforms();
    this.dispatch(encoder, "clear_stats", 4);
    this.build(encoder);
    this.dispatch(encoder, "predict", this.settings.count);
    this.current = 1 - this.current;
    const iterations = Math.min(
      this.settings.iterations,
      this.settings.count > 32768 ? 1 : 4,
    );
    for (let k = 0; k < iterations; k++) {
      this.build(encoder);
      this.dispatch(encoder, "solve_density", this.settings.count);
      this.dispatch(encoder, "project", this.settings.count);
      this.current = 1 - this.current;
    }
    this.dispatch(encoder, "finalize", this.settings.count);
    this.current = 1 - this.current;
    this.tick++;
  }
  texture(format, usage) {
    const t = this.device.createTexture({
      size: [this.width, this.height],
      format,
      usage,
    });
    this.targets.push(t);
    return t;
  }
  resize() {
    const limits = {
      draft: [960, 540, 0.8],
      balanced: [1280, 720, 1],
      high: [1920, 1080, 1.25],
      ultra: [3840, 2160, 2],
    };
    const [mw, mh, scale] = limits[this.settings.quality];
    const ratio = Math.min(
      mw / innerWidth,
      mh / innerHeight,
      Math.min(devicePixelRatio, scale),
    );
    const w = Math.max(64, Math.floor(innerWidth * ratio)),
      h = Math.max(64, Math.floor(innerHeight * ratio));
    if (w === this.width && h === this.height) return;
    const estimate =
      w * h * 52 + this.settings.count * 112 + this.gridCells * 8;
    if (estimate > SCENE.max_memory_mib * 1024 ** 2)
      throw Error(
        "A qualidade excede o orçamento estimado de buffers/texturas.",
      );
    for (const t of this.targets) t.destroy();
    this.targets = [];
    for (const b of this.filterAxis) b.destroy();
    this.filterAxis = [];
    this.width = w;
    this.height = h;
    this.canvas.width = w;
    this.canvas.height = h;
    const color =
        GPUTextureUsage.RENDER_ATTACHMENT | GPUTextureUsage.TEXTURE_BINDING,
      filtered =
        GPUTextureUsage.STORAGE_BINDING | GPUTextureUsage.TEXTURE_BINDING;
    this.depths = [
      this.texture("r32float", color),
      this.texture("r32float", filtered),
      this.texture("r32float", filtered),
    ];
    this.pigments = [
      this.texture("rgba16float", color),
      this.texture("rgba16float", filtered),
      this.texture("rgba16float", filtered),
    ];
    this.hardwareDepth = this.texture(
      "depth32float",
      GPUTextureUsage.RENDER_ATTACHMENT,
    );
    this.hdr = this.texture("rgba16float", color);
    this.filterGroups = [];
    for (let passIndex = 0; passIndex < 6; passIndex++) {
      const axis = passIndex % 2;
      const source = passIndex === 0 ? 0 : axis === 0 ? 2 : 1;
      const target = axis === 0 ? 1 : 2;
      const data = new ArrayBuffer(32);
      new Int32Array(data).set([
        axis === 0 ? 1 : 0,
        axis === 1 ? 1 : 0,
        this.settings.filter,
        0,
      ]);
      new Float32Array(data).set(
        [9, Math.max(0.0001, this.settings.radius ** 2 * 8), 0, 0],
        4,
      );
      const buffer = this.device.createBuffer({
        size: 32,
        usage: GPUBufferUsage.UNIFORM | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
      });
      this.device.queue.writeBuffer(buffer, 0, data);
      this.filterAxis.push(buffer);
      this.filterGroups.push(
        this.device.createBindGroup({
          layout: this.filterPipeline.getBindGroupLayout(0),
          entries: [
            { binding: 0, resource: this.depths[source].createView() },
            { binding: 1, resource: this.pigments[source].createView() },
            { binding: 2, resource: this.depths[target].createView() },
            { binding: 3, resource: this.pigments[target].createView() },
            { binding: 4, resource: { buffer } },
          ],
        }),
      );
    }
    this.lightingGroup = this.device.createBindGroup({
      layout: this.lightingPipeline.getBindGroupLayout(0),
      entries: [
        { binding: 0, resource: this.depths[2].createView() },
        { binding: 1, resource: this.pigments[2].createView() },
        { binding: 2, resource: { buffer: this.cameraUniform } },
      ],
    });
    this.postGroup = this.device.createBindGroup({
      layout: this.postPipeline.getBindGroupLayout(0),
      entries: [
        { binding: 0, resource: this.hdr.createView() },
        { binding: 1, resource: this.depths[2].createView() },
        { binding: 2, resource: this.sampler },
        { binding: 3, resource: { buffer: this.postUniform } },
      ],
    });
    this.estimatedBytes = estimate;
  }
  camera() {
    const s = this.settings;
    const eye = [
        this.distance * Math.cos(this.pitch) * Math.sin(this.yaw),
        this.distance * Math.sin(this.pitch),
        this.distance * Math.cos(this.pitch) * Math.cos(this.yaw),
      ],
      forward = normalize(eye.map((x) => -x)),
      right = normalize(cross(forward, [0, 1, 0])),
      up = cross(right, forward);
    const data = [
      ...eye,
      0,
      ...right,
      0,
      ...up,
      0,
      ...forward,
      Math.tan(0.7 / 2),
      this.width,
      this.height,
      s.radius * (8192 / s.count) ** (1 / 3),
      0,
      s.roughness,
      s.absorption,
      s.sss,
      s.film,
      0,
      0,
      0.35,
      s.iridescence,
      0,
      0,
      0,
      0,
    ];
    this.device.queue.writeBuffer(
      this.cameraUniform,
      0,
      new Float32Array(data),
    );
    this.device.queue.writeBuffer(
      this.postUniform,
      0,
      new Float32Array([
        this.width,
        this.height,
        this.tick,
        0,
        s.focus,
        s.aperture,
        s.bloom,
        s.exposure,
        0.5,
        0.004,
        0,
        0,
      ]),
    );
  }
  render(encoder) {
    const attachments = [
      {
        view: this.depths[0].createView(),
        clearValue: { r: 10, g: 0, b: 0, a: 0 },
        loadOp: "clear",
        storeOp: "store",
      },
    ];
    let pass = encoder.beginRenderPass({
      colorAttachments: attachments,
      depthStencilAttachment: {
        view: this.hardwareDepth.createView(),
        depthClearValue: 1,
        depthLoadOp: "clear",
        depthStoreOp: "store",
      },
    });
    pass.setPipeline(this.depthPipeline);
    pass.setBindGroup(0, this.surfaceGroups[this.current]);
    pass.draw(6, this.settings.count);
    pass.end();
    pass = encoder.beginRenderPass({
      colorAttachments: [
        {
          view: this.pigments[0].createView(),
          clearValue: { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, a: 0 },
          loadOp: "clear",
          storeOp: "store",
        },
      ],
    });
    pass.setPipeline(this.thicknessPipeline);
    pass.setBindGroup(0, this.surfaceGroups[this.current]);
    pass.draw(6, this.settings.count);
    pass.end();
    for (let axis = 0; axis < this.filterGroups.length; axis++) {
      const p = encoder.beginComputePass();
      p.setPipeline(this.filterPipeline);
      p.setBindGroup(0, this.filterGroups[axis]);
      p.dispatchWorkgroups(
        Math.ceil(this.width / 8),
        Math.ceil(this.height / 8),
      );
      p.end();
    }
    pass = encoder.beginRenderPass({
      colorAttachments: [
        {
          view: this.hdr.createView(),
          clearValue: { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, a: 1 },
          loadOp: "clear",
          storeOp: "store",
        },
      ],
    });
    pass.setPipeline(this.lightingPipeline);
    pass.setBindGroup(0, this.lightingGroup);
    pass.draw(3);
    pass.end();
    const target = this.context.getCurrentTexture();
    pass = encoder.beginRenderPass({
      colorAttachments: [
        {
          view: target.createView(),
          clearValue: { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, a: 1 },
          loadOp: "clear",
          storeOp: "store",
        },
      ],
    });
    pass.setPipeline(this.postPipeline);
    pass.setBindGroup(0, this.postGroup);
    pass.draw(3);
    pass.end();
    return target;
  }
  async readState() {
    const size = this.settings.count * 48,
      buffer = this.device.createBuffer({
        size,
        usage: GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST | GPUBufferUsage.MAP_READ,
      });
    const encoder = this.device.createCommandEncoder();
    encoder.copyBufferToBuffer(
      this.particles[this.current],
      0,
      buffer,
      0,
      size,
    );
    this.device.queue.submit([encoder.finish()]);
    try {
      await buffer.mapAsync(GPUMapMode.READ);
      const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer.getMappedRange()).slice();
      return bytes;
    } finally {
      buffer.unmap();
      buffer.destroy();
    }
  }
  async exportState(includeData) {
    if (!includeData && this.tick > 20000)
      throw Error(
        "Exportação de semente limitada a 20.000 ticks; exporte o checkpoint completo.",
      );
    const bytes = await this.readState();
    const payload = {
      protocol: "NOVA-CHROMATIC-v1",
      engine_sha256: SCENE.engine_sha256,
      settings: clone(this.settings),
      initial: clone(this.initial),
      matrix: this.matrix.slice(),
      palette: this.palette.slice(),
      tick: this.tick,
      timeline: clone(this.events),
      camera: { yaw: this.yaw, pitch: this.pitch, distance: this.distance },
      state_sha256: await sha(bytes),
      reproducibility: "f32 GPU; neighbor ordering is not bit-portable",
      science: "visual/synthetic state; not a verified physical truth",
    };
    if (includeData) payload.data = toBase64(bytes);
    download(
      JSON.stringify(payload),
      `nova-${this.settings.seed}-${this.tick}-${includeData ? "checkpoint" : "seed"}.json`,
    );
  }
  async importState(file) {
    if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) throw Error("Arquivo maior que 8 MiB.");
    const saved = JSON.parse(await file.text());
    if (
      saved.protocol !== "NOVA-CHROMATIC-v1" ||
      saved.engine_sha256 !== SCENE.engine_sha256 ||
      !Number.isInteger(saved.tick) ||
      saved.tick < 0 ||
      saved.tick > 0xffffffff ||
      (!saved.data && saved.tick > 20000) ||
      !/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(saved.state_sha256)
    )
      throw Error("Motor, protocolo ou relógio incompatível.");
    validateSettings(saved.settings);
    validateSettings(saved.initial);
    if (
      saved.initial.count !== saved.settings.count ||
      saved.initial.seed !== saved.settings.seed ||
      saved.initial.preset !== saved.settings.preset ||
      saved.initial.damping !== saved.settings.damping ||
      saved.initial.iterations !== saved.settings.iterations
    )
      throw Error("Genesis inconsistente.");
    checkMatrix(saved.matrix);
    if (
      !Array.isArray(saved.palette) ||
      saved.palette.length !== 8 ||
      saved.palette.some((c) => !/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(c))
    )
      throw Error("Paleta inválida.");
    if (
      !Array.isArray(saved.timeline) ||
      saved.timeline.length > 4096 ||
      saved.timeline.some((e) => e.tick > saved.tick)
    )
      throw Error("Histórico inválido.");
    this.validateEvents(saved.timeline, saved.initial);
    if (saved.camera) {
      const { yaw, pitch, distance } = saved.camera;
      if (
        ![yaw, pitch, distance].every(Number.isFinite) ||
        Math.abs(yaw) > 1e4 ||
        Math.abs(pitch) > 1.4 ||
        distance < 1.5 ||
        distance > 7
      )
        throw Error("Câmera inválida.");
    }
    if (saved.data) {
      if (typeof saved.data !== "string" || saved.data.length > 6400000)
        throw Error("Dados fora do orçamento.");
      const raw = Uint8Array.from(atob(saved.data), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
      if (
        raw.length !== saved.settings.count * 48 ||
        (await sha(raw)) !== saved.state_sha256
      )
        throw Error("Checkpoint corrompido.");
      const data = new Float32Array(raw.buffer);
      for (let i = 0; i < saved.settings.count; i++) {
        const p = i * 12;
        if (
          !data.subarray(p, p + 12).every(Number.isFinite) ||
          [0, 1, 2, 8, 9, 10].some((k) => Math.abs(data[p + k]) > 1.001) ||
          [4, 5, 6].some((k) => Math.abs(data[p + k]) > 5) ||
          !Number.isInteger(data[p + 3]) ||
          data[p + 3] < 0 ||
          data[p + 3] > 7 ||
          data[p + 7] < 0 ||
          data[p + 7] > 1
        )
          throw Error("Estado de partícula inválido.");
      }
      saved.decoded = data;
    }
    await this.reset(saved.settings, saved);
    if (saved.camera) {
      const { yaw, pitch, distance } = saved.camera;
      if (
        ![yaw, pitch, distance].every(Number.isFinite) ||
        Math.abs(yaw) > 1e4 ||
        Math.abs(pitch) > 1.4 ||
        distance < 1.5 ||
        distance > 7
      )
        throw Error("Câmera inválida.");
      this.yaw = yaw;
      this.pitch = pitch;
      this.distance = distance;
    }
  }
  async screenshot(target, encoder) {
    const stride = Math.ceil((this.width * 4) / 256) * 256;
    const buffer = this.device.createBuffer({
      size: stride * this.height,
      usage: GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST | GPUBufferUsage.MAP_READ,
    });
    encoder.copyTextureToBuffer(
      { texture: target },
      { buffer, bytesPerRow: stride },
      [this.width, this.height],
    );
    return { buffer, stride, width: this.width, height: this.height };
  }
  async finishScreenshot(capture) {
    const { buffer, stride, width, height } = capture;
    try {
      await buffer.mapAsync(GPUMapMode.READ);
      const data = new Uint8Array(buffer.getMappedRange());
      const pixels = new Uint8ClampedArray(width * height * 4);
      for (let y = 0; y < height; y++)
        pixels.set(
          data.subarray(y * stride, y * stride + width * 4),
          y * width * 4,
        );
      if (this.format.startsWith("bgra"))
        for (let i = 0; i < pixels.length; i += 4) {
          const r = pixels[i];
          pixels[i] = pixels[i + 2];
          pixels[i + 2] = r;
        }
      const out = new OffscreenCanvas(width, height);
      out
        .getContext("2d")
        .putImageData(new ImageData(pixels, width, height), 0, 0);
      download(
        await out.convertToBlob({ type: "image/png" }),
        `nova-${this.tick}.png`,
      );
    } finally {
      buffer.unmap();
      buffer.destroy();
    }
  }
  async frame(time) {
    if (!this.particles) return;
    this.resize();
    this.camera();
    const encoder = this.device.createCommandEncoder();
    let steps = 0;
    if (this.running) {
      if (this.replay)
        steps = Math.min(
          this.settings.count > 32768 ? 1 : 2,
          this.replay.target - this.tick,
        );
      else {
        this.accumulator = Math.min(
          this.accumulator + Math.min((time - this.lastTime) / 1000, 0.05),
          0.05,
        );
        steps = Math.min(2, Math.floor(this.accumulator * 120));
        this.accumulator -= steps / 120;
      }
    }
    this.lastTime = time;
    // Each step has its own submission because queue.writeBuffer must precede the
    // dispatch using that step's uniform values. A frame never queues unbounded work.
    for (let k = 0; k < steps; k++) {
      const stepEncoder = this.device.createCommandEncoder();
      this.step(stepEncoder);
      this.device.queue.submit([stepEncoder.finish()]);
    }
    const target = this.render(encoder);
    let capture = null;
    if (this.capture) {
      capture = await this.screenshot(target, encoder);
      this.capture = false;
    }
    const readStats = time - this.lastStats > 1000;
    if (readStats)
      encoder.copyBufferToBuffer(this.statsBuffer, 0, this.statsRead, 0, 16);
    this.device.queue.submit([encoder.finish()]);
    await this.device.queue.onSubmittedWorkDone();
    if (capture) await this.finishScreenshot(capture);
    if (readStats) {
      await this.statsRead.mapAsync(GPUMapMode.READ);
      const metrics = new Uint32Array(this.statsRead.getMappedRange()).slice();
      this.statsRead.unmap();
      this.statusMetrics = {
        truncated_queries: metrics[0],
        speed_clamps: metrics[1],
        max_density: metrics[2] / 1000,
        correction_clamps: metrics[3],
      };
      this.fps = (this.frameCount * 1000) / Math.max(1, time - this.lastStats);
      this.frameCount = 0;
      this.lastStats = time;
    }
    $("status").textContent =
      `${this.replay ? "REPRODUZINDO · " : ""}${this.settings.count.toLocaleString("pt-BR")} partículas · tick ${this.tick} · ${this.width} × ${this.height} · ${this.fps.toFixed(0)} FPS nesta sessão${this.statusMetrics.truncated_queries ? "\nVizinhança limitada: aproximação ativa; veja diagnósticos." : ""}`;
    $("stats").textContent = JSON.stringify(
      {
        backend: "browser WebGPU",
        estimated_gpu_mib: Math.round(this.estimatedBytes / 1024 ** 2),
        neighbor_query_limit: this.settings.count > 32768 ? 512 : 2048,
        fluid_iterations: Math.min(
          this.settings.iterations,
          this.settings.count > 32768 ? 1 : 4,
        ),
        ...this.statusMetrics,
      },
      null,
      2,
    );
    this.frameCount++;
    if (this.replay && this.tick >= this.replay.target) {
      while (
        this.eventCursor < this.events.length &&
        this.events[this.eventCursor].tick === this.tick
      )
        this.applyEvent(this.events[this.eventCursor++], false);
      const actual = await sha(await this.readState());
      $("status").textContent +=
        actual === this.replay.expected
          ? "\nHash de estado reproduzido."
          : "\nHash divergente: a execução GPU não foi idêntica.";
      this.replay = null;
      this.running = false;
      this.syncUI();
      $("pause").textContent = "Continuar";
    }
  }
  record() {
    if (this.recording) {
      this.recording.stop();
      return;
    }
    if (!this.canvas.captureStream || typeof MediaRecorder === "undefined")
      throw Error("Gravação WebM indisponível neste navegador.");
    const mime = MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported("video/webm;codecs=vp9")
      ? "video/webm;codecs=vp9"
      : "video/webm";
    const stream = this.canvas.captureStream(30),
      recorder = new MediaRecorder(stream, {
        mimeType: mime,
        videoBitsPerSecond: 8000000,
      });
    let chunks = [],
      size = 0;
    recorder.ondataavailable = (e) => {
      chunks.push(e.data);
      size += e.data.size;
      if (size > 60 * 1024 ** 2 && recorder.state === "recording")
        recorder.stop();
    };
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
      if (recorder.state === "recording") recorder.stop();
    }, 30000);
    recorder.onstop = () => {
      clearTimeout(timeout);
      download(new Blob(chunks, { type: mime }), `nova-${this.tick}.webm`);
      stream.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
      this.recording = null;
      $("record").textContent = "Gravar WebM";
    };
    this.recording = recorder;
    recorder.start(1000);
    $("record").textContent = "Parar gravação";
  }
}
let app;
async function launch() {
  if (new Uint8Array(new Uint32Array([1]).buffer)[0] !== 1)
    throw Error("Este formato requer f32 little-endian.");
  if (!navigator.gpu)
    throw Error(
      "WebGPU indisponível. Use um navegador com WebGPU em localhost/HTTPS. O módulo CPU Particle Life continua disponível pela CLI.",
    );
  const adapter = await navigator.gpu.requestAdapter({
    powerPreference: "high-performance",
  });
  if (!adapter) throw Error("Nenhum adaptador WebGPU disponível.");
  const device = await adapter.requestDevice();
  const canvas = $("universe"),
    context = canvas.getContext("webgpu"),
    format = navigator.gpu.getPreferredCanvasFormat();
  context.configure({
    device,
    format,
    alphaMode: "opaque",
    usage: GPUTextureUsage.RENDER_ATTACHMENT | GPUTextureUsage.COPY_SRC,
  });
  app = new Chromatic(device, context, format);
  app.adapter = adapter; // Retain the adapter for the full lifetime of the device.
  device.addEventListener("uncapturederror", (e) => app.error(e.error));
  device.lost.then((info) => {
    app.running = false;
    app.dead = true;
    app.error(Error("Dispositivo GPU perdido: " + info.message));
  });
  await app.pipelines();
  const palette = $("palette");
  COLORS.forEach((c, i) => {
    const input = document.createElement("input");
    input.type = "color";
    input.value = c;
    input.id = "color" + i;
    input.oninput = () =>
      app.enqueue(() =>
        app.applyEvent({ kind: "palette", index: i, value: input.value }),
      );
    palette.append(input);
  });
  for (let i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
    const b = document.createElement("button");
    b.id = "m" + i;
    b.onclick = (e) =>
      app.enqueue(() =>
        app.applyEvent({
          kind: "matrix",
          index: i,
          value: Math.max(
            -1,
            Math.min(
              1,
              Math.round((app.matrix[i] + (e.shiftKey ? -0.2 : 0.2)) * 10) / 10,
            ),
          ),
        }),
      );
    $("matrix").append(b);
  }
  let settings = {
    ...PRESETS[SCENE.preset || "ink"],
    preset: SCENE.preset || "ink",
    seed: String(SCENE.seed || "42"),
    count: SCENE.count || 8192,
    quality: SCENE.quality || "balanced",
    radius: 0.043,
    exposure: 1,
    bloom: 0.3,
    focus: 3.2,
    aperture: 0.8,
    planar: false,
  };
  await app.reset(settings);
  const reset = () => {
    const preset = $("preset").value;
    const next = {
      ...app.settings,
      ...PRESETS[preset],
      preset,
      seed: $("seed").value,
      count: Number($("count").value),
      quality: $("quality").value,
    };
    return app.enqueue(() => app.reset(next));
  };
  $("reset").onclick = reset;
  $("planar").onchange = () => {
    const value = $("planar").value === "1";
    app.enqueue(() =>
      app.applyEvent({ kind: "parameter", name: "planar", value }),
    );
  };
  for (const name of ["preset", "count"]) $(name).onchange = reset;
  $("quality").onchange = () =>
    app.enqueue(() => {
      app.settings.quality = $("quality").value;
      app.width = 0;
    });
  $("seed").onchange = reset;
  for (const name of ["strength", "flow"])
    $(name).oninput = () => {
      const value = Number($(name).value);
      app.enqueue(() => app.applyEvent({ kind: "parameter", name, value }));
    };
  for (const name of [
    "radius",
    "roughness",
    "absorption",
    "sss",
    "film",
    "iridescence",
    "exposure",
    "bloom",
    "focus",
    "aperture",
  ])
    $(name).oninput = () => {
      const value = Number($(name).value);
      app.enqueue(() => {
        app.settings[name] = value;
        app.labels();
        if (name === "radius") app.width = 0;
      });
    };
  $("pause").onclick = () =>
    app.enqueue(() => {
      app.running = !app.running;
      app.accumulator = 0;
      $("pause").textContent = app.running ? "Pausar" : "Continuar";
    });
  $("snapshot").onclick = () => {
    app.capture = true;
  };
  $("record").onclick = () => {
    try {
      app.record();
    } catch (e) {
      app.error(e);
    }
  };
  $("saveSeed").onclick = () => app.enqueue(() => app.exportState(false));
  $("saveState").onclick = () => app.enqueue(() => app.exportState(true));
  $("load").onclick = () => $("file").click();
  $("file").onchange = () => {
    const f = $("file").files[0];
    if (f) app.enqueue(() => app.importState(f));
  };
  let drag = null;
  canvas.onpointerdown = (e) => {
    drag = [e.clientX, e.clientY];
    canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
  };
  canvas.onpointermove = (e) => {
    if (drag) {
      app.yaw += (e.clientX - drag[0]) * 0.006;
      app.pitch = Math.max(
        -1.4,
        Math.min(1.4, app.pitch + (e.clientY - drag[1]) * 0.006),
      );
      drag = [e.clientX, e.clientY];
    }
  };
  canvas.onpointerup = () => (drag = null);
  canvas.onwheel = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    app.distance = Math.max(1.5, Math.min(7, app.distance + e.deltaY * 0.002));
  };
  addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
    if (e.target.matches("input,select")) return;
    if (e.code === "Space") {
      e.preventDefault();
      $("pause").click();
    }
    if (e.key.toLowerCase() === "s") app.capture = true;
  });
  const frame = async (time) => {
    try {
      if (!app.dead) await app.enqueue(() => app.frame(time));
    } catch {
    } finally {
      requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    }
  };
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}
launch().catch((error) => {
  $("error").textContent = error.message;
  $("status").textContent = "Inicialização interrompida.";
  console.error(error);
});


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/visuals/cinematic.py
========================================================================================
"""Self-contained Particle Life/WebGPU liquid studio, served on loopback or exported."""

import hashlib
import webbrowser
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from pathlib import Path

from nova.bioma.determinism import SplitMix64, canonical_json

SHADERS = (
    "particle_life",
    "fluid_surface",
    "fluid_filter",
    "fluid_lighting",
    "fluid_post",
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CinematicConfig:
    preset: str = "ink"
    count: int = 8192
    seed: int = 42
    quality: str = "balanced"
    max_memory_mib: int = 512

    def __post_init__(self):
        if (
            self.preset not in ("ink", "life", "iridescent", "nebula")
            or not 256 <= self.count <= 100000
            or self.quality not in ("draft", "balanced", "high", "ultra")
            or not 64 <= self.max_memory_mib <= 2048
        ):
            raise ValueError("cinematic scene/pixel/particle budget exceeded")
        SplitMix64(self.seed)


def build_html(config=None):
    config = config or CinematicConfig()
    root = Path(__file__).parent
    engine = (root / "cinematic.js").read_text()
    sources = {
        name: (root / "shaders" / (name + ".wgsl")).read_text() for name in SHADERS
    }
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    for name, value in [("cinematic.js", engine), *sorted(sources.items())]:
        digest.update(name.encode())
        digest.update(value.encode())
    settings = {
        **asdict(config),
        "seed": str(config.seed),
        "engine_sha256": digest.hexdigest(),
    }
    html = (
        (root / "cinematic.html")
        .read_text()
        .replace(
            "/*SCENE_CONFIG*/{}",
            canonical_json(settings).decode().replace("<", "\\u003c"),
        )
        .replace(
            "/*SHADER_SOURCES*/{}",
            canonical_json(sources).decode().replace("<", "\\u003c"),
        )
        .replace("/*ENGINE_CODE*/", engine)
    )
    return html, settings


def export(config, path):
    html, settings = build_html(config)
    target = Path(path)
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    target.write_text(html)
    return {
        "html": str(target),
        "scene": settings,
        "runtime_executed": False,
        "dependencies": "browser WebGPU only; no CDN",
        "scientific_truth_proved": False,
    }


def serve(config=None, *, port=8768, open_browser=False):
    if not 1 <= port <= 65535:
        raise ValueError("invalid local port")
    html, settings = build_html(config)
    body = html.encode()

    class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
        def log_message(self, *args):
            pass

        def do_GET(self):
            if self.headers.get("Host") not in (
                f"127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}",
                f"localhost:{self.server.server_port}",
            ):
                self.send_error(403)
                return
            if self.path not in ("/", "/scene.json"):
                self.send_error(404)
                return
            data = canonical_json(settings) if self.path == "/scene.json" else body
            self.send_response(200)
            self.send_header(
                "Content-Type",
                "application/json"
                if self.path == "/scene.json"
                else "text/html; charset=utf-8",
            )
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data)))
            self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
            self.send_header("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
            self.send_header("Referrer-Policy", "no-referrer")
            self.send_header(
                "Content-Security-Policy",
                "default-src 'none'; script-src 'unsafe-inline'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; img-src blob: data:; connect-src 'self'; object-src 'none'; base-uri 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'",
            )
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(data)

    class Server(ThreadingHTTPServer):
        daemon_threads = True

        def get_request(self):
            connection, address = super().get_request()
            connection.settimeout(10)
            return connection, address

    server = Server(("127.0.0.1", port), Handler)
    url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_port}"
    print(f"NOVA Chromatic: {url}", flush=True)
    if open_browser:
        webbrowser.open(url)
    try:
        server.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        pass
    finally:
        server.server_close()

"""Launch the local WebGPU/WebGL viewer with optional planetary orchestration."""

import webbrowser
from .server import serve


def start(
    ecosystem, *, port=8765, renderer="webgl", planetary=None, open_browser=False
):
    if renderer not in ("webgl", "webgpu"):
        raise ValueError("unknown browser renderer")
    if planetary:
        planetary.start()
    try:
        serve(
            ecosystem,
            port=port,
            lock=planetary.lock if planetary else None,
            atlas=planetary.atlas if planetary else None,
            renderer=renderer,
            control=planetary.control if planetary else None,
            on_ready=webbrowser.open if open_browser else None,
        )
    finally:
        if planetary:
            planetary.close()


def export_atlas(data, path):
    from pathlib import Path
    from nova.bioma.determinism import canonical_json

    template = Path(__file__).with_name("science_atlas.html").read_text()
    html = template.replace(
        "/*ATLAS_DATA*/null", canonical_json(data).decode().replace("<", "\\u003c")
    )
    target = Path(path)
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    target.write_text(html)
    return str(target)

ARQUIVO: nova/visuals/renderer.py

"""Bounded volume rendering; appearance is illustrative, not a light solver."""

from __future__ import annotations
import base64
import json
from pathlib import Path
import struct
import zlib
import numpy as np


def write_png(path, rgb):
    pixels = np.asarray(rgb, dtype=np.uint8)
    if pixels.ndim != 3 or pixels.shape[2] != 3:
        raise ValueError("RGB image required")
    h, w, _ = pixels.shape

    def chunk(kind, data):
        return (
            struct.pack(">I", len(data))
            + kind
            + data
            + struct.pack(">I", zlib.crc32(kind + data) & 0xFFFFFFFF)
        )

    raw = b"".join(b"\0" + row.tobytes() for row in pixels)
    data = (
        b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n"
        + chunk(b"IHDR", struct.pack(">IIBBBBB", w, h, 8, 2, 0, 0, 0))
        + chunk(b"IDAT", zlib.compress(raw))
        + chunk(b"IEND", b"")
    )
    target = Path(path)
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    target.write_bytes(data)


class FieldRenderer:
    @staticmethod
    def density(ecosystem):
        world = ecosystem.world
        mode = world.config.mode
        if mode == "conway":
            field = world.life.astype(float)
        elif mode == "wave":
            field = np.clip(np.abs(world.wave), 0, 1)
        elif mode == "nca":
            field = np.clip(world.channels[0], 0, 1)
        elif mode == "particles":
            field = np.zeros(world.shape)
            coords = np.floor(world.particle_positions).astype(int) % np.asarray(
                world.shape
            )
            np.add.at(field, tuple(coords.T), 1.0)
            field = np.clip(field, 0, 1)
        else:
            field = np.clip(world.v * 3, 0, 1)
        field = field.copy()
        # Developed occupancy follows the organism's point body; no mesh or
        # resolved soft-body mechanics is implied by the rendered appearance.
        for organism in ecosystem.organisms:
            body = organism.body["density"]
            indices = np.argwhere(body > 0.1)
            if len(indices):
                offsets = (indices / body.shape[0] - 0.5) * 4
                positions = np.floor(
                    organism.position + offsets[:, : world.ndim]
                ).astype(int) % np.asarray(world.shape)
                np.maximum.at(
                    field,
                    tuple(positions.T),
                    np.minimum(1.0, body[tuple(indices.T)] + 0.25),
                )
        if field.ndim == 2:
            field = np.repeat(field[None], 4, axis=0)
        return np.ascontiguousarray(field, dtype=float)

    @classmethod
    def frame(cls, ecosystem):
        density = cls.density(ecosystem)
        image = np.rint(density * 255).astype(np.uint8)
        topology = (
            ecosystem.organisms[0].genome.develop_brain()
            if ecosystem.organisms
            else None
        )
        return {
            "shape": list(image.shape),
            "volume": base64.b64encode(image.tobytes()).decode(),
            "metrics": ecosystem.metrics(),
            "communications": ecosystem.interpreter.report()
            if hasattr(ecosystem, "interpreter")
            else None,
            "mode": ecosystem.world.config.mode,
            "architecture": None
            if topology is None
            else {
                "coordinates": topology["coordinates"].tolist(),
                "types": topology["types"].tolist(),
                "families": topology["families"].tolist(),
                "edges": [
                    [int(i), int(j), float(topology["weights"][i, j])]
                    for i, j in zip(*np.nonzero(topology["weights"]))
                ],
            },
        }

    @classmethod
    def render(cls, ecosystem, output, *, kind="volume", resolution=384):
        if not 32 <= resolution <= 1024:
            raise ValueError("image resolution out of bounds")
        density = cls.density(ecosystem)
        if kind == "slice":
            scalar = density[len(density) // 2]
            rgb = np.stack((scalar, np.sqrt(scalar) * 0.7, 1 - scalar), -1)
        elif kind == "volume":
            h, w = density.shape[1:]
            transmission = np.ones((h, w))
            rgb = np.zeros((h, w, 3))
            gradient = np.gradient(density)
            normal = np.sqrt(sum(g * g for g in gradient)) + 1e-8
            for z in range(len(density)):
                d = density[z]
                alpha = 1 - np.exp(-d * 6 / len(density))
                light = 0.25 + 0.75 * np.abs(gradient[0][z]) / normal[z]
                color = (
                    np.stack((0.15 + 0.8 * d, 0.35 + 0.6 * d, 0.8 - 0.35 * d), -1)
                    * light[..., None]
                )
                rgb += transmission[..., None] * alpha[..., None] * color
                transmission *= 1 - alpha
            rgb += transmission[..., None] * np.array([0.015, 0.025, 0.06])
        else:
            raise ValueError("unknown renderer")
        y = np.linspace(0, rgb.shape[0] - 1, resolution).astype(int)
        x = np.linspace(0, rgb.shape[1] - 1, resolution).astype(int)
        write_png(
            output,
            np.rint(np.clip(rgb[y[:, None], x[None, :]], 0, 1) * 255).astype(np.uint8),
        )
        return {"image": str(output), "renderer": kind, "physical_radiometry": False}

    @classmethod
    def export_html(cls, ecosystem, path):
        template = Path(__file__).with_name("viewer.html").read_text()
        frame = json.dumps(cls.frame(ecosystem), allow_nan=False).replace(
            "<", "\\u003c"
        )
        html = template.replace("/*INITIAL_FRAME*/null", frame).replace(
            "/*LIVE_CONFIG*/null", "null"
        )
        target = Path(path)
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        target.write_text(html)
        return {"viewer": str(target), "offline": True}

ARQUIVO: nova/visuals/server.py

"""Loopback-only interactive viewer. Simulation mutations require a session token."""

from __future__ import annotations
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from pathlib import Path
import secrets
import threading
from nova.bioma.determinism import canonical_json, strict_json
from .renderer import FieldRenderer


def serve(
    ecosystem,
    *,
    port=8765,
    lock=None,
    atlas=None,
    renderer="webgl",
    control=None,
    on_ready=None,
):
    from nova.bioma.presets import create_ecosystem

    token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
    lock = lock or threading.RLock()
    state = {"ecosystem": ecosystem}

    class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
        def log_message(self, *args):
            pass

        def reply(self, status, data, kind="application/json"):
            self.send_response(status)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", kind)
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data)))
            self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
            self.send_header("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(data)

        def allowed(self):
            return self.headers.get("Host") in (
                f"127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_port}",
                f"localhost:{self.server.server_port}",
            )

        def do_GET(self):
            if not self.allowed():
                return self.reply(403, b"{}")
            if self.path == "/frame":
                with lock:
                    frame = FieldRenderer.frame(state["ecosystem"])
                return self.reply(200, canonical_json(frame))
            if self.path in ("/atlas", "/atlas.json"):
                data = (
                    atlas()
                    if atlas
                    else {
                        "status": "local_only",
                        "simulation": state["ecosystem"].metrics(),
                        "ancestry": {"lineages": []},
                    }
                )
                if self.path == "/atlas.json":
                    return self.reply(200, canonical_json(data))
                page = (
                    Path(__file__)
                    .with_name("science_atlas.html")
                    .read_text()
                    .replace(
                        "/*ATLAS_DATA*/null",
                        canonical_json(data).decode().replace("<", "\\u003c"),
                    )
                    .replace("/*ATLAS_LIVE*/false", "true")
                )
                return self.reply(200, page.encode(), "text/html; charset=utf-8")
            if self.path != "/":
                return self.reply(404, b"{}")
            with lock:
                frame = FieldRenderer.frame(state["ecosystem"])
            html = (
                Path(__file__)
                .with_name(
                    "webgpu_canvas.html" if renderer == "webgpu" else "viewer.html"
                )
                .read_text()
                .replace(
                    "/*INITIAL_FRAME*/null",
                    canonical_json(frame).decode().replace("<", "\\u003c"),
                )
                .replace(
                    "/*LIVE_CONFIG*/null",
                    canonical_json(
                        {"token": token, "autonomous": atlas is not None}
                    ).decode(),
                )
            )
            self.reply(200, html.encode(), "text/html; charset=utf-8")

        def do_POST(self):
            if not self.allowed() or not secrets.compare_digest(
                self.headers.get("X-Nova-Session", ""), token
            ):
                return self.reply(403, b"{}")
            try:
                length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", "0"))
                if not 0 < length <= 4096:
                    raise ValueError("request body size out of range")
                request = strict_json(self.rfile.read(length), 4096)
                with lock:
                    if self.path == "/control":
                        if control is None:
                            raise ValueError("autonomous controls unavailable")
                        control(request["paused"])
                    elif self.path == "/step":
                        ticks = request.get("ticks", 1)
                        if not isinstance(ticks, int) or not 1 <= ticks <= 8:
                            raise ValueError("tick limit exceeded")
                        state["ecosystem"].step(ticks)
                    elif self.path == "/reset":
                        if atlas is not None or not hasattr(
                            state["ecosystem"], "genesis_genomes"
                        ):
                            raise ValueError(
                                "reset is disabled for orchestrated/native sessions; restart with the desired preset"
                            )
                        old = state["ecosystem"]
                        state["ecosystem"] = create_ecosystem(
                            request["preset"],
                            seed=old.world.config.seed,
                            size=old.world.shape[0],
                        )
                    else:
                        return self.reply(404, b"{}")
                    frame = FieldRenderer.frame(state["ecosystem"])
                self.reply(200, canonical_json(frame))
            except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as error:
                self.reply(400, canonical_json({"error": str(error)}))

    class Server(ThreadingHTTPServer):
        daemon_threads = True

        def get_request(self):
            connection, address = super().get_request()
            connection.settimeout(10)
            return connection, address

    server = Server(("127.0.0.1", port), Handler)
    if on_ready:
        on_ready(f"http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_port}")
    print(
        f"NOVA Biocosm: http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_port} (Ctrl-C para encerrar)",
        flush=True,
    )
    try:
        server.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        pass
    finally:
        server.server_close()

ARQUIVO: nova/visuals/shaders/fluid_filter.wgsl

// Separable bilateral depth + guided pigment/thickness smoothing.
struct Filter {
    axis:vec4<i32>,values:vec4<f32>
}
@group(0) @binding(0) var depth:texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var pigment:texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(2) var output_depth:texture_storage_2d<r32float,write>;
@group(0) @binding(3) var output_pigment:texture_storage_2d<rgba16float,write>;
@group(0) @binding(4) var<uniform> cfg:Filter;
@compute @workgroup_size(8,8)
fn bilateral_main(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id:vec3<u32>){
    let p=vec2<i32>(id.xy);
    let size=vec2<i32>(textureDimensions(depth));
    if(any(p>=size)){
        return;
    }
    let center=textureLoad(depth,p,0).r;
    if(center>=9.99){
        textureStore(output_depth,p,vec4(10.));
        textureStore(output_pigment,p,vec4(0.));
        return;
    }
    var sum=0.;
    var z=0.;
    var color=vec4(0.);
    for(var k=-8;k<=8;k++){
        if(abs(k)>cfg.axis.z){
            continue;
        }
        let q=clamp(p+cfg.axis.xy*k,vec2(0),size-1);
        let d=textureLoad(depth,q,0).r;
        if(d>=9.99){
            continue;
        }
        let spatial=exp(-.5*f32(k*k)/cfg.values.x);
        let range=exp(-.5*(d-center)*(d-center)/cfg.values.y);
        let w=spatial*range;
        sum+=w;
        z+=w*d;
        color+=w*textureLoad(pigment,q,0);
    }
    textureStore(output_depth,p,vec4(z/max(sum,1e-8)));
    textureStore(output_pigment,p,color/max(sum,1e-8));
}


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/visuals/shaders/fluid_lighting.wgsl
========================================================================================
// GGX surface, Beer-Lambert thickness, approximate SSS/HG and thin-film tint.
struct Camera {
    eye:vec4<f32>,right:vec4<f32>,up:vec4<f32>,forward:vec4<f32>,view:vec4<f32>,material:vec4<f32>,light:vec4<f32>,post:vec4<f32>
}
@group(0) @binding(0) var depth:texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var pigment:texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> cam:Camera;
struct Quad {
    @builtin(position) pos:vec4<f32>,@location(0) uv:vec2<f32>
}
@vertex fn fullscreen(@builtin(vertex_index) i:u32)->Quad{
    var p=array<vec2<f32>,3>(vec2(-1.,-1.),vec2(3.,-1.),vec2(-1.,3.));
    var out:Quad;
    out.pos=vec4(p[i],0.,1.);
    out.uv=p[i]*vec2(.5,-.5)+.5;
    return out;
}
fn ray(p:vec2<f32>)->vec3<f32>{
    let uv=(p/cam.view.xy*2.-1.)*vec2(1.,-1.);
    return normalize(cam.forward.xyz+cam.right.xyz*uv.x*cam.forward.w*cam.view.x/cam.view.y+cam.up.xyz*uv.y*cam.forward.w);
}
fn position(p:vec2<i32>,fallback:f32)->vec3<f32>{
    let size=vec2<i32>(textureDimensions(depth));
    let q=clamp(p,vec2(0),size-1);
    var z=textureLoad(depth,q,0).r;
    if(z>=9.99){
        z=fallback;
    }
    let r=ray(vec2<f32>(q)+.5);
    return cam.eye.xyz+r*z/max(dot(r,cam.forward.xyz),.01);
}
fn environment(r:vec3<f32>)->vec3<f32>{
    let ceiling=pow(max(r.y,0.),5.);
    let panel=pow(max(dot(r,normalize(vec3(-.5,.7,.4))),0.),45.);
    return mix(vec3(.012,.018,.035),vec3(.15,.19,.25),r.y*.5+.5)+vec3(.9,.8,.7)*ceiling+vec3(5.,5.5,6.)*panel;
}
fn phase(cosine:f32,g:f32)->f32{
    return (1.-g*g)/(12.5663706*pow(max(1.+g*g-2.*g*cosine,.001),1.5));
}
fn brdf(n:vec3<f32>,v:vec3<f32>,l:vec3<f32>,albedo:vec3<f32>,F0:vec3<f32>)->vec3<f32>{
    let h=normalize(v+l);
    let nl=max(dot(n,l),0.);
    let nv=max(dot(n,v),.001);
    let nh=max(dot(n,h),0.);
    let vh=max(dot(v,h),0.);
    let a=max(.025,cam.material.x*cam.material.x);
    let a2=a*a;
    let denom=nh*nh*(a2-1.)+1.;
    let D=a2/(3.14159265*denom*denom);
    let k=(cam.material.x+1.)*(cam.material.x+1.)/8.;
    let G=nv/(nv*(1.-k)+k)*nl/max(nl*(1.-k)+k,.001);
    let F=F0+(1.-F0)*pow(1.-vh,5.);
    return ((1.-F)*albedo/3.14159265+D*G*F/max(4.*nl*nv,.001))*nl;
}
@fragment fn shade(in:Quad)->@location(0) vec4<f32>{
    let pixel=vec2<i32>(in.pos.xy);
    let z=textureLoad(depth,pixel,0).r;
    let r=ray(in.pos.xy);
    let background=environment(r)*.12+vec3(.008,.006,.018);
    if(z>=9.99){
        return vec4(background,1.);
    }
    let p=position(pixel,z);
    let xp=position(pixel+vec2(1,0),z)-p;
    let xm=p-position(pixel-vec2(1,0),z);
    let yp=position(pixel+vec2(0,1),z)-p;
    let ym=p-position(pixel-vec2(0,1),z);
    let dx=select(xm,xp,abs(dot(xp,cam.forward.xyz))<abs(dot(xm,cam.forward.xyz)));
    let dy=select(ym,yp,abs(dot(yp,cam.forward.xyz))<abs(dot(ym,cam.forward.xyz)));
    var n=normalize(cross(dx,dy)+vec3(1e-8));
    let v=normalize(cam.eye.xyz-p);
    if(dot(n,v)<0.){
        n=-n;
    }
    let raw=textureLoad(pigment,pixel,0);
    let thickness=clamp(raw.a,0.,4.);
    let albedo=clamp(raw.rgb/max(raw.a,.00001),vec3(.015),vec3(.98));
    let sigma=-log(albedo)*cam.material.y;
    let trans=exp(-sigma*thickness);
    let nv=max(dot(n,v),0.);
    let wavelength=vec3(650.,510.,475.);
    let film=.5+.5*cos(12.5663706*1.33*cam.material.w*max(.2,nv)/wavelength);
    let F0=mix(vec3(.035),vec3(.035)+.18*film,cam.light.w);
    var color=vec3(0.);
    let l1=normalize(vec3(-.6,.9,.8));
    let l2=normalize(vec3(.8,.1,-.6));
    let l3=normalize(vec3(-.4,-.5,-.8));
    color+=brdf(n,v,l1,albedo,F0)*vec3(4.,3.8,3.5);
    color+=brdf(n,v,l2,albedo,F0)*vec3(1.1,1.5,2.4);
    color+=brdf(n,v,l3,albedo,F0)*vec3(1.8,.6,1.2);
    let reflection=reflect(-v,n);
    let F=F0+(1.-F0)*pow(1.-nv,5.);
    color+=environment(reflection)*F;
    let back=pow(max(dot(-n,l2),0.),2.)+phase(dot(-v,l2),cam.light.z)*2.;
    color+=cam.material.z*(1.-trans)*albedo*(.4+back)*vec3(.9,1.1,1.5);
    let bend=r+(.18*(1.-trans.r))*n;
    let behind=environment(normalize(bend))*.2;
    color+=trans*behind*(1.-F);
    color+=.02*albedo*pow(1.-nv,3.);
    return vec4(max(color,vec3(0.)),1.);
}

// Bounded image-space lens/bloom; ACES fitted display curve, not full ACES OCIO.
struct Post {
    view:vec4<f32>,lens:vec4<f32>,style:vec4<f32>
}
@group(0) @binding(0) var hdr:texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var depth:texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(2) var linear:sampler;
@group(0) @binding(3) var<uniform> cfg:Post;
struct Quad {
    @builtin(position) pos:vec4<f32>,@location(0) uv:vec2<f32>
}
@vertex fn fullscreen(@builtin(vertex_index) i:u32)->Quad{
    var p=array<vec2<f32>,3>(vec2(-1.,-1.),vec2(3.,-1.),vec2(-1.,3.));
    var out:Quad;
    out.pos=vec4(p[i],0.,1.);
    out.uv=p[i]*vec2(.5,-.5)+.5;
    return out;
}
fn aces(x:vec3<f32>)->vec3<f32>{
    return clamp((x*(2.51*x+.03))/(x*(2.43*x+.59)+.14),vec3(0.),vec3(1.));
}
@fragment fn post(in:Quad)->@location(0) vec4<f32>{
    let uv=in.uv;
    let size=cfg.view.xy;
    let z=textureLoad(depth,vec2<i32>(clamp(in.pos.xy,vec2(0.),size-1.)),0).r;
    let coc=min(12.,cfg.lens.y*abs(z-cfg.lens.x)/max(z,.1));
    var color=textureSampleLevel(hdr,linear,uv,0.).rgb;
    var weight=1.;
    var bloom=vec3(0.);
    for(var i=0;i<16;i++){
        let a=f32(i)*2.399963;
        let radius=sqrt((f32(i)+.5)/16.);
        let direction=vec2(cos(a),sin(a));
        let aperture=direction*radius*coc/size;
        let sample=textureSampleLevel(hdr,linear,clamp(uv+aperture,vec2(0.),vec2(1.)),0.).rgb;
        if(coc>.5){
            color+=sample;
            weight+=1.;
        }
        let light=textureSampleLevel(hdr,linear,clamp(uv+direction*radius*24./size,vec2(0.),vec2(1.)),0.).rgb;
        bloom+=max(light-vec3(1.),vec3(0.))/16.;
    }
    color=color/weight+cfg.lens.z*bloom;
    let vignette=1.-cfg.style.x*dot(uv-.5,uv-.5);
    let noise=fract(sin(dot(in.pos.xy,vec2(12.9898,78.233))+cfg.view.z*.13)*43758.5453)-.5;
    let mapped=pow(aces(color*cfg.lens.w),vec3(1./2.2))*vignette+noise*cfg.style.y;
    return vec4(clamp(mapped,vec3(0.),vec3(1.)),1.);
}

ARQUIVO: nova/visuals/shaders/fluid_surface.wgsl
