import torch
from nova.jepa import JEPA
from nova.environments import LinearPoint, Pendulum, GridWorld


def test_target_stop_gradient_and_ema():
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    model = JEPA(6, 4, 2)
    old = [p.clone() for p in model.target.parameters()]
    loss, metrics = model.loss(
        torch.randn(32, 6), torch.randn(32, 2), torch.randn(32, 6)
    )
    loss.backward()
    assert all(p.grad is None for p in model.target.parameters())
    assert any(p.grad is not None for p in model.context.parameters())
    with torch.no_grad():
        for p in model.context.parameters():
            p.add_(0.1)
    model.target.update(model.context, 0.5)
    for before, after in zip(old, model.target.parameters()):
        torch.testing.assert_close(after, before + 0.05)
    assert metrics["latent_std"] > 0


def test_environments():
    for env, action in [(LinearPoint(), [0, 0]), (Pendulum(), [0]), (GridWorld(), 0)]:
        assert env.reset().ndim == 1
        for _ in range(100):
            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action)
            assert torch.isfinite(torch.as_tensor(obs)).all()


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: tests/test_memory_planning.py
========================================================================================
import numpy as np
from nova.memory import ModernHopfield, kwta, WorkingMemory, SemanticGraph
from nova.synapse import DiffusiveCascade, ThreeFactorRule
from nova.planning import LatentPlanner
from nova.neuromod import Neuromodulator


def test_retrieval_and_energy():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(1)
    patterns = kwta(rng.normal(size=(16, 128)), 0.05)
    patterns /= np.linalg.norm(patterns, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    h = ModernHopfield(patterns, beta=40)
    for pattern in patterns:
        q = pattern + rng.normal(0, 0.03, 128)
        result = h.retrieve(q)
        assert np.argmax(patterns @ result) == np.argmax(patterns @ pattern)
        assert h.energy(result) <= h.energy(q) + 1e-10


def test_cascade_against_analytic_and_mass():
    c = DiffusiveCascade(reservoirs=2, leak=0)
    c.state[0, 0] = 1
    expected = c.analytic(2.0)
    for _ in range(200):
        c.step(dt=0.01)
    np.testing.assert_allclose(c.state, expected, atol=1e-9)
    mass = c.state @ c.capacity
    c.consolidate(0.5)
    np.testing.assert_allclose(c.state @ c.capacity, mass)


def test_stdp_timing_and_modulation():
    rule = ThreeFactorRule(1, 1)
    rule.step([1], [0], 1)
    assert rule.step([0], [1], 1)[0, 0] > 0
    rule = ThreeFactorRule(1, 1)
    rule.step([0], [1], 1)
    assert rule.step([1], [0], 1)[0, 0] < 0
    assert np.all(rule.step([1], [1], 0) == 0)


def test_cem_and_ablation_signals():
    planner = LatentPlanner(lambda z, a: z + 0.2 * a, lambda z: (z * z).sum(1), seed=1)
    result = planner.plan(np.array([1.0]))
    assert result.action[0] < -0.5
    assert result.iterations == 4
    mod = Neuromodulator().update(2.0, reward=1.0, uncertainty=0.8)
    ablated = Neuromodulator(frozenset()).update(2.0, reward=1.0, uncertainty=0.8)
    assert mod.learning_rate(0.1) > ablated.learning_rate(0.1)
    assert mod.sensory_weight() > ablated.sensory_weight()


def test_bounded_working_memory_and_conflicts():
    memory = WorkingMemory()
    for _ in range(100):
        memory.update(np.ones(8))
    assert np.max(memory.state) <= 1
    graph = SemanticGraph()
    graph.add("a", "job", "x", "source1")
    graph.add("a", "job", "y", "source2")
    assert len(graph.query("a", "job")) == 2


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: tests/test_native.py
========================================================================================
import numpy as np
import pytest
from nova.core import TernaryLinear, fast_hadamard_transform, available

pytestmark = pytest.mark.skipif(
    not available(), reason="native extension not installed"
)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("shape", [(7, 5), (64, 16), (1, 1)])
def test_packed_equivalence(shape):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(8)
    w = rng.integers(-1, 2, shape, dtype=np.int8)
    x = rng.normal(size=(4, shape[0]))
    native = TernaryLinear(w)
    np.testing.assert_allclose(native(x), x @ w, atol=1e-12)
    assert native.nbytes == (w.size + 3) // 4
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        native(np.ones((2, shape[0] + 1)))


def test_native_hadamard():
    x = np.arange(16, dtype=float)
    np.testing.assert_allclose(fast_hadamard_transform(fast_hadamard_transform(x)), x)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        fast_hadamard_transform([1, 2, 3])


def test_gpu_kernel_when_available():
    torch = pytest.importorskip("torch")
    if not torch.cuda.is_available():
        pytest.skip("CUDA hardware unavailable")
    pytest.importorskip("triton")
    from nova.kernels.triton_ternary import pack_ternary, matmul

    x = torch.randn(4, 13, device="cuda")
    w = torch.randint(-1, 2, (13, 9), device="cuda")
    torch.testing.assert_close(
        matmul(x, pack_ternary(w), 9), x @ w.float(), atol=1e-5, rtol=1e-5
    )


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: tests/test_neuron_perception.py
========================================================================================
import numpy as np
import pytest
import torch
from nova.neuron import RateNeuron, AdExPopulation, HodgkinHuxleyCompartments
from nova.brain import Microcircuit
from nova.perception import (
    StreamingAudioEncoder,
    VisionEncoder,
    TextEncoder,
    SlotAttention,
    PredictiveHierarchy,
    MultimodalFusion,
)
from nova.core import available
from nova.synapse import DiffusiveCascade, NativeDiffusiveCascade


def test_adex_spikes_and_native_parity():
    py = AdExPopulation(20, seed=3)
    native = AdExPopulation(20, seed=3, backend="rust") if available() else None
    count = 0
    for _ in range(1000):
        spikes, state = py.step((500.0, 10.0))
        count += spikes.sum()
        if native:
            other, ns = native.step((500.0, 10.0))
            np.testing.assert_array_equal(spikes, other)
            for key in state:
                np.testing.assert_allclose(state[key], ns[key], atol=1e-10)
    assert count > 0 and np.isfinite(py.v).all()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        py.step((0, 0), dt=2)


def test_dale_and_n2():
    circuit = Microcircuit(100)
    assert circuit.check_dale()
    model = HodgkinHuxleyCompartments()
    spikes = 0
    for _ in range(1000):
        output, state = model.step((10.0, 0.0))
        spikes += output.sum()
    assert spikes > 0 and np.isfinite(state["v"]).all()


def test_n0_sparse_apical():
    model = RateNeuron(4, 20, k=0.1)
    out, state = model.step((np.ones(4), np.zeros(4)))
    assert np.count_nonzero(out) <= 2
    assert np.isfinite(out).all()


def test_streaming_chunk_equivalence():
    torch.manual_seed(4)
    model = StreamingAudioEncoder()
    signal = torch.randn(2048)
    expected = model(signal).detach()
    model.reset()
    result = torch.cat([model(signal[i : i + 117]) for i in range(0, len(signal), 117)])
    torch.testing.assert_close(expected, result, atol=1e-6, rtol=1e-5)


def test_perception_and_precision_relaxation():
    assert VisionEncoder()(torch.randn(2, 3, 32, 32)).shape == (2, 16)
    text = TextEncoder()
    assert text(["Olá", ""]).shape == (2, 16)
    tokens = torch.randn(2, 8, 16)
    slots = SlotAttention()(tokens)
    assert slots.shape == (2, 4, 16)
    model = PredictiveHierarchy()
    states, energy = model.infer(np.arange(8) / 8)
    assert energy[-1] < energy[0]
    fusion = MultimodalFusion()
    x = torch.zeros(2, 16)
    y = torch.ones(2, 16)
    torch.testing.assert_close(
        fusion([x, y], torch.tensor([[1.0, 3.0], [1.0, 3.0]])), torch.full_like(x, 0.75)
    )


def test_native_cascade_parity():
    if not available():
        pytest.skip("native extension unavailable")
    a = DiffusiveCascade(4)
    b = NativeDiffusiveCascade(4)
    for _ in range(40):
        a.step(0.2)
        b.step(0.2)
    np.testing.assert_allclose(a.state, b.state, atol=1e-12)


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: tests/test_protected.py
========================================================================================
import numpy as np
import pytest
from nova.continual import ProtectedLinear


def fixture(seed=4):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    q, _ = np.linalg.qr(rng.normal(size=(16, 16)))
    x = rng.normal(size=(40, 6)) @ q[:, :6].T
    layer = ProtectedLinear(16, 3, seed=seed)
    for _ in range(30):
        layer.step(x, np.ones((40, 3)), 0.03)
    return rng, layer, x


def test_retention_and_incremental_span():
    rng, layer, x = fixture()
    layer.consolidate(x[:20])
    layer.consolidate(x[20:])
    assert layer.free_dims == 10
    assert np.linalg.norm(layer.U.T @ layer.U - np.eye(6)) < 1e-12
    before = layer(x)
    for _ in range(100):
        layer.step(rng.normal(size=(40, 16)), rng.normal(size=(40, 3)), 0.1)
    assert layer.retention_error(x, before) < 1e-10
    assert layer.eps_update < 1e-12
    np.testing.assert_allclose(layer.forward_addition_only(x), layer(x), atol=1e-12)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("violation", ["quantization", "rank", "drift"])
def test_hypothesis_violations(violation):
    rng, layer, x = fixture()
    if violation == "rank":
        layer.rank_max = 2
    layer.consolidate(x)
    before = layer(x)
    for _ in range(30):
        layer.step(rng.normal(size=(40, 16)), rng.normal(size=(40, 3)), 0.1)
    if violation == "quantization":
        layer.dW[:] = np.round(layer.dW)
    if violation == "drift":
        x = x @ np.linalg.qr(rng.normal(size=(16, 16)))[0]
    assert layer.retention_error(x, before) > 1e-4


def test_saturation_and_expansion():
    layer = ProtectedLinear(8, 2)
    layer.consolidate(np.eye(8))
    before = layer.dW.copy()
    layer.step(np.eye(8), np.ones((8, 2)))
    np.testing.assert_allclose(before, layer.dW, atol=1e-14)
    assert layer.free_dims == 0
    reference = layer(np.eye(8))
    layer.expand(3)
    np.testing.assert_allclose(reference, layer(np.pad(np.eye(8), ((0, 0), (0, 3)))))
    assert layer.free_dims == 3


def test_invalid_and_immutable():
    layer = ProtectedLinear(3, 2)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        layer.W_base[0, 0] = 0
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        layer.consolidate(np.empty((0, 3)))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        layer.step(np.ones((2, 3)), np.ones((2, 1)))


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: tests/test_reasoning_runtime.py
========================================================================================
import numpy as np
import pytest
from nova.reasoning import (
    Calculator,
    grid_search,
    hierarchical_route,
)
from nova.runtime import CognitiveRuntime, RuntimeConfig
from nova.values import InternalState, OutputPolicy, SIMULATION_LABEL


def test_calculator_verification_and_rejection():
    c = Calculator()
    assert c("(3+7)*2**3/5") == 16
    for bad in ["__import__('os').system('true')", "2**100000", "[1][0]", "1/0"]:
        with pytest.raises((ValueError, ZeroDivisionError)):
            c(bad)


def test_search_budget_and_hierarchy():
    grid = np.zeros((8, 8), dtype=bool)
    grid[3, :7] = True
    assert not grid_search(grid, (0, 0), (7, 7), 1).verified
    result = hierarchical_route(grid, (0, 0), [(0, 7), (7, 7)], 200)
    assert result.verified
    for a, b in zip(result.path[:-1], result.path[1:]):
        assert np.abs(np.subtract(a, b)).sum() == 1


def test_checkpoint_continuation_and_idle(tmp_path):
    config = RuntimeConfig(seed=2, planning=True, neuromodulation=True, sleep_every=4)
    model = CognitiveRuntime(config)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(1)
    for _ in range(8):
        model.tick(rng.normal(size=12))
    model.save(tmp_path / "state.npz")
    restored = CognitiveRuntime.load(tmp_path / "state.npz")
    for _ in range(8):
        x = rng.normal(size=12)
        a = model.tick(x)
        b = restored.tick(x)
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(a["action"], b["action"])
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(model.continual.dW, restored.continual.dW)
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(model.cascade.state, restored.cascade.state)
    for _ in range(200):
        restored.tick()
    assert np.isfinite(restored.z).all() and np.linalg.norm(restored.z) > 0
    assert np.linalg.norm(restored.z) < 5


def test_noise_curiosity_and_output_gate():
    state = InternalState()
    for _ in range(100):
        state.update(1.0, 0.1, 0.5)
    assert state.curiosity == 0
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        OutputPolicy.render_claims([{"text": "invented"}])
    with pytest.raises(PermissionError):
        OutputPolicy.render_claims([], identity_mode=True)
    assert SIMULATION_LABEL in OutputPolicy.render_claims(
        [], identity_mode=True, consented=True
    )


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: tests/test_research.py
========================================================================================
import numpy as np
import torch
from nova.research import (
    PhaseOscillatorLayer,
    QuadraticEquilibriumPropagation,
    SheafCoherence,
)
from nova.core import available


def test_oscillator_coupling_and_native_parity():
    coupled = PhaseOscillatorLayer(seed=1)
    off = PhaseOscillatorLayer(coupling=0, seed=1)
    if available():
        native = PhaseOscillatorLayer(seed=1, backend="rust")
        np.testing.assert_allclose(coupled.step(), native.step(), atol=1e-12)
    for _ in range(1000):
        coupled.step()
        off.step()
    assert coupled.order > 0.95 and coupled.order > off.order


def test_equilibrium_gradient():
    model = QuadraticEquilibriumPropagation()
    x = np.array([0.5, -1.0, 0.2])
    y = np.array([0.2, 0.7])
    estimate = model.gradient(x, y)
    b = torch.tensor(model.B, requires_grad=True)
    state = torch.linalg.solve(torch.tensor(model.A), b @ torch.tensor(x))
    loss = 0.5 * (torch.tensor(model.P) @ state - torch.tensor(y)).square().sum()
    loss.backward()
    cosine = np.dot(estimate.ravel(), b.grad.numpy().ravel()) / (
        np.linalg.norm(estimate) * np.linalg.norm(b.grad.numpy())
    )
    assert cosine > 0.999
    assert min(model.last_iterations) > 0


def test_sheaf_diffusion():
    model = SheafCoherence(3, 2, [(0, 1), (1, 2)])
    x = np.arange(6).reshape(3, 2)
    assert model.energy(model.diffuse(x)) < model.energy(x)
    assert np.linalg.eigvalsh(model.L).min() > -1e-12
